oṃnamaḥśukrabṛhaspatibhyāṃ --
01101|pṛthivyā lābhe pālane ca yāvantyarthaśāstrāṇi pūrvācāryaiḥ prasthāpitāni prāyaśastāni saṃhṛtyaikamidamarthaśāstraṃ kṛtam ||
01102| tasyāyaṃ prakaraṇādhikaraṇasamuddeśaḥ ||
01103  vidyāsamuddeśaḥ, vṛddhasamuddeśaḥ, indriyajayaḥ, amātyotpattiḥ, mantripurohitotpattiḥ, upadhābhiḥ śaucāśaucajñānamamātyānām, - -
01103  gūḍhapuruṣapraṇidhiḥ, svaviṣaye kṛtyākṛtyapakṣarakṣaṇam, paraviṣaye kṛtyākṛtyapakṣopagrahaḥ, -
01103  mantrādhikāraḥ, dūtapraṇidhiḥ, rājaputrarakṣaṇam, aparuddhavṛttam, aparuddhe vṛttiḥ, rājapraṇidhiḥ, niśāntapraṇidhiḥ, ātmarakṣitakam, --, iti vinayādhikārikaṃ prathamamadhikaraṇam ||
01104  janapadaniveśaḥ, bhūmicchidrāpidhānam, durgavidhānam, durganiveśaḥ, sannidhātṛnicayakarma, samāhartṛsamudayaprasthāpanam, akṣapaṭale gāṇanikyādhikāraḥ, -
01104  samudayasya yuktāpahṛtasya pratyānayanam, upayuktaparīkṣā, śāsanādhikāraḥ, kośapraveśyaratnaparīkṣā, ākarakarmāntapravartanam, akṣaśālāyāṃ suvarṇādhyakṣaḥ -
01104  viśikhāyāṃ sauvarṇikapracāraḥ, koṣṭhāgārādhyakṣaḥ, paṇyādhyakṣaḥ, kupyādhyakṣaḥ, āyudhādhyakṣaḥ, tulāmānapautavam, -
01104  deśakālamānam, śulkādhyakṣaḥ, sūtrādhyakṣaḥ, sītādhyakṣaḥ, surādhyakṣaḥ, sūnādhyakṣaḥ, gaṇikādhyakṣaḥ, -
01104  nāvadhyakṣaḥ, goadhyakṣaḥ, aśvādhyakṣaḥ, hastyadhyakṣaḥ, rathādhyakṣaḥ, pattyadhyakṣaḥ, senāpatipracāraḥ, mudrādhyakṣaḥ, vivītādhyakṣaḥ, samāhartṛpracāraḥ, -
01104  gṛhapatikavaidehakatāpasavyañjanāḥ praṇidhayaḥ, nāgarikapraṇidhiḥ -- ityadhyakṣapracāro dvitīyamadhikaraṇam ||
01105  vyavahārasthāpanā, vivādapadanibandhaḥ, vivāhasamyuktam, dāyavibhāgaḥ, vāstukam, samayasya anapākarma, ṛṇādānam, aupanidhikam, dāsakarmakarakalpaḥ, -
01105  sambhūya samutthānam, vikrītakrītānuśayaḥ, dattasya anapākarma, asvāmivikrayaḥ, svasvāmisambandhaḥ, sāhasam, vākpāruṣyam, daṇḍapāruṣyam, dyūtasamāhvayam, prakīrṇakaṃ - iti, dharmasthīyaṃ tṛtīyamadhikaraṇam ||
01106  kārukarakṣaṇam, vaidehakarakṣaṇam, upanipātapratīkāraḥ, gūḍhājīvināṃ rakṣā, siddhavyañjanairmāṇavaprakāśanam, śaṅkārūpakarmābhigrahaḥ, -
01106  āśumṛtakaparīkṣā, vākyakarmānuyogaḥ, sarvādhikaraṇarakṣaṇaṃ -
01106  ekāṅgavadhaniṣkrayaḥ, śuddhaścitraśca daṇḍa kalpaḥ, kanyāprakarma, aticāradaṇḍāḥ - iti kaṇṭakaśodhanaṃ caturthamadhikaraṇam ||
01107| dāṇḍakarmikam, kośābhisaṃharaṇam, bhṛtyabharaṇīyam, anujīvivṛttam, samayācārikam, rājyapratisaṃdhānam, ekāiśvaryaṃ - iti yogavṛttaṃ pañcamamadhikaraṇam ||
01108| prakṛtisampadaḥ, śamavyāyāmikaṃ - iti maṇḍalayoniḥ ṣaṣṭhamadhikaraṇam ||
01109  ṣāḍguṇyasamuddeśaḥ, kṣayasthānavṛddhiniścayaḥ, saṃśrayavṛttiḥ, samahīnajyāyasāṃ guṇābhiniveśaḥ, hīnasaṃdhayaḥ, vigṛhya āsanam, saṃdhāya āsanam, vigṛhya yānam, saṃdhāya yānam, -
01109  sambhūya prayāṇam, yātavyāmitrayorabhigrahacintā, kṣayalobhavirāgahetavaḥ prakṛtīnām, sāmavāyikaviparimarśaḥ, -
01109  saṃhita prayāṇikam, paripaṇitāparipaṇitāpasṛtāḥ saṃdhayaḥ, dvaidhībhāvikāḥ saṃdhivikramāḥ, yātavyavṛttiḥ, anugrāhyamitraviśeṣāḥ, -
01109  mitrahiraṇyabhūmikarmasaṃdhayaḥ, pārṣṇigrāhacintā, hīnaśaktipūraṇam, balavatā vigṛhya uparodhahetavaḥ, daṇḍopanatavṛttam, -
01109  daṇḍopanāyivṛttam, saṃdhikarma, samādhimokṣaḥ, madhyamacaritam, udāsīnacaritam, maṇḍalacaritaṃ - iti ṣāḍguṇyaṃ saptamamadhikaraṇam ||
01110| prakṛtivyasanavargaḥ, rājarājyayorvyasanacintā, puruṣavyasanavargaḥ, pīḍanavargaḥ, stambhavargaḥ, kośasaṅgavargaḥ, mitravyasanavargaḥ - iti vyasanādhikārikamaṣṭamamadhikaraṇam ||
01111  śaktideśakālabalābalajñānam, yātrākālāḥ, balopādānakālāḥ, sannāhaguṇāḥ, pratibalakarma, paścātkopacintā, bāhyābhyantaraprakṛtikopapratīkārāḥ -
01111  kṣayavyayalābhaviparimarśaḥ, bāhyābhyantarāścāpadaḥ, duṣyaśatrusamyuktāḥ, arthānarthasaṃśayayuktāḥ, tāsāmupāyavikalpajāḥ siddhayaḥ - ityabhiyāsyatkarma navamamadhikaraṇam ||
01112| skandhāvāraniveśaḥ, skandhāvāraprayāṇam, balavyasanāvaskandakālarakṣaṇam, kūṭayuddhavikalpāḥ, svasainyotsāhanam, svabalānyabalavyāyogaḥ, yuddhabhūmayaḥ, pattyaśvarathahastikarmāṇi, pakṣakakṣorasyānāṃ balāgrato vyūhavibhāgaḥ, sāraphalgubalavibhāgaḥ, pattyaśvarathahastiyuddhāni,
daṇḍabhogamaṇḍalāsaṃhatavyūhavyūhanam, tasya prativyūhasthāpanaṃ - iti sāṃgrāmikaṃ daśamamadhikaraṇam ||
01113| bhedopādānāni, upāṃśudaṇḍāḥ - iti saṃghavṛttamekādaśamadhikaraṇam ||
01114| dūtakarma, mantrayuddham, senāmukhyavadhaḥ, maṇḍalaprotsāhanam, śastrāgnirasapraṇidhayaḥ, vīvadhāsāraprasāravadhaḥ, yogātisaṃdhānam, daṇḍātisaṃdhānam, ekavijayaḥ - ityābalīyasaṃ dvādaśamadhikaraṇam ||
01115| upajāpaḥ, yogavāmanam, apasarpapraṇidhiḥ, paryupāsanakarma, avamardaḥ, labdhapraśamanaṃ - iti durgalambhopāyastrayodaśamadhikaraṇam ||
01116| parabalaghātaprayogaḥ, pralambhanam, svabalopaghātapratīkāraḥ - ityaupaniṣadikaṃ caturdaśamadhikaraṇam ||
01117| tantrayuktayaḥ - iti tantrayuktiḥ pañcadaśamadhikaraṇam ||
01118| śāstrasamuddeśaḥ pañcadaśādhikaraṇāni sāśītiprakaraṇaśataṃ sa-pañcāśadadhyāyaśataṃ ṣaṭślokasahasrāṇīti ||
01119  sukhagrahaṇavijñeyaṃ tattvārthapadaniścitam |
01119  kauṭilyena kṛtaṃ śāstraṃ vimuktagranthavistaram ||E

(Chap2, ṣection1: Enumerationof the sciences)
((i) Establishing(the necessityof) philosophy)
01201| ānvīkṣikī trayī vārttā daṇḍanītiśceti vidyāḥ ||
01202| trayī vārttā daṇḍa nītiśceti mānavāḥ ||
01203| trayī viśeṣo hyānvīkṣikīti ||
01204| vārttā daṇḍanītiśceti bārhaspatyāḥ ||
01205| saṃvaraṇamātraṃ hi trayī lokayātrāvida iti ||
01206| daṇḍanītirekā vidyetyauśanasāḥ ||
01207| tasyāṃ hi sarvavidyārambhāḥ pratibaddhā iti ||
01208| catasra eva vidyā iti kauṭilyaḥ ||
01209| tābhirdharmārthau yadvidyāttadvidyānāṃ vidyātvam ||
01210| sāṃkhyaṃ yogo lokāyataṃ cetyānvīkṣikī ||
01211| dharmādharmau trayyāmarthānarthau vārttāyāṃ nayānayau daṇḍanītyāṃ balābale ca etāsāṃ hetubhiranvīkṣamāṇā lokasya upakaroti vyasanebhyudaye ca buddhimavasthāpayati prajñāvākyakriyāvaiśāradyaṃ ca karoti ||
01212  pradīpaḥ sarvavidyānāmupāyaḥ sarvakarmaṇām |
01212  āśrayaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ śaśvadānvīkṣikī matā ||E

(Chap3, ṣection1, (ī) Establishing(the necessityof) the Vedicḷore)
01301| sāmargyajurvedāstrayastrayī ||
01302| atharvavedetihāsavedau ca vedāḥ ||
01303| śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ niruktaṃ chandovicitirjyotiṣamiti cāṅgāni ||
01304| eṣa trayīdharmaścaturṇāṃ varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca svadharmasthāpanādaupakārikaḥ ||
01305| svadharmo brāhmaṇasya adhyayanamadhyāpanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ dānaṃ pratigrahaśca ||
01306| kṣatriyasyādhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ dānaṃ śastrājīvo bhūtarakṣaṇaṃ ca ||
01307| vaiśyasyādhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ dānaṃ kṛṣipāśupālye vaṇijyā ca ||
01308| śūdrasya dvijātiśuśrūṣā vārttā kārukuśīlavakarma ca ||
01309| gṛhasthasya svadharmājīvastulyairasamānarṣibhirvaivāhyamṛtugāmitvaṃ devapitratithipūjā bhṛtyeṣu tyāgaḥ śeṣabhojanaṃ ca ||
01310| brahmacāriṇaḥ svādhyāyo agnikāryābhiṣekau bhaikṣavratitvamācārye prāṇāntikī vṛttistadabhāve guruputre sa-brahmacāriṇi vā ||
01311| vānaprasthasya brahmacaryaṃ bhūmau śayyā jaṭājinadhāraṇamagnihotrābhiṣekau devatāpitratithipūjā vanyaścāhāraḥ ||
01312| parivrājakasya jitendriyatvamanārambho niṣkiṃcanatvaṃ saṅgatyāgo bhaikṣavratamanekatrāraṇye ca vāso bāhyābhyantaraṃ ca śaucam ||
01313| sarveṣāmahiṃsā satyaṃ śaucamanasūya ānṛśaṃsyaṃ kṣamā ca ||
01314| svadharmaḥ svargāyānantyāya ca ||
01315| tasyātikrame lokaḥ saṃkarāducchidyeta ||
01316  tasmātsvadharmaṃ bhūtānāṃ rājā na vyabhicārayet |
01316  svadharmaṃ saṃdadhāno hi pretya ceha ca nandati ||
01317  vyavasthitāryamaryādaḥ kṛtavarṇāśramasthitiḥ |
01317  trayyābhirakṣito lokaḥ prasīdati na sīdati ||E

((īi) Establishing(the necessityof) Economics, and(iv) the ṣcience of ড়olitics)
01401| kṛṣipāśupālye vaṇijyā ca vārtā, dhānyapaśuhiraṇyakupyaviṣṭipradānādaupakārikī ||
01402| tayā svapakṣaṃ parapakṣaṃ ca vaśīkaroti kośadaṇḍābhyām ||
01403| ānvīkṣikī trayī vārttānāṃ yogakṣemasādhano daṇḍaḥ, tasya nītirdaṇḍa nītiḥ, alabdhalābhārthā labdhaparirakṣaṇī rakṣitavivardhanī vṛddhasya tīrthe pratipādanī ca ||
01404| tasyāmāyattā lokayātrā ||
01405| "tasmāllokayātrārthī nityamudyatadaṇḍaḥ syāt ||
01406| na hyevaṃvidhaṃ vaśopanayanamasti bhūtānāṃ yathā daṇḍaḥ |" ityācāryāḥ ||
01407| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
01408| tīkṣṇadaṇḍo hi bhūtānāmudvejanīyo bhavati ||
01409| mṛdudaṇḍaḥ paribhūyate ||
01410| yathārhadaṇḍaḥ pūjyate ||
01411| suvijñātapraṇīto hi daṇḍaḥ prajā dharmārthakāmairyojayati ||
01412| duṣpraṇītaḥ kāmakrodhābhyāmavajñānādvā vānaprasthaparivrājakānapi kopayati, kiṃṅga punargṛhasthān ||
01413| apraṇītastu mātsyanyāyamudbhāvayati ||
01414| balīyānabalaṃ hi grasate daṇḍadharābhāve ||
01415| sa tena guptaḥ prabhavati iti ||
01416  caturvarṇāśramo loko rājñā daṇḍena pālitaḥ |
01416  svadharmakarmābhirato vartate sveṣu vartmasu ||E

(ṣection2: āssociation withelders)
01501| tasmāddaṇḍamūlāstisro vidyāḥ ||
01502| vinayamūlo daṇḍaḥ prāṇabhṛtāṃ yogakṣemāvahaḥ ||
01503| kṛtakaḥ svābhāvikaśca vinayaḥ ||
01504| kriyā hi dravyaṃ vinayati nādravyam ||
01505| śuśrūṣā śravaṇagrahaṇadhāraṇavijñānohāpohatattvābhiniviṣṭabuddhiṃ vidyā vinayati netaram ||
01506| vidyānāṃ tu yathāsvamācāryaprāmāṇyādvinayo niyamaśca ||
01507| vṛttacaulakarmā lipiṃ saṃkhyānaṃ copayuñjīta ||
01508| vṛttopanayanastrayīmānvīkṣikīṃ ca śiṣṭebhyo vārttāmadhyakṣebhyo daṇḍanītiṃ vaktṛprayoktṛbhyaḥ ||
01509| brahmacaryaṃ ca ṣoḍaśādvarṣāt ||
01510| ato godānaṃ dārakarma cāsya ||
01511| nityaśca vidyāvṛddhasamyogo vinayavṛddhyartham, tanmūlatvādvinayasya ||
01512| pūrvamaharbhāgaṃ hastyaśvarathapraharaṇavidyāsu vinayaṃ gacchet |
01513| paścimamitihāsaśravaṇe ||
01514| purāṇamitivṛttamākhyāyikodāharaṇaṃ dharmaśāstramarthaśāstraṃ cetītihāsaḥ ||
01515| śeṣamahorātrabhāgamapūrvagrahaṇaṃ gṛhītaparicayaṃ ca kuryāt, agṛhītānāmābhīkṣṇyaśravaṇaṃ ca ||
01516| śrutāddhi prajñopajāyate prajñāyā yogo yogādātmavatteti vidyānāṃ sāmarthyam ||
01517  vidyāvinīto rājā hi prajānāṃ vinaye rataḥ |
01517  ananyāṃ pṛthivīṃ bhuṅkte sarvabhūtahite rataḥ ||E

(ṣection3: Controloverthe senses, (i) Castingoutthe groupof six enemies)
01601| vidyā vinayaheturindriyajayaḥ kāmakrodhalobhamānamadaharṣatyāgātkāryaḥ ||
01602| karṇatvagakṣijihvāghrāṇendriyāṇāṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandheṣvavipratipattirindriyajayaḥ, śāstrānuṣṭhānaṃ vā ||
01603| kṛtsnaṃ hi śāstramidamindriyajayaḥ ||
01604| tadviruddhavṛttiravaśyendriyaścāturanto api rājā sadyo vinaśyati ||
01605| yathā dāṇḍakyo nāma bhojaḥ kāmādbrāhmaṇakanyāmabhimanyamānaḥ sa-bandhurāṣṭro vinanāśa, karālaśca vaidehaḥ ||
01606| kopājjanamejayo brāhmaṇeṣu vikrāntaḥ, tālajaṅghaśca bhṛguṣu ||
01607| lobhādailaścāturvarṇyamatyāhārayamāṇaḥ, sauvīraścājabinduḥ |
01608| mānādrāvaṇaḥ paradārānaprayacchan, duryodhano rājyādaṃśaṃ ca ||
01609| madāddambhodbhavo bhūtāvamānī, haihayaścārjunaḥ ||
01610| harṣādvātāpiragastyamatyāsādayan, vṛṣṇisaṃghaśca dvaipāyanamiti ||
01611  ete cānye ca bahavaḥ śatruṣaḍvargamāśritāḥ |
01611  sa-bandhurāṣṭrā rājāno vineśurajitendriyāḥ ||
01612  śatruṣaḍvargamutsṛjya jāmadagnyo jitendriyaḥ |
01612  ambarīṣaśca nābhāgo bubhujāte ciraṃ mahīm ||E

((ī) ṭhe life of a sage-like king)
01701| tasmādariṣaḍvargatyāgenendriyajayaṃ kurvīta, vṛddhasamyogena prajñām, cāreṇa cakṣuḥ, utthānena yogakṣemasādhanam, kāryānuśāsanena svadharmasthāpanam, vinayaṃ vidyopadeśena, lokapriyatvamarthasamyogena vṛttim ||
01702| evaṃ vaśyendriyaḥ parastrīdravyahiṃsāśca varjayet, svapnaṃ laulyamanṛtamuddhataveṣatvamanarthyasamyogamadharmasamyuktamanarthasamyuktaṃ ca vyavahāram ||
01703| dharmārthāvirodhena kāmaṃ seveta, na nihsukhaḥ syāt ||
01704| samaṃ vā trivargamanyonyānubaddham ||
01705| eko hyatyāsevito dharmārthakāmānāmātmānamitarau ca pīḍayati ||
01706| arthaiva pradhāneti kauṭilyaḥ ||
01707| arthamūlau hi dharmakāmāviti ||
01708| maryādāṃ sthāpayedācāryānamātyān vā, yainamapāya sthānebhyo vārayeyuḥ, chāyānālikāpratodena vā rahasi pramādyantamabhitudeyuḥ ||
01709  sahāyasādhyaṃ rājatvaṃ cakramekaṃ na vartate |
01709  kurvīta sacivāṃstasmātteṣāṃ ca śṛṇuyānmatam ||E

(āppointmentof ministers)
01801| "sahādhyāyino amātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭaśaucasāmarthyatvāt" iti bhāradvājaḥ ||
01802| "te hyasya viśvāsyā bhavanti" iti ||
01803| neti viśālākṣaḥ ||
01804| "sahakrīḍitatvātparibhavantyenam ||
01805| ye hyasya guhyasadharmāṇastānamātyān kurvīta, samānaśīlavyasanatvāt ||
01806| te hyasya marmajñabhayānnāparādhyanti" iti ||
01807| "sādhāraṇaiṣa doṣaḥ" iti pārāśarāḥ ||
01808| "teṣāmapi marmajñabhayātkṛtākṛtānyanuvarteta ||
01809  yāvadbhyo guhyamācaṣṭe janebhyaḥ puruṣādhipaḥ |
01809  avaśaḥ karmaṇā tena vaśyo bhavati tāvatām ||
01810| yainamāpatsu prāṇābādhayuktāsvanugṛhṇīyustānamātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭānurāgatvāt" iti ||
01811| neti piśunaḥ ||
01812| "bhaktireṣā na buddhiguṇaḥ ||
01813| saṃkhyātārtheṣu karmasu niyuktā ye yathādiṣṭamarthaṃ saviśeṣaṃ vā kuryustānamātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭaguṇatvāt" iti ||
01814| neti kauṇapadantaḥ ||
01815| "anyairamātyaguṇairayuktā hyete ||
01816| pitṛpaitāmahānamātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭāvadānatvāt ||
01817| te hyenamapacarantamapi na tyajanti, sagandhatvāt ||
01818| amānuṣeṣvapi caitaddṛśyate ||
01819| gāvo hyasagandhaṃ gogaṇamatikramya sagandheṣvevāvatiṣṭhante" iti ||
01820| neti vātavyādhiḥ ||
01821| "te hyasya sarvamavagṛhya svāmivatpracaranti ||
01822| tasmānnītivido navānamātyān kurvīta ||
01823| navāstu yamasthāne daṇḍadharaṃ manyamānā nāparādhyanti" iti ||
01824| neti bāhudantī putraḥ ||
01825| "śāstravidadṛṣṭakarmā karmasu viṣādaṃ gacchet ||
01826| tasmādabhijanaprajñāśaucaśauryānurāgayuktānamātyān kurvīta, guṇaprādhānyāt" iti ||
01827| sarvamupapannamiti kauṭilyaḥ ||
01828| kāryasāmarthyāddhi puruṣasāmarthyaṃ kalpyate ||
01829  sāmarthyaśca -- vibhajyāmātyavibhavaṃ deśakālau ca karma ca |
01829  amātyāḥ sarva evaite kāryāḥ syurna tu mantriṇaḥ ||E

(āppointmentof counsellorsandchaplain)
01901| jānapado abhijātaḥ svavagrahaḥ kṛtaśilpaścakṣuṣmān prājño dhārayiṣṇurdakṣo vāgmī pragalbhaḥ pratipattimānutsāhaprabhāvayuktaḥ kleśasahaḥ śucirmaitro dṛḍhabhaktiḥ śīlabalārogyasattvayuktaḥ stambhacāpalahīnaḥ sampriyo vairāṇāmakartetyamātyasampat ||
01902| ataḥ pādārghaguṇahīnau madhyamāvarau ||
01903| teṣāṃ janapadabhijanamavagrahaṃ cāptataḥ parīkṣeta, samānavidyebhyaḥ śilpaṃ śāstracakṣuṣmattāṃ ca, karmārambheṣu prajñāṃ dhārayiṣṇutāṃ dākṣyaṃ ca, kathāyogeṣu vāgmitvaṃ prāgalbhyaṃ pratibhānavattvaṃ ca, saṃvāsibhyaḥ śīlabalārogyasattvayogamastambhamacāpalaṃ ca, pratyakṣataḥ sampriyatvamavairatvaṃ ca ||
01904| pratyakṣaparokṣānumeyā hi rājavṛttiḥ ||
01905| svayaṃ drṣṭaṃ pratyakṣam ||
01906| paropadiṣṭaṃ parokṣam ||
01907| karmasu kṛtenākṛtāvekṣaṇamanumeyam ||
01908| yaugapadyāttu karmaṇāmanekatvādanekasthatvācca deśakālātyayo mā bhūditi parokṣamamātyaiḥ kārayet || ityamātyakarma |
01909| purohitamuditoditakulaśīlaṃ sāṅge vede daive nimitte daṇḍanītyāṃ cābhivinītamāpadāṃ daivamānuṣīṇāmatharvabhirupāyaiśca pratikartāraṃ kurvīta ||
01910| tamācāryaṃ śiṣyaḥ pitaraṃ putro bhṛtyaḥ svāminamiva cānuvarteta ||
01911  brāhmaṇenaidhitaṃ kṣatraṃ mantrimantrābhimantritam |
01911  jayatyajitamatyantaṃ śāstrānugamaśastritam ||E

(āscertainmentof the integrityorthe absence of integrityof ministerbymeansof secrettests)
011001| mantripurohitasakhaḥ sāmānyeṣvadhikaraṇeṣu sthāpayitvāmātyānupadhābhiḥ śodhayet ||
011002| purohitamayājyayājanādhyāpane niyuktamamṛṣyamāṇaṃ rājāvakṣipet ||
011003| sa sattribhiḥ śapathapūrvamekaikamamātyamupajāpayet- "adhārmiko ayaṃ rājā, sādhu dhārmikamanyamasya tatkulīnamaparuddhaṃ kulyamekapragrahaṃ sāmantamāṭavikamaupapādikaṃ vā pratipādayāmaḥ, sarveṣāmetadrocate, kathaṃ vā tava" iti ||
011004| pratyākhyāne śuciḥ | iti dharmopadhā ||
011005| senāpatirasatpragraheṇāvakṣiptaḥ sattribhirekaikamamātyamupajāpayetlobhanīyenārthena rājavināśāya, "sarveṣāmetadrocate, kathaṃ vā tava" iti ||
011006| pratyākhyāne śuciḥ | ityarthopadhā ||
011007| parivrājikā labdhaviśvāsāntaḥpure kṛtasatkārā mahāmātramekaikamupajapet- "rājamahiṣī tvāṃ kāmayate kṛtasamāgamopāyā, mahānarthaśca te bhaviṣyati" iti ||
011008| pratyākhyāne śuciḥ | iti kāmopadhā ||
011009| prahavaṇanimittameko amātyaḥ sarvānamātyānāvāhayet ||
011010| tenodvegena rājā tānavarundhyāt ||
011011| kāpaṭikaścātra pūrvāvaruddhasteṣāmarthamānāvakṣiptamekaikamamātyamupajapet- "asatpravṛtto ayaṃ rājā, sādhvenaṃ hatvānyaṃ pratipādayāmaḥ, sarveṣāmetadrocate, kathaṃ vā tava" iti ||
011012| pratyākhyāne śuciḥ | iti bhayopadhā ||
011013| tatra dharmopadhāśuddhān dharmasthīyakaṇṭakaśodhaneṣu karmasu sthāpayet, arthopadhāśuddhān samāhartṛsannidhātṛnicayakarmasu, kāmopadhā śuddhān bāhyābhyantaravihārarakṣāsu, bhayopadhāśuddhānāsannakāryeṣu rājñaḥ ||
011014| sarvopadhāśuddhānmantriṇaḥ kuryāt ||
011015| sarvatrāśucīn khanidravyahastivanakarmānteṣu upayojayet ||
011016  trivargabhayasaṃśuddhānamātyān sveṣu karmasu |
011016  adhikuryādyathā śaucamityācāryā vyavasthitāḥ ||
011017  na tveva kuryādātmānaṃ devīṃ vā lakṣyamīśvaraḥ |
011017  śaucahetoramātyānāmetatkauṭilyadarśanam ||
011018  na dūṣaṇamaduṣṭasya viṣeṇevāmbhasaścaret |
011018  kadāciddhi praduṣṭasya nādhigamyeta bheṣajam ||
011019  kṛtā ca kaluṣābuddhirupadhābhiścaturvidhā |
011019  nāgatvāntaṃ nivarteta sthitā sattvavatāṃ dhṛtau ||
011020  tasmādbāhyamadhiṣṭhānaṃ kṛtvā kārye caturvidhe |
011020  śaucāśaucamamātyānāṃ rājā mārgeta sattribhiḥ ||E

(āppointmentof personsin secretservice)
011101| upadhābhiḥ śuddhāmātyavargo gūḍhapuruṣānutpādayetkāpaṭikodāsthitagṛhapatikavaidehakatāpasavyañjanān sattritīṣkṇarasadabhikṣukīśca ||
011102| paramarmajñaḥ pragalbhaśchātraḥ kāpaṭikaḥ ||
011103| tamarthamānābhyāṃ protsāhya mantrī brūyāt- "rājānaṃ māṃ ca pramāṇaṃ kṛtvā yasya yadakuśalaṃ paśyasi tattadānīmeva pratyādiśa" iti ||
011104| pravrajyā pratyavasitaḥ prajñāśaucayukta udāsthitaḥ ||
011105| sa vārttākarmapradiṣṭāyāṃ bhūmau prabhūtahiraṇyāntevāsī karma kārayet ||
011106| karmaphalācca sarvapravrajitānāṃ grāsācchādanāvasathān pratividadhyāt ||
011107| vṛttikāmāṃścopajapet- "etenaiva veṣeṇa rājārthaścaritavyo bhaktavetanakāle copasthātavyam" iti ||
011108| sarvapravrajitāśca svaṃ svaṃ vargamevamupajapeyuḥ ||
011109| karṣako vṛttikṣīṇaḥ prajñāśaucayukto gṛhapatikavyañjanaḥ ||
011110| sa kṛṣikarmapradiṣṭāyāṃ bhūmau - iti samānaṃ pūrveṇa ||
011111| vāṇijako vṛttikṣīṇaḥ prajñāśaucayukto vaidehakavyañjanaḥ ||
011112| sa vaṇikkarmapradiṣṭāyāṃ bhūmau - iti samānaṃ pūrveṇa ||
011113| muṇḍo jaṭilo vā vṛttikāmastāpasavyañjanaḥ ||
011114| sa nagarābhyāśe prabhūtamuṇḍajaṭilāntevāsī śākaṃ yavamuṣṭiṃ vā māsadvimāsāntaraṃ prakāśamaśnīyāt, gūḍhamiṣṭamāhāram ||
011115| vaidehakāntevāsinaścainaṃ samiddhayogairarcayeyuḥ ||
011116| śiṣyāścāsyāvedayeyuḥ - "asau siddhaḥ sāmedhikaḥ" iti ||
011117| samedhāśāstibhiścābhigatānāmaṅgavidyayā śiṣyasaṃjñābhiśca karmāṇyabhijane avasitānyādiśet- alpalābhamagnidāhaṃ corabhayaṃ dūṣyavadhaṃ tuṣṭidānaṃ videśapravṛttijñānam, "idamadya śvo vā bhaviṣyati, idaṃ vā rājā kariṣyati" iti ||
011118| tadasya gūḍhāḥ sattriṇaśca sampādayeyuḥ ||
011119| sattvaprajñāvākyaśaktisampannānāṃ rājabhāgyamanuvyāharet, mantrisamyogaṃ ca brūyāt ||
011120| mantrī caiṣāṃ vṛttikarmabhyāṃ viyateta ||
011121| ye ca kāraṇādabhikruddhāstānarthamānābhyāṃ śamayet, akāraṇakruddhāṃstūṣṇīṃ daṇḍena, rājadviṣṭakāriṇaśca ||
011122  pūjitāścārthamānābhyāṃ rājñā rājopajīvinām |
011122  jānīyuḥ śaucamityetāḥ pañcasaṃsthāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||E

(āppointmentof rovingspiesṛulesforsecretservants)
011201| ye cāpyasmabandhino avaśyabhartavyāste lakṣaṇamaṅgavidyāṃ jambhakavidyāṃ māyāgatamāśramadharmaṃ nimittamantaracakramityadhīyānāḥ sattriṇaḥ, saṃsargavidyāṃ ca ||
011202| ye janapade śūrāstyaktātmāno hastinaṃ vyālaṃ vā dravyahetoḥ pratiyodhayeyuste tīkṣṇāḥ ||
011203| ye bandhuṣu nihsnehāḥ krūrā alasāśca te rasadāḥ ||
011204| parivrājikā vṛttikāmā daridrā vidhavā pragalbhā brāhmaṇyantaḥpure kṛtasatkārā mahāmātrakulānyabhigacchet ||
011205| etayā muṇḍā vṛṣalyo vyākhyātāḥ || iti saṃcārāḥ |
011206| tān rājā svaviṣaye mantripurohitasenāpatiyuvarājadauvārikāntarvaṃśikapraśāstṛsamāhartṛsannidhātṛpradeṣṭṛnāyakapauravyāvahārikakārmāntikamantripariṣadadhyakṣadaṇḍadurgāntapālāṭavikeṣu śraddheyadeśaveṣaśilpabhāṣābhijanāpadeśān bhaktitaḥ sāmarthyayogāccāpasarpayet ||
011207| teṣāṃ bāhyaṃ cāraṃ chatrabhṛṅgāravyajanapādukāsanayānavāhanopagrāhiṇastīkṣṇā vidyuḥ ||
011208| taṃ sattriṇaḥ saṃsthāsvarpayeyuḥ ||
011209| sūdārālikasnāpakasaṃvāhakāstarakakalpakaprasādhakodakaparicārakā rasadāḥ kubjavāmanakirātamūkabadhirajaḍāndhacchadmāno naṭanartakagāyanavādakavāgjīvanakuśīlavāḥ striyaścābhyantaraṃ cāraṃ vidyuḥ ||
011210| taṃ bhikṣkyaḥ saṃsthāsvaprayeyuḥ ||
011211| saṃsthānāmantevāsinaḥ saṃjñālipibhiścārasaṃcāraṃ kuryuḥ ||
011212| na cānyonyaṃ saṃsthāste vā vidyuḥ ||
011213| bhikṣukīpratiṣedhe dvāhsthaparamparā mātāpitṛvyañjanāḥ śilpakārikāḥ kuśīlavā dāsyo vā gītapāṭhyavādyabhāṇḍagūḍhalekhyasaṃjñābhirvā cāraṃ nirhareyuḥ||
011214| dīrgharogonmādāgnirasavisargeṇa vā gūḍhanirgamanam ||
011215| trayāṇāmekavākye sampratyayaḥ ||
011216| teṣāmabhīkṣṇavinipāte tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ ||
011217| kaṇṭakaśodhanoktāścāpasarpāḥ pareṣu kṛtavetanā vaseyurasampātinaścārārtham ||
011218| ta ubhayavetanāḥ ||
011219  gṛhītaputradārāṃśca kuryādubhayavetanān |
011219  tāṃścāriprahitān vidyātteṣāṃ śaucaṃ ca tadvidhaiḥ ||
011220  evaṃ śatrau ca mitre ca madhyame cāvapeccarān |
011220  udāsīne ca teṣāṃ ca tīrtheṣvaṣṭādaśasvapi ||
011221  antargṛhacarāsteṣāṃ kubjavāmanapaṇḍakāḥ |
011221  śilpavatyaḥ striyo mūkāścitrāśca mlecchajātayaḥ ||
011222  durgeṣu vaṇijaḥ saṃsthā durgānte siddhatāpasāḥ |
011222  karṣakodāsthitā rāṣṭre rāṣṭrānte vrajavāsinaḥ ||
011223  vane vanacarāḥ kāryāḥ śramaṇāṭavikādayaḥ |
011223  parapravṛttijñānārthāḥ śīghrāścāraparamparāḥ ||
011224  parasya caite boddhavyāstādṛśaireva tādṛśāḥ |
011224  cārasaṃcāriṇaḥ saṃsthā gūḍhāścāgūḍhasaṃjñitāḥ ||
011225  akṛtyān kṛtyapakṣīyairdarśitān kāryahetubhiḥ |
011225  parāpasarpajñānārthaṃ mukhyānanteṣu vāsayet ||E

(Keepinga watchoverthe seducible andnon-seducible partiesinone'sown territory)

011301| kṛtamahāmātrāpasarpaḥ paurajānapadānapasarpayet ||
011302| sattriṇo dvandvinastīrthasabhāpūgajanasamavāyeṣu vivādaṃ kuryuḥ ||
011303| "sarvaguṇasampannaścāyaṃ rājā śrūyate, na cāsya kaścidguṇo dṛśyate yaḥ paurajānapadān daṇḍakarābhyāṃ pīḍayati" iti ||
011304| tatra yenupraśaṃseyustānitarastaṃ ca pratiṣedhayet ||
011305| "mātsyanyāyābhibhūtāḥ prajā manuṃ vaivasvataṃ rājānaṃ cakrire ||
011306| dhānyaṣaḍbhāgaṃ paṇyadaśabhāgaṃ hiraṇyaṃ cāsya bhāgadheyaṃ prakalpayām-āsuḥ ||
011307| tena bhṛtā rājānaḥ prajānāṃ yogakṣemāvahāḥ ||
011308| teṣāṃ kilbiṣamadaṇḍakarā harantyayogakṣemāvahāśca prajānām ||
011309| tasmāduñchaṣaḍbhāgamāraṇyakāpi nirvapanti - "tasyaitadbhāgadheyaṃ yoasmān gopāyati" iti ||
011310| indrayamasthānametadrājānaḥ pratyakṣaheḍaprasādāḥ ||
011311| tānavamanyamānān daivoapi daṇḍaḥ spṛśati ||
011312| tasmādrājāno nāvamantavyāḥ ||
011313| ityevaṃ kṣudrakān pratiṣedhayet ||
011314| kiṃvadantīṃ ca vidyuḥ ||
011315| ye cāsya dhānyapaśuhiraṇyānyājīvanti, tairupakurvanti vyasanebhyudaye vā, kupitaṃ bandhuṃ rāṣṭraṃ vā vyāvartayanti, amitramāṭavikaṃ vā pratiṣedhayanti, teṣāṃ muṇḍajaṭilavyañjanāstuṣṭātuṣṭatvaṃ vidyuḥ ||
011316| tuṣṭān bhūyoarthamānābhyāṃ pūjayet ||
011317| atuṣṭāṃstuṣṭihetostyāgena sāmnā ca prasādayet ||
011318| parasparādvā bhedayedenān, sāmantāṭavikatatkulīnāparuddhebhyaśca ||
011319| tathāpyatuṣyato daṇḍakarasādhanādhikāreṇa janapadavidveṣaṃ grāhayet ||
011320| viviṣṭānupāṃśudaṇḍena janapadakopena vā sādhayet ||
011321| guptaputradārānākarakarmānteṣu vā vāsayetpareṣāmāspadabhayāt ||
011322| kruddhalubdhabhītamāninastu pareṣāṃ kṛtyāḥ ||
011323| teṣāṃ kārtāntikanaimittikamauhūrtikavyañjanāḥ parasparābhisambandhamamitrāṭavikasambandhaṃ vā vidyuḥ ||
011324| tuṣṭānarthamānābhyāṃ pūjayet |
011325| atuṣṭān sāmadānabhedadaṇḍaiḥ sādhayet ||
011326  evaṃ svaviṣaye kṛtyānakṛtyāṃśca vicakṣaṇaḥ |
011326  paropajāpātsamrakṣetpradhānān kṣudrakānapi ||E

(ḹinningoverthe seducible andnon-seducible partiesin the enemy"sterritory)

011401| kṛtyākṛtyapakṣopagrahaḥ svaviṣaye vyākhyātaḥ, paraviṣaye vācyaḥ ||
011402| saṃśrutyārthān vipralabdhaḥ, tulyakāriṇoḥ śilpe vopakāre vā vimānitaḥ, vallabhāvaruddhaḥ, samāhūya parājitaḥ, pravāsopataptaḥ, kṛtvā vyayamalabdhakāryaḥ, svadharmāddāyādyādvoparuddhaḥ, mānādhikārābhyāṃ bhraṣṭaḥ, kulyairantarhitaḥ, prasabhābhimṛṣṭastrīkaḥ, kārābhinyastaḥ, paroktadaṇḍitaḥ, mithyācāravāritaḥ, sarvasvam
āhāritaḥ, bandhanaparikliṣṭaḥ, pravāsitabandhuḥ - iti kruddhavargaḥ ||
011403| svayamupahataḥ, viprakṛtaḥ, pāpakarmābhikhyātaḥ, tulyadoṣadaṇḍenodvignaḥ, paryāttabhūmiḥ, daṇḍenopanataḥ, sarvādhikaraṇasthaḥ, sahasopacitārthaḥ, tatkulīnopāśaṃsuḥ, pradviṣṭo rājñā, rājadveṣī ca - iti bhītavargaḥ ||
011404| parikṣīṇaḥ, anyāttasvaḥ, kadaryaḥ, vyasanī, atyāhitavyavahāraśca - iti lubdhavargaḥ ||
011405| ātmasambhāvitaḥ, mānakāmaḥ, śatrupūjāmarṣitaḥ, nīcairupahitaḥ, tīkṣṇaḥ, sāhasikaḥ, bhogenāsaṃtuṣṭaḥ - iti mānivargaḥ ||
011406| teṣāṃ muṇḍajaṭilavyañjanairyo yadbhaktiḥ kṛtyapakṣīyastaṃ tenopajāpayet ||
011407| "yathā madāndho hastī mattenādhiṣṭhito yadyadāsādayati tatsarvaṃ pramṛdnāti, evamayamaśāstracakṣurandho rājā paurajānapadavadhāyābhyutthitaḥ, śakyamasya pratihastiprotsāhanenāpakartum, amarṣaḥ kriyatām" iti kruddhavargamupajāpayet ||
011408| "yathā līnaḥ sarpo yasmādbhayaṃ paśyati tatra viṣamutsṛjati, evamayaṃ rājā jātadoṣāśaṅkastvayi purā krodhaviṣamutsṛjati, anyatra gamyatām" iti bhītavargaṃupajāpayet ||
011409| "yathā śvagaṇināṃ dhenuḥ śvabhyo duhyate na brāhmaṇebhyaḥ, evamayaṃ rājā sattvaprajñāvākyaśaktihīnebhyo duhyate nātmaguṇasampannebhyaḥ, asau rājā puruṣaviśeṣajñaḥ, tatra gamyatām" iti lubdhavargaṃupajāpayet ||
011410| "yathā caṇḍālodapānaścaṇḍālānāmevopabhogyo nānyeṣām, evamayaṃ rājā nīco nīcānāmevopabhogyo na tvadvidhānāmāryāṇām, asau rājā puruṣaviśeṣajñaḥ, tatra gamyatām" iti mānivargamupajāpayet ||
011411  tatheti pratipannāṃstān saṃhitān paṇakarmaṇā |
011411  yojayeta yathāśakti sāpasarpān svakarmasu ||
011412  labheta sāmadānābhyāṃ kṛtyāṃśca parabhūmiṣu |
011412  akṛtyān bhedadaṇḍābhyāṃ paradoṣāṃśca darśayan ||E

(ṭhe topicof counsel)
011501| kṛtasvapakṣaparapakṣopagrahaḥ kāryārambhāṃścintayet ||
011502| mantrapūrvāḥ sarvārambhāḥ ||
011503| taduddeśaḥ saṃvṛtaḥ kathānāmanihśrāvī pakṣibhirapyanālokyaḥ syāt |
011504| śrūyate hi śukasārikābhirmantro bhinnaḥ, śvabhirapyanyaiśca tiryagyonibhiriti ||
011505| tasmānmantroddeśamanāyukto nopagacchet ||
011506| ucchidyeta mantrabhedī ||
011507| mantrabhedo hi dūtāmātyasvāmināmiṅgitākārābhyām ||
011508| iṅgitamanyathāvṛttiḥ ||
011509| ākṛtigrahaṇamākāraḥ ||
011510| tasya saṃvaraṇamāyuktapuruṣarakṣaṇamākāryakālāditi ||
011511| teṣāṃ hi pramādamadasuptapralāpāḥ, kāmādirutsekaḥ, pracchannoavamato vā mantraṃ bhinatti ||
011512| tasmādādrakṣenmantram ||
011513| "mantrabhedo hyayogakṣemakaro rājñastadāyuktapuruṣāṇāṃ ca ||
011514| tasmādguhyameko mantrayeta" iti bhāradvājaḥ ||
011515| "mantriṇāmapi hi mantriṇo bhavanti, teṣāmapyanye ||
011516| saiṣā mantriparamparā mantraṃ bhinatti ||
011517  "tasmānnāsya pare vidyuḥ karma kiṃciccikīrṣitam |
011517  ārabdhārastu jānīyurārabdhaṃ kṛtameva vā ||
011518| "naikasya mantrasiddhirasti" iti viśālākṣaḥ ||
011519| "pratyakṣaparokṣānumeyā hi rājavṛttiḥ ||
011520| anupalabdhasya jñānamupalabdhasya niścitabalādhānamarthadvaidhasya saṃśayacchedanamekadeśadṛṣṭasya śeṣopalabdhiriti mantrisādhyametat ||
011521| tasmādbuddhivṛddhaiḥ sārdhamadhyāsīta mantram ||
011522  "na kaṃcidavamanyeta sarvasya śṛṇuyānmatam |
011522  bālasyāpyarthavadvākyamupayuñjīta paṇḍitaḥ ||"
011523| "etanmantrajñānam, naitanmantrarakṣaṇam" iti pārāśarāḥ ||
011524| "yadasya kāryamabhipretaṃ tatpratirūpakaṃ mantriṇaḥ pṛcchet- "kāryamidamevamāsīt, evaṃ vā yadi bhavet, tatkathaṃ kartavyam" iti ||
011525| te yathā brūyustatkuryāt ||
011526| evaṃ mantropalabdhiḥ saṃvṛtiśca bhavati" iti ||
011527| neti piśunaḥ ||
011528| "mantriṇo hi vyavahitamarthaṃ vṛttamavṛttaṃ vā pṛṣṭā anādareṇa bruvanti prakāśayanti vā ||
011529| sa doṣaḥ ||
011530| tasmātkarmasu ye yeṣvabhipretāstaiḥ saha mantrayeta ||
011531| tairmantrayamāṇo hi mantrasiddhiṃ guptiṃ ca labhate" iti ||
011532| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
011533| anavasthā hyeṣā ||
011534| mantribhistribhiścaturbhirvā saha mantrayeta ||
011535| mantrayamāṇo hyekenārthakṛcchreṣu niścayaṃ nādhigacchet ||
011536| ekaśca mantrī yatheṣṭamanavagrahaścarati ||
011537| dvābhyāṃ mantrayamāṇo dvābhyāṃ saṃhatābhyāmavagṛhyate, vigṛhītābhyāṃ vināśyate ||
011538| tattriṣu catuṣu vā kṛcchreṇopapadyate ||
011539| mahādoṣamupapannaṃ tu bhavati ||
011540| tataḥ pareṣu kṛcchreṇārthaniścayo gamyate, mantro vā rakṣyate ||
011541| deśakālakāryavaśena tvekena saha dvābhyāmeko vā yathāsāmarthyaṃ mantrayeta ||(alterṇative viewsapproved)
011542| karmaṇāmārambhopāyaḥ puruṣadravyasampaddeśakālavibhāgo vinipātapratīkāraḥ kāryasiddhiriti pañcāṅgo mantraḥ ||
011543| tānekaikaśaḥ pṛcchetsamastāṃśca ||
011544| hetubhiścaiṣāṃ matipravivekān vidyāt ||
011545| avāptārthaḥ kālaṃ nātikrāmayet ||
011546| na dīrghakālaṃ mantrayeta, na teṣāṃ pakṣīyairyeṣāmapakuryāt ||
011547| "mantripariṣadaṃ dvādaśāmātyān kurvīta" iti mānavāḥ ||
011548| "ṣoḍaśa" iti bārhaspatyāḥ ||
011549| "viṃśatim" ityauśanasāḥ ||
011550| yathāsāmarthyamiti kauṭilyaḥ ||
011551| te hyasya svapakṣaṃ parapakṣaṃ ca cintayeyuḥ ||
011552| akṛtārambhamārabdhānuṣṭhānamanuṣṭhitaviśeṣaṃ niyogasampadaṃ ca karmaṇāṃ kuryuḥ ||
011553| āsannaiḥ saha karmāṇi paśyet ||
011554| anāsannaiḥ saha pattrasampreṣaṇena mantrayeta ||
011555| indrasya hi mantripariṣadṛṣīṇāṃ sahasram ||
011556| sa taccakṣuḥ ||
011557| tasmādimaṃ dvyakṣaṃ sahasrākṣamāhuḥ ||
011558| ātyayike kārye mantriṇo mantripariṣadaṃ cāhūya brūyāt ||
011559| tatra yadbhūyiṣṭhā brūyuḥ kāryasiddhikaraṃ vā tatkuryāt ||
011560| kurvataśca --
011560  nāsya guhyaṃ pare vidyuśchidraṃ vidyātparasya ca |
011560  gūhetkūrmevāṅgāni yatsyādvivṛtamātmanaḥ ||
011561  yathā hyaśrotriyaḥ śrāddhaṃ na satāṃ bhoktumarhati |
011561  evamaśrutaśāstrārtho na mantraṃ śrotumarhati ||E

(ṛulesforthe envoy)
011601| udvṛttamantro dūtapraṇidhiḥ ||
011602| amātyasampadopeto nisṛṣṭārthaḥ ||

011604| ardhaguṇahīnaḥ śāsanaharaḥ ||
011605| suprativihitayānavāhanapuruṣaparivāpaḥ pratiṣṭheta ||

011608| anīkasthānayuddhapratigrahāpasārabhūmīrātmanaḥ parasya cāvekṣeta ||
011609| durgarāṣṭrapramāṇaṃ sāravṛttigupticchidrāṇi copalabheta ||
011610| parādhiṣṭhānamanujñātaḥ praviśet ||
011611| śāsanaṃ ca yathoktaṃ brūyāt, prāṇābādhepi dṛṣṭe ||

011613| taṃ brūyāt- "dūtamukhā hi rājānaḥ, tvaṃ cānye ca ||

011616| parasyaitadvākyam ||

011618| vasedavisṛṣṭaḥ pūjayā notsiktaḥ ||

011620| vākyamaniṣṭaṃ saheta ||
011621| striyaḥ pānaṃ ca varjayet ||
011622| ekaḥ śayīta ||
011623| suptamattayorhi bhāvajñānaṃ dṛṣṭam ||
011624| kṛtyapakṣopajāpamakṛtyapakṣe gūḍhapraṇidhānaṃ rāgāparāgau bhartari randhraṃ ca prakṛtīnāṃ tāpasavaidehakavyañjanābhyāmupalabheta, tayorantevāsibhiścikitsakapāṣaṇḍavyañjanobhayavetanairvā ||
011625| teṣāmasambhāṣāyāṃ yācakamattonmattasuptapralāpaiḥ puṇyasthānadevagṛhacitralekhyasaṃjñābhirvā cāramupalabheta ||
011626| upalabdhasyopajāpamupeyāt ||

011629| kāryasyāsiddhāvuparudhyamānastarkayet- "kiṃ bharturme vyasanamāsannaṃ paśyan, svaṃ vā vyasanaṃ pratikartukāmaḥ, pārṣṇigrāhamāsāramantaḥkopamāṭavikaṃ vā samutthāpayitukāmaḥ, mitramākrandaṃ vā vyāghātayitukāmaḥ, svaṃ vā parato vigrahamantaḥkopamāṭavikaṃ vā pratikartukāmaḥ, saṃsiddhaṃ vā me bharturyātrākālamabhihantukāmaḥ,
sasyapaṇyakupyasaṃgrahaṃ durgakarma balasamuddānaṃ vā kartukāmaḥ, svasainyānāṃ vā vyāyāmasya deśakālāvākāṅkṣamāṇaḥ, paribhavapramādābhyāṃ vā, saṃsargānubandhārthī vā, māmuparuṇaddhi" iti ||
011630| jñātvā vasedapasaredvā ||
011631| prayojanamiṣṭamavekṣeta vā ||

011633  preṣaṇaṃ saṃdhipālatvaṃ pratāpo mitrasaṃgrahaḥ |
011633  upajāpaḥ suhṛdbhedo gūḍhadaṇḍātisāraṇam ||
011634  bandhuratnāpaharaṇaṃ cārajñānaṃ parākramaḥ |

(ṅuardingagainstprinces)
011701| rakṣito rājā rājyaṃ rakṣatyāsannebhyaḥ parebhyaśca, pūrvaṃ dārebhyaḥ putrebhyaśca ||

011703| "putrarakṣaṇaṃ tu ||
011704| "janmaprabhṛti rājaputrān rakṣet ||

011706| teṣāmajātasnehe pitaryupāṃśudaṇḍaḥ śreyān" iti bhāradvājaḥ ||
011707| "nṛśaṃsamaduṣṭavadhaḥ kṣatrabījavināśaśca" iti viśālākṣaḥ ||

011709| ahibhayametad" iti pārāśarāḥ ||
011710| "kumāro hi "vikramabhayānmāṃ pitāvaruṇaddhi" iti jñātvā tamevāṅke kuryāt ||
011711| tasmādantapāladurge vāsaḥ śreyān" iti ||
011712| "aurabhraṃ bhayametad" iti piśunaḥ ||

011714| tasmātsvaviṣayādapakṛṣṭe sāmantadurge vāsaḥ śreyān" iti ||
011715| "vatsasthānametad" iti kauṇapadantaḥ ||

011717| tasmānmātṛbandhuṣu vāsaḥ śreyān" iti ||
011718| "dhvajasthānametad" iti vātavyādhiḥ ||
011719| "tena hi dhvajenāditikauśikavadasya mātṛbāndhavā bhikṣeran ||

(ṭhe conductof a prince in disfavour)
(Behaviourtowardsa prince in disfavour)
011801| vinīto rājaputraḥ kṛcchravṛttirasadṛśe karmaṇi niyuktaḥ pitaramanuvarteta, anyatra prāṇābādhakaprakṛtikopakapātakebhyaḥ ||
011802| puṇye karmaṇi niyuktaḥ puruṣamadhiṣṭhātāraṃ yācet ||
011803| puruṣādhiṣṭhitaśca saviśeṣamādeśamanutiṣṭhet ||
011804| abhirūpaṃ ca karmaphalamaupāyanikaṃ ca lābhaṃ piturupanāyayet ||
011805| tathāpyatuṣyantamanyasmin putre dāreṣu vā snihyantamaraṇyāyāpṛccheta ||
011806| bandhavadhabhayādvā yaḥ sāmanto nyāyavṛttirdhārmikaḥ satyavāgavisaṃvādakaḥ pratigrahītā mānayitā cābhipannānāṃ tamāśrayeta ||
011807| tatrasthaḥ kośadaṇḍasampannaḥ pravīrapuruṣakanyāsambandhamaṭavīsambandhaṃ kṛtyapakṣopagrahaṃ ca kuryāt ||
011808| ekacaraḥ suvarṇapākamaṇirāgahemarūpyapaṇyākarakarmāntānājīvet ||
011809| pāṣaṇḍasaṃghadravyamaśrotriyopabhogyaṃ vā devadravyamāḍhyavidhavādravyaṃ vā gūḍhamanupraviśya sārthayānapātrāṇi ca madanarasayogenātisaṃdhāyāpaharet ||
011810| pāragrāmikaṃ vā yogamātiṣṭhet ||
011811| mātuḥ parijanopagraheṇa vā ceṣṭeta ||
011812| kāruśilpikuśīlavacikitsakavāgjīvanapāṣaṇḍacchadmabhirvā naṣṭarūpastadvyañjanasakhaśchidreṣu praviśya rājñaḥ śastrarasābhyāṃ prahṛtya brūyāt- "ahamasau kumāraḥ, sahabhogyamidaṃ rājyam, eko nārhati bhoktum, ye kāmayante māṃ bhartuṃ tānahaṃ dviguṇena bhaktavetanenopasthāsyāmi" iti || ityaparuddhavṛttam |
011813| aparuddhaṃ tu mukhyaputrāpasarpāḥ pratipādyānayeyuḥ, mātā vā pratigṛhītā ||
011814| tyaktaṃ gūḍhapuruṣāḥ śastrarasābhyāṃ hanyuḥ ||
011815| atyaktaṃ tulyaśīlābhiḥ strībhiḥ pānena mṛgayayā vā prasañjayitvā rātrāvupagṛhyānayeyuḥ ||
011816  upasthitaṃ ca rājyena madūrdhvamiti sāntvayet |
011816  ekasthamatha samrundhyātputravāṃstu pravāsayet ||E

(ṛulesforthe king)
011901| rājānamutthitamanūttiṣṭhante bhṛtyāḥ ||
011902| pramādyantamanupramādyanti ||

011908| taiḥ paścimā vyākhyātāḥ ||
011909| tatra pūrve divasasyāṣṭabhāge rakṣāvidhānamāyavyayau ca śṛṇuyāt ||
011910| dvitīye paurajānapadānāṃ kāryāṇi paśyet ||
011911| tṛtīye snānabhojanaṃ seveta, svādhyāyaṃ ca kurvīta ||

011913| pañcame mantripariṣadā pattrasampreṣaṇena mantrayeta, cāraguhyabodhanīyāni ca budhyeta ||
011914| ṣaṣṭhe svairavihāraṃ mantraṃ vā seveta ||
011915| saptame hastyaśvarathāyudhīyān paśyet ||
011916| aṣṭame senāpatisakho vikramaṃ cintayet ||
011917| pratiṣṭhitehani saṃdhyāmupāsīta ||
011918| prathame rātribhāge gūḍhapuruṣān paśyet ||
011919| dvitīye snānabhojanaṃ kurvīta, svādhyāyaṃ ca ||
011920| tṛtīye tūryaghoṣeṇa saṃviṣṭaścaturthapañcamau śayīta ||

011922| saptame mantramadhyāsīta, gūḍhapuruṣāṃśca preṣayet ||
011923| aṣṭame ṛtvigācāryapurohitasvastyayanāni pratigṛhṇīyāt, cikitsakamāhānasikamauhūrtikāṃśca paśyet ||
011924| savastāṃ dhenuṃ vṛṣabhaṃ ca pradakṣiṇīkṛtyopasthānaṃ gacchet ||
011925| ātmabalānukūlyena vā niśāharbhāgān pravibhajya kāryāṇi seveta ||

011927| durdarśo hi rājā kāryākāryaviparyāsamāsannaiḥ kāryate ||

011930  sarvamātyayikaṃ kāryaṃ śṛṇuyānnātipātayet |[ś]
011930  kṛcchrasādhyamatikrāntamasādhyaṃ vāpi jāyate ||[ś]

011931  purohitācāryasakhaḥ pratyutthāyābhivādya ca ||[ś]

011932  māyāyogavidāṃ caiva na svayaṃ kopakāraṇāt ||[ś]
011933  rājño hi vratamutthānaṃ yajñaḥ kāryānuśāsanam |[ś]
011933  dakṣiṇā vṛttisāmyaṃ tu dīkṣā tasyābhiṣecanam ||[ś]
011934  prajāsukhe sukhaṃ rājñaḥ prajānāṃ ca hite hitam |[ś]
011934  nātmapriyaṃ hitaṃ rājñaḥ prajānāṃ tu priyaṃ hitam ||[ś]
011935  tasmānnityotthito rājā kuryādarthānuśāsanam |
011935  arthasya mūlamutthānamanarthasya viparyayaḥ ||[ś]
011936  anutthāne dhruvo nāśaḥ prāptasyānāgatasya ca |[ś]

ṛegulationsforthe royalresideñce
012001| vāstukapraśaste deśe saprākāraparikhādvāramanekakakṣyāparigatamantaḥpuraṃ kārayet ||
Kā012002| kośagṛhavidhānena madhye vāsagṛham, gūḍhabhittisaṃcāraṃ mohanagṛhaṃ tanmadhye vā vāsagṛham, bhūmigṛhaṃ vāsannacaityakāṣṭhadevatāpidhānadvāramanekasuruṅgāsaṃcāraṃ tasyopari prāsādaṃ gūḍhabhittisopānaṃ suṣirastambhapraveśāpasāraṃ vā vāsagṛhaṃ yantrabaddhatalāvapātaṃ kārayet, āpatpratīkārārthamāpadi vā ||
012003| atoanyathā vā vikalpayet, sahādhyāyibhayāt ||
012004| mānuṣeṇāgninā trirapasavyaṃ parigatamantaḥpuramagniranyo na dahati, na cātrānyoagnirjvalati, vaidyutena bhasmanā mṛtsamyuktena karakavāriṇāvaliptaṃ ca ||
012005| jīvantīśvetāmuṣkakapuṣpavandākābhirakṣīve jātasyāśvatthasya pratānena guptaṃ sarpā viṣāṇi vā na prabhavanti ||
012006| mayūranakulapṛṣatotsargaḥ sarpān bhakṣayati ||
012007| śukaḥ sārikā bhṛṅgarājo vā sarpaviṣaśaṅkāyāṃ krośati ||
012008| krauñco viṣābhyāśe mādyati, glāyati jīvaṃjīvakaḥ, mriyate mattakokilaḥ, cakorasyākṣiṇī virajyete ||
012009| ityevamagniviṣasarpebhyaḥ pratikurvīta ||
012010| pṛṣṭhataḥ kakṣyāvibhāge strīniveśo garbhavyādhisaṃsthā vṛkṣodakasthānaṃ ca ||
012011| bahiḥ kanyākumārapuram ||
012012| purastādalaṅkārabhūmirmantrabhūmirupasthānaṃ kumārādhyakṣasthānaṃ ca ||
012013| kakṣyāntareṣvantarvaṃśikasainyaṃ tiṣṭhet ||
012014| antargṛhagataḥ sthavirastrīpariśuddhāṃ devīṃ paśyet ||
012015| devīgṛhe līno hi bhrātā bhadrasenaṃ jaghāna, mātuḥ śayyāntargataśca putraḥ kārūṣam ||
012016| lājānmadhuneti viṣeṇa paryasya devī kāśirājam, viṣadigdhena nūpreṇa vairantyam, mekhalāmaṇinā sauvīram, jālūthamādarśena, veṇyāṃ gūḍhaṃ śastraṃ kṛtvā devī vidūrathaṃ jaghāna ||
012017| tasmādetānyāspadāni pariharet ||
012018| muṇḍajaṭilakuhakapratisaṃsargaṃ bāhyābhiśca dāsībhiḥ pratiṣedhayet ||
012019| na caināḥ kulyāḥ paśyeyuḥ, anyatra garbhavyādhisaṃsthābhyaḥ ||
012020| rūpājīvāḥ snānapragharṣaśuddhaśarīrāḥ parivartitavastrālaṃkārāḥ paśyeyuḥ ||
012021| aśītikāḥ puruṣāḥ pañcāśatkāḥ striyo vā mātāpitṛvyañjanāḥ sthaviravarṣadharābhyāgārikāścāvarodhānāṃ śaucāśaucaṃ vidyuḥ, sthāpayeyuśca svāmihite, ||
012022  svabhūmau ca vasetsarvaḥ parabhūmau na saṃcaret |[ś]
012022  na ca bāhyena saṃsargaṃ kaścidābhyantaro vrajet ||[ś]
012023  sarvaṃ cāvekṣitaṃ dravyaṃ nibaddhāgamanirgamam |[ś]
012023  nirgacchedabhigacchedvā mudrāsaṃkrāntabhūmikam ||[ś] E

(Concerningthe protectionof (the king"s) own person)
012101| śayanādutthitaḥ strīgaṇairdhanvibhiḥ parigṛhyate, dvitīyasyāṃ kakṣyāyāṃ kañcukoṣṇīṣibhirvarṣadharābhyāgārikaiḥ, tṛtīyasyāṃ kubjavāmanakirātaiḥ, caturthyāṃ mantribhiḥ sambandhibhirdauvārikaiśca prāsapāṇibhiḥ ||
012102| pitṛpaitāmahaṃ sambandhānubaddhaṃ śikṣitamanuraktaṃ kṛtakarmāṇaṃ ca janamāsannaṃ kurvīta, nānyatodeśīyamakṛtārthamānaṃ svadeśīyaṃ vāpyapakṛtyopagṛhītam ||
012103| antarvaṃśikasainyaṃ rājānamantaḥpuraṃ ca rakṣet ||
012104| gupte deśe māhānasikaḥ sarvamāsvādabāhulyena karma kārayet ||
012105| tadrajā tathaiva pratibhuñjīta pūrvamagnaye vayobhyaśca baliṃ kṛtvā ||
012106| agnerjvālādhūmanīlatā śabdasphoṭanaṃ ca viṣayuktasya, vayasāṃ vipattiśca ||
012107  annasya ūṣmā mayūragrīvābhaḥ śaityamāśu kliṣṭasyeva vaivarṇyaṃ sodakatvamaklinnatvaṃ ca -
012107  vyañjanānāmāśu śuṣkatvaṃ ca kvāthadhyāmaphenapaṭalavicchinnabhāvo gandhasparśarasavadhaśca -
012107  draveṣu hīnātiriktacchāyādarśanaṃ phenapaṭalasīmantordhvarājīdarśanaṃ ca -
012107  rasasya madhye nīlā rājī, payasastāmrā, madyatoyayoḥ kālī, dadhnaḥ śyāmā, madhunaḥ śvetā, dravyāṇāmārdrāṇāmāśu pramlānatvamutpakvabhāvaḥ kvāthanīlaśyāvatā ca -
012107  śuṣkāṇāmāśu śātanaṃ vaivarṇyaṃ ca, -
012107  kaṭhinānāṃ mṛdutvaṃ mṛdūnāṃ ca kaṭhinatvam, tadabhyāśe kṣudrasattvavadhaśca, -
012107  | āstaraṇapravaraṇānāṃ dhyāmamaṇḍalatā tanturomapakṣmaśātanaṃ ca, -
012107  | lohamaṇimayānāṃ paṅkamalopadehatā sneharāgagauravaprabhāvavarṇasparśavadhaśca - iti viṣayuktasya liṅgāni ||
012108| viṣapradasya tu śuṣkaśyāvavaktratā vāksaṅgaḥ svedo vijṛmbhaṇaṃ cātimātraṃ vepathuḥ praskhalanaṃ vākyaviprekṣaṇamāvegaḥ karmaṇi svabhūmau cānavasthānamiti ||
012109| tasmādasya jāṅgulīvido bhiṣajaścāsannāḥ syuḥ ||
012110| bhiṣagbhaiṣajyāgārādāsvādaviśuddhamauṣadhaṃ gṛhītvā pācakapeṣakābhyāmātmanā ca pratisvādya rājñe prayacchet ||
012111| pānaṃ pānīyaṃ cāuṣadhena vyākhyātam ||
012112| kalpakaprasādhakāḥ snānaśuddhavastrahastāḥ samudramupakaraṇamantarvaṃśikahastādādāya paricareyuḥ ||
012113| snāpakasaṃvāhakāstarakarajakamālākārakarma dāsyaḥ prasiddhaśaucāḥ kuryuḥ, tābhiradhiṣṭhitā vā śilpinaḥ ||
012114| ātmacakṣuṣi niveśya vastramālyaṃ dadyuḥ, snānānulepanapragharṣacūrṇavāsasnānīyāni ca svavakṣobāhuṣu ca ||
012115| etena parasmādāgatakaṃ vyākhyātam ||
012116| kuśīlavāḥ śastrāgnirasakrīḍāvarjaṃ narmayeyuḥ ||
012117| ātodyāni caiṣāmantastiṣṭheyuḥ, aśvarathadvipālaṃkārāśca ||
012118| āptapuruṣādhiṣṭhitaṃ yānavāhanamārohet, nāvaṃ cāptanāvikādhiṣṭhitam ||
012119| anyanaupratibaddhāṃ vātavegavaśāṃ ca nopeyāt ||
012120| udakānte sainyamāsīta ||
012121| matsyagrāhaviśuddhamudakamavagāheta ||
012122| vyālagrāhaviśuddhamudyānaṃ gacchet ||
012123| lubdhakaśvagaṇibhirapāstastenavyālaparābādhabhayaṃ calalakṣyaparicayārthaṃ mṛgāraṇyaṃ gacchet ||
012124| āptaśastragrāhādhiṣṭhitaḥ siddhatāpasaṃ paśyet, mantripariṣadā saha sāmantadūtam ||
012125| sannaddhoaśvaṃ hastinaṃ vārūḍhaḥ sannaddhamanīkaṃ paśyet ||
012126| niryāṇebhiyāne ca rājamārgamubhayataḥ kṛtārakṣaṃ śastribhirdaṇḍibhiścāpāstaśastrahastapravrajitavyaṅgaṃ gacchet ||
012127| na puruṣasambādhamavagāheta ||
012128| yātrāsamājotsavaprahavaṇāni ca daśavargikādhiṣṭhitāni gacchet ||
012129  yathā ca yogapuruṣairanyān rājādhitiṣṭhati |[ś]
012130  tathāyamanyābādhebhyo rakṣedātmānamātmavān ||[ś] E

(Bookṭwo: ṭhe activityof the headsof departments)
(Chap, 1, ṣec, 19: ṣettlementof the countryside)
020101| bhūtapūrvamabhūtapūrvaṃ vā janapadaṃ paradeśāpavāhanena svadeśābhiṣyandavamanena vā niveśayet ||
020102| śūdrakarṣakaprāyaṃ kulaśatāvaraṃ pañcakulaśataparaṃ grāmaṃ krośadvikrośasīmānamanyonyārakṣaṃ niveśayet ||
020103| nalīśailavanabhṛṣṭidarīsetubandhaśamīśālmalīkṣīravṛkṣānanteṣu sīmnāṃ sthāpayet ||
020104| aṣṭaśatagrāmyā madhye sthānīyam, catuhśatagrāmyā droṇamukham, dviśatagrāmyāḥ kārvaṭikam, daśagrāmīsaṃgraheṇa saṃgrahaṃ sthāpayet ||
020105| anteṣvantapāladurgāṇi janapadadvārāṇyantapālādhiṣṭhitāni sthāpayet ||
020106| teṣāmantarāṇi vāgurikaśabarapulindacaṇḍālāraṇyacarā rakṣeyuḥ ||
020107  ṛtvigācāryapurohitaśrotriyebhyo brahmadeyānyadaṇḍakarāṇyabhirūpadāyādakāni prayacchet-
020107  adhyakṣasaṃkhyāyakādibhyo gopasthānikānīkasthacikitsakāśvadamakajaṅghākārikebhyaśca vikrayādhānavarjāni ||
020108| karadebhyaḥ kṛtakṣetrāṇyaikapuruṣikāṇi prayacchet ||
020109| akṛtāni kartṛbhyo nādeyāni ||
020110| akṛṣatāmāchidyānyebhyaḥ prayacchet ||
020111| grāmabhṛtakavaidehakā vā kṛṣeyuḥ ||
020112| akṛṣanto vāvahīnaṃ dadyuḥ ||
020113| dhānyapaśuhiraṇyaiścaitānanugṛhṇīyāt ||
020114| tānyanu sukhena dadyuḥ ||
020115| anugrahaparihārau caitebbhyaḥ kośavṛddhikarau dadyāt, kośopaghātakau varjayet ||
020116| alpakośo hi rājā paurajānapadāneva grasate ||
020117| niveśasamakālaṃ yathāgatakaṃ vā parihāraṃ dadyāt ||
020118| nivṛttaparihārān pitevānugṛhṇīyāt ||
020119| ākarakarmāntadravyahastivanavrajavaṇikpathapracārān vāristhalapathapaṇyapattanāni ca niveśayet ||
020120| sahodakamāhāryodakaṃ vā setuṃ bandhayet ||
020121| anyeṣāṃ vā badhnatāṃ bhūmimārgavṛkṣopakaraṇānugrahaṃ kuryāt, puṇyasthānārāmāṇāṃ ca ||
020122| sambhūyasetubandhādapakrāmataḥ karmakarabalīvardāḥ karma kuryuḥ ||
020123| vyayakarmaṇi ca bhāgī syāt, na cāṃśaṃ labheta ||
020124| matsyaplavaharitapaṇyānāṃ setuṣu rājā svāmyaṃ gacchet ||
020125| dāsāhitakabandhūnaśṛṇvato rājā vinayaṃ grāhayet ||
020126| bālavṛddhavyasanyanāthāṃśca rājā bibhṛyāt, striyamaprajātāṃ prajātāyaśca putrān ||
020127| bāladravyaṃ grāmavṛddhā vardhayeyurā vyavahāraprāpaṇāt, devadravyaṃ ca ||
020128| apatyadāraṃ mātāpitarau bhrātṛṛnaprāptavyavahārān bhaginīḥ kanyā vidhavāścābibhrataḥ śaktimato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra patitebhyaḥ, anyatra mātuḥ ||
020129| putradāramapratividhāya pravrajataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, striyaṃ ca pravrājayataḥ ||
020130| luptavyāyāmaḥ pravrajedāpṛcchya dharmasthān ||
020131| anyathā niyamyeta ||
020132| vānaprasthādanyaḥ pravrajitabhāvaḥ, sajātādanyaḥ saṃghaḥ, sāmutthāyikādanyaḥ samayānubandho vā nāsya janapadamupaniviśeta ||
020133| na ca tatrārāmā vihārārthā vā śālāḥ syuḥ ||
020134| naṭanartakagāyanavādakavāgjīvanakuśīlavā na karmavighnaṃ kuryuḥ ||
020135| nirāśrayatvādgrāmāṇāṃ kṣetrābhiratatvācca puruṣāṇāṃ kośaviṣṭidravyadhānyarasavṛddhirbhavati ||
020136  paracakrāṭavīgrastaṃ vyādhidurbhikṣapīḍitam |[ś]
020136  deśaṃ pariharedrājā vyayakrīḍāśca vārayet ||[ś]
020137  daṇḍaviṣṭikarābādhai rakṣedupahatāṃ kṛṣim |[ś]
020137  stenavyālaviṣagrāhairvyādhibhiśca paśuvrajān ||[ś]
020138  vallabhaiḥ kārmikaiḥ stenairantapālaiśca pīḍitam |[ś]
020138  śodhayetpaśusaṃghaiśca kṣīyamāṇaṃ vaṇikpatham ||[ś]
020139  evaṃ dravyadvipavanaṃ setubandhamathākarān |[ś]
020139  rakṣetpūrvakṛtān rājā navāṃścābhipravartayet ||[ś] E

(ḍisposalof non-agriculturalland)
02201| akṛṣyāyāṃ bhūmau paśubhyo vivītāni prayacchet ||
02202| pradiṣṭābhayasthāvarajaṅgamāni ca brahmasomāraṇyāni tapasvibhyo gorutaparāṇi prayacchet ||
02203| tāvanmātramekadvāraṃ khātaguptaṃ svāduphalagulmagucchamakaṇṭakidrumamuttānatoyāśayaṃ dāntamṛgacatuṣpadaṃ bhagnanakhadaṃṣṭravyālaṃ mārgayukahastihastinīkalabhaṃ mṛgavanaṃ vihārārthaṃ rājñaḥ kārayet ||
02204| sarvātithimṛgaṃ pratyante cānyanmṛgavanaṃ bhūmivaśena vā niveśayet ||
02205| kupyapradiṣṭānāṃ ca dravyāṇāmekaikaśo vanāni niveśayet, dravyavanakarmāntānaṭavīśca dravyavanāpāśrayāḥ ||
02206| pratyante hastivanamaṭavyārakṣaṃ niveśayet ||
02207| nāgavanādhyakṣaḥ pārvataṃ nādeyaṃ sārasamānūpaṃ ca nāgavanaṃ viditaparyantapraveśaniṣkāsaṃ nāgavanapālaiḥ pālayet ||
02208| hastighātinaṃ hanyuḥ ||
02209| dantayugaṃ svayaṃmṛtasyāharataḥ sapādacatuṣpaṇo lābhaḥ ||
02210| nāgavanapālā hastipakapādapāśikasaimikavanacarakapārikarmikasakhā hastimūtrapurīṣacchannagandhā bhallātakīśākhāpracchannāḥ pañcabhiḥ saptabhirvā hastibandhakībhiḥ saha carantaḥ śayyāsthānapadyāleṇḍakūlaghātoddeśena hastikulaparyagraṃ vidyuḥ ||
02211| yūthacaramekacaraṃ niryūthaṃ yūthapatiṃ hastinaṃ vyālaṃ mattaṃ potaṃ bandhamuktaṃ ca nibandhena vidyuḥ ||
02212| anīkasthapramāṇaiḥ praśastavyañjanācārān hastino gṛhṇīyuḥ ||
02213| hastipradhānaṃ vijayo rājñaḥ ||
02214| parānīkavyūhadurgaskandhāvārapramardanā hyatipramāṇaśarīrāḥ prāṇaharakarmāṇo hastinaḥ ||
02215  kāliṅgāṅgarajāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ prācyāścedikarūṣajāḥ |[ś]
02215  dāśārṇāścāparāntāśca dvipānāṃ madhyamā matāḥ ||[ś]
02216  saurāṣṭrikāḥ pāñcanadāsteṣāṃ pratyavarāḥ smṛtāḥ |[ś]
02216  sarveṣāṃ karmaṇā vīryaṃ javastejaśca vardhate ||[ś] E

(Constructionof forts)
02301| caturdiśaṃ janapadānte sāmparāyikaṃ daivakṛtaṃ durgaṃ kārayet, antardvīpaṃ sthalaṃ vā nimnāvaruddhamaudakam, prāstaraṃ guhāṃ vā pārvatam, nirudakastambamiriṇaṃ vā dhānvanam, khañjanodakaṃ stambagahanaṃ vā vanadurgam ||
02302| teṣāṃ nadīparvatadurgaṃ janapadārakṣasthānam, dhānvanavanadurgamaṭavīsthānamāpadyapasāro vā ||
02303| janapadamadhye samudayasthānaṃ sthānīyaṃ niveśayet, vāstukapraśaste deśe nadīsaṅgame hradasyāviśoṣasyāṅke sarasastaṭākasya vā, vṛttaṃ dīrghaṃ caturaśraṃ vā vāstuvaśena vā pradakṣiṇodakaṃ paṇyapuṭabhedanamaṃsapathavāripathābhyāmupetam ||
02304| tasya parikhāstisro daṇḍāntarāḥ kārayetcaturdaśa dvādaśa daśeti daṇḍān vistīrṇāḥ, vistārādavagāḍhāḥ pādonamardhaṃ vā, tribhāgamūlāḥ, mūlacaturaśrā vā, pāṣāṇopahitāḥ pāṣāṇeṣṭakābaddhapārśvā vā, toyāntikīrāgantutoyapūrṇā vā saparivāhāḥ padmagrāhavatīśca ||
02305| caturdaṇḍāpakṛṣṭaṃ parikhāyāḥ ṣaḍdaṇḍocchritamavaruddhaṃ taddviguṇaviṣkambhaṃ khātādvapraṃ kārayedūrdhvacayaṃ mañcapṛṣṭhaṃ kumbhakukṣikaṃ vā hastibhirgobhiśca kṣuṇṇaṃ kaṇṭakigulmaviṣavallīpratānavantam ||
02306| pāṃsuśeṣeṇa vāstucchidraṃ rājabhavanaṃ vā pūrayet ||
02307  vaprasyopari prākāraṃ viṣkambhadviguṇotsedhamaiṣṭakaṃ dvādaśahastādūrdhvamojaṃ yugmaṃ vā ā caturviṃśatihastāditi kārayet-
02307  rathacaryāsaṃcāraṃ tālamūlaṃ murajakaiḥ kapiśīrṣakaiścācitāgram ||
02308| pṛthuśilāsaṃhataṃ vā śailaṃ kārayet, na tveva kāṣṭamayam ||
02309| agniravahito hi tasmin vasati ||
02310| viṣkambhacaturaśramaṭṭālakamutsedhasamāvakṣepasopānaṃ kārayettriṃśaddaṇḍāntaraṃ ca ||
02311| dvayoraṭṭālakayormadhye saharmyadvitalāmadhyardhāyāyāmāṃ pratolīṃ kārayet ||
02312| aṭṭālakapratolīmadhye tridhānuṣkādhiṣṭhānaṃ sāpidhānacchidraphalakasaṃhatamindrakośaṃ kārayet ||
02313| antareṣu dvihastaviṣkambhaṃ pārśve caturguṇāyāmaṃ devapathaṃ kārayet ||
02314| daṇḍāntarā dvidaṇḍāntarā vā caryāḥ kārayet, agrāhye deśe pradhāvanikāṃ niṣkiradvāraṃ ca ||
02315| bahirjānubhañjanīśūlaprakarakūpakūṭāvapātakaṇṭakapratisarāhipṛṣṭhatālapattraśṛṅgāṭakaśvadaṃṣṭrārgalopaskandanapādukāmbarīṣodapānakaiḥ praticchannaṃ channapathaṃ kārayet ||
02316| prākāramubhayato meṇḍhakamadhyardhadaṇḍaṃ kṛtvā pratolīṣaṭtulāntaraṃ dvāraṃ niveśayetpañcadaṇḍādekottaramāṣṭadaṇḍāditi caturaśraṃ ṣaḍbhāgamāyāmādadhikamaṣṭabhāgaṃ vā ||
02317| pañcadaśahastādekottaramāṣṭādaśahastāditi talotsedhaḥ ||
02318| stambhasya parikṣepaḥ ṣaḍāyāmo, dviguṇo nikhātaḥ, cūlikāyāścaturbhāgaḥ ||
02319| āditalasya pañcabhāgāḥ śālā vāpī sīmāgṛhaṃ ca ||
02320| daśabhāgikau dvau pratimañcau, antaramāṇīharmyaṃ ca ||
02321| samucchrayādardhatale sthūṇābandhaśca ||
02322| ardhavāstukamuttamāgāram, tribhāgāntaraṃ vā, iṣṭakāvabaddhapārśvam, vāmataḥ pradakṣiṇasopānaṃ gūḍhabhittisopānamitarataḥ ||
02323| dvihastaṃ toraṇaśiraḥ ||
02324| tripañcabhāgikau dvau kapāṭayogau ||
02325| dvau parighau ||
02326| aratnirindrakīlaḥ ||

02401| trayaḥ prācīnā rājamārgāstraya udīcīnā iti vāstuvibhāgaḥ ||

02411| tataḥ paraṃ nagaradhānyavyāvahārikakārmāntikabalādhyakṣāḥ pakvānnasurāmāṃsapaṇyā rūpājīvāstālāvacarā vaiśyāśca dakṣiṇāṃ diśamadhivaseyuḥ ||
02412| paścimadakṣiṇaṃ bhāgaṃ kharoṣṭraguptisthānaṃ karmagṛhaṃ ca, paścimottaraṃ bhāgaṃ yānarathaśālāḥ ||
02413| tataḥ paramūrṇāsūtraveṇucarmavarmaśastrāvaraṇakāravaḥ śūdrāśca paścimāṃ diśamadhivaseyuḥ ||
02414| uttarapaścimaṃ bhāgaṃ paṇyabhaiṣajyagṛham, uttarapūrvaṃ bhāgaṃ kośo gavāśvaṃ ca ||

02601| samāhartā durgaṃ rāṣṭraṃ khaniṃ setuṃ vanaṃ vrajaṃ vaṇikpathaṃ cāvekṣeta ||

(ṭhe topicof accountsin the recordsandauditoffice)
02701| akṣapaṭalamadhyakṣaḥ prānmukhamudanmukhaṃ vā vibhaktopasthānaṃ nibandhapustakasthānaṃ kārayet ||

pragrahapradeśabhogaparihārabhaktavetanalābham, rājñaśca patnīputrāṇāṃ ratnabhūmilābhaṃ nirdeśotpātikapratīkāralābham, mitrāmitrāṇāṃ ca saṃdhivigrahapradānādānaṃ nibandhapustakasthaṃ kārayet ||

lobhena ||

(ṛecoveryof revenue misappropriatedbystate employees)
02801| kośapūrvāḥ sarvārambhāḥ ||

02819| tatra dvādaśaguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
02820| teṣāṃ haraṇopāyāścatvāriṃśat ||
02821  pūrvaṃ siddhaṃ paścādavatāritam, paścātsiddhaṃ pūrvamavatāritam, sādhyaṃ na siddham, asādhyaṃ siddham, siddhamasiddhaṃ kṛtam, asiddhaṃ siddhaṃ kṛtam, alpasiddhaṃ bahu kṛtam, bahusiddhamalpaṃ kṛtam, anyatsiddhamanyatkṛtam, anyataḥ siddhamanyataḥ kṛtam,-
02821  deyaṃ na dattam, adeyaṃ dattam, kāle na dattam, akāle dattam, alpaṃ dattaṃ bahu kṛtam, bahu dattamalpaṃ kṛtam, anyaddattamanyatkṛtam, anyato dattamanyataḥ kṛtam,-
02821  praviṣṭamapraviṣṭaṃ kṛtam, apraviṣṭaṃ praviṣṭaṃ kṛtam, kupyamadattamūlyaṃ praviṣṭam, dattamūlyaṃ na praviṣṭaṃ -
02821  saṃkṣepo vikṣepaḥ kṛtaḥ, vikṣepaḥ saṃkṣepo vā, mahārghamalpārgheṇa parivartitam, alpārghaṃ mahārgheṇa vā -
02821  samāropitoarghaḥ, pratyavaropito vā, saṃvatsaro māsaviṣamaḥ kṛtaḥ, māso divasaviṣamo vā, samāgamaviṣamaḥ, mukhaviṣamaḥ, kārmikaviṣamaḥ -
02821  nirvartanaviṣamaḥ, piṇḍaviṣamaḥ, varṇaviṣamaḥ, arghaviṣamaḥ, mānaviṣamaḥ, māpanaviṣamaḥ, bhājanaviṣamaḥ - iti haraṇopāyāḥ || -
02822| tatropayuktanidhāyakanibandhakapratigrāhakadāyakadāpakamantrivaiyāvṛtyakarānekaikaśoanuyuñjīta ||
02823| mithyāvāde caiṣāṃ yuktasamo daṇḍaḥ ||
02824| pracāre cāvaghoṣayet"amunā prakṛtenopahatāḥ prajñāpayantu" iti ||
02825| prajñāpayato yathopaghātaṃ dāpayet ||
02826| anekeṣu cābhiyogeṣvapavyayamānaḥ sakṛdeva paroktaḥ sarvaṃ bhajeta ||
02827| vaiṣamye sarvatrānuyogaṃ dadyāt ||
02828| mahatyarthāpahāre cālpenāpi siddhaḥ sarvaṃ bhajeta ||
02829| kṛtapratighātāvasthaḥ sūcako niṣpannārthaḥ ṣaṣṭhamaṃśaṃ labheta, dvādaśamaṃśaṃ bhṛtakaḥ ||
02830| prabhūtābhiyogādalpaniṣpattau niṣpannasyāṃśaṃ labheta ||
02831| aniṣpanne śārīraṃ hairaṇyaṃ vā daṇḍaṃ labheta, na cānugrāhyaḥ ||
02832  niṣpattau nikṣipedvādamātmānaṃ vāpavāhayet |[ś]
02832  abhiyuktopajāpāttu sūcako vadhamāpnuyāt ||[ś] E

(īnspectionof the ṛorkof officers)
02901| amātyasampadopetāḥ sarvādhyakṣāḥ śaktitaḥ karmasu niyojyāḥ ||
02902| karmasu caiṣāṃ nityaṃ parīkṣāṃ kārayet, cittānityatvātmanuṣyānām ||
02903| aśvasadharmāṇo hi manuṣyā niyuktāḥ karmasu vikurvate ||
02904| tasmātkartāraṃ karaṇaṃ deśaṃ kālaṃ kāryaṃ prakṣepamudayaṃ caiṣu vidyāt ||
02905| te yathāsaṃdeśamasaṃhatā avigṛhītāḥ karmāṇi kuryuḥ ||
02906| saṃhatā bhakṣayeyuḥ, vigṛhītā vināśayeyuḥ ||
02907| na cānivedya bhartuḥ kaṃcidārambhaṃ kuryuḥ, anyatrāpatpratīkārebhyaḥ ||
02908| pramādasthāneṣu caiṣāmatyayaṃ sthāpayeddivasavetanavyayadviguṇam ||
02909| yaścaiṣāṃ yathādiṣṭamarthaṃ saviśeṣaṃ vā karoti sa sthānamānau labheta ||
02910| "alpāyatiścetmahāvyayo bhakṣayati ||
02911| viparyaye yathāyativyayaśca na bhakṣayati" ityācāryāḥ ||
02912| apasarpeṇaivopalabhyeteti kauṭilyaḥ ||
02913| yaḥ samudayaṃ parihāpayati sa rājārthaṃ bhakṣayati ||
02914| sa cedajñānādibhiḥ parihāpayati tadenaṃ yathāguṇaṃ dāpayet ||
02915| yaḥ samudayaṃ dviguṇamudbhāvayati sa janapadaṃ bhakṣayati ||
02916| sa cedrājārthamupanayatyalpāparādhe vārayitavyaḥ, mahati yathāparādhaṃ daṇḍayitavyaḥ ||
02917| yaḥ samudayaṃ vyayamupanayati sa puruṣakarmāṇi bhakṣayati ||
02918| sa karmadivasadravyamūlyapuruṣavetanāpahāreṣu yathāparādhaṃ daṇḍayitavyaḥ ||
02919| tasmādasya yo yasminnadhikaraṇe śāsanasthaḥ sa tasya karmaṇo yāthātathyamāyavyayau ca vyāsasamāsābhyāmācakṣīta ||
02920| mūlaharatādātvikakadaryāṃśca pratiṣedhayet ||
02921| yaḥ pitṛpaitāmahamarthamanyāyena bhakṣayati sa mūlaharaḥ ||
02922| yo yadyadutpadyate tattadbhakṣayati sa tādātvikaḥ ||
02923| yo bhṛtyātmapīḍābhyāmupacinotyarthaṃ sa kadaryaḥ ||
02924| sa pakṣavāṃścedanādeyaḥ, viparyaye paryādātavyaḥ ||
02925| yo mahatyarthasamudaye sthitaḥ kadaryaḥ sannidhattevanidhattevasrāvayati vā - sannidhatte svaveśmani, avanidhatte paurajānapadeṣu, avasrāvayati paraviṣaye - tasya sattrī mantrimitrabhṛtyabandhupakṣamāgatiṃ gatiṃ ca dravyāṇāmupalabheta ||
02926| yaścāsya paraviṣaye saṃcāraṃ kuryāttamanupraviśya mantraṃ vidyāt ||
02927| suvidite śatruśāsanāpadeśenainaṃ ghātayet ||
02928| tasmādasyādhyakṣāḥ saṃkhyāyakalekhakarūpadarśakanīvīgrāhakottarādhyakṣasakhāḥ karmaṇi kuryuḥ ||
02929| uttarādhyakṣā hastyaśvarathārohāḥ ||
02930| teṣāmantevāsinaḥ śilpaśaucayuktāḥ saṃkhyāyakādīnāmapasarpāḥ ||
02931| bahumukhyamanityaṃ cādhikaraṇaṃ sthāpayet ||
02932  yathā hyanāsvādayituṃ na śakyaṃ  jihvātalasthaṃ madhu7 vā viṣaṃ vā | [ś]
02932  arthastathā hyarthacareṇa rājñaḥ  svalpoapyanāsvādayituṃ na śakyaḥ ||[ś]
02933  matsyā yathāntaḥ salile caranto  jñātuṃ na śakyāḥ salilaṃ pibantaḥ |[ś]
02933  yuktāstathā kāryavidhau niyuktā jñātuṃ na śakyā dhanamādadānāḥ ||[ś]
02934  api śakyā gatirjñātuṃ patatāṃ khe patatriṇām |[ś]
02934  na tu pracchannabhāvānāṃ yuktānāṃ caratāṃ gatiḥ ||[ś]
02935  āsrāvayeccopacitān viparyasyecca karmasu |[ś]
02935  yathā na bhakṣayantyarthaṃ bhakṣitaṃ nirvamanti vā ||[ś]
02936  na bhakṣayanti ye tvarthānnyāyato vardhayanti ca |[ś]
02936  nityādhikārāḥ kāryāste rājñaḥ priyahite ratāḥ ||[ś] E

(Onedicts)
॰021001| śāsane śāsanamityācakṣate ||
021002| śāsanapradhānā hi rājānaḥ, tanmūlatvātsaṃdhivigrahayoḥ ||
021003| tasmādamātyasampadopetaḥ sarvasamayavidāśugranthaścārvakṣaro lekhanavācanasamartho lekhakaḥ syāt ||
021004| soavyagramanā rājñaḥ saṃdeśaṃ śrutvā niścitārthaṃ lekhaṃ vidadhyātdeśāiśvaryavaṃśanāmadheyopacāramīśvarasya, deśanāmadheyopacāramanīśvarasya ||
021005  jātiṃ kulaṃ sthānavayaḥśrutāni karmarddhiśīlānyatha deśakālau |[ś]
021005  yaunānubandhaṃ ca samīkṣya kārye lekhaṃ vidadhyātpuruṣānurūpam ||[ś]
021006| arthakramaḥ sambandhaḥ paripūrṇatā mādhuryamaudāryaṃ spaṣṭatvamiti lekhasampat ||
021007| tatra yathāvadanupūrvakriyā pradhānasyārthasya pūrvamabhiniveśa ityarthakramaḥ ||
021008| prastutasyārthasyānuparodhāduttarasya vidhānamāsamāpteriti sambandhaḥ ||
021009| arthapadākṣarāṇāmanyūnātiriktatā hetūdāharaṇadṛṣṭāntairarthopavarṇanāśrāntapadateti paripūrṇatā ||
021010| sukhopanītacārvarthaśabdābhidhānaṃ mādhuryam ||
021011| agrāmyaśabdābhidhānamaudāryam ||
021012| pratītaśabdaprayogaḥ spaṣṭatvamiti ||
021013| akārādayo varṇāstriṣaṣṭiḥ ||
021014| varṇasaṃghātaḥ padam ||
021015| taccaturvidhaṃ nāmākhyātopasarganipātāśceti ||
021016| tatra nāma sattvābhidhāyi ||
021017| aviśiṣṭaliṅgamākhyātaṃ kriyāvāci ||
021018| kriyāviśeṣakāḥ prādaya upasargāḥ ||
021019| avyayāścādayo nipātāḥ ||
021020| padasamūho vākyamarthaparisamāptau ||
021021| ekapadāvarastripadaparaḥ parapadārthānuparodhena vargaḥ kāryaḥ ||
021022| lekhaparisaṃharaṇārtha itiśabdo vācikamasyeti ca ||
021023  nindā praśaṃsā pṛcchā ca tathākhyānamathārthanā |[ś]
021023  pratyākhyānamupālambhaḥ pratiṣedhoatha codanā ||[ś]
021024  sāntvamabhyupapattiśca bhartsanānunayau tathā |[ś]
021024  eteṣvarthāḥ pravartante trayodaśasu lekhajāḥ ||[ś]
021025| tatrābhijanaśarīrakarmaṇāṃ doṣavacanaṃ nindā ||
021026| guṇavacanameteṣāmeva praśaṃsā ||
021027| "kathametad" iti pṛcchā ||
021028| "evam" ityākhyānam ||
021029| "dehi" ityarthanā ||
021030| "na prayacchāmi" iti pratyākhyānam ||
021031| "ananurūpaṃ bhavataḥ" ityupālambhaḥ ||
021032| "mā kārṣīḥ" iti pratiṣedhaḥ ||
021033| "idaṃ kriyatām" iti codanā ||
021034| "yoahaṃ sa bhavān, yanmama dravyaṃ tadbhavataḥ" ityupagrahaḥ sāntvam ||
021035| vyasanasāhāyyamabhyupapattiḥ ||
021036| sadoṣamāyatipradarśanamabhibhartsanam ||
021037| anunayastrividhoarthakṛtāvatikrame puruṣādivyasane ceti ||
021038  prajñāpanājñāparidānalekhāstathā parīhāranisṛṣṭilekhau |[ś]
021038  prāvṛttikaśca pratilekha eva sarvatragaśceti hi śāsanāni ||[ś]
021039  anena vijñāpitamevamāha taddīyatāṃ cedyadi tattvamasti |[ś]
021039  rājñaḥ samīpe varakāramāha prajñāpanaiṣā vividhopadiṣṭā ||[ś]
021040  bharturājñā bhavedyatra nigrahānugrahau prati |[ś]
021040  viśeṣeṇa tu bhṛtyeṣu tadājñālekhalakṣaṇam ||[ś]
021041  yathārhaguṇasamyuktā pūjā yatropalakṣyate |[ś]
021041  apyādhau paridāne vā bhavatastāvupagrahau ||[ś]
021042  jāterviśeṣeṣu pareṣu caiva grāmeṣu deśeṣu ca teṣu teṣu |[ś]
02|1042  anugraho yo nṛpternideśāttajjñaḥ parīhāra iti vyavasyet ||[ś]
021043  nisṛṣṭisthāpanā kāryakaraṇe vacane tathā |[ś]
021043  eṣa vācikalekhaḥ syādbhavennaisṛṣṭikoapi vā ||[ś]
021044  vividhāṃ daivasamyuktāṃ tattvajāṃ caiva mānuṣīm |[ś]
021044  dvividhāṃ tāṃ vyavasyanti pravṛttiṃ śāsanaṃ prati ||[ś]
021045  dṛṣṭvā lekhaṃ yathātattvaṃ tataḥ pratyanubhāṣya ca |[ś]
021045  pratilekho bhavetkāryo yathā rājavacastathā ||[ś]
021046  yatreśvarāṃścādhikṛtāṃśca rājā rakṣopakārau pathikārthamāha |[ś]
021046  sarvatrago nāma bhavetsa mārge deśe ca sarvatra ca veditavyaḥ ||
021047| upāyāḥ sāmopapradānabhedadaṇḍāḥ ||
021048| tatra sāma pañcavidhaṃ - guṇasaṃkīrtanam, sambandhopākhyānam, parasparopakārasaṃdarśanam, āyatipradarśanam, ātmopanidhānamiti ||
021049| tatrābhijanaśarīrakarmaprakṛtiśrutadravyādīnāṃ guṇagrahaṇaṃ praśaṃsā stutirguṇasaṃkīrtanam ||
021050| jñātiyaunamaukhasrauvakulahṛdayamitrasaṃkīrtanaṃ sambandhopākhyānam ||
021051| svapakṣaparapakṣayoranyonyopakārasaṃkīrtanaṃ parasparopakārasaṃdarśanam ||
021052| "asminnevaṃ kṛta idamāvayorbhavati" ityāśājananamāyatipradarśanam ||
021053| "yoahaṃ sa bhavān, yanmama dravyaṃ tadbhavatā svakṛtyeṣu prayojyatām" ityātmopanidhānam | iti ||
021054| upapradānamarthopakāraḥ ||
021055| śaṅkājananaṃ nirbhartsanaṃ ca bhedaḥ ||
021056| vadhaḥ parikleśoarthaharaṇaṃ daṇḍaḥ | iti ||
021057| akāntirvyāghātaḥ punaruktamapaśabdaḥ samplava iti lekhadoṣaḥ ||
021058| tatra kālapattrakamacāruviṣamavirāgākṣaratvamakāntiḥ ||
021059| pūrveṇa paścimasyānupapattirvyāghātaḥ ||
021060| uktasyāviśeṣeṇa dvitīyamuccāraṇaṃ punaruktam ||
021061| liṅgavacanakālakārakāṇāmanyathāprayogoapaśabdaḥ ||
021062| avarge vargakaraṇaṃ cāvargakriyā guṇaviparyāsaḥ samplavaḥ | iti ||
021063  sarvaśāstrāṇyanukramya prayogamupalabhya ca |[ś]
021063  kauṭilyena narendrārthe śāsanasya vidhiḥ kṛtaḥ ||[ś] E

(Examinationof the preciousarticlesto be receivedinto the treasury)
021101| kośādhyakṣaḥ kośapraveśyaṃ ratnaṃ sāraṃ phalguṃ kupyaṃ vā tajjātakaraṇādhiṣṭhitaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
021102| tāmraparṇikaṃ pāṇḍyakavāṭakaṃ pāśikyaṃ kauleyaṃ caurṇeyaṃ māhendraṃ kārdamikaṃ srautasīyaṃ hrādīyaṃ haimavataṃ ca mauktikam ||
021103| śuktiḥ śaṅkhaḥ prakīrṇakaṃ ca yonayaḥ ||
021104| masūrakaṃ tripuṭakaṃ kūrmakamardhacandrakaṃ kañcukitaṃ yamakaṃ kartakaṃ kharakaṃ siktakaṃ kāmaṇḍalukaṃ śyāvaṃ nīlaṃ durviddhaṃ cāpraśastam ||
021105| sthūlaṃ vṛttaṃ nistalaṃ bhrājiṣṇu śvetaṃ guru snigdhaṃ deśaviddhaṃ ca praśastam ||
021106| śīrṣakamupaśīrṣakaṃ prakāṇḍakamavaghāṭakaṃ taralapratibaddhaṃ ceti yaṣṭiprabhedāḥ ||
021107| yaṣṭīnāmaṣṭasahasramindracchandaḥ ||
021108| tatoardhaṃ vijayacchandaḥ ||
021109| catuṣṣaṣṭirardhahāraḥ ||
021110| catuṣpañcāśadraśmikalāpaḥ ||
021111| dvātriṃśadgucchaḥ ||
021112| saptaviṃśatirnakṣatramālā ||
021113| caturviṃśatirardhagucchaḥ ||
021114| viṃśatirmāṇavakaḥ ||
021115| tatoardhamardhamāṇavakaḥ ||
021116| eta eva maṇimadhyāstanmāṇavakā bhavanti ||
021117| ekaśīrṣakaḥ śuddho hāraḥ ||
021118| tadvatśeṣāḥ ||
021119| maṇimadhyoardhamāṇavakaḥ ||
021120| triphalakaḥ phalakahāraḥ, pañcaphalako vā ||
021121| sūtramekāvalī śuddhā ||
021122| saiva maṇimadhyā yaṣṭiḥ ||
021123| hemamaṇicitrā ratnāvalī ||
021124| hemamaṇimuktāntaroapavartakaḥ ||
021125| suvarṇasūtrāntaraṃ sopānakam ||
021126| maṇimadhyaṃ vā maṇisopānakam ||
021127| tena śirohastapādakaṭīkalāpajālakavikalpā vyākhyātāḥ ||
021128| maṇiḥ kauṭomāleyakaḥ pārasamudrakaśca ||
021129| saugandhikaḥ padmarāgoanavadyarāgaḥ pārijātapuṣpako bālasūryakaḥ ||
021130| vaiḍūryamutpalavarṇaḥ śirīṣapuṣpaka udakavarṇo vaṃśarāgaḥ śukapattravarṇaḥ puṣyarāgo gomūtrako gomedakaḥ ||
021131| indranīlo nīlāvalīyaḥ kalāyapuṣpako mahānīlo jambvābho jīmūtaprabho nandakaḥ sravanmadhyaḥ ||
021132| śuddhasphaṭiko mūlāṭavarṇaḥ śītavṛṣṭiḥ sūryakāntaśca | iti maṇayaḥ ||
021133| ṣaḍaśraścaturaśro vṛtto vā tīvrarāgaḥ saṃsthānavānachaḥ snigdho gururarciṣmānantargataprabhaḥ prabhānulepī ceti maṇiguṇāḥ ||
021134| mandarāgaprabhaḥ sa-śarkaraḥ puṣpacchidraḥ khaṇḍo durviddho lekhākīrṇa iti doṣāḥ ||
021135| vimalakaḥ sasyakoañjanamūlakaḥ pittakaḥ sulabhako lohitākṣo mṛgāśmako jyotīrasako māleyakoahicchatrakaḥ kūrpaḥ pratikūrpaḥ sugandhikūrpaḥ kṣīravakaḥ śśukticūrṇakaḥ śilāpravālakaḥ pulakaḥ śuklapulaka ityantarajātayaḥ ||
021136| śeṣāḥ kācamaṇayaḥ ||
021137| sabhārāṣṭrakaṃ tajjamārāṣṭrakaṃ kāstīrarāṣṭrakaṃ śrīkaṭanakaṃ maṇimantakamindravānakaṃ ca vajram ||
021138| khaniḥ srotaḥ prakīrṇakaṃ ca yonayaḥ ||
021139| mārjārākṣakaṃ śirīṣapuṣpakaṃ gomūtrakaṃ gomedakaṃ śuddhasphaṭikaṃ mūlāṭīvarṇaṃ maṇivarṇānāmanyatamavarṇamiti vajravarṇāḥ ||
021140| sthūlaṃ guru prahārasahaṃ samakoṭikaṃ bhājanalekhi tarkubhrāmi bhrājiṣṇu ca praśastam ||
021141| naṣṭakoṇaṃ nirāśri pārśvāpavṛttaṃ cāpraśastam ||
021142| pravālakamālakandakaṃ vaivarṇikaṃ ca, raktaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca karaṭagarbhiṇikāvarjamiti ||
021143| candanaṃ sātanaṃ raktaṃ bhūmigandhi ||
021144| gośīrṣakaṃ kālatāmraṃ matsyagandhi ||
021145| haricandanaṃ śukapattravarṇamāmragandhi, tārṇasaṃ ca ||
021146| grāmerukaṃ raktaṃ raktakālaṃ vā bastamūtragandhi ||
021147| daivasabheyaṃ raktaṃ padmagandhi, jāpakaṃ ca ||
021148| joṅgakaṃ raktaṃ raktakālaṃ vā snigdham, taurūpaṃ ca ||
021149| māleyakaṃ pāṇḍuraktam ||
021150| kucandanaṃ rūkṣamagurukālaṃ raktaṃ raktakālaṃ vā ||
021151| kālaparvatakaṃ raktakālamanavadyavarṇaṃ vā ||
021152| kośāgāraparvatakaṃ kālaṃ kālacitraṃ vā ||
021153| śītodakīyaṃ padmābhaṃ kālasnigdhaṃ vā ||
021154| nāgaparvatakaṃ rūkṣaṃ śaivalavarṇaṃ vā ||
021155| śākalaṃ kapilam | iti ||
021156| laghu snigdhamaśyānaṃ sarpiḥsnehalepi gandhasukhaṃ tvaganusāryanulbaṇamavirāgyuṣṇasahaṃ dāhagrāhi sukhasparśanamiti candanaguṇāḥ ||
021157| aguru joṅgakaṃ kālaṃ kālacitraṃ maṇḍalacitraṃ vā ||

021410| varṇahīne māṣāvare pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, pramāṇahīne madhyamaḥ, tulāpratimānopadhāvuttamaḥ, kṛtabhāṇḍopadhau ca ||
021411| sauvarṇikenādṛṣṭamanyatra vā prayogaṃ kārayato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
021412| karturdviguṇaḥ sāpasāraścet ||
021413| anapasāraḥ kaṇṭakaśodhanāya nīyeta ||
021414| kartuśca dviśato daṇḍaḥ paṇacchedanaṃ vā ||
021415| tulāpratimānabhāṇḍaṃ pautavahastātkrīṇīyuḥ ||
021416| anyathā dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
021417| ghanaṃ suṣiraṃ samyūhyamavalepyaṃ saṃghātyaṃ vāsitakaṃ ca kārukarma ||
021418| tulāviṣamamapasāraṇaṃ visrāvaṇaṃ peṭakaḥ piṅkaśceti haraṇopāyāḥ ||
021419| sannāminyutkīrṇikā bhinnamastakopakaṇṭhī kuśikyā sakaṭukakṣyā parivelyāyaskāntā ca duṣṭatulāḥ ||
021420| rūpyasya dvau bhāgāvekaḥ śulbasya tripuṭakam ||
021421| tenākarodgatamapasāryate tattripuṭakāpasāritam ||
021422| śulbena śulbāpasāritam, vellakena vellakāpasāritam, śulbārdhasāreṇa hemnā hemāpasāritam ||
021423| mūkamūṣā pūtikiṭṭaḥ karaṭukamukhaṃ nālī saṃdaṃśo joṅganī suvarcikālavaṇaṃ tadeva suvarṇamityapasāraṇamārgāḥ ||
021424| pūrvapraṇihitā vā piṇḍavālukā mūṣābhedādagniṣṭhāduddhriyante ||
021425| paścādbandhane ācitakapattraparīkṣāyāṃ vā rūpyarūpeṇa parivartanaṃ visrāvaṇam, piṇḍavālukānāṃ lohapiṇḍavālukābhirvā ||
021426| gāḍhaścābhyuddhāryaśca peṭakaḥ samyūhyāvalepyasaṃghātyeṣu kriyate ||
021427| sīsarūpaṃ suvarṇapattreṇāvaliptamabhyantaramaṣṭakena baddhaṃ gāḍhapeṭakaḥ ||
021428| sa eva paṭalasampuṭeṣvabhyuddhāryaḥ ||
021429| pattramāśliṣṭaṃ yamakapattraṃ vāvalepyeṣu kriyate ||
021430| śulbaṃ tāraṃ vā garbhaḥ pattrāṇāṃ saṃghātyeṣu kriyate ||
021431| śulbarūpaṃ suvarṇapattrasaṃhataṃ pramṛṣṭaṃ supārśvam, tadeva yamakapattrasaṃhataṃ pramṛṣṭaṃ tāmratārarupaṃ cottaravarṇakaḥ ||
021432| tadubhayaṃ tāpanikaṣābhyāṃ nihśabdollekhanābhyāṃ vā vidyāt ||
021433| abhyuddhāryaṃ badarāmle lavaṇodake vā sādayanti || iti peṭakaḥ ||
021434| ghane suṣire vā rūpe suvarṇamṛnmālukāhiṅgulukakalpo vā taptoavatiṣṭhate ||
021435| dṛḍhavāstuke vā rūpe vālukāmiśraṃ jatu gāndhārapaṅko vā taptoavatiṣṭhate ||
021436| tayostāpanamavadhvaṃsanaṃ vā śuddhiḥ ||
021437| sa-paribhāṇḍe vā rūpe lavaṇamulkayā kaṭuśarkarayā taptamavatiṣṭhate ||
021438| tasya kvāthanaṃ śuddhiḥ ||
021439| abhrapaṭalamaṣṭakena dviguṇavāstuke vā rūpe badhyate ||
021440| tasyāpihitakācakasyodake nimajjata ekadeśaḥ sīdati, paṭalāntareṣu vā sūcyā bhidyate ||
021441| maṇayo rūpyaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā ghanasuṣirāṇāṃ piṅkaḥ ||
021442| tasya tāpanamavadhvaṃsanaṃ vā śuddhiḥ | iti piṅkaḥ ||
021443| tasmādvajramaṇimuktāpravālarūpāṇāṃ jātirūpavarṇapramāṇapudgalalakṣaṇānyupalabheta ||
021444| kṛtabhāṇḍaparīkṣāyāṃ purāṇabhāṇḍapratisaṃskāre vā catvāro haraṇopāyāḥ - parikuṭṭanamavacchedanamullekhanaṃ parimardanaṃ vā ||
021445| peṭakāpadeśena pṛṣataṃ guṇaṃ piṭakāṃ vā yatpariśātayanti tatparikuṭṭanam ||
021446| yaddviguṇavāstukānāṃ vā rūpe sīsarūpaṃ prakṣipyābhyantaramavacchindanti tadavacchedanam ||
021447| yadghanānāṃ tīkṣṇenollikhanti tadullekhanam ||
021448| haritālamanaḥśilāhiṅgulukacūrṇānāmanyatamena kuruvindacūrṇena vā vastraṃ samyūhya yatparimṛdnanti tatparimardanam ||
021449| tena sauvarṇarājatāni bhāṇḍāni kṣīyante, na caiṣāṃ kiṃcidavarugṇaṃ bhavati ||
021450| bhagnakhaṇḍaghṛṣṭānāṃ samyūhyānāṃ sadṛśenānumānaṃ kuryāt ||021451| avalepyānāṃ yāvadutpāṭitaṃ tāvadutpāṭyānumānaṃ kuryāt ||
021452| virūpāṇāṃ vā tāpanamudakapeṣaṇaṃ ca bahuśaḥ kuryāt ||
021453| avakṣepaḥ pratimānamagnirgaṇḍikā bhaṇḍikādhikaraṇī piñchaḥ sūtraṃ cellaṃ bollanaṃ śira utsaṅgo makṣikā svakāyekṣā dṛtirudakaśarāvamagniṣṭhamiti kācaṃ vidyāt ||
021454| rājatānāṃ visraṃ malagrāhi paruṣaṃ prastīnaṃ vivarṇaṃ vā duṣṭamiti vidyāt ||
021455  evaṃ navaṃ ca jīrṇaṃ ca virūpaṃ cāpi bhāṇḍakam |
021455  parīkṣetātyayaṃ caiṣāṃ yathoddiṣṭaṃ prakalpayet ||E

(ṣuperintendentof the magaśin)
021501| koṣṭhāgārādhyakṣaḥ sītārāṣṭrakrayimaparivartakaprāmityakāpamityakasaṃhanikānyajātavyayapratyāyopasthānānyupalabhet ||
021502| sītādhyakṣopanītaḥ sasyavarṇakaḥ sītā ||
021503| piṇḍakaraḥ ṣaḍbhāgaḥ senābhaktaṃ baliḥ kara utsaṅgaḥ pārśvaṃ pārihīṇikamaupāyanikaṃ kauṣṭheyakaṃ ca rāṣṭram ||
021504| dhānyamūlyaṃ kośanirhāraḥ prayogapratyādānaṃ ca krayimam ||
021505| sasyavarṇānāmarghāntareṇa vinimayaḥ parivartakaḥ ||
021506| sasyayācanamanyataḥ prāmityakam ||
021507| tadeva pratidānārthamāpamityakam ||
021508| kuṭṭakarocakasaktuśuktapiṣṭakarma tajjīvaneṣu tailapīḍanamaudracākrikeṣvikṣūṇāṃ ca kṣārakarma saṃhanikā ||
021509| naṣṭaprasmṛtādiranyajātaḥ ||
021510| vikṣepavyādhitāntarārambhaśeṣaṃ ca vyayapratyāyaḥ ||
021511| tulāmānāntaraṃ hastapūraṇamutkaro vyājī paryuṣitaṃ prārjitaṃ copasthānam | iti ||
021512| dhānyasnehakṣāralavaṇānāṃ dhānyakalpaṃ sītādhyakṣe vakṣyāmaḥ ||
021513| sarpistailavasāmajjānaḥ snehāḥ ||
021514| phāṇitaguḍamatsyaṇḍikākhaṇḍaśarkarāḥ kṣāravargaḥ ||
021515| saindhavasāmudrabiḍayavakṣārasauvarcalodbhedajā lavaṇavargaḥ ||
021516| kṣaudraṃ mārdvīkaṃ ca madhu ||
021517| ikṣurasaguḍamadhuphāṇitajāmbavapanasānāmanyatamo meṣaśṛṅgīpippalīkvāthābhiṣuto māsikaḥ ṣāṇmāsikaḥ sāṃvatsariko vā cidbhiṭorvārukekṣukāṇḍāmraphalāmalakāvasutaḥ śuddho vā śuktavargaḥ ||
021518| vṛkṣāmlakaramardāmravidalāmalakamātuluṅgakolabadarasauvīrakaparūṣakādiḥ phalāmlavargaḥ ||
021519| dadhidhānyāmlādirdravāmlavargaḥ ||
021520| pippalīmaricaśṛṅgiberājājīkirātatiktagaurasarṣapakustumburucorakadamanakamaruvakaśigrukāṇḍādiḥ kaṭukavargaḥ ||
021521| śuṣkamatsyamāṃsakandamūlaphalaśākādi ca śākavargaḥ ||
021522| tatoardhamāpadarthaṃ jānapadānāṃ sthāpayed, ardhamupayuñjīta ||
021523| navena cānavaṃ śodhayet ||
021524| kṣuṇṇaghṛṣṭapiṣṭabhṛṣṭānāmārdraśuṣkasiddhānāṃ ca dhānyānāṃ vṛddhikṣayapramāṇāni pratyakṣīkurvīta ||
021525| kodravavrīhīṇāmardhaṃ sāraḥ, śālīnāmardhabhāgonaḥ, tribhāgono varakāṇām ||
021526| priyaṅgūṇāmardhaṃ sāro navabhāgavṛddhiśca ||
021527| udārakastulyaḥ, yavā godhūmāśca kṣuṇṇāḥ, tilā yavā mudgamāṣāśca ghṛṣṭāḥ ||
021528| pañcabhāgavṛddhirgodhūmaḥ, saktavaśca ||
021529| pādonā kalāyacamasī ||
021530| mudgamāṣāṇāmardhapādonā ||
021531|śaumbyānāmardhaṃ sāraḥ, tribhāgono masūrāṇām ||
021532| piṣṭamāmaṃ kulmāṣāścādhyardhaguṇāḥ ||
021533| dviguṇo yāvakaḥ, pulākaḥ, piṣṭaṃ ca siddham ||
021534| kodravavarakodārakapriyaṅgūṇāṃ triguṇamannam, caturguṇaṃ vrīhīṇām, pañcaguṇaṃ śālīnām ||
021535| timitamaparānnaṃ dviguṇam, ardhādhikaṃ virūḍhānām ||
021536| pañcabhāgavṛddhirbhṛṣṭānām ||
021537| kalāyo dviguṇaḥ, lājā bharujāśca ||
021538|ṣaṭkaṃ tailamatasīnām ||
021539| nimbakuśāmrakapitthādīnāṃ pañcabhāgaḥ ||
021540| caturbhāgikāstilakusumbhamadhūkeṅgudīsnehāḥ ||
021541| kārpāsakṣaumāṇāṃ pañcapale palaṃ sūtram ||
021542| pañcadroṇe śālīnāṃ dvādaśāḍhakaṃ taṇḍulānāṃ kalabhabhojanam, ekādaśakaṃ vyālānām, daśakamaupavāhyānāṃ navakaṃ sāmnāhyānām, aṣṭakaṃ pattīnām, saptakaṃ mukhyānām, ṣaṭkaṃ devīkumārāṇām, pañcakaṃ rājñām, akhaṇḍapariśuddhānāṃ vā tuaṇḍulānāṃ prasthaḥ ||
021543| taṇḍulānāṃ prasthaḥ caturbhāgaḥ sūpaḥ sūpaṣoḍaśo lavaṇasyāṃśaḥ caturbhāgaḥ sarpiṣastailasya vaikamāryabhaktaṃ puṃsaḥ ||
021544|ṣaḍbhāgaḥ sūpaḥ ardhasnehamavarāṇām ||
021545| pādonaṃ strīṇām ||
021546| ardhaṃ bālānām ||
021547| māṃsapalaviṃśatyā snehārdhakuḍubaḥ paliko lavaṇasyāṃśaḥ kṣārapalayogo dvidharaṇikaḥ kaṭukayogo dadhnuścārdhaprasthaḥ ||
021548| tenottaraṃ vyākhyātam ||
021549| śākānāmadhyardhaguṇaḥ, śuṣkāṇāṃ dviguṇaḥ, sa caiva yogaḥ ||
021550| hastyaśvayostadadhyakṣe vidhāpramāṇaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ ||
021551| balīvardānāṃ māṣadroṇaṃ yavānāṃ vā pulākaḥ, śeṣamaśvavidhānam ||
021552| viśeṣo ghāṇapiṇyākatulā, kaṇakuṇḍakaṃ daśāḍhakaṃ vā ||
021553| dviguṇaṃ mahiṣoṣṭrāṇām ||
021554| ardhadroṇaṃ kharapṛṣatarohitānām ||
021555| āḍhakameṇakuraṅgāṇām ||
021556| ardhāḍhakamajaiḍakavarāhāṇām, dviguṇaṃ vā kaṇakuṇḍakam ||
021557| prasthaudanaḥ śunām ||
021558| haṃsakrauñcamayūrāṇāmardhaprasthaḥ ||
021559| śeṣāṇāmato mṛgapaśupakṣivyālānāmekabhaktādanumānaṃ grāhayet ||
021560| aṅgārāṃstuṣān lohakarmāntabhittilepyānāṃ hārayet ||
021561| kaṇikā dāsakarmakarasūpakārāṇām, atoanyadaudanikāpūpikebhyaḥ prayacchet ||
021562| tulāmānabhāṇḍaṃ rocanīdṛṣanmusalolūkhalakuṭṭakarocakayantrapattrakaśūrpacālanikākaṇḍolīpiṭakasammārjanyaścopakaraṇāni ||
021563| mārjakarakṣakadharakamāyakamāpakadāyakadāpakaśalākāpratigrāhakadāsakarmakaravargaśca viṣṭiḥ ||
021564  uccairdhānyasya nikṣepo mūtāḥ kṣārasya saṃhatāḥ |
021564  mṛtkāṣṭhakoṣṭhāḥ snehasya pṛthivī lavaṇasya ca ||E

(ḍirectorof trade)
021601| paṇyādhyakṣaḥ sthalajalajānāṃ nānāvidhānāṃ paṇyānāṃ sthalapathavāripathopayātānāṃ sāraphalgvarghāntaraṃ priyāpriyatāṃ ca vidyāt, tathā vikṣepasaṃkṣepakrayavikrayaprayogakālān ||
021602| yacca paṇyaṃ pracuraṃ syāttadekīkṛtyārghamāropayet ||
021603| prāpterghe vārghāntaraṃ kārayet ||
021604| svabhūmijānāṃ rājapaṇyānāmekamukhaṃ vyavahāraṃ sthāpayet, parabhūmijānāmanekamukham ||
021605| ubhayaṃ ca prajānāmanugraheṇa vikrāpayet ||
021606| sthūlamapi ca lābhaṃ prajānāmaupaghātikaṃ vārayet ||
021607| ajasrapaṇyānāṃ kāloparodhaṃ saṃkuladoṣaṃ vā notpādayet ||
021608| bahumukhaṃ vā rājapaṇyaṃ vaidehakāḥ kṛtārghaṃ vikrīṇīran ||
021609| chedānurūpaṃ ca vaidharaṇaṃ dadyuḥ ||
021610| ṣoḍaśabhāgo mānavyājī, viṃśatibhāgastulāmānam, gaṇyapaṇyānāmekādaśabhāgaḥ ||
021611| parabhūmijaṃ paṇyamanugraheṇāvāhayet ||
021612| nāvikasārthavāhebhyaśca parihāramāyatikṣamaṃ dadyāt ||
021613| anabhiyogaścārtheṣvāgantūnām, anyatra sabhyopakāribhyaḥ ||
021614| paṇyādhiṣṭhātāraḥ paṇyamūlyamekamukhaṃ kāṣṭhadroṇyāmekacchidrāpidhānāyāṃ nidadhyuḥ ||
021615| ahnaścāṣṭame bhāge paṇyādhyakṣasyārpayeyuḥ - "idaṃ vikrītam, idaṃ śeṣam" iti ||
021616| tulāmānabhāṇḍaṃ cārpayeyuḥ ||
021617| iti svaviṣaye vyākhyātam ||
021618| paraviṣaye tu - paṇyapratipaṇyayorarghaṃ mūlyaṃ cāgamayya śulkavartanyātivāhikagulmataradeyabhaktabhāgavyayaśuddhamudayaṃ paśyet ||
021619| asatyudaye bhāṇḍanirvahaṇena paṇyapratipaṇyānayanena vā lābhaṃ paśyet ||
021620| tataḥ sārapādena sthalavyavahāramadhvanā kṣemeṇa prayojayet ||
021621| aṭavyantapālapurarāṣṭramukhyaiśca pratisaṃsargaṃ gacchedanugrahārtham ||
021622| āpadi sāramātmānaṃ vā mokṣayet ||
021623| ātmano vā bhūmiṃ prāptaḥ sarvadeyaviśuddhaṃ vyavahareta ||
021624| vāripathe vā yānabhāgakapathyadanapaṇyapratipaṇyārghapramāṇayātrākālabhayapratīkārapaṇyapattanacāritrāṇyupalabheta ||
021625  nadīpathe ca vijñāya vyavahāraṃ caritrataḥ |
021625  yato lābhastato gacchedalābhaṃ parivarjayet ||E

(ḍirectorof forestproduce)
021701| kupyādhyakṣo dravyavanapālaiḥ kupyamānāyayet ||
021702| dravyavanakarmāntāṃśca prayojayet ||
021703| dravyavanacchidrāṃ ca deyamatyayaṃ ca sthāpayedanyatrāpadbhyaḥ ||
021704| kupyavargaḥ - śākatiniśadhanvanārjunamadhūkatilakasālaśiṃśapārimedarājādanaśirīṣakhadirasaralatālasarjāśvakarṇasomavalkakuśāmrapriyakadhavādiḥ sāradāruvargaḥ ||
021705| uṭajacimiyacāpaveṇuvaṃśasātinakaṇṭakabhāllūkādirveṇuvargaḥ ||
021706| vetraśīkavallīvāśīśyāmalatānāgalatādirvallīvargaḥ ||
021707| mālatīmūrvārkaśaṇagavedhukātasyādirvalkavargaḥ ||
021708| muñjabalbajādi rajjubhāṇḍam ||
021709| tālītālabhūrjānāṃ pattram ||
021710| kiṃśukakusumbhakuṅkumānāṃ puṣpam ||
021711| kandamūlaphalādirauṣadhavargaḥ ||
021712| kālakūṭavatsanābhahālāhalameṣaśṛṅgamustākuṣṭhamahāviṣavellitakagaurārdrabālakamārkaṭahaimavatakāliṅgakadāradakāṅkolasārakoṣṭrakādīni viṣāṇi, sarpāḥ kīṭāśca ta eva kumbhagatāḥ viṣavargaḥ ||
021713| godhāserakadvīpyṛkṣaśiṃśumārasiṃhavyāghrahastimahiṣacamarasṛmarakhaḍgagomṛgagavayānāṃ carmāsthipittasnāyvakṣidantaśṛṅgakhurapucchāni, anyeṣāṃ vāpi mṛgapaśupakṣivyālānām ||
021714| kālāyasatāmravṛttakaṃsasīsatrapuvaikṛntakārakūṭāni lohāni ||
021715| vidalamṛttikāmayaṃ bhāṇḍam ||
021716| aṅgāratuṣabhasmāni, mṛgapaśupakṣivyālavāṭāḥ kāṣṭhatṛṇavāṭāśca | iti ||
021717  bahirantaśca karmāntā vibhaktāḥ sārvabhāṇḍikāḥ |
021717  ājīvapurarakṣārthāḥ kāryāḥ kupyopajīvinā ||E

(ṣuperintendentof the armoury)
021801| āyudhāgārādhyakṣaḥ sāṃgrāmikaṃ daurgakarmikaṃ parapurābhighātikaṃ ca yantramāyudhamāvaraṇamupakaraṇaṃ ca tajjātakāruśilpibhiḥ kṛtakarmapramāṇakālavetanaphalaniṣpattibhiḥ kārayet, svabhūmiṣu ca sthāpayet ||
021802| sthānaparivartanamātapapravātapradānaṃ ca bahuśaḥ kuryāt ||

021804| jātirūpalakṣaṇapramāṇāgamamūlyanikṣepaiścopalabheta ||

021807| śaktiprāsakuntahāṭakabhiṇḍipālaśūlatomaravarāhakarṇakaṇayakarpaṇatrāsikādīni ca hulamukhāni ||
021808| tālacāpadāravaśārṅgāṇi kārmukakodaṇḍadrūṇā dhanūṃṣi ||
