022401| sītādhyakṣaḥ kṛṣitantraśulbavṛkṣāyurvedajñastajjñasakho vā sarvadhānyapuṣpaphalaśākakandamūlavāllikyakṣaumakārpāsabījāni yathākālaṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||
022402| bahuhalaparikṛṣṭāyāṃ svabhūmau dāsakarmakaradaṇḍapratikartṛbhirvāpayet ||
022403| karṣaṇayantropakaraṇabalīvardaiścaiṣāmasaṅgaṃ kārayet, kārubhiśca karmārakuṭṭākamedakarajjuvartakasarpagrāhādibhiśca ||
022404| teṣāṃ karmaphalavinipāte tatphalahānaṃ daṇḍaḥ ||
022405| ṣoḍaśadroṇaṃ jāṅgalānāṃ varṣapramāṇam, adhyardhamānūpānāṃ deśavāpānām, ardhatrayodaśāśmakānām, trayoviṃśatiravantīnām, amitamaparāntānāṃ haimanyānāṃ ca, kulyāvāpānāṃ ca kālataḥ ||
022406| varṣatribhāgaḥ pūrvapaścimamāsayoḥ, dvau tribhāgau madhyamayoḥ - suṣamārūpam ||
022407| tasyopaladhirbṛhaspateḥ sthānagamanagarbhādhānebhyaḥ śukrodayāstamayacārebhyaḥ sūryasya prakṛtivaikṛtācca ||
022408| sūryādbījasiddhiḥ, bṛhaspateḥ sasyānāṃ stambakāritā, śukrādvṛṣṭiḥ | iti ||
022409  trayaḥ saptāhikā meghā aśītiḥ kaṇaśīkarāḥ |
022409  ṣaṣṭirātapameghānāmeṣā vṛṣṭiḥ samā hitā ||
022410  vātamātapayogaṃ ca vibhajan yatra varṣati |
022410  trīn karīṣāṃśca janayaṃstatra sasyāgamo dhruvaḥ ||
022411| tataḥ prabhūtodakamalpodakaṃ vā sasyaṃ vāpayet ||
022412| śālivrīhikodravatilapriyaṅgūdārakavarakāḥ pūrvavāpāḥ ||
022413| mudgamāṣaśaimbyā madhyavāpāḥ ||
022414| kusumbhamasūrakulatthayavagodhūmakalāyātasīsarṣapāḥ paścādvāpāḥ ||
022415| yathartuvaśena vā bījavāpāḥ ||
O22416| vāpātiriktamardhasītikāḥ kuryuḥ, svavīryopajīvino vā caturthapañcabhāgikāḥ ||
ū22417| yatheṣṭamanavasitabhāgaṃ dadyuḥ, anyatra kṛcchrebhyaḥ ||
ū22418| svasetubhyo hastaprāvartimamudakabhāgaṃ pañcamaṃ dadyuḥ, skandhaprāvartimaṃ caturtham, srotoyantraprāvartimaṃ ca tṛtīyam, caturthaṃ nadīsarastaṭākakūpoddhāṭam ||
ū22419| karmodakapramāṇena kaidāraṃ haimanaṃ graiṣmikaṃ vā sasyaṃ sthāpayet ||
ū22420| śālyādi jyeṣṭham, ṣaṇḍo madhyamaḥ, ikṣuḥ pratyavaraḥ ||
022421| ikṣavo hi bahvābādhā vyayagrāhiṇaśca ||
022422| phenāghāto vallīphalānām, parīvāhāntāḥ pippalīmṛdvīkekṣūṇām, kūpaparyantāḥ śākamūlānām, haraṇīparyantā haritakānām, pālyo lavānāṃ gandhabhaiṣajyośīrahrīberapiṇḍālukādīnām ||
022423| yathāsvaṃ bhūmiṣu ca sthālyāścānūpyāścauṣadhīḥ sthāpayet ||
022424| tuṣārapāyanamuṣṇaśoṣaṇaṃ cāsaptarātrāditi dhānyabījānām, trirātraṃ vā pañcarātraṃ vā kośīdhānyānām, madhughṛtasūkaravasābhiḥ śakṛdyuktābhiḥ kāṇḍabījānāṃ chedalepo, madhughṛtena kandānām, asthibījānāṃ śakṛdālepaḥ, śākhināṃ gartadāho goasthiśakṛdbhiḥ kāle dauhrdaṃ ca ||
022425| prarūḍhāṃścāśuṣkakaṭumatsyāṃśca snuhikṣīreṇa pāyayet ||
022426  kārpāsasāraṃ nirmokaṃ sarpasya ca samāharet |
022426  na sarpāstatra tiṣṭhanti dhūmo yatraiṣa tiṣṭhati ||
022427| sarvajījānāṃ tu prathamavāpe suvarṇodakasamplutāṃ pūrvamuṣṭiṃ vāpayed, amuṃ ca mantraṃ brūyāt- "prajāpataye kāśyapāya devāya ca namaḥ sadā | sītā me ṛdhyatāṃ devī bījeṣu ca dhaneṣu ca ||
022428| ṣaṇḍavāṭagopālakadāsakarmakarebhyo yathāpuruṣaparivāpaṃ bhaktaṃ kuryāt, sa-pādapaṇikaṃ ca māsaṃ dadyāt ||
022429| karmānurūpaṃ kārubhyo bhaktavetanam ||
022430| praśīrṇaṃ ca puṣpaphalaṃ devakāryārthaṃ vrīhiyavamāgrayaṇārthaṃ śrotriyāstapasvinaścāhareyuḥ, rāśimūlamuñchavṛttayaḥ ||
022431  yathākālaṃ ca sasyādi jātaṃ jātaṃ praveśayet |
022431  na kṣetre sthāpayetkiṃcitpalālamapi paṇḍitaḥ ||
022432  prākārāṇāṃ samucchrayān valabhīrvā tathāvidhāḥ |
022432  na saṃhatāni kurvīta na tucchāni śirāṃsi ca ||
022433  khalasya prakarān kuryānmaṇḍalānte samāśritān |
022433  anagnikāḥ sodakāśca khale syuḥ parikarmiṇaḥ ||E

(Controllerof spiritualliṝuors)
022501| surādhyakṣaḥ surākiṇvavyavahārān durge janapade skandhāvāre vā tajjātasurākiṇvavyavahāribhiḥ kārayed, ekamukhamanekamukhaṃ vā vikrayakrayavaśena vā ||
022502| ṣaṭśatamatyayamanyatra kartṛkretṛvikretṛṛṇāṃ sthāpayet ||
022503| grāmādanirṇayaṇamasampātaṃ ca surāyāḥ, pramādabhayātkarmasu ñjirdiṣṭānām, maryādātikramabhayādāryāṇām, utsāhabhayācca tīṣkṇānām ||
022504| lakṣitamalpaṃ vā caturbhāgamardhakuḍubaṃ kuḍubamardhaprasthaṃ prasthaṃ veti jñātaśaucā nirhareyuḥ ||
022505| pānāgāreṣu vā pibeyurasaṃcāriṇaḥ ||
022506| nikṣepopanidhiprayogāpahṛtānāmaniṣṭopagatānāṃ ca dravyāṇāṃ jñānārthamasvāmikaṃ kupyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ copalabhya niṣkeptāramanyatra vyapadeśena grāhayed, ativyayakartāramanāyativyayaṃ ca ||
022507| na cānargheṇa kālikāṃ vā surāṃ dadyād, anyatra duṣṭasurāyāḥ ||
022508| tāmanyatra vikrāpayet ||
022509| dāsakarmakarebhyo vā vetanaṃ dadyāt ||
022510| vāhanapratipānaṃ sūkarapoṣaṇaṃ vā dadyāt ||
022511| pānāgārāṇyanekakakṣyāṇi vibhaktaśayanāsanavanti pānoddeśāni gandhamālyodakavanti ṛtusukhāni kārayet ||
022512| tatrasthāḥ prakṛtyautpattikau vyayau gūḍhā vidyuḥ, āgantūṃśca ||
022513| kretṛṛṇāṃ mattasuptānāmalaṅkārātchādanahiraṇyāni ca vidyuḥ ||
022514| tannāśe vaṇijastacca tāvacca daṇḍaṃ dadyuḥ ||
022515| vaṇijaśstu saṃvṛteṣu kakṣyāvibhāgeṣu svadāsībhiḥ peśalarūpābhirāgantūnāṃ vāstavyānāṃ cāryarūpāṇāṃ mattasuptānāṃ bhāvaṃ vidyuḥ ||
022516| medakaprasannāsavāriṣṭamaireyamadhūnām ||
022517| udakadroṇaṃ taṇḍulānāmardhāḍhakaṃ trayaḥ prasthāḥ kiṇvasyeti medakayogaḥ ||
022518| dvādaśāḍhakaṃ piṣṭasya pañca prasthāḥ kiṇvasya kramukatvakphalayukto vā jātisambhāraḥ prasannāyogaḥ ||
022519| kapitthatulā phāṇitaṃ pañcataulikaṃ prastho madhuna ityāsavayogaḥ ||
ū22520| pādadhiko jyeṣṭhaḥ pādahīnaḥ kaniṣṭhaḥ ||
ū22521| cikitsakapramāṇāḥ pratyekaśo vikārāṇāmariṣṭāḥ ||
ū22522| meṣaśṛṅgītvakkvāthābhiṣuto guḍapratīvāpaḥ pippalīmaricasambhārastriphalāyukto vā maireyaḥ ||
ẏ22523| guḍayuktānāṃ vā sarveṣāṃ triphalāsambhāraḥ ||
ū22524| mṛdvīkāraso madhu ||
u22525| tasya svadeśo vyākhyānaṃ kāpiśāyanaṃ hārahūrakamiti ||
ū22526| māṣakalanīdroṇamāmaṃ siddhaṃ vā tribhāgādhikataṇḍulaṃ moraṭādīnāṃ kārṣikabhāgayuktaṃ kiṇvabandhaḥ ||
022527| pāṭhāloghratejovatyelāvālukamadhukamadhurasāpriyaṅgudāruharidrāmaricapippalīnāṃ ca pañcakārṣikaḥ sambhārayogo medakasya prasannāyāśca ||
022528| madhukaniryūhayuktā kaṭaśarkarā varṇaprasādanī ca ||
022529| cocacitrakavilaṅgagajapippalīnāṃ ca kārṣikaḥ kramukamadhukamustālodhrāṇāṃ dvikārṣikaścāsavasambhāraḥ ||
022530| daśabhāgaścaiṣāṃ bījabandhaḥ ||
022531| prasannāyogaḥ śvetasurāyāḥ ||
022532| sahakārasurā rasottarā bījottarā vā mahāsurā sambhārikī vā ||
022533| tāsāṃ moraṭāpalāśapattūrameṣaśṛṅgīkarañjakṣīravṛkṣakaṣāyabhāvitaṃ dagdhakaṭaśarkarācūrṇaṃ loghracitrakavilaṅgapāṭhāmustākaliṅgayavadāruharidrendīvaraśatapuṣpāpāmārgasaptaparṇanimbāsphotakalkārdhayuktamantarnakho muṣṭiḥ kumbhīṃ rājapeyāṃ prasādayati ||
022534| phāṇitaḥ pañcapalikaścātra rasavṛddhirdeyaḥ ||
022535| kuṭumbinaḥ kṛtyeṣu śvetasurām, auṣadhārthaṃ vāriṣṭam, anyadvā kartuṃ labheran ||
022536| utsavasamājayātrāsu caturahaḥ sauriko deyaḥ ||
022537| teṣvananujñātānāṃ prahavanāntaṃ daivasikamatyayaṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||
022538| surākiṇvavicayaṃ striyo bālāśca kuryuḥ ||
022539| arājapaṇyāḥ pañcakaṃ śataṃ śulkaṃ dadyuḥ, surakāmedakāriṣṭamadhuphalāmlāmlaśīdhūnāṃ ca ||
022540  ahnaśca vikrayaṃ jñātvā vyājīṃ mānahiraṇyayoḥ |
022540  tathā vaidharaṇaṃ kuryāducitaṃ cānuvartayet ||E

(ṣupervisorof (animal-)slaughter)
022601| sūnādhyakṣaḥ pradiṣṭābhayānāmabhayavanavāsināṃ ca mṛgapaśupakṣimatsyānāṃ bandhavadhahiṃsāyāmuttamaṃ daṇḍaṃ kārayet, kuṭumbināmabhayavanaparigraheṣu madhyamam ||
022602| apravṛttavadhānāṃ matsyapakṣiṇāṃ bandhavadhahiṃsāyāṃ pādonasaptaviṃśatipaṇamatyayaṃ kuryāt, mṛgapaśūnāṃ dviguṇam ||
022603| pravṛttahiṃsānāmaparigṛhītānāṃ ṣaḍbhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt, matsyapakṣiṇāṃ daśabhāgaṃ vādhikam, mṛgapaśūnāṃ śulkaṃ vādhikam ||
022604| pakṣimṛgāṇāṃ jīvatṣaḍbhāgamabhayavaneṣu pramuñcet ||
022605| sāmudrahastyaśvapuruṣavṛṣagardabhākṛtayo matsyāḥ sārasā nādeyāstaṭākakulyodbhavā vā krauñcotkrośakadātyūhahaṃsacakravākajīvanjīvakabhṛṅgarājacakoramattakokilamayūraśukamadanaśārikā vihārapakṣiṇo maṅgalyāścānyepi prāṇinaḥ pakṣimṛgā hiṃsābādhebhyo rakṣyāḥ ||
022606| rakṣātikrame pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
022607| mṛgapaśūnāmanasthimāṃsaṃ sadyohataṃ vikrīṇīran ||
022608| asthimataḥ pratipātaṃ dadyuḥ ||
022609| tulāhīne hīnāṣṭaguṇam ||
022610| vatso vṛṣo dhenuścaiṣāmavadhyāḥ ||
022611| ghnataḥ pañcāśatko daṇḍaḥ, kliṣṭaghātaṃ ghātayataśca ||
022612| pariśūnamaśiraḥpādāsthi vigandhaṃ svayaṃmṛtaṃ ca na vikrīṇīran ||
022613| anyathā dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
022614  duṣṭāḥ paśumṛgavyālā matsyaścābhayacāriṇaḥ |
022614  anyatra guptisthānebhyo vadhabandhamavāpnuyuḥ ||E

(ṣuperintendentof courtesans)
022701| gaṇikādhyakṣo gaṇikānvayāmagaṇikānvayāṃ vā rūpayauvanaśilpasampannāṃ sahasreṇa gaṇikāṃ kārayet, kuṭumbārdhena pratigaṇikām ||
022702| niṣpatitāpretayorduhitā bhaginī vā kuṭumbaṃ bhareta, mātā vā pratigaṇikāṃ sthāpayet ||
022703| tāsāmabhāve rājā haret ||
022704| saubhāgyālaṃkāravṛddhyā sahasreṇa vāraṃ kaniṣṭhaṃ madhyamamuttamaṃ vāropayetchatrabhṛṅgāravyajanaśibikāpīṭhikāratheṣu ca viśeṣārtham ||
022705| saubhāgyabhaṅge mātṛkāṃ kuryāt ||
022706| niṣkrayaścaturviṃśatisāhasro gaṇikāyāḥ, dvādaśasāhasro gaṇikāputrasya ||
022707| aṣṭavarṣātprabhṛti rājñaḥ kuśīlavakarma kuryāt ||
022708| gaṇikādāsī bhagnabhogā koṣṭhāgāre mahānase vā karma kuryāt ||
022709| aviśantī sapādapaṇamavaruddhā māsavetanaṃ dadyāt ||
022710| bhogaṃ dāyamāyaṃ vyayamāyatiṃ ca gaṇikāyā nibandhayet, ativyayakarma ca vārayet ||
022711| mātṛhastādanyatra abharaṇanyāse sapādacatuṣpaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
022712| svāpateyaṃ vikrayamādhānaṃ vā nayantyāḥ sapādapañcāśatpaṇaḥ paṇoardhapaṇacchedane ||
022713| akāmāyāḥ kumāryā vā sāhase uttamo daṇḍaḥ, sakāmāyāḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
022714| gaṇikāmakāmāṃ rundhato niṣpātayato vā vraṇavidāraṇena vā rūpaṃupaghnataḥ sahasraṃ daṇḍaḥ ||
022715| sthānviśeṣeṇa vā daṇḍavṛddhiḥ āniṣkrayadviguṇāt ||
022716| prāptādhikāraṃ gaṇikāṃ ghatayato niṣkrayatriguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
022717| mātṛkāduhitṛkārūpadāsīnāṃ ghāte uttamaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
022718| sarvatra prathameparādhe prathamaḥ, dvitīye dviguṇaḥ, tṛtīye triguṇaḥ, caturthe yathākāmī syāt ||
022719| rājājñayā puruṣamanabhigacchantī gaṇikā śiphāsahasraṃ labheeta, pañcasahasraṃ vā daṇḍaḥ ||
022720| bhogaṃ gṛhītvā dviṣatyā bhogadviguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
022721| vasatibhogāpahāre bhogamaṣṭaguṇaṃ dadyādanyatra vyādhipuruṣadoṣebhyaḥ ||
022722| puruṣaṃ ghnatyāścitāpratāpepsu praveśanaṃ vā ||
022723| gaṇikābharaṇamarthaṃ bhogaṃ vāpaharatoaṣṭaguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
022724| gaṇikā bhogamāyatiṃ puruṣaṃ ca nivedayet ||
022725| etena naṭanartakagāyanavādakavāgjīvanakuśīlavaplavakasaubhikacāraṇānāṃ strīvyavahāriṇāṃ striyo gūḍhājīvāśca vyākhyātāḥ ||
022726| teṣāṃ tūryamāgantukaṃ pañcapaṇaṃ prekṣāvetanaṃ dadyāt ||
022727| rūpājīvā bhogadvayaguṇaṃ māsaṃ dadyuḥ ||
022728| gītavādyapāṭhyanṛtyanāṭyākṣaracitravīṇāveṇumṛdaṅgaparacittajñānagandhamālyasamyūhanasaṃvādanasaṃvāhanavaiśikakalājñānāni gaṇikā dāsī raṅgopajīvinīśca grāhayato rājamaṇḍalādājīvaṃ kuryāt ||
022729| gaṇikāputrān raṅgopajīvināṃ ca mukhyānniṣpādayeyuḥ, sarvatālāvacarāṇāṃ ca ||
022730  saṃjñābhāṣāntarajñāśca striyasteṣāmanātmasu |
022730  cāraghātapramādārthaṃ prayojyā bandhuvāhanāḥ ||E

(Controllerof shipping)
022801| nāvadhyakṣaḥ samudrasamyānanadīmukhatarapracārān devasarovisaronadītarāṃśca sthānīyādiṣvavekṣeta ||
022802| tadvelākūlagrāmāḥ klṛptaṃ dadyuḥ ||
022803| matsyabandhakā naukābhāṭakaṃ ṣaḍbhāgaṃ dadyuḥ ||
022804| pattanānuvṛttaṃ śulkabhāgaṃ vaṇijo dadyuḥ, yātrāvetanaṃ rājanaubhiḥ sampatantaḥ ||
022805| śaṅkhamuktāgrāhiṇo naubhāṭakaṃ dadyuḥ, svanaubhirvā tareyuḥ ||
022806| adhyakṣaścaiṣāṃ khanyadhyakṣeṇa vyākhyātaḥ ||
022807| pattanādhyakṣanibaddhaṃ paṇyapattanacāritraṃ nāvadhyakṣaḥ pālayet ||
022808| mūḍhavātāhatā nāvaḥ pitevānugṛhṇīyāt ||
022809| udakaprāptaṃ paṇyamaśulkamardhaśulkaṃ vā kuryāt ||
022810| yathānirdiṣṭāścaitāḥ paṇyapattanayātrākāleṣu preṣayet ||
022811| samyātīrnāvaḥ kṣetrānugatāḥ śulkaṃ yācet ||
022812| hiṃsrikā nirghātayet, amitraviṣayātigāḥ paṇyapattanacāritropaghātikāśca ||
022813| śāsakaniryāmakadātraraśmigrāhakotsecakādhiṣṭhitāśca mahānāvo hemantagrīṣmatāryāsu mahānadīṣu prayojayet, kṣudrikāḥ kṣudrikāsu varṣāsrāviṇīṣu ||
022814| bādhatīrthāścaitāḥ kāryā rājadviṣṭakāriṇāṃ taraṇabhayāt ||
022815| akāletīrthe ca tarataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
022816| kāle tīrthe cāniṣṛṣṭatāriṇaḥ pādonasaptaviṃśatipaṇastarātyayaḥ ||
022817| kaivartakāṣṭatṛṇabhārapuṣpaphalavāṭaṣaṇḍagopālakānāmanatyayaḥ, sambhāvyadūtānupātināṃ ca senābhāṇḍaprayogāṇāṃ ca svataraṇaistaratām, bījabhaktadravyopaskarāṃścānūpagrāmāṇāṃ tārayatām ||
022818| brāhmaṇapravrajitabālavṛddhavyādhitaśāsanaharagarbhiṇyo nāvadhyakṣamudrābhistareyuḥ ||
022819| kṛtapraveśāḥ pāraviṣayikāḥ sārthapramāṇā vā praviśeyuḥ ||
022820| parasya bhāryāṃ kanyāṃ vittaṃ vāpaharantaṃ śavittaṃ vāpaharantaṃ śaṅkitamāvignamudbhāṇḍīkṛtaṃ mahābhāṇḍena mūrdhni bhāreṇāvacchādayantaṃ sadyogṛhītaliṅginamaliṅginaṃ vā pravrajitamalakṣyavyādhitaṃ bhayavikāriṇaṃ gūḍhasārabhāṇḍaśāsanaśastrāgniyogaṃ viṣahastaṃ dīrghapathikamamudraṃ copagrāhayet ||
022821| kṣudrapaśurmanuṣyaśca sabhāro māṣakaṃ dadyāt, śirobhāraḥ kāyabhāro gavāśvaṃ ca dvau, uṣṭramahiṣaṃ caturaḥ, pañca labhuyānam, ṣaḍgoliṅgam, sapta śakaṭam, panyabhāraḥ pādam ||
022822| tena bhāṇḍabhāro vyākhyātaḥ ||
022823| dviguṇo mahānadīṣu taraḥ ||
022824| klṛptamānūpagrāmā bhaktavetanaṃ dadyuḥ ||
022825| pratyanteṣu tarāḥ śulkamātivāhikaṃ vartanīṃ ca gṛhṇīyuḥ, nirgacchataścāmudradravyasya bhāṇḍaṃ hareyuḥ, atibhāreṇāvelāyāmatirthe tarataśca ||
022826| puruṣopakaraṇahīnāyāmasaṃskṛtāyāṃ vā nāvi vipannāyāṃ nāvadhhyakṣo naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ vābhyāvahet ||
022827  saptāhavṛttāmāṣāḍhīṃ kārttikīṃ cāntarā taraḥ |
022827  kārmikaḥ pratyayaṃ dadyānnityaṃ cāhnikamāvahet ||E
(ṣuperintendentof cattle)
022901| goadhyakṣo vetanopagrāhikaṃ karapratikaraṃ bhagnotsṛṣṭakaṃ bhāgānupraviṣṭakaṃ vrajaparyagraṃ naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ kṣīraghṛtasaṃjātaṃ copalabheta ||
022902| gopālakapiṇḍārakadohakamanthakalubdhakāḥ śataṃ śataṃ dhenūnāṃ hiraṇyabhṛtāḥ pālayeyuḥ ||
022903| kṣīraghṛtabhṛtā hi vatsānupahanyuḥ | iti vetanopagrāhikam ||
022904| jaradgudhenugarbhiṇīpaṣṭhauhīvatsatarīṇāṃ samavibhāgaṃ rūpaśatamekaḥ pālayet ||
022905| ghṛtasyāṣṭau vārakān paṇikaṃ pucchamaṅkacarma ca vārṣikaṃ dadyāt | iti karapratikaraḥ ||
022906| vyādhitānyaṅgānanyadohīdurdohāputraghnīnāṃ ca samavibhāgaṃ rūpaśataṃ pālayantastajjātikaṃ bhāgaṃ dadyuḥ | iti bhagnotṣṛṣṭakam ||
022907| paracakrāṭavībhayādanupraviṣṭānāṃ paśūnāṃ pālanadharmeṇa daśabhagaṃ dadyuḥ | iti bhāgānupraviṣṭakam ||
022908| vatsā vatsatarā damyā vahino vṛṣā ukṣāṇaśca puṃgavāḥ, yugavāhanaśakaṭavahā vṛṣabhāḥ sūnāmahiṣāḥ pṛṣṭaskandhavāhinaśca mahiṣāḥ, vatsikā vatsatarī paṣṭahuhī garbhiṇī dhenuścāprajātā vandhyāśca gāvo mahiṣyaśca, māsadvimāsajātāstāsāmupajā vatsā vatsikāśca ||
022909| māsadvimāsajātānaṅkayet ||
022910| māsadvimāsaparyuṣitamaṅkayet ||
022911| aṅkaṃ cihnaṃ varṇaṃ śṛṅgāntaraṃ ca lakṣaṇamevamupajā nibandhayet | iti vrajaparyagram ||
022912| corahṛtamanyayūthapraviṣṭamavalīnaṃ vā naṣṭam ||
022913| paṅkaviṣamavyādhijarātoyāhārāvasannaṃ vṛkṣataṭakāṣṭhaśilābhihatamīśānavyālasarpagrāhadāvāgnivipannaṃ vinaṣṭam ||
022914| pramādādabhyāvaheyuḥ ||
022915| evaṃ rūpāgraṃ vidyāt ||
022916| svayaṃ hantā ghātayitā hartā hārayitā ca vadhyaḥ ||
022917| parapaśūnāṃ rājāṅkena parivartayitā rūpasya pūrvaṃ sāhasadaṇḍaṃ dadyāt ||
022918| svadeśīyānāṃ corahṛtaṃ pratyānīya paṇitaṃ rūpaṃ haret ||
022919| paradeśīyānāṃ mokṣayitārdhaṃ haret ||
022920| bālavṛddhavyādhitānāṃ gopālakāḥ pratikuryuḥ ||
022921| lubdhakaśvagaṇibhirapāstastenāvyālaparābādhabhayamṛtuvibhaktamaraṇyaṃ cārayeyuḥ ||
022922| sarpavyālatrāsanārthaṃ gocarānupātajñānārthaṃ ca trasnūnāṃ ghaṇṭātūryaṃ ca badhnīyuḥ ||
022923| samavyūḍhatīrthamakardamagrāhamudakamavatārayeyuḥ pālayeyuśca ||
022924| stenavyālasarpagrāhagṛhītaṃ vyādhijarāvasannaṃ cāvedayeyuḥ, anyathā rūpamūlyaṃ bhajeran ||
022925| kāraṇamṛtasyāṅkacarma gomahiṣasya, karṇalakṣaṇamajāvikānām, pucchamaṅkacarma cāśvakharoṣṭrāṇām, bālacarmabastipittasnāyudantakhuraśṛṅgāsthīni cāhareyuḥ ||
022926| māṃsamārdraṃ śuṣkaṃ vā vikrīṇīyuḥ ||
022927| udaśvitśvavarāhebhyo dadyuḥ ||
022928| kūrcikāṃ senābhaktārthamāhareyuḥ ||
022929| kilāṭo ghāṇapiṇyākakledārthaḥ ||
022930| paśuvikretā pādikaṃ rūpaṃ dadyāt ||
022931| varṣāśaraddhemantānubhayataḥkālaṃ duhyuḥ, śiśiravasantagrīṣmānekakālam ||
022932| dvitīyakāladogdhuraṅguṣṭhacchedo daṇḍaḥ ||
022933| dohanakālamatikrāmatastatphalahānaṃ daṇḍaḥ ||
022934| etena nasyadamyayugapiṅganavartanakālā vyākhyātāḥ ||
022935| kṣīradroṇe gavāṃ ghṛtaprasthaḥ, pañcabhāgādhiko mahiṣīṇām, dvibhāgādhikoajāvīnām ||
022936| mantho vā sarveṣāṃ pramāṇam ||
022937| bhūmitṛṇodakaviśeṣāddhi kṣīraghṛtavṛddhirbhavati ||
022938| yūthavṛṣaṃ vṛṣeṇāvapātayataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, ghātayata uttamaḥ ||
022939| varṇāvarodhena daśatī rakṣā ||
022940| upaniveśadigvibhāgo gopracārādbalānvayato vā gavāṃ rakṣāsāmarthyācc |
022941| ajāvīnāṃ ṣaṇmāsikīmūrṇāṃ grāhayet ||
022942| tenāśvakharoṣṭravarāhavrajā vyākhyātāḥ ||
022943| balīvardānāṃ nasyāśvabhadragativāhināṃ yavasasyārdhabhārastṛṇasya dviguṇam, tulā ghāṇapiṇyākasya, daśāḍhakaṃ kaṇakuṇḍakasya, pañcapalikaṃ mukhalavanām, tailakuḍubo nasyaṃ prasthaḥ pānaṃ, māṃsatulā, dadhnaścāḍhakam, yavadroṇaṃ māṣāṇāṃ vā pulākaḥ, kṣīradroṇamardhāḍhakaṃ vā surāyāḥ snehaprasthaḥ kṣāradaśapalaṃ śṛṅgiberapalaṃ
ca pratipānam ||
022944| pādonamaśvataragokharāṇām, dviguṇaṃ mahiṣoṣṭrāṇām ||
022945| karmakarabalīvardānāṃ pāyanārthānāṃ ca dhenūnāṃ karmakālataḥ phalataśca vidhādānam ||
022946| sarveṣāṃ tṛṇodakaprākāmyam ||
022947| iti gomaṇḍalaṃ vyākhyātam ||
022948  pañcarṣabhaṃ kharāśvānāmajāvīnāṃ daśarṣabham |
022948  śatyaṃ gomahiṣoṣṭrāṇāṃ yūthaṃ kuryāccaturvṛṣam ||E

(ṣuperintendentof horses)
023001| aśvādhyakṣaḥ paṇyāgārikaṃ krayopāgatamāhavalabdhamājātaṃ sāhāyyāgatakaṃ paṇasthitaṃ yāvatkālikaṃ vāśvaparyagraṃ kulavayovarṇacihnavargāgamairlekhayet ||
023002| apraśastanyaṅgavyādhitāṃścāvedayet ||
023003| kośakoṣṭhāgārābhyāṃ ca gṛhītvā māsalābhamaśvavāhaścintayet ||
023004| aśvavibhavenāyatāmaśvāyāmadviguṇavistārāṃ caturdvāropāvartanamadhyāṃ sa-pragrīvāṃ pradvārāsanaphalakayuktānāṃ vānaramayūrapṛṣatanakulacakoraśukasārikākīrṇāṃ śālāṃ niveśayet ||
023005| aśvāyāmacaturaśraślakṣṇaphalakāstāraṃ sa-khādanakoṣṭhakaṃ sa-mūtrapurīṣotsargamekaikaśaḥ prānmukhamudanmukhaṃ vā sthānaṃ niveśayet ||
023006| śālāvaśena vā digvibhāgaṃ kalpayet ||
023007| vaḍavāvṛṣakiśorāṇāmekānteṣu ||
023008| vaḍavāyāḥ prajatāyāstrirātraṃ ghṛtaprasthaḥ pānam ||
023009| ata ūrdhvaṃ saktuprasthaḥ snehabhaiṣajyapratipānaṃ daśarātram ||
023010| tataḥ pulāko yavasamārtavaścāhāraḥ ||
023011| daśarātrādūrdhvaṃ kiśorasya ghṛtacaturbhāgaḥ saktukuḍubaḥ kṣīraprasthaścāhāra āṣaṇmāsāt ||
023012| tataḥ paraṃ māsottaramardhavṛddhiryavaprastha ātrivarṣāt, droṇa ācaturvarṣāt ||
023013| ata ūrdhvaṃ caturvarṣaḥ pañcavarṣo vā karmaṇyaḥ pūrṇapramāṇaḥ ||
023014| dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ mukhamuttamāśvasya, pañcamukhānyāyāmo, viṃśatyaṅgulā jaṅghā, caturjaṅgha utsedhaḥ ||
023015| tryaṅgulāvaraṃ madhyamāvarayoḥ ||
023016| śatāṅgulaḥ pariṇāhaḥ ||
023017| pañcabhāgāvaro madhyamāvarayoḥ ||
023018| uttamāśvasya dvidroṇaṃ śālivrīhiyavapriyaṅgūṇāmardhaśuṣkamardhasiddhaṃ vā mudgamāṣāṇāṃ vā pulākaḥ snehaprasthaśca, pañcapalaṃ lavaṇasya, māṃsaṃ pañcāśatpalikaṃ rasasyāḍhakaṃ dviguṇaṃ vā dadhnaḥ piṇḍakledanārtham, kṣārapañcapalikaḥ surāyāḥ prasthaḥ payaso vā dviguṇaḥ pratipānam ||
023019| dīrghapathabhāraklāntānāṃ ca khādanārthaṃ snehaprasthoanuvāsanaṃ kuḍubo nasyakarmaṇaḥ, yavasasyārdhabhārastṛṇasya dviguṇaḥ ṣaḍaratniparikṣepaḥ puñjīlagraho vā ||
023020| pādāvarametanmadhyamāvarayoḥ ||
023021| uttamasamo rathyo vṛṣaśca madhyamaḥ ||
023022| madhyamasamaścāvaraḥ ||
023023| pādahīnaṃ vaḍavānāṃ pāraśamānāṃ ca ||
023024| atoardhaṃ kiśorāṇāṃ ca ||
023025| iti vidhāyogaḥ ||
023026| vidhāpācakasūtragrāhakacikitsakāḥ pratisvādabhājaḥ ||
023027| yuddhavyādhijarākarmakṣīṇāḥ piṇḍagocarikāḥ syuḥ ||023028| asamaraprayogyāḥ paurajānapadānāmarthena vṛṣā vaḍavāsvāyojyāḥ ||
023029| prayogyānāmuttamāḥ kāmbojasaindhavāraṭṭavanāyujāḥ, madhyamā bāhlīkapāpeyakasauvīrakataitalāḥ, śeṣāḥ pratyavarāḥ ||
023030| teṣāṃ tīṣkṇabhadramandavaśena sāmnāhyamaupavāhyakaṃ vā karma prayojayet ||
023031| caturaśraṃ karmāśvasya sāmnāhyam ||
023032| valgano nīcairgato laṅghano ghoraṇo nāroṣṭraścāupavāhyāḥ ||
023033| tatrāupaveṇuko vardhamānako yamaka ālīḍhaplutaḥ pṛthugastrikacālī ca valganaḥ ||
023034| sa eva śiraḥkarṇaviśuddho nīcairgataḥ, ṣoḍaśamārgo vā ||
023035| prakīrṇakaḥ prakīrṇottaro niṣaṇṇaḥ pārśvānuvṛtta ūrmimārgaḥ śarabhakrīḍitaḥ śarabhaplutastritālo bāhyānuvṛttaḥ pañcapāṇiḥ siṃhāyataḥ svādhūtaḥ kliṣṭaḥ śliṅgito bṛṃhitaḥ puṣpābhikīrṇaśceti nīcairgatamārgaḥ ||
023036| kapipluto bhekapluteṇaplutaikapādaplutaḥ kokilasaṃcāryurasyo bakacārī ca laṅghanaḥ ||
023037| kāṅko vārikāṅko māyūroardhamāyūro nākulordhanākulo vārāhoardhavārāhaśceti dhoraṇaḥ ||
023038| saṃjñāpratikāro nāroṣṭreti ||
023039| ṣaṇṇava dvādaśeti yojanānydhvā rathyānām, pañca yojanānyardhāṣṭamāni daśeti pṛṣṭhavāhināmaśvānāmadhvā ||
023040| vikramo bhadrāśvāso bhāravāhya iti mārgāḥ ||
023041| vikramo valgitamupakaṇṭhamupajavo javaśca dhārāḥ ||
023042| teṣāṃ bandhanopakaraṇaṃ yogyācāryāḥ pratidiśeyuḥ, sāṃgrāmikaṃ rathāśvālaṃkāraṃ ca sūtāḥ ||
023043| aśvānāṃ cikitsakāḥ śarīrahrāsavṛddhipratīkāramṛtuvibhaktaṃ cāhāram ||
023044| sūtragrāhakāśvabandhakayāvasikavidhāpācakasthānapālakeśakārajāṅgulīvidaśca svakarmabhiraśvānārādhayeyuḥ ||
023045| karmātikrame caiṣāṃ divasavetanacchedanaṃ kuryāt ||
023046| nīrājanoparuddhaṃ vāhayataścikitsakoparuddhaṃ vā dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
023047| kriyābhaiṣajyasaṅgena vyādhivṛddhau pratīkāradviguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
023048| tadaparādhena vailomye pattramūlyaṃ daṇḍaḥ ||
023049| tena gomaṇḍalaṃ kharoṣṭramahiṣamajāvikaṃ ca vyākhyātam ||
023050  dvirahnaḥ snānamaśvānāṃ gandhamālyaṃ ca dāpayet |
023050  kṛṣṇasaṃdhiṣu bhūtejyāḥ śukleṣu svastivācanam ||
023051  nīrājanāmāśvayuje kārayennavamehani |
023051  yātrādāvavasāne vā vyādhau vā śāntike rataḥ ||E

(ṣuperintendentof elephants)
023101| hastyadhyakṣo hastivanarakṣāṃ damyakarmakṣāntānāṃ hastihastinīkalabhānāṃ śālāsthānaśayyākarmavidhāyavasapramāṇaṃ karmasvāyogaṃ bandhanopakaraṇaṃ sāṃgrāmikamalaṃkāraṃ cikitsakānīkasthāupasthāyikavargaṃ cānutiṣṭhet ||
023102| hastyāyāmadviguṇotsedhaviṣkambhāyāmāṃ hastinīsthānādhikāṃ sapragrīvāṃ kumārīsaṃgrahāṃ prānmukhīmudanmukhīṃ vā śālāṃ niveśayet ||
023103| hastyāyāmacaturaśraślakṣṇālānastambhaphalakāstarakaṃ sa-mūtrapurīṣotsargaṃ sthānaṃ niveśayet ||
023104| sthānasamāṃ śayyāmardhāpāśrayāṃ durge sāmnāhyāupavāhyānāṃ bahirdamyavyālānām ||
023105| prathamasaptama aṣṭamabhāgāvahnaḥ snānakālau, tadanantaraṃ vidhāyāḥ ||
023106| pūrvāhne vyāyāmakālaḥ, paścāhnaḥ pratipānakālaḥ ||
023107| rātribhāgau dvau svapnakālā, tribhāgaḥ saṃveśanotthānikaḥ ||
023108| grīṣme grahaṇakālaḥ ||
023109| viṃśativarṣo grāhyaḥ ||
023110| vikko moḍho makkaṇo vyāthito garbhiṇī dhenukā hastinī cāgrāhyāḥ ||
023111| saptāratni utsedho navāyāmo daśa pariṇāhaḥ pramāṇataścatvāriṃśadvarṣo bhavatyuttamaḥ, triṃśadvarṣo madhyamaḥ, pañcaviṃśativarṣoavaraḥ ||
023112| tayoḥ pādāvaro vidhāvidhiḥ ||
023113| aratnau taṇuladroṇaḥ, ardhāḍhakaṃ tailasya, sarpiṣastrayaḥ prasthāḥ, daśapalaṃ lavaṇasya, māṃsaṃ pañcāśatpalikam, rasasyāḍhakaṃ dviguṇaṃ vā dadhnaḥ piṇḍakledanārtham, kṣāradaśapalikaṃ madyasyāḍhakaṃ dviguṇaṃ vā payasaḥ pratipānam, gātrāvasekastailaprasthaḥ, śirasoaṣṭabhāgaḥ prādīpikaśca, yavasasya dvau bhārau sa-pādau,
śaṣpasya śuṣkasyārdhatṛtīyo bhāraḥ, kaḍaṅkarasyāniyamaḥ ||
023114| saptāratninā tulyabhojanoaṣṭāratniratyarālaḥ ||
023115| yathāhastamavaśeṣaḥ ṣaḍaratniḥ pañcāratniśca ||
023116| kṣīrayāvasiko vikkaḥ krīḍārthaṃ grāhyaḥ ||
023117| saṃjātalohitā praticchannā samliptapakṣā samakakṣyā vyatikīrṇamāṃsā samatalpatalā jātadroṇiketi śobhāḥ ||
023118  śobhāvaśena vyāyāmaṃ bhadrma mandaṃ ca kārayet |
023118  mṛgaṃ saṃkīrṇaliṅgaṃ ca karmasvṛtuvaśena vā ||

(āctivityof elephants)
023201| karmaskandhāścatvāro damyaḥ sāmnāhya aupavāhyo vyālaśca ||
023202| tatra damyaḥ pañcavidhaḥ skandhagataḥ stambhagato vārigatoavapātagato yūthagataśceti ||
023203| tasyopavicāro vikkakarma ||
023204| sāmnāhyaḥ saptakriyāpatha upasthānaṃ saṃvartanaṃ samyānaṃ vadhāvadho hastiyuddhaṃ nāgarāyaṇaṃ sāṃgrāmikaṃ ca ||
023205| tasyopavicāraḥ kakṣyākarma graiveyakarma yūthakarma ca ||
023206| aupavāhyoaṣṭavidha ācaraṇaḥ kuñjarāupavāhyo dhoraṇa ādhānagatiko yaṣṭyupavāhyastotropavāhyaḥ śuddhopavāhyo mārgayukaśceti ||
023207| tasyopavicāraḥ śāradakarma hīnakarma nāroṣṭrakarma ca ||
023208| vyālaikakriyāpathaḥ śaṅkitoavaruddho viṣamaḥ prabhinnaḥ prabhinnaviniścayo madahetuviniścayaśca ||
023209| tasyopavicāra āyamyaikarakṣākarma ||
023210| kriyāvipanno vyālaḥ śuddhaḥ suvrato viṣamaḥ sarvadoṣapraduṣṭaśca ||
023211| teṣāṃ bandhanopakaraṇamanīkasthapramāṇam ||
023212| ālānagraiveyakakṣyāpārāyaṇaparikṣepottarādikaṃ bandhanam ||
023213| aṅkuśaveṇuyantrādikamupakaraṇam ||
023214| vaijayantīkṣurapramālāstaraṇakuthādikaṃ bhūṣaṇam ||
023215| varmatomaraśarāvāpayantrādikaḥ sāṃgrāmikālaṃkāraḥ ||
023216| cikitsakānīkasthārohakādhoraṇahastipakaupacārikavidhāpācakayāvasikapādapāśikakuṭīrrakṣakāupaśayaikādiraupasthāyikavargaḥ ||
023217| cikitsakakuṭīrakṣavidhāpācakāḥ prasthaudanaṃ snehaprasṛtiṃ kṣāralavaṇayośca dvipalikaṃ hareyuḥ, daśapalaṃ māṃsasya, anyatra cikitsakebhyaḥ ||
023218| pathivyādhikarmamadajarābhitaptānāṃ cikitsakāḥ pratikuryuḥ ||
023219| sthānasyāśuddhiryavasasyāgrahaṇaṃ sthale śāyanamabhāge ghātaḥ parārohaṇamakāle yānamabhūmāvatīrthevatāraṇaṃ taruṣaṇḍa ityatyayasthānāni ||
023220| tameṣāṃ bhaktavetanādādadīta ||
023221  tisro nīrājanāḥ kāryāścāturmāsyartusaṃdhiṣu |
023221  bhūtānāṃ kṛṣṇasaṃdhījyāḥ senānyaḥ śuklasaṃdhuṣu ||
023222  dantamūlaparīṇāhadviguṇaṃ projjhya kalpayet |
023222  abde dvyardhe nadījānāṃ pañcābde parvataukasām ||E

(āctivityof the commandantof the army)
023301| aśvādhyakṣeṇa rathādhyakṣo vyākhyātaḥ ||
023302| sa rathakarmāntān kārayet ||
023303| daśapuruṣo dvādaśāntaro rathaḥ ||

03228| putravatī vindamānā strīdhanaṃ jīyeta ||
03229| tattu strīdhanaṃ putrā hareyuḥ ||
03230| putrabharaṇārthaṃ vā vindamānā putrārthaṃ sphātīkuryāt ||
03231| bahupuruṣaprajānāṃ putrāṇāṃ yathāpitṛdattaṃ strīdhanamavasthāpayet ||
03232| kāmakaraṇīyamapi strīdhanaṃ vindamānā putrasaṃsthaṃ kuryāt ||
03233| aputrā patiśayanaṃ pālayantī gurusamīpe strīdhanamāyuḥkṣayādbhuñjīta ||
03234| āpadarthaṃ hi strīdhanam ||
03235| ūrdhvaṃ dāyādaṃ gacchet ||
03236| jīvati bhartari mṛtāyāḥ putrā duhitaraśca strīdhanaṃ vibhajeran, aputrāyā duhitaraḥ, tadabhāve bhartā ||
03237| śulkamanvādheyamanyadvā bandhubhirdattaṃ bāndhavā hareyuḥ || iti strīdhanakalpaḥ |
03238| varṣāṇyaṣṭāvaprajāyamānāmaputrāṃ vandhyāṃ cākāṅkṣeta, daśa nindum, dvādaśa kanyāprasavinīm ||
03239| tataḥ putrārthī dvitīyāṃ vindeta ||
03240| tasyātikrame śulkaṃ strīdhanamardhaṃ cādhivedanikaṃ dadyāt, caturviṃśatipaṇaparaṃ ca daṇḍam ||
03241| śulkaṃ strīdhanamaśulkastrīdhanāyāstatpramāṇamādhivedanikamanurūpāṃ ca vṛttiṃ dattvā bahvīrapi vindeta ||
03242| putrārthā hi striyaḥ ||
03243| tīrthasamavāye cāsāṃ yathāvivāhaṃ pūrvoḍhāṃ jīvatputrāṃ vā pūrvaṃ gacchet ||
03244| tīrthagūhanāgamane ṣaṇṇavatirdaṇḍaḥ ||
03245| putravatīṃ dharmakāmāṃ vandhyāṃ ninduṃ nīrajaskāṃ vā nākāmāmupeyāt ||
03246| na cākāmaḥ puruṣaḥ kuṣṭhinīmunmattāṃ vā gacchet ||
03247| strī tu putrārthamevaṃbhūtaṃ vopagacchet ||
03248  nīcatvaṃ paradeśaṃ vā prasthito rājakilbiṣī |
03248  prāṇābhihantā patitastyājyaḥ klīboapi vā patiḥ ||E

((iv) ṃritalduty(v) ṃaintenance (vi) Cruelty(vī) ḍisaffection (vīi) ṃisconduct(ix) ড়rohibitionof favoursanddealings)
03301| dvādaśavarṣā strī prāptavyavahārā bhavati, ṣoḍaśavarṣaḥ pumān ||
03302| ata ūrdhvamaśuśrūṣāyāṃ dvādaśapaṇaḥ striyā daṇḍaḥ, puṃso dviguṇaḥ || iti śuśrūṣā |
03303| bharmaṇyāyāmanirdiṣṭakālāyāṃ grāsācchādanaṃ vādhikaṃ yathāpuruṣaparivāpaṃ saviśeṣaṃ dadyāt ||
03304| nirdiṣṭakālāyāṃ tadeva saṃkhyāya bandhaṃ ca dadyāt ||
03305| śulkastrīdhanādhivedanikānāmanādāne ca ||
03306| śvaśurakulapraviṣṭāyāṃ vibhaktāyāṃ vā nābhiyojyaḥ patiḥ || iti bharma |
03307| "naṣṭe" "vinaṣṭe" "nyaṅge" "apitṛke" "amātṛke" ityanirdeśena vinayagrāhaṇam ||
03308| veṇudalarajjuhastānāmanyatamena vā pṛṣṭhe trirāghātaḥ ||
03309| tasyātikrame vāgdaṇḍapāruṣyadaṇḍābhyāmardhadaṇḍāḥ ||
03310| tadeva striyā bhartari prasiddhadoṣāyāḥ ||
03311| īrṣyayā bāhyavihāreṣu dvāreṣvatyayo yathānirdiṣṭaḥ || iti pāruṣyam |
03312| bhartāraṃ dviṣatī strī saptārtavānyamaṇḍayamānā tadānīmeva sthāpyābharaṇaṃ nidhāya bhartāramanyayā saha śayānamanuśayīta ||
03313| bhikṣukyanvādhijñātikulānāmanyatame vā bhartā dviṣan striyamekāmanuśayīta ||
03314| dṛṣṭaliṅge maithunāpahāre savarṇāpasarpopagame vā mithyāvādī dvādaśapaṇaṃ dadyāt ||
03315| amokṣyā bharturakāmasya dviṣatī bhāryā, bhāryāyāśca bhartā ||
03316| parasparaṃdveṣānmokṣaḥ ||
03317| strīviprakārādvā puruṣaścenmokṣamicchedyathāgṛhītamasyai dadyāt ||
03318| puruṣaviprakārādvā strī cenmokṣamicchennāsyai yathāgṛhītaṃ dadyāt ||
03319| amokṣo dharmavivāhānām || iti dveṣaḥ |
03320| pratiṣiddhā strī darpamadyakrīḍāyāṃ tripaṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyāt ||
03321| divā strīprekṣāvihāragamane ṣaṭpaṇo daṇḍaḥ, puruṣaprekṣāvihāragamane dvādaśapaṇaḥ ||
03322| rātrau dviguṇaḥ ||
03323| suptamattapravrajane bharturadāne ca dvārasya dvādaśapaṇaḥ ||
03324| rātrau niṣkasane dviguṇaḥ ||
03325| strīpuṃsayormaithunārthenāṅgaviceṣṭāyāṃ rahoaślīlasambhāṣāyāṃ vā caturviṃśatipaṇaḥ striyā daṇḍaḥ, puṃso dviguṇaḥ ||
03326| keśanīvidantanakhālambaneṣu pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, puṃso dviguṇaḥ ||
03327| śaṅkitasthāne sambhāṣāyāṃ ca paṇasthāne śiphādaṇḍaḥ ||
03328| strīṇāṃ grāmamadhye caṇḍālaḥ pakṣāntare pañcaśiphā dadyāt ||
03329| paṇikaṃ vā prahāraṃ mokṣayet || ityatīcāraḥ |
03330| pratiṣiddhayoḥ strīpuṃsayoranyonyopakāre kṣudrakadravyāṇāṃ dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, sthūlakadravyāṇāṃ caturviṃśatipaṇaḥ, hiraṇyasuvarṇayoścatuṣpañcāśatpaṇaḥ striyā daṇḍaḥ, puṃsordviguṇaḥ ||
03331| ta evāgamyayorardhadaṇḍāḥ, tathā pratiṣiddhapuruṣavyavahāreṣu ca || iti pratiṣedhaḥ |
03332  rājadviṣṭāticārābhyāmātmāpakramaṇena ca |
03332  strīdhanānītaśulkānāmasvāmyaṃ jāyate striyāḥ ||E

(ḻeavinghome ṅoingaway(witha man) ṣhortabsence froṃ home ḻongabsence froṃ home)
03401| patikulānniṣpatitāyāḥ striyāḥ ṣaṭpaṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra viprakārāt ||
03402| pratiṣiddhāyāṃ dvādaśapaṇaḥ ||
03403| prativeśagṛhātigatāyāḥ ṣaṭpaṇaḥ ||
03404| prātiveśikabhikṣukavaidehakānāmavakāśabhikṣāpaṇyadāne dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
03405| pratiṣiddhānāṃ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
03406| paragṛhātigatāyāścaturviṃśatipaṇaḥ ||
03407| parabhāryāvakāśadāne śatyo daṇḍaḥ, anyatrāpadbhyaḥ ||
03408| vāraṇājñānayornirdoṣaḥ ||
03409| "pativiprakārātpatijñātisukhāvasthagrāmikānvādhibhikṣukījñātikulānāmanyatamamapuruṣaṃ gantumadoṣaḥ" iti ācāryāḥ ||
03410| sa-puruṣaṃ vā jñātikulam ||
03411| kuto hi sādhvījanasyacchalam ||
03412| sukhametadavaboddhum, iti kauṭilyaḥ ||
03413| pretavyādhivyasanagarbhanimittamapratiṣiddhameva jñātikulagamanam ||
03414| tannimittaṃ vārayato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
03415| tatrāpi gūhamānā strīdhanaṃ jīyeta, jñātayo vā chādayantaḥ śulkaśeṣam || iti niṣpatanam |
03416| patikulānniṣpatya grāmāntaragamane dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ sthāpyābharaṇalopaśca ||
03417| gamyena vā puṃsā saha prasthāne caturviṃśatipaṇaḥ sarvadharmalopaśca, anyatra bharmadānatīrthagamanābhyām ||
03418| puṃsaḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ tulyaśreyasoḥ, pāpīyaso madhyamaḥ ||
03419| bandhuradaṇḍyaḥ ||
03420| pratiṣedherdhadaṇḍāḥ ||
03421| pathi vyantare gūḍhadeśābhigamane maithunārthena śaṅkitapratiṣiddhāyāṃ vā pathyanusaraṇe saṃgrahaṇaṃ vidyāt ||
03422| tālāvacaracāraṇamatsyabandhakalubdhakagopālakaśauṇḍikānāmanyeṣāṃ ca prasṛṣṭastrīkāṇāṃ pathyanusaraṇamadoṣaḥ ||
03423| pratiṣiddhe vā nayataḥ puṃsaḥ striyo vā gacchantyāsta evārdhadaṇḍāḥ || iti pathyanusaraṇam |
03424| hrasvapravāsināṃ śūdravaiśyakṣatriyabrāhmaṇānāṃ bhāryāḥ saṃvatsarottaraṃ kālamākāṅkṣeranaprajātāḥ, saṃvatsarādhikaṃ prajātāḥ ||
03425| prativihitā dviguṇaṃ kālam ||
03426| aprativihitāḥ sukhāvasthā bibhṛyuḥ, paraṃ catvāri varṣāṇyaṣṭau vā jñātayaḥ ||
03427| tato yathādattamādāya pramuñceyuḥ ||
03428| brāhmaṇamadhīyānaṃ daśavarṣāṇyaprajātā, dvādaśa prajātā, rājapuruṣamāyuḥkṣayādākāṅkṣeta ||
03429| savarṇataśca prajātā nāpavādaṃ labheta ||
03430| kuṭumbarddhilope vā sukhāvasthairvimuktā yatheṣṭaṃ vindeta, jīvitārthamāpadgatā vā ||
03431| dharmavivāhātkumārī parigrahītāramanākhyāya proṣitamaśrūyamāṇaṃ sapta tīrthānyākāṅkṣeta, saṃvatsaraṃ śrūyamāṇam ||
03432| ākhyāya proṣitamaśrūyamāṇaṃ pañca tīrthānyākāṅkṣeta, daśa śrūyamāṇam ||
03433| ekadeśadattaśulkaṃ trīṇi tīrthānyaśrūyamāṇam, śrūyamāṇaṃ sapta tīrthānyākāṅkṣeta ||
03434| dattaśulkaṃ pañca tīrthānyaśrūyamāṇam, daśa śrūyamāṇam ||
03435| tataḥ paraṃ dharmasthairvisṛṣṭā yatheṣṭaṃ vindeta ||
03436| tīrthoparodho hi dharmavadha iti kauṭilyaḥ || iti hrasvapravāsaḥ ||
03437| dīrghapravāsinaḥ pravrajitasya pretasya vā bhāryā sapta tīrthānyākāṅkṣeta, saṃvatsaraṃ prajātā ||
03438| tataḥ patisodaryaṃ gacchet ||
03439| bahuṣu pratyāsannaṃ dhārmikaṃ bharmasamarthaṃ kaniṣṭhamabhāryaṃ vā ||
03440| tadabhāvepyasodaryaṃ sapiṇḍaṃ kulyaṃ vāsannam ||
03441| eteṣāmeṣa eva kramaḥ ||
03442  etānutkramya dāyādān vedane jārakarmaṇi |

(Orderof inheritance)
03501| anīśvarāḥ pitṛmantaḥ sthitapitṛmātṛkāḥ putrāḥ ||
03502| teṣāmūrdhvaṃ pitṛto dāyavibhāgaḥ pitṛdravyāṇām ||
03503| svayaṃārjitamavibhājyam, anyatra pitṛdravyādutthitebhyaḥ ||
03504| pitṛdravyādavibhaktopagatānāṃ putrāḥ pautrā vā ācaturthādityaṃśabhājaḥ ||
03505| tāvadavicchinnaḥ piṇḍo bhavati ||
03506| vicchinnapiṇḍāḥ sarve samaṃ vibhajeran ||
03507| apitṛdravyā vibhaktapitṛdravyā vā saha jīvantaḥ punarvibhajeran ||
03508| yataścottiṣṭheta sa dvyaṃśaṃ labheta ||
03509| dravyamaputrasya sodaryā bhrātaraḥ sahajīvino vā hareyuḥ kanyāśca ||
03510| rikthaṃ putravataḥ putrā duhitaro vā dharmiṣṭheṣu vivāheṣu jātāḥ ||
03511| tadabhāve pitā dharamāṇaḥ ||
03512| pitrabhāve bhrātaro bhrātṛputrāśca ||
03513| apitṛkā bahavoapi ca bhrātaro bhrātṛputrāśca piturekamaṃśaṃ hareyuḥ ||
03514| sodaryāṇāmanekapitṛkāṇāṃ pitṛto dāyavibhāgaḥ ||03515| pitṛbhrātṛputrāṇāṃ pūrve vidyamāne nāparamavalambante, jyeṣṭhe ca kaniṣṭhamarthagrāhiṇam ||
03516| jīvadvibhāge pitā naikaṃ viśeṣayet ||
03517| na caikamakāraṇānnirvibhajeta ||
03518| piturasatyarthe jyeṣṭhāḥ kaniṣṭhānanugṛhṇīyuḥ, anyatra mithyāvṛttebhyaḥ ||
03519| prāptavyavahārāṇāṃ vibhāgaḥ ||
03520| aprāptavyavahārāṇāṃ deyaviśuddhaṃ mātṛbandhuṣu grāmavṛddheṣu vā sthāpayeyuḥ āvyavahāraprāpaṇāt, proṣitasya vā ||
03521| sanniviṣṭasamamasanniviṣṭebhyo naiveśanikaṃ dadyuḥ, kanyābhyaśca prādānikam ||
03522| ṛṇarikthayoḥ samo vibhāgaḥ ||

03525| satoarthasya vibhāgo nāsataḥ ||
03526| etāvānarthaḥ sāmānyastasyaitāvān pratyaṃśa ityanubhāṣya bruvan sākṣiṣu vibhāgaṃ kārayet ||
03527| durvibhaktamanyonyāpahṛtamantarhitamavijñātotpannaṃ vā punarvibhajeran ||
03528| adāyādakaṃ rājā haretstrīvṛttipretakāryavarjam, anyatra śrotriyadravyāt ||
03529| tattraivedyebhyaḥ prayacchet ||

(ḍivisioninto shares)
03601| ekastrīputrāṇāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃśaḥ - brāhmaṇānāmajāḥ, kṣatriyāṇāmaśvāḥ, vaiśyānāṃ gāvaḥ, śūdrāṇāmavayaḥ ||
03602| kāṇalaṅgāsteṣāṃ madhyamāṃśaḥ, bhinnavarṇāḥ kaniṣṭhāṃśaḥ ||
03603| catuṣpadābhāve ratnavarjānāṃ daśānāṃ bhāgaṃ dravyāṇāmekaṃ jyeṣṭho haret ||
03604| pratimuktasvadhāpāśo hi bhavati ||
03605| ityauśanaso vibhāgaḥ ||
03606| pituḥ parivāpādyānamābharaṇaṃ ca jyeṣṭhāṃśaḥ, śayanāsanaṃ bhuktakāṃsyaṃ ca madhyamāṃśaḥ, kṛṣṇaṃ dhānyāyasaṃ gṛhaparivāpo gośakaṭaṃ ca kaniṣṭhāṃśaḥ ||
03607| śeṣadravyāṇāmekadravyasya vā samo vibhāgaḥ ||
03608| adāyādā bhaginyaḥ, mātuḥ parivāpādbhuktakāṃsyābharaṇabhāginyaḥ ||
03609| mānuṣahīno jyeṣṭhastṛtīyamaṃśaṃ jyeṣṭhāṃśāllabheta, caturthamanyāyavṛttiḥ, nivṛttadharmakāryo vā ||
03610| kāmācāraḥ sarvaṃ jīyeta ||
03611| tena madhyamakaniṣṭhau vyākhyātau ||
03612| tayormānuṣopeto jyeṣṭhāṃśādardhaṃ labheta ||

03614| sūtamāgadhavrātyarathakārāṇāmaiśvaryato vibhāgaḥ ||
03615| śeṣāstamupajīveyuḥ ||
03616| anīśvarāḥ samavibhāgāḥ ||

03618| tena trivarṇadvivarṇaputravibhāgaḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayorvyākhyātaḥ ||
03619| brāhmaṇasyānantarāputrastulyāṃśaḥ ||

03623| tadabhāve piturācāryoantevāsī vā ||
03624  kṣetre vā janayedasya niyuktaḥ kṣetrajaṃ sutam |

(Classificationof sons)
03701| "paraparigrahe bījamutsṛṣṭaṃ kṣetriṇaḥ" ityācāryāḥ ||

03740  ucitastasya tenaiva dāyadharmaṃ prakalpayet ||E

03901| jñātisāmantadhanikāḥ krameṇa bhūmiparigrahān kretumabhyābhaveyuḥ ||
03902| tatoanye bāhyāḥ ||
03903| sāmantacatvāriṃśatkulyeṣu gṛhapratimukhe veśma śrāvayeyuḥ, sāmantagrāmavṛddheṣu kṣetramārāmaṃ setubandhaṃ taṭākamādhāraṃ vā maryādāsu yathāsetubhogaṃ "anenārgheṇa kaḥ kretā" iti ||
03904| trirāghuṣitamavyāhataṃ kretā kretuṃ labheta ||
03905| spardhayā vā mūlyavardhane mūlyavṛddhiḥ sa-śulkā kośaṃ gacchet ||
03906| vikrayapratikroṣṭā śulkaṃ dadyāt ||
03907| asvāmipratikrośe caturviṃśatipaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
03908| saptarātrādūrdhvamanabhisarataḥ pratikruṣṭo vikrīṇīta ||
03909| pratikruṣṭātikrame vāstuni dviśato daṇḍaḥ, anyatra caturviṃśatipaṇo daṇḍaḥ || iti vāstuvikrayaḥ |
03910| sīmavivādaṃ grāmayorubhayoḥ sāmantā pañcagrāmī daśagrāmī vā setubhiḥ sthāvaraiḥ kṛtrimairvā kuryāt ||
03911| karṣakagopālakavṛddhāḥ pūrvabhuktikā vā bāhyāḥ setūnāmabhijñā bahava eko vā nirdiśya sīmasetūn viparītaveṣāḥ sīmānaṃ nayeyuḥ ||
03912| uddiṣṭānāṃ setūnāmadarśane sahasraṃ daṇḍaḥ |
03913| tadeva nīte sīmāpahāriṇāṃ setucchidāṃ ca kuryāt ||
03914| pranaṣṭasetubhogaṃ vā sīmānaṃ rājā yathopakāraṃ vibhajet || iti sīmavivādaḥ |
03915| kṣetravivādaṃ sāmantagrāmavṛddhāḥ kuryuḥ ||
03916| teṣāṃ dvaidhībhāve yato bahavaḥ śucayoanumatā vā tato niyaccheyuḥ madhyaṃ vā gṛhṇīyuḥ ||
03917| tadubhayaparoktaṃ vāstu rājā haret, pranaṣṭasvāmikaṃ ca ||
03918| yathopakāraṃ vā vibhajet ||
03919| prasahyādāne vāstuni steyadaṇḍaḥ ||
03920| kāraṇādāne prayāsamājīvaṃ ca parisaṃkhyāya bandhaṃ dadyāt || iti kṣetravivādaḥ |
03921| maryādāpaharaṇe pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
03922| maryādābhede caturviṃśatipaṇaḥ ||
03923| tena tapovanavivītamahāpathaśmaśānadevakulayajanapuṇyasthānavivādā vyākhyātāḥ || iti maryādāsthāpanam |
03924| sarva eva vivādāḥ sāmantapratyayāḥ ||
03925| vivītasthalakedāraṣaṇḍakhalaveśmavāhanakoṣṭhānāṃ pūrvaṃpūrvamābādhaṃ saheta ||
03926| brahmasomāraṇyadevayajanapuṇyasthānavarjāḥ sthalapradeśāḥ ||
03927| ādhāraparivāhakedāropabhogaiḥ parakṣetrakṛṣṭabījahiṃsāyāṃ yathopaghātaṃ mūlyaṃ dadyuḥ ||
03928| kedārārāmasetubandhānāṃ parasparahiṃsāyāṃ hiṃsādviguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
03929| paścānniviṣṭamadharataṭākaṃ noparitaṭākasya kedāramudakenāplāvayet ||
03930| upariniviṣṭaṃ nādharataṭākasya pūrāsrāvaṃ vārayed, anyatra trivarṣoparatakarmaṇaḥ ||
03931| tasyātikrame pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, taṭākavāmanaṃ ca ||
03932| pañcavarṣoparatakarmaṇaḥ setubandhasya svāmyaṃ lupyeta, anyatrāpadbhyaḥ ||

03934| svātmādhāne vikraye ca ||

03936| prakrayāvakrayādhibhāgabhoganiṣṛṣṭopabhoktāraścaiṣāṃ pratikuryuḥ ||

031001| karmodakamārgamucitaṃ rundhataḥ kurvatoanucitaṃ vā pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, setukūpapuṇyasthānacaityadevāyatanāni ca parabhūmau niveśayataḥ ||
031002| pūrvānuvṛttaṃ dharmasetumādhānaṃ vikrayaṃ vā nayato nāyayato vā madhyamaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, śrotṛṛṇāmuttamaḥ, anyatra bhagnotsṛṣṭāt ||
031003| svāmyabhāve grāmāḥ puṇyaśīlā vā pratikuryuḥ ||
031004| pathipramāṇaṃ durganiveśe vyākhyātam ||
031005| kṣudrapaśumanuṣyapathaṃ rundhato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, mahāpaśupathaṃ caturviṃśatipaṇaḥ, hastikṣetrapathaṃ catuṣpañcāśatpaṇaḥ, setuvanapathaṃ ṣaṭśataḥ, śmaśānagrāmapathaṃ dviśataḥ, droṇamukhapathaṃ pañcaśataḥ, sthānīyarāṣṭravivītapathaṃ sāhasraḥ ||

031009| karadāḥ karadeṣvādhānaṃ vikrayaṃ vā kuryuḥ, brahmadeyikā brahmadeyikeṣu ||
031010| anyathā pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
031011| karadasya vākaradagrāmaṃ praviśataḥ ||

031028| tadeva ṣaṇḍabhakṣaṇe kuryāt ||

031101| sapādapaṇā dharmyā māsavṛddhiḥ paṇaśatasya, pañcapaṇā vyāvahārikī, daśapaṇā kāntāragāṇām, viṃśatipaṇā sāmudrāṇām ||
031102| tataḥ paraṃ kartuḥ kārayituśca pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, śrotṛṛṇāmekaikaṃ pratyardhadaṇḍaḥ ||
031103| rājanyayogakṣemāvahe tu dhanikadhāraṇikayoścaritramavekṣeta ||
031104| dhānyavṛddhiḥ sasyaniṣpattāvupārdhā, paraṃ mūlyakṛtā vardheta ||
031105| prakṣepavṛddhirudayādardhaṃ sannidhānasannā vārṣikī deyā ||
031106| cirapravāsaḥ stambhapraviṣṭo vā mūlyadviguṇaṃ dadyāt ||
031107| akṛtvā vṛddhiṃ sādhayato vardhayato vā, mūlyaṃ vā vṛddhimāropya śrāvayato bandhacaturguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
031108| tucchaśrāvaṇāyāmabhūtacaturguṇaḥ ||
031109| tasya tribhāgamādātā dadyāt, śeṣaṃ pradātā ||
031110| dīrghasattravyādhigurukuloparuddhaṃ bālamasāraṃ vā narṇamanuvardheta ||
031111| mucyamānamṛṇamapratigṛhṇato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
031112| kāraṇāpadeśena nivṛttavṛddhikamanyatra tiṣṭhet ||
031113| daśavarṣopekṣitamṛṇamapratigrāhyam, anyatra bālavṛddhavyādhitavyasaniproṣitadeśatyāgarājyavibhramebhyaḥ ||
031114| pretasya putrāḥ kusīdaṃ dadyuḥ, dāyādā vā rikthaharāḥ, sahagrāhiṇaḥ, pratibhuvo vā ||
031115| na prātibhāvyamanyat ||
031116| asāraṃ bālaprātibhāvyam ||
031117| asaṃkhyātadeśakālaṃ tu putrāḥ pautrā dāyādā vā rikthaṃ haramāṇā dadyuḥ ||
031118| jīvitavivāhabhūmiprātibhāvyamasaṃkhyātadeśakālaṃ tu putrāḥ pautrā vā vaheyuḥ ||
031119| nānarṇasamavāye tu naikaṃ dvau yugapadabhivadeyātām, anyatra pratiṣṭhamānāt ||
031120| tatrāpi gṛhītānupūrvyā rājaśrotriyadravyaṃ vā pūrvaṃ pratipādayet ||
031121| dampatyoḥ pitāputrayoḥ bhrātṛṛṇāṃ cāvibhaktānāṃ parasparakṛtamṛṇamasādhyam ||
031122| agrāhyāḥ karmakāleṣu karṣakā rājapuruṣāśca ||
031123| strī cāpratiśrāviṇī patikṛtamṛṇam, anyatra gopālakārdhasītikebhyaḥ ||
031124| patistu grāhyaḥ strīkṛtamṛṇam, apratividhāya proṣita iti ||
031125| sampratipattāvuttamaḥ ||

031127| pakṣānumatau vā dvau, ṛṇaṃ prati na tvevaikaḥ ||
031128| pratiṣiddhāḥ syālasahāyānvarthidhanikadhāraṇikavairinyaṅgadhṛtadaṇḍāḥ, pūrve cāvyavahāryāḥ ||
031129| rājaśrotriyagrāmabhṛtakakuṣṭhivraṇinaḥ patitacaṇḍālakutsitakarmāṇoandhabadhiramūkāhaṃvādinaḥ strīrājapuruṣāśca, anyatra svavargebhyaḥ ||
031130| pāruṣyasteyasaṃgrahaṇeṣu tu vairisyālasahāyavarjāḥ ||
031131| rahasyavyavahāreṣvekā strī puruṣa upaśrotā upadraṣṭā vā sākṣī syādrājatāpasavarjam ||
031132| svāmino bhṛtyānāmṛtvigācāryāḥ śiṣyāṇāṃ mātāpitarau putrāṇāṃ cānigraheṇa sākṣyaṃ kuryuḥ, tesāmitare vā ||
031133| parasparābhiyoge caiṣāmuttamāḥ paroktā daśabandhaṃ dadyuḥ, avarāḥ pañcabandham || iti sākṣyadhikāraḥ ||
031134| brāhmaṇodakumbhāgnisakāśe sākṣiṇaḥ parigṛhṇīyāt ||
031135| tatra brāhmaṇaṃ brūyāt"satyaṃ brūhi" iti ||
031136| rājanyaṃ vaiśyaṃ vā "mā taveṣṭāpūrtaphalam, kapālahastaḥ śatrukulaṃ bhikṣārthī gaccheḥ" iti ||
031137| śūdraṃ "janmamaraṇāntare yadvaḥ puṇyaphalaṃ tadrājānaṃ gacched, rājñaśca kilbiṣaṃ yuṣmānanyathāvāde, daṇḍaścānubaddhaḥ, paścādapi jñāyeta yathādṛṣṭaśrutam, ekamantrāḥ satyamupaharata" iti ||
031138| anupaharatāṃ saptarātrādūrdhvaṃ dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, tripakṣādūrdhvamabhiyogaṃ dadyuḥ ||
031139| sākṣibhede yato bahavaḥ śucayoanumatā vā tato niyaccheyuḥ, madhyaṃ vā gṛhṇīyuḥ ||
031140| tadvā dravyaṃ rājā haret ||
031141| sākṣiṇaścedabhiyogādūnaṃ brūyuratiriktasyābhiyoktā bandhaṃ dadyāt ||
031142| atiriktaṃ vā brūyustadatiriktaṃ rājā haret ||
031143| bāliśyādabhiyokturvā duhśrutaṃ durlikhitaṃ pretābhiniveśaṃ vā samīkṣya sākṣipratyayameva syāt ||
031144| "sākṣibāliṣyeṣveva pṛthaganuyoge deśakālakāryāṇāṃ pūrvamadhyamottamā daṇḍāḥ" ityauśanasāḥ ||
031145| "kūṭasākṣiṇo yamarthamabhūtaṃ kuryurbhūtaṃ vā nāśayeyustaddaśaguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyuḥ" iti mānavāḥ ||
031146| "bāliśyādvā visaṃvādayatāṃ citro ghātaḥ" iti bārhaspatyāḥ ||
031147| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
031148| dhruvaṃ hi sākṣibhiḥ śrotavyam ||
031149| aśṛṇvatāṃ caturviṃśatipaṇo daṇḍaḥ, tatoardhamabruvāṇānām ||
031150  deśakālāvidūrasthān sākṣiṇaḥ pratipādayet |

(ḍeposits)
031201| upanidhirṛṇena vyākhyātaḥ ||
031202| paracakrāṭavikābhyāṃ durgarāṣṭravilope vā, pratirodhakairvā grāmasārthavrajavilope, cakrayuktanāśe vā, grāmamadhyāgnyudakābādhe jvālāvegoparuddhe vā, nāvi nimagnāyāṃ muṣitāyāṃ vā svayamuparūḍho nopanidhimabhyāvahet ||
031203| upanidhibhoktā deśakālānurūpaṃ bhogavetanaṃ dadyāt, dvādaśapaṇaṃ ca daṇḍam ||
031204| upabhoganimittaṃ naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ vābhyāvahet, caturviṃśatipaṇaśca daṇḍaḥ, anyathā vā niṣpatane ||
031205| pretaṃ vyasanagataṃ vā nopanidhimabhyāvahet ||
031206| ādhānavikrayāpavyayaneṣu cāsya caturguṇapañcabandho daṇḍaḥ ||
031207| parivartane niṣpātane vā mūlyasamaḥ ||
031208| tenādhipraṇāśopabhogavikrayādhānāpahārā vyākhyātāḥ ||
031209| nādhiḥ sopakāraḥ sīdet, na cāsya mūlyaṃ vardheta, anyatra nisargāt ||
031210| nirupakāraḥ sīdet, mūlyaṃ cāsya vardheta ||
031211| upasthitasyādhimaprayacchato dvādaśaṇpaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
031212| prayojakāsannidhāne vā grāmavṛddheṣu sthāpayitvā niṣkrayamādhiṃ pratipadyeta ||
031213| nivṛttavṛddhiko vādhistatkālakṛtamūlyastatraivāvatiṣṭheta, anāśavināśakaraṇādhiṣṭhito vā ||
031214| dhāraṇikāsannidhāne vā vināśabhayādudgatārghaṃ dharmasthānujñāto vikrīṇīta, ādhipālapratyayo vā ||
031215| sthāvarastu prayāsabhogyaḥ phalabhogyo vā prakṣepavṛddhimūlyaśuddhamājīvamamūlyakṣayeṇopanayet ||
031216| anisṛṣṭopabhoktā mūlyaśuddhamājīvaṃ bandhaṃ ca dadyāt ||
031217| śeṣamupanidhinā vyākhyātam ||
031218| etenādeśoanvādhiśca vyākhyātau ||
031219| sārthenānvādhihasto vā pradiṣṭāṃ bhūmimaprāptaścorairbhagnotsṛṣṭo vā nānvādhimabhyāvahet ||
031220| antare vā mṛtasya dāyādoapi nābhyāvahet ||
031221| śeṣamupanidhinā vyakahyātam ||
031222| yācitakamavakrītakaṃ vā yathāvidhaṃ gṛhṇīyustathāvidhamevārpayeyuḥ ||
031223| bhreṣopanipātābhyāṃ deśakāloparodhi dattaṃ naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ vā nābhyāvaheyuḥ ||
031224| śeṣamupanidhinā vyākhyātam ||
031225| vaiyāvṛtyavikrayastu - vaiyāvṛtyakarā yathādeśakālaṃ vikrīṇānāḥ paṇyaṃ yathājātaṃ mūlyamudayaṃ ca dadyuḥ ||
031226| deśakālātipātane vā parihīṇaṃ sampradānakālikenārgheṇa mūlyamudayaṃ ca dadyuḥ ||
031227| yathāsambhāṣitaṃ vā vikrīṇānā nodayamadhigaccheyuḥ, mūlyameva dadyuḥ ||
031228| arghapatane vā parihīṇaṃ yathāparihīṇaṃ mūlyamūnaṃ dadyuḥ ||
031229| sāṃvyavahārikeṣu vā prātyayikeṣvarājavācyeṣu bhreṣopanipātābhyāṃ naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ vā mūlyamapi na dadyuḥ ||
031230| deśakālāntaritānāṃ tu paṇyānāṃ kṣayavyayaviśuddhaṃ mūlyamudayaṃ ca dadyuḥ, paṇyasamavāyānāṃ ca pratyaṃśam ||
031231| śeṣamupanidhinā vyākhyātam ||
031232| etena vaiyāvṛtyavikrayo vyākhyātaḥ ||
031233| nikṣepaścopanidhinā ||
031234| tamanyena nikṣpitamanyasyārpayato hīyeta ||
031235| nikṣepāpahāre pūrvāpadānaṃ nikṣeptāraśca pramāṇam ||
031236| aśucayo hi kāravaḥ ||
031237| naiṣāṃ karaṇapūrvo nikṣepadharmaḥ ||
031238| karaṇahīnaṃ nikṣepamapavyayamānaṃ gūḍhabhittinyastān sākṣiṇo nikṣeptā rahasi praṇipātena prajñāpayet, vanānte vā madyaprahavaṇaviśvāsena ||
031239| rahasi vṛddho vyādhito vā vaidehakaḥ kaścitkṛtalakṣaṇaṃ dravyamasya haste nikṣipyāpagacchet ||
031240| tasya pratideśena putro bhrātā vābhigamya nikṣepaṃ yāceta ||
031241| dāne śuciḥ, anyathā nikṣepaṃ steyadaṇḍaṃ ca dadyāt ||
031242| pravrajyābhimukho vā śraddheyaḥ kaścitkṛtalakṣaṇaṃ dravyamasya haste nikṣipya pratiṣṭheta ||
031243| tataḥ kālāntarāgato yāceta ||
031244| dāne śuciḥ, anyathā nikṣepaṃ steyadaṇḍaṃ ca dadyāt ||
031245| kṛtalakṣaṇena vā dravyeṇa pratyānayedenam ||
031246| bāliśajātīyo vā rātrau rājadāyikākṣaṇabhītaḥ sāramasya haste nikṣipyāpagacchet ||
031247| sa enaṃ bandhanāgāragato yāceta ||
031248| dāne śuciḥ, anyathā nikṣepaṃ steyadaṇḍaṃ ca dadyāt ||
031249| abhijñānena cāsya gṛhe janamubhayaṃ yāceta ||
031250| anyatartādāne yathoktaṃ purastāt ||
031251| dravyabhogānāmāgamaṃ cāsyānuyuñjīta, tasya cārthasya vyavahāropaliṅganam, abhiyoktuścārthasāmarthyam ||
031252| etena mithaḥsamavāyo vyākhyātaḥ ||
031253  tasmātsākṣimadacchannaṃ kuryātsamyagvibhāṣitam |
031253  sve pare vā jane kāryaṃ deśakālāgravarṇataḥ ||E

(ḻaw concerningslavesandlabourers)
031301| udaradāsavarjamāryaprāṇamaprāptavyavahāraṃ śūdraṃ vikrayādhānaṃ nayataḥ svajanasya dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, vaiśyaṃ dviguṇaḥ, kṣatriyaṃ triguṇaḥ, brāhmaṇaṃ caturguṇaḥ ||
031302| parajanasya pūrvamadhyamottamavadhā daṇḍāḥ, kretṛśrotṛṛṇāṃ ca ||
031303| mlecchānāmadoṣaḥ prajāṃ vikretumādhātuṃ vā ||
031304| na tvevāryasya dāsabhāvaḥ ||
031305| athavāryamādhāya kulabandhana āryāṇāmāpadi, niṣkrayaṃ cādhigamya bālaṃ sāhāyyadātāraṃ vā pūrvaṃ niṣkrīṇīran ||
031306| sakṛdātmādhātā niṣpatitaḥ sīdet, dviranyenāhitakaḥ, sakṛdubhau paraviṣayābhimukhau ||
031307| vittāpahāriṇo vā dāsasyāryabhāvamapaharatoardhadaṇḍaḥ ||
031308| niṣpatitapretavyasanināmādhātā mūlyaṃ bhajeta ||
031309| pretaviṇmūtrocchiṣṭagrāhaṇamāhitasya nagnasnāpanaṃ daṇḍapreṣaṇamatikramaṇaṃ ca strīṇāṃ mūlyanāśakaraṃ, dhātrīparicārikārdhasītikopacārikāṇāṃ ca mokṣakaram ||
031310| siddhamupacārakasyābhiprajātasyāpakramaṇam ||
031311| dhātrīmāhitikāṃ vākāmāṃ svavaśāṃ gacchataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, paravaśāṃ madhyamaḥ ||
031312| kanyāmāhitikāṃ vā svayamanyena vā duṣayato mūlyanāśaḥ śulkaṃ taddvuguṇaśca daṇḍaḥ ||
031313| ātmavikrayiṇaḥ prajāmāryāṃ vidyāt ||
031314| ātmādhigataṃ svāmikarmāviruddhaṃ labheta, pitryaṃ ca dāyam ||
031315| mūlyena cāryatvaṃ gacchet ||
031316| tenodaradāsāhitakau vyākhyātau ||
031317| prakṣepānurūpaścāsya niṣkrayaḥ ||
031318| daṇḍapraṇītaḥ karmaṇā daṇḍamupanayet ||
031319| āryaprāṇo dhvajāhṛtaḥ karmakālānurūpeṇa mūlyārdhena vā vimucyeta ||
031320| gṛhejātadāyāgatalabdhakrītānāmanyatamaṃ dāsamūnāṣṭavarṣaṃ vibandhumakāmaṃ nīce karmaṇi videśe dāsīṃ vā sagarbhāmaprativihitagarbhabharmaṇyāṃ vikrayādhānaṃ nayataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, kretṛśrotṛṛṇāṃ ca ||
031321| dāsamanurūpeṇa niṣkrayeṇāryamakurvato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, saṃrodhaścākaraṇāt ||
031322| dāsadravyasya jñātayo dāyādāḥ, teṣāmabhāve svāmī ||
031323| svāminaḥ svasyāṃ dāsyāṃ jātaṃ samātṛkamadāsaṃ vidyāt ||
031324| gṛhyā cetkuṭumbārthacintanī mātā bhrātā bhaginī cāsyā adāsāḥ syuḥ ||
031325| dāsaṃ dāsīṃ vā niṣkrīya punarvikrayādhānaṃ nayato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra svayaṃvādibhyaḥ || iti dāsakalpaḥ |
031326| karmakarasya karmasambandhamāsannā vidyuḥ ||
031327| yathāsambhāṣitaṃ vetanaṃ labheta, karmakālānurūpamasambhāṣitavetanaḥ ||
031328| karṣakaḥ sasyānāṃ gopālakaḥ sarpiṣāṃ vaidehakaḥ paṇyānāmātmanā vyavahṛtānāṃ daśabhāgamasambhāṣitavetano labheta ||
031329| sambhāṣitavetanastu yathāsambhāṣitam ||
031330| kāruśilpikuśīlavacikitsakavāgjīvanaparicārakādirāśākārikavargastu yathānyastadvidhaḥ kuryādyathā vā kuśalāḥ kalpayeyustathā vetanaṃ labheta ||
031331| sākṣipratyayameva syāt ||
031332| sākṣiṇāmabhāve yataḥ karma tatoanuyuñjīta ||
031333| vetanādāne daśabandho daṇḍaḥ, ṣaṭpaṇo vā ||
031334| apavyayamāne dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, pañcabandho vā ||
031335| nadīvegajvālāstenavyāloparuddhaḥ sarvasvaputradārātmadānenārtastrātāramāhūya niṣtīrṇaḥ kuśalapradiṣṭaṃ vetanaṃ dadyāt ||
031336| tena sarvatrārtadānānuśayā vyākhyātāḥ ||
031337  labheta puṃścalī bhogaṃ saṃgamasyopaliṅganāt |
031337  atiyācnā tu jīyeta daurmatyāvinayena vā ||E

(ḍutiesof servants)
(ūndertakingin partnership)
031401| gṛhītvā vetanaṃ karmākurvato bhṛtakasya dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, saṃrodhaścākaraṇāt ||
031402| aśaktaḥ kutsite karmaṇi vyādhau vyasane vānuśayaṃ labheta, pareṇa vā kārayitum ||
031403| tasyavyayakarmaṇā labheta bhartā vā kārayitum ||
031404| "nānyastvayā kārayitavyo, mayā vā nānyasya kartavyam" ityavarodhe bharturakārayato bhṛtakasyākurvato vā dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
031405| karmaniṣṭhāpane bharturanyatra gṛhītavetano nāsakāmaḥ kuryāt ||
031406| "upasthitamakārayataḥ kṛtameva vidyād" ityācāryāḥ ||
031407| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
031408| kṛtasya vetanaṃ nākṛtasyāsti ||
031409| sa cedalpamapi kārayitvā na kārayetkṛtamevāsya vidyāt ||
031410| deśakālātipātanena karmaṇāmanyathākaraṇe vā nāsakāmaḥ kṛtamanumanyeta ||
031411| sambhāṣitādadhikakriyāyāṃ prayāsaṃ na moghaṃ kuryāt ||
031412| tena saṃghabhṛtā vyākhyātāḥ ||
031413| teṣāmādhiḥ saptarātramāsīta ||
031414| tatoanyamupasthāpayet, karmaniṣpākaṃ ca ||
031415| na cānivedya bhartuḥ saṃghaḥ kaṃcitpariharedupanayedvā ||
031416| tasyātikrame caturviṃśatipaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
031417| saṃghena parihṛtasyārdhadaṇḍaḥ || iti bhṛtakādhikāraḥ ||
031418| saṃghabhṛtāḥ sambhūyasamutthātāro vā yathāsambhāṣitaṃ vetanaṃ samaṃ vā vibhajeran ||
031419| karṣaṇavaidehakā vā sasyapaṇyārambhaparyavasānāntare sannasya yathākṛtasya karmaṇaḥ pratyaṃśaṃ dadyuḥ ||
031420| puruṣopasthāne samagramaṃśaṃ dadyuḥ ||
031421| saṃsiddhe tūddhṛtapaṇye sannasya tadānīmeva pratyaṃśaṃ dadyuḥ ||
031422| sāmānyā hi pathisiddhiścāsiddhiśca ||
031423| prakrānte tu karmaṇi svasthasyāpakrāmato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
031424| na ca prākāmyamapakramaṇe ||
031425| coraṃ tvabhayapūrvaṃ karmaṇaḥ pratyaṃśena grāhayed, dadyātpratyaṃśamabhayaṃ ca ||
031426| punaḥsteye pravāsanam, anyatragamane ca ||
031427| mahāparādhe tu dūṣyavadācaret ||
031428| yājakāḥ svāpracāradravyavarjaṃ yathāsambhāṣitaṃ vetanaṃ samaṃ vā vibhajeran ||
031429| agniṣṭomādiṣu ca kratuṣu dīkṣaṇādūrdhvaṃ tṛtīyamaṃśaṃ, madhyamopasada ūrdhvamardhamaṃśaṃ, sutye prātaḥsavanādūrdhvaṃ pādonamaṃśam ||
031430| mādhyandinātsavanādūrdhvaṃ samagramaṃśaṃ labheta ||
031431| nītā hi dakṣiṇā bhavanti ||
031432| bṛhaspatisavavarjaṃ pratisavanaṃ hi dakṣiṇā dīyante ||
031433| tenāhargaṇadakṣiṇā vyākhyātāḥ ||
031434| sanānāmādaśāhorātrātśeṣabhṛtāḥ karma kuryuḥ, anye vā svapratyayāḥ ||
031435| karmaṇyasamāpte tu yajamānaḥ sīded, ṛtvijaḥ karma samāpayya dakṣiṇāṃ hareyuḥ ||
031436| asamāpte tu karmaṇi yājyaṃ yājakaṃ vā tyajataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
031437  anāhitāgniḥ śataguruyajvā ca sahasraguḥ |
031437  surāpo vṛṣalībhartā brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ ||
031438  asatpratigrahe yuktaḥ stenaḥ kutsitayājakaḥ |
031438  adoṣastyaktumanyonyaṃ karmasaṃkaraniścayāt ||E

(ṛescissionof sale andpurchase)
031501| vikrīya paṇyamaprayacchato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra doṣopanipātāviṣahyebhyaḥ ||
031502| paṇyadoṣo doṣaḥ ||
031503| rājacorāgnyudakabādha upanipātaḥ ||
031504| bahuguṇahīnamārtakṛtaṃ vāviṣahyam ||
031505| vaidehakānāmekarātramanuśayaḥ, karṣakāṇāṃ trirātraṃ, gorakṣakāṇāṃ pañcarātram ||
031506| vyāmiśrāṇāmuttamānāṃ ca varṇānāṃ vṛttivikraye saptarātram ||
031507| ātipātikānāṃ paṇyānāṃ "anyatrāvikreyam" ityavarodhenānuśayo deyaḥ ||
031508| tasyātikrame caturviṃśatipaṇo daṇḍaḥ, paṇyadaśabhāgo vā ||031509| krītvā paṇyamapratigṛhṇato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra doṣopanipātāviṣahyebhyaḥ ||
031510| samānaścānuśayo vikreturanuśayena ||
031511| vivāhānāṃ tu trayāṇāṃ pūrveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ pāṇigrahaṇātsiddhamupāvartanaṃ, śūdrāṇāṃ ca prakarmaṇaḥ ||
031512| vṛttapāṇigrahaṇayorapi doṣamaupaśāyikaṃ dṛṣṭvā siddhamupāvartanam ||
031513| na tvevābhiprajātayoḥ ||
031514| kanyādoṣamaupaśāyikamanākhyāya prayacchataḥ kanyāṃ ṣaṇṇavatirdaṇḍaḥ, śulkastrīdhanapratidānaṃ ca ||
031515| varayiturvā varadoṣamanākhyāya vindato dviguṇaḥ, śulkastrīdhananāśaśca ||
031516| dvipadacatuṣpadānāṃ tu kuṇṭhavyādhitāśucīnāmutsāhasvāsthyaśucīnāmākhyāne dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
031517| ātripakṣāditi catuṣpadānāmupāvartanam, āsaṃvatsarāditi manuṣyāṇām ||
031518| tāvatā hi kālena śakyaṃ śaucāśauce jñātum ||
031519  dātā pratigrahītā ca syātāṃ nopahatau yathā |
031519  dāne kraye vānuśayaṃ tathā kuryuḥ sabhāsadaḥ ||E

(ṛelationof ownership)
031601| dattasyāpradānamṛṇādānena vyākhyātam ||
031602| dattamavyavahāryamekatrānuśaye varteta ||
031603| sarvasvaṃ putradāramātmānaṃ vā pradāyānuśayinaḥ prayacchet ||
031604| dharmadānamasādhuṣu karmasu cāupaghātikeṣu vā, arthadānamanupakāriṣvapakāriṣu vā, kāmadānamanarheṣu ca ||
031605| yathā ca dātā pratigrahītā ca nopahatau syātāṃ tathānuśayaṃ kuśalāḥ kalpayeyuḥ ||
031606| daṇḍabhayādākrośabhayādanarthabhayādvā bhayadānaṃ pratigṛhṇataḥ steyadaṇḍaḥ, prayacchataśca ||
031607| roṣadānaṃ parahiṃsāyāṃ, rājñāmupari darpadānaṃ ca ||
031608| tatrottamo daṇḍaḥ ||
031609| prātibhāvyaṃ daṇḍaśulkaśeṣamākṣikaṃ saurikaṃ ca nākāmaḥ putro dāyādo vā rikthaharo dadyāt || iti dattasyānapākarma |
031610| asvāmivikrayastu - naṣṭāpahṛtamāsādya svāmī dharmasthena grāhayet ||
031611| deśakālātipattau vā svayaṃ gṛhītvopaharet ||
031612| dharmasthaśca svāminamanuyuñjīta "kutaste labdham" iti ||
031613| sa cedācārakramaṃ darśayeta, na vikretāraṃ, tasya dravyasyātisargeṇa mucyeta ||
031614| vikretā ceddṛśyeta, mūlyaṃ steyadaṇḍaṃ ca dadyāt ||
031615| sa cedapasāramadhigacchedapasaredāpasārakṣayāt ||
031616| kṣaye mūlyaṃ steyadaṇḍaṃ ca dadyāt ||
031617| nāṣṭikaśca svakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā naṣṭapratyāhṛtaṃ labheta ||
031618| svakaraṇābhāve pañcabandho daṇḍaḥ ||
031619| tacca dravyaṃ rājadharmyaṃ syāt ||
031620| naṣṭāpahṛtamanivedyotkarṣataḥ svāminaḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
031621| śulkasthāne naṣṭāpahṛtotpannaṃ tiṣṭhet ||
031622| tripakṣādūrdhvamanabhisāraṃ rājā haret, svāmī vā svakaraṇena ||
031623| pañcapaṇikaṃ dvipadarūpasya niṣkrayaṃ dadyāt, catuṣpaṇikamekakhurasya, dvipaṇikaṃ gomahiṣasya, pādikaṃ kṣudrapaśūnām ||
031624| ratnasāraphalgukupyānāṃ pañcakaṃ śataṃ dadyāt ||
031625| paracakrāṭavīhṛtaṃ tu pratyānīya rājā yathāsvaṃ prayacchet ||

(Forcible seiśure)
031701| sāhasamanvayavatprasabhakarma ||
031702| niranvaye steyam, apavyayane ca ||

031707| kālāyasakāṣṭharajjudravyakṣudrapaśupaṭādīnāṃ sthūlakadravyāṇāṃ caturviṃśatipaṇāvaroaṣṭacatvāriṃśatpaṇaparo daṇḍaḥ ||
031708| tāmravṛttakaṃsakācadantabhāṇḍādīnāṃ sthūlakadravyāṇāmaṣṭacatvāriṃśatpaṇāvaraḥ ṣaṇṇavatiparaḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
031709| mahāpaśumanuṣyakṣetragṛhahiraṇyasuvarṇasūkṣmavastrādīnāṃ sthūlakadravyāṇāṃ dviśatāvaraḥ pañcaśataparo madhyamaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
031710| striyaṃ puruṣaṃ vābhiṣahya badhnato bandhayato bandhaṃ vā mokṣayataḥ pañcaśatāvaraḥ sahasrapara uttamaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ || ityācāryāḥ ||
031711| "yaḥ sāhasaṃ "pratipattā" iti kārayati sa dviguṇaṃ dadyāt ||
031712| "yāvaddhiraṇyamupayokṣyate tāvaddāsyāmi" iti sa caturguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyāt ||
031713| yaḥ "etāvaddhiraṇyaṃ dāsyāmi" iti pramāṇamuddiśya kārayati sa yathoktaṃ hiraṇyaṃ daṇḍaṃ ca dadyāt" iti bārhaspatyāḥ ||
031714| sa cetkopaṃ madaṃ mohaṃ vāpadiśedyathoktavaddaṇḍamenaṃ kuryāditi kauṭilyaḥ ||
031715  daṇḍakarmasu sarveṣu rūpamaṣṭapaṇaṃ śatam |

031716  prajānāṃ doṣabāhulyādrājñāṃ vā bhāvadoṣataḥ |

031808| tena śrutopavādo vāgjīvanānāṃ, kārukuśīlavānāṃ vṛttyupavādaḥ, prājjūṇakagāndhārādīnāṃ ca janapadopavādā vyākhyātāḥ ||

031810| aśaktaḥ kopaṃ madaṃ mohaṃ vāpadiśeddvādaśapaṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyāt ||

032014| prakīrṇakaṃ tu - yācitakāvakrītakāhitakanikṣepakāṇāṃ yathādeśakālamadāne, yāmacchāyāsamupaveśasaṃsthitīnāṃ vā deśakālātipātane, gulmataradeyaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sādhayataḥ, prativeśānuveśayorupari nimantraṇe ca dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||

032017| śapathavākyānuyogamaniṣṛṣṭaṃ kurvataḥ, yuktakarma cāyuktasya, kṣudrapaśuvṛṣāṇāṃ puṃstvopaghātinaḥ, dāsyā garbhamauṣadhena pātayataśca pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||

032019| puruṣamabandhanīyaṃ badhnato bandhayato bandhaṃ vā mokṣayato, bālamaprāptavyavahāraṃ badhnato bandhayato vā sahasraṃ daṇḍaḥ ||

04138| tenottaraṃ vyākhyātam ||

04140| kākaṇī cāsya palavetanam ||
04141| kālāyasapiṇḍaḥ pañcabhāgakṣayaḥ ||
04142| kākaṇīdvayaṃ cāsya palavetanam ||
04143| tenottaraṃ vyākhyātam ||
04144| rūpadarśakasya sthitāṃ paṇayātrāmakopyāṃ kopayataḥ kopyāmakopayato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04145| vyājīpariśuddhau paṇayātrā ||
04146| paṇānmāṣakamupajīvato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04147| tenottaraṃ vyākhyātam ||
04148| kūṭarūpaṃ kārayataḥ pratigṛhṇato niryāpayato vā sahasraṃ daṇḍaḥ, kośe prakṣipato vadhaḥ ||
04149| carakapāṃsudhāvakāḥ sāratribhāgaṃ, dvau rājā ratnaṃ ca ||
04150| ratnāpahāra uttamo daṇḍaḥ ||
04151| khaniratnanidhinivedaneṣu ṣaṣṭhamaṃśaṃ nivettā labheta, dvādaśamaṃśaṃ bhṛtakaḥ ||
04152| śatasahasrādūrdhvaṃ rājagāmī nidhiḥ ||
04153| ūne ṣaṣṭhamaṃśaṃ dadyāt ||
04154| paurvapauruṣikaṃ nidhiṃ jānapadaḥ śuciḥ svakaraṇena samagraṃ labheta ||
04155| svakaraṇābhāve pañcaśato daṇḍaḥ, pracchannādāne sahasram ||
04156| bhiṣajaḥ prāṇābādhikamanākhyāyopakramamāṇasya vipattau pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, karmāparādhena vipattau madhyamaḥ ||
04157| marmavadhavaiguṇyakaraṇe daṇḍapāruṣyaṃ vidyāt ||
04158| kuśīlavā varṣārātramekasthā vaseyuḥ ||
04159| kāmadānamatimātramekasyātivādaṃ ca varjayeyuḥ ||
04160| tasyātikrame dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04161| kāmaṃ deśajātigotracaraṇamaithunāvahāsena narmayeyuḥ ||
04162| kuśīlavaiścāraṇā bhikṣukāśca vyākhyātāḥ ||
04163| teṣāmayaḥśūlena yāvataḥ paṇānabhivadeyustāvantaḥ śiphāprahārā daṇḍāḥ ||
04164| śeṣāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ niṣpattivetanaṃ śilpināṃ kalpayet ||
04165  evaṃ corānacorākhyān vaṇikkārukuśīlavān |
04166  bhikṣukān kuhakāṃścānyān vārayeddeśapīḍanāt ||E

(Keepinga watchovertraders)
04201| saṃsthādhyakṣaḥ paṇyasaṃsthāyāṃ purāṇabhāṇḍānāṃ svakaraṇaviśuddhānāmādhānaṃ vikrayaṃ vā sthāpayet ||
04202| tulāmānabhāṇḍāni cāvekṣeta pautavāpacārāt ||
04203| parimāṇīdroṇayorardhapalahīnātiriktamadoṣaḥ ||
04204| palahīnātirikte dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04205| tena palottarā daṇḍavṛddhirvyākhyātā ||
04206| tulāyāḥ karṣahīnātiriktamadoṣaḥ ||
04207| dvikarṣahīnātirikte ṣaṭpaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04208| tena karṣottarā daṇḍavṛddhirvyākhyātā ||
04209| āḍhakasyārdhakarṣahīnātiriktamadoṣaḥ ||
04210| karṣahīnātirikte tripaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04211| tena karṣottarā daṇḍavṛddhirvyākhyātā ||
04212| tulāmānaviśeṣāṇāmatoanyeṣāmanumānaṃ kuryāt ||
04213| tulāmānābhyāmatiriktābhyāṃ krītvā hīnābhyāṃ vikrīṇānasya ta eva dviguṇā daṇḍāḥ ||
04214| gaṇyapaṇyeṣvaṣṭabhāgaṃ paṇyamūlyeṣvapaharataḥ ṣaṇṇavatirdaṇḍaḥ ||
04215| kāṣṭhalohamaṇimayaṃ rajjucarmamṛṇmayaṃ sūtravalkaromamayaṃ vā jātyamityajātyaṃ vikrayādhānaṃ nayato mūlyāṣṭaguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04216| sārabhāṇḍamityasārabhāṇḍaṃ tajjātamityatajjātaṃ rādhāyuktamityupadhiyuktaṃ samudgaparivartimaṃ vā vikrayādhānaṃ nayato hīnamūlyaṃ catuṣpañcāśatpaṇo daṇḍaḥ, paṇamūlyaṃ dviguṇo, dvipaṇamūlyaṃ dviśataḥ ||
04217| tenārghavṛddhau daṇḍavṛddhirvyākhyātā ||
04218| kāruśilpināṃ karmaguṇāpakarṣamājīvaṃ vikrayakrayopaghātaṃ vā sambhūya samutthāpayatāṃ sahasraṃ daṇḍaḥ ||
04219| vaidehakānāṃ vā sambhūya paṇyamavarundhatāmanargheṇa vikrīṇatāṃ vā sahasraṃ daṇḍaḥ ||
04220| tulāmānāntaramarghavarṇāntaraṃ vā - dharakasya māyakasya vā paṇamūlyādaṣṭabhāgaṃ hastadoṣeṇācarato dviśato daṇḍaḥ ||
04221| tena dviśatottarā daṇḍavṛddhirvyākhyātā ||
04222| dhānyasnehakṣāralavaṇagandhabhaiṣajyadravyāṇāṃ samavarṇopadhāne dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04223| yanniṣṛṣṭamupajīveyustadeṣāṃ divasasaṃjātaṃ saṃkhyāya vaṇiksthāpayet ||
04224| kretṛvikretrorantarapatitamādāyādanyadbhavati ||
04225| tena dhānyapaṇyanicayāṃścānujñātāḥ kuryuḥ ||
04226| anyathānicitameṣāṃ paṇyādhyakṣo gṛhṇīyāt ||
04227| tena dhānyapaṇyavikraye vyavaharetānugraheṇa prajānām ||
04228| anujñātakrayādupari caiṣāṃ svadeśīyānāṃ paṇyānāṃ pañcakaṃ śatamājīvaṃ sthāpayet, paradeśīyānāṃ daśakam ||
04229| tataḥ paramarghaṃ vardhayatāṃ kraye vikraye vā bhāvayatāṃ paṇaśate pañcapaṇāddviśato daṇḍaḥ ||
04230| tenārghavṛddhau daṇḍavṛddhirvyākhyātā ||
04231| sambhūyakraye caiṣāmavikrīte nānyaṃ sambhūyakrayaṃ dadyāt ||
04232| paṇyopaghāte caiṣāmanugrahaṃ kuryāt ||
04233| paṇyabāhulyātpaṇyādhyakṣaḥ sarvapaṇyānyekamukhāni vikrīṇīta ||
04234| teṣvavikrīteṣu nānye vikrīṇīran ||
04235| tāni divasavetanena vikrīṇīrannanugraheṇa prajānām ||
04236  deśakālāntaritānāṃ tu paṇyānāṃ - prakṣepaṃ paṇyaniṣpattiṃ śulkaṃ vṛddhimavakrayam |
04236  vyayānanyāṃśca saṃkhyāya sthāpayedarghamarghavit ||E

(ṛemedialmeasuresduringcalamities)
04301| daivānyaṣṭau mahābhayāni - agnirudakaṃ vyādhirdurbhikṣaṃ mūṣikā vyālāḥ sarpā rakṣāṃsīti ||
04302| tebhyo janapadaṃ rakṣet ||
04303| grīṣme bahiradhiśrayaṇaṃ grāmāḥ kuryuḥ, daśamūlīsaṃgraheṇādhiṣṭhitā vā ||
04304| nāgarikapraṇidhāvagnipratiṣedho vyākhyātaḥ, niśāntapraṇidhau rājaparigrahe ca ||
04305| balihomasvastivācanaiḥ parvasu cāgnipūjāḥ kārayet ||
04306| varṣārātramānūpagrāmāḥ pūravelāmutsṛjya vaseyuḥ ||
04307| kāṣṭhaveṇunāvaścopagṛhṇīyuḥ ||
04308| uhyamānamalābudṛtiplavagaṇḍikāveṇikābhistārayeyuḥ ||
04309| anabhisaratāṃ dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra plavahīnebhyaḥ ||
04310| parvasu ca nadīpūjāḥ kārayet ||
04311| māyāyogavido vedavido vā varṣamabhicareyuḥ ||
04312| varṣāvagrahe śacīnāthagaṅgāparvatamahākacchapūjāḥ kārayet ||
04313| vyādhibhayamaupaniṣadikaiḥ pratīkāraiḥ pratikuryuḥ, auṣadhaiścikitsakāḥ śāntiprāyaścittairvā siddhatāpasāḥ ||
04314| tena marako vyākhyātaḥ ||
04315| tīrthābhiṣecanaṃ mahākacchavardhanaṃ gavāṃ śmaśānāvadohanaṃ kabandhadahanaṃ devarātriṃ ca kārayet ||
04316| paśuvyādhimarake sthānārthanīrājanaṃ svadaivatapūjanaṃ ca kārayet ||
04317| durbhikṣe rājā bījabhaktopagrahaṃ kṛtvānugrahaṃ kuryāt, durgasetukarma vā bhaktānugraheṇa, bhaktasaṃvibhāgaṃ vā, deśanikṣepaṃ vā ||
04318| mitrāṇi vā vyapāśrayeta, karśanaṃ vamanaṃ vā kuryāt ||
04319| niṣpannasasyamanyaviṣayaṃ vā sajanapado yāyāt, samudrasarastaṭākāni vā saṃśrayeta ||
04320| dhānyaśākamūlaphalāvāpān vā setuṣu kurvīta, mṛgapaśupakṣivyālamatsyārambhān vā ||
04321| mūṣikabhaye mārjāranakulotsargaḥ ||
04322| teṣāṃ grahaṇahiṃsāyāṃ dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, śunāmanigrahe cānyatrāraṇyacarebhyaḥ ||
04323| snuhikṣīraliptāni dhānyāni visṛjed, upaniṣadyogayuktāni vā ||
04324| mūṣikakaraṃ vā prayuñjīta ||
04325| śāntiṃ vā siddhatāpasāḥ kuryuḥ ||
04326| parvasu ca mūṣikapūjāḥ kārayet ||
04327| tena śalabhapakṣikrimibhayapratīkārā vyākhyātāḥ ||
04328| vyālabhaye madanarasayuktāni paśuśavāni prasṛjet, madanakodravapūrṇānyaudaryāṇi vā ||
04329| lubdhakāḥ śvagaṇino vā kūṭapañjarāvapātaiścareyuḥ ||
04330| āvaraṇinaḥ śastrapāṇayo vyālānabhihanyuḥ ||
04331| anabhisarturdvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
04332| sa eva lābho vyālaghātinaḥ ||
04333| parvasu ca parvatapūjāḥ kārayet ||
04334| tena mṛgapaśupakṣisaṃghagrāhapratīkārā vyākhyātāḥ ||
04335| sarpabhaye mantrairoṣadhibhiśca jāṅgulīvidaścareyuḥ ||
04336| sambhūya vāpi sarpān hanyuḥ ||
04337| atharvavedavido vābhicareyuḥ ||
04338| parvasu ca nāgapūjāḥ kārayet ||
04339| tenodakaprāṇibhayapratīkārā vyākhyātāḥ ||
04340| rakṣobhaye rakṣoghnānyatharvavedavido māyāyogavido vā karmāṇi kuryuḥ ||
04341| parvasu ca vitardicchatrollopikāhastapatākācchāgopahāraiścaityapūjāḥ kārayet ||
04342| "caruṃ vaścarāmaḥ" ityevaṃ sarvabhayeṣvahorātraṃ careyuḥ ||
04343| sarvatra copahatān pitevānugṛhṇīyāt ||
04344  māyāyogavidastasmādviṣaye siddhatāpasāḥ |
04344  vaseyuḥ pūjitā rājñā daivāpatpratikāriṇaḥ ||E

(ṅuardingagainstpersonswithsecretmeansof income)
04401| samāhartṛpraṇidhau janapadarakṣaṇamuktam ||
04402| tasya kaṇṭakaśodhanaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ ||
04403| samāhartā janapade siddhatāpasapravrajitacakracaracāraṇakuhakapracchandakakārtāntikanaimittikamauhūrtikacikitsakonmattamūkabadhirajaḍāndhavaidehakakāruśilpikuśīlavaveśaśauṇḍikāpūpikapākvamāṃsikāudanikavyañjanān praṇidadhyāt ||
04404| te grāmāṇāmadhyakṣāṇāṃ ca śaucāśaucaṃ vidyuḥ ||
04405| yaṃ cātra gūḍhājīvinaṃ śaṅketa taṃ sattriṇāpasarpayet ||
04406| dharmasthaṃ viśvāsopagataṃ sattrī brūyāt- "asau me bandhurabhiyuktaḥ, tasyāyamanarthaḥ pratikriyatām, ayaṃ cārthaḥ pratigṛhyatām" iti ||
04407| sa cettathā kuryādupadāgrāhaka iti pravāsyeta ||
04408| tena pradeṣṭāro vyākhyātāḥ ||
04409| grāmakūṭamadhyakṣaṃ vā sattrī brūyāt- "asau jālmaḥ prabhūtadravyaḥ, tasyāyamanarthaḥ, tenainamāhārayasva" iti ||
04410| sa cettathā kuryādutkocaka iti pravāsyeta ||
04411| kṛtakābhiyukto vā kūṭasākṣiṇoabhijñātānarthavaipulyenārabheta ||
04412| te cettathā kuryuḥ kūṭasākṣiṇa iti pravāsyeran ||
04413| tena kūṭaśrāvaṇakārakā vyākhyātāḥ ||
04414| yaṃ vā mantrayogamūlakarmabhiḥ śmāśānikairvā saṃvadanakarakaṃ manyeta taṃ sattrī brūyāt- "amuṣya bhāryāṃ snuṣāṃ duhitaraṃ vā kāmaye, sā māṃ pratikāmayatām, ayaṃ cārthaḥ pratigṛhyatām" iti ||
04415| sa cettathā kuryātsaṃvadanakāraka iti pravāsyeta ||
04416| tena kṛtyābhicāraśīlau vyākhyātau ||
04417| yaṃ vā rasasya kartāraṃ kretāraṃ vikretāraṃ bhaiṣajyāhāravyavahāriṇaṃ vā rasadaṃ manyeta taṃ sattrī brūyāt- "asau me śatruḥ, tasyopaghātaḥ kriyatām, ayaṃ cārthaḥ pratigṛhyatām" iti ||
04418| sa cettathā kuryādrasada iti pravāsyeta ||
04419| tena madanayogavyavahārī vyākhyātaḥ ||
04420| yaṃ vā nānālohakṣārāṇāmaṅgārabhasmāsaṃdaṃśamuṣṭikādhikaraṇībimbaṭaṅkamūṣāṇāmabhīkṣṇakretāraṃ maṣībhasmadhūmadigdhahastavastraliṅgaṃ karmāropakaraṇasaṃsargaṃ kūṭarūpakārakaṃ manyeta taṃ sattrī śiṣyatvena saṃvyavahāreṇa cānupraviśya prajñāpayet ||
04421| prajñātaḥ kūṭarūpakāraka iti pravāsyeta ||
04422| tena rāgasyāpahartā kūṭasuvarṇavyavahārī ca vyākhyātaḥ ||
04423  ārabdhārastu hiṃsāyāṃ gūḍhājīvāstrayodaśa |
04423  pravāsyā niṣkrayārthaṃ vā dadyurdoṣaviśeṣataḥ ||E

(ḍetectionof criminalsthroughsecretagentsin the disguise of holymen)
04501| sattriprayogādūrdhvaṃ siddhavyañjanā māṇavānmāṇavavidyābhiḥ pralobhayeyuḥ, prasvāpanāntardhānadvārāpohamantreṇa pratirodhakān, saṃvadanamantreṇa pāratalpikān ||
04502| teṣāṃ kṛtotsāhānāṃ mahāntaṃ saṃghamādāya rātrāvanyaṃ grāmamuddiśyānyaṃ grāmaṃ kṛtakastrīpuruṣaṃ gatvā brūyuḥ - "ihaiva vidyāprabhāvo dṛśyatāṃ, kṛcchraḥ paragrāmo gantum" iti ||
04503| tato dvārāpohamantreṇa dvārāṇyapohya "praviśyatām" iti brūyuḥ ||
04504| antardhānamantreṇa jāgratāmārakṣiṇāṃ madhyena māṇavānatikrāmayeyuḥ ||
04505| prasvāpanamantreṇa prasvāpayitvā rakṣiṇaḥ śayābhirmāṇavaiḥ saṃcārayeyuḥ ||
04506| saṃvadanamantreṇa bhāryāvyañjanāḥ pareṣāṃ māṇavaiḥ sammodayeyuḥ ||
04507| upalabdhavidyāprabhāvāṇāṃ puraścaraṇādyādiśeyurabhijñānārtham ||
04508| kṛtalakṣaṇadravyeṣu vā veśmasu karma kārayeyuḥ ||
04509| anupraviṣṭā vaikatra grāhayeyuḥ ||
04510| kṛtalakṣaṇadravyakrayavikrayādhāneṣu yogasurāmattān vā grāhayeyuḥ ||
04511| gṛhītān pūrvāpadānasahāyānanuyuñjīta ||
04512| purāṇacoravyañjanā vā corānanupraviṣṭāstathaiva karma kārayeyurgrāhayeyuśca ||
04513| gṛhītān samāhartā paurajānapadānāṃ darśayet- "coragrahaṇīṃ vidyāmadhīte rājā, tasyopadeśādime corā gṛhītāḥ, bhūyaśca grahīṣyāmi, vārayitavyo vaḥ svajanaḥ pāpācārah" iti ||
04514| yaṃ cātrāpasarpopadeśena śamyāpratodādīnāmapahartāraṃ jānīyāttameṣāṃ pratyādiśet"eṣa rājñaḥ prabhāvaḥ" iti ||
04515| purāṇacoragopālakavyādhaśvagaṇinaśca vanacorāṭavikānanupraviṣṭāḥ prabhūtakūṭahiraṇyakupyabhāṇḍeṣu sārthavrajagrāmeṣvenānabhiyojayeyuḥ ||
04516| abhiyoge gūḍhabalairghātayeyuḥ, madanarasayuktena vā pathyadanena ||
04517| gṛhītaloptrabhārānāyatagatapariśrāntān prasvapataḥ prahavaṇeṣu yogasurāmattān vā grāhayeyuḥ ||
04518  pūrvavacca gṛhītvainān samāhartā prarūpayet |
04518  sarvajñakhyāpanaṃ rājñaḥ kārayan rāṣṭravāsiṣu ||E

(ārreston suspicion, withthe stolenarticle andbyindicationsof the act)
04601| siddhaprayogādūrdhvaṃ śaṅkārūpakarmābhigrahaḥ ||
04602  kṣīṇadāyakuṭumbam, alpanirveśaṃ, viparītadeśajātigotranāmakarmāpadeśaṃ, pracchannavṛttikarmāṇaṃ,-
04602  māṃsasurābhakṣyabhojanagandhamālyavastravibhūṣaṇeṣu prasaktam, ativyayakartāraṃ, puṃścalīdyūtaśauṇḍikeṣu prasaktam,-
04602  abhīkṣṇapravāsinam, avijñātasthānagamanam, ekāntāraṇyaniṣkuṭavikālacāriṇaṃ, pracchanne sāmiṣe vā deśe bahumantrasaṃnipātaṃ,-
04602  sadyaḥkṣatavraṇānāṃ gūḍhapratīkārakārayitāram, antargṛhanityam, abhyadhigantāraṃ, kāntāparaṃ,-
04602  paraparigrahāṇāṃ parastrīdravyaveśmanāmabhīkṣṇapraṣṭāraṃ, kutsitakarmaśāstropakaraṇasaṃsargaṃ,-
04602  virātre channakuḍyacchāyāsaṃcāriṇaṃ, virūpadravyāṇāmadeśakālavikretāraṃ, jātavairaśayaṃ, hīnakarmajātiṃ,-
04602  | vigūhamānarūpaṃ, liṅgenāliṅginaṃ, liṅginaṃ vā bhinnācāraṃ, pūrvakṛtāpadānaṃ, svakarmabhirapadiṣṭaṃ,-
04602  | nāgarikamahāmātradarśane guhamānamapasarantamanucchvāsopaveśinamāvignaṃ śuṣkabhinnasvaramukhavarṇaṃ,-
04602 | śastrahastamanuṣyasampātatrāsinaṃ, hiṃsrastenanidhinikṣepāpahāraparaprayogagūḍhājīvināmanyatamaṃ śaṅketa || iti śaṅkābhigrahaḥ ||
04603| rūpābhigrahastu - naṣṭāpahṛtamavidyamānaṃ tajjātavyavahāriṣu nivedayet ||
04604| taccenniveditamāsādya pracchādayeyuḥ sācivyakaradoṣamāpnuyuḥ ||04605| ajānantoasya dravyasyātisargeṇa mucyeran ||
04606| na cānivedya saṃsthādhyakṣasya purāṇabhāṇḍānāmādhānaṃ vikrayaṃ vā kuryuḥ ||
04607| taccenniveditamāsādyeta, rūpābhigṛhītamāgamaṃ pṛcchet"kutaste labdham" iti ||
04608| sa cetbrūyāt"dāyādyādavāptam, amuṣmāllabdhaṃ krītaṃ kāritamādhipracchannam, ayamasya deśaḥ kālaścopasamprāpteḥ, ayamasyārghaḥ pramāṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ mūlyaṃ ca" iti, tasyāgamasamādhau mucyeta ||
04609| nāṣṭikaścettadeva pratisaṃdadhyāt, yasyā pūrvo dīrghaśca paribhogaḥ śucirvā deśastasya dravyamiti vidyāt ||
04610| catuṣpadadvipadānāmapi hi rūpaliṅgasāmānyaṃ bhavati, kimaṅga punarekayonidravyakartṛprasūtānāṃ kupyābharaṇabhāṇḍānāmiti ||
04611| sa cedbrūyāt"yācitakamavakrītakamāhitakaṃ nikṣepamupanidhiṃ vaiyāvṛtyakarma vāmuṣya" iti, tasyāpasārapratisaṃdhānena mucyeta ||
04612| "naivam" ityapasāro vā brūyāt, rūpābhigṛhītaḥ parasya dānakāraṇamātmanaḥ pratigrahakāraṇamupaliṅganaṃ vā dāyakadāpakanibandhakapratigrāhakopadraṣṭṛbhirupaśrotṛbhirvā pratisamānayet ||
04613| ujjhitapranaṣṭaniṣpatitopalabdhasya deśakālalābhopaliṅganena śuddhiḥ ||
04614| aśuddhastacca tāvacca daṇḍaṃ dadyāt ||
04615| anyathā steyadaṇḍaṃ bhajeta || iti rūpābhigrahaḥ |
04616| karmābhigrahastu - muṣitaveśmanaḥ praveśaniṣkasanamadvāreṇa, dvārasya saṃdhinā bījena vā vedham, uttamāgārasya jālavātāyananīpravedham, ārohaṇāvataraṇe ca kuḍyasya vedham, upakhananaṃ vā gūḍhadravyanikṣepaṇagrahaṇopāyam, upadeśopalabhyamabhyantaracchedotkaraparimardopakaraṇamabhyantarakṛtaṃ vidyāt ||
04617| viparyaye bāhyakṛtam, ubhayata ubhayakṛtam ||
04618  abhyantarakṛte puruṣamāsannaṃ vyasaninaṃ krūrasahāyaṃ taskaropakaraṇasaṃsargaṃ, striyaṃ vā daridrakulāmanyaprasaktāṃ vā,-
04618  paricārakajanaṃ vā tadvidhācāram, atisvapnaṃ, nidrāklāntam, āvignaṃ śuṣkabhinnasvaramukhavarṇam, anavasthitam,-
04618  atipralāpinam, uccārohaṇasaṃrabdhagātraṃ, vilūnanighṛṣṭabhinnapāṭitaśarīravastraṃ, jātakiṇasaṃrabdhahastapādaṃ,-
04618  pāṃsupūrṇakeśanakhaṃ vilūnabhugnakeśanakhaṃ vā, samyaksnātānuliptaṃ tailapramṛṣṭagātraṃ sadyodautahastapādaṃ vā,-
04618  pāṃsupicchileṣu tulyapādapadanikṣepaṃ, praveśaniṣkasanayorvā tulyamālyamadyagandhavastracchedavilepanasvedaṃ parīkṣeta ||
04619| coraṃ pāradārikaṃ vā vidyāt ||
04620  sagopasthāniko bāhyaṃ pradeṣṭā coramārgaṇam |
04620  kuryānnāgarikaścāntardurge nirdiṣṭahetubhiḥ ||E

(īnṝuestof sudden deaths)
04701| tailābhyaktamāśumṛtakaṃ parīkṣeta ||
04702| niṣkīrṇamūtrapurīṣaṃ vātapūrṇakoṣṭhatvakkaṃ śūnapādapāṇimānmīlitākṣaṃ savyañjanakaṇṭhaṃ pīṭananiruddhocchvāsahataṃ vidyāt ||
04703| tameva saṃkucitabāhusakthimudbandhahataṃ vidyāt ||
04704| śūnapāṇipādodaramapagatākṣamudvṛttanābhimavaropitaṃ vidyāt ||
04705| nistabdhagudākṣaṃ saṃdaṣṭajihvamādhmātodaramudakahataṃ vidyāt ||
04706| śoṇitānusiktaṃ bhagnabhinnagātraṃ kāṣṭhairaśmabhirvā hataṃ vidyāt ||
04707| sambhagnasphuṭitagātramavakṣiptaṃ vidyāt ||
04708| śyāvapāṇipādadantanakhaṃ śithilamāṃsaromacarmāṇaṃ phenopadigdhamukhaṃ viṣahataṃ vidyāt ||
04709| tameva sa-śoṇitadaṃśaṃ sarpakīṭahataṃ vidyāt |
04710| vikṣiptavastragātramativantaviriktaṃ madanayogahataṃ vidyāt ||
04711| atoanyatamena kāraṇena hataṃ hatvā vā daṇḍabhayādudbaddhanikṛttakaṇṭhaṃ vidyāt ||
04712| viṣahatasya bhojanaśeṣaṃ vayobhiḥ parīkṣeta ||
04713| hṛdayāduddhṛtyāgnau prakṣiptaṃ ciṭiciṭāyadindradhanurvarṇaṃ vā viṣayuktaṃ vidyāt, dagdhasya hṛdayamadagdhaṃ dṛṣṭvā vā ||
04714| tasya paricārakajanaṃ vāgdaṇḍapāruṣyātilabdhaṃ mārgeta, duḥkhopahatamanyaprasaktaṃ vā strījanaṃ, dāyavṛttistrījanābhimantāraṃ vā bandhum ||
04715| tadeva hatodbaddhasya parīkṣeta ||
04716| svayamudbaddhasya vā viprakāramayuktaṃ mārgeta ||
04717| sarveṣāṃ vā strīdāyādyadoṣaḥ karmaspardhā pratipakṣadveṣaḥ paṇyasaṃsthāsamavāyo vā vivādapadānāmanyatamadvā roṣasthānam ||
04718| roṣanimitto ghātaḥ ||
04719| svayaṃādiṣṭapuruṣairvā, corairarthanimittaṃ, sādṛśyādanyavairibhirvā hatasya ghātamāsannebhyaḥ parīkṣeta ||
04720| yenāhūtaḥ saha sthitaḥ prasthito hatabhūmimānīto vā tamanuyuñjīta ||
04721| ye cāsya hatabhūmāvāsannacarāstānekaikaśaḥ pṛcchet"kenāyamihānīto hato vā, kaḥ saśastraḥ saṃgūhamāna udvigno vā yuṣmābhirdṛṣṭaḥ" iti ||
04722| te yathā brūyustathānuyuñjīta ||
04723  anāthasya śarīrasthamupabhogaṃ paricchadam |
04723  vastraṃ veṣaṃ vibhūṣāṃ vā dṛṣṭvā tadvyavahāriṇaḥ ||
04724  anuyuñjīta saṃyogaṃ nivāsaṃ vāsakāraṇam |
04724  karma ca vyavahāraṃ ca tato mārgaṇamācaret ||
04725  rajjuśastraviṣairvāpi kāmakrodhavaśena yaḥ |
04725  ghātayetsvayamātmānaṃ strī vā pāpena mohitā ||
04726  rajjunā rājamārge tāṃścaṇḍālenāpakarṣayet |
04726  na śmaśānavidhisteṣāṃ na sambandhikriyāstathā ||
04727  bandhusteṣāṃ tu yaḥ kuryātpretakāryakriyāvidhim |
04727  tadgatiṃ sa caretpaścātsvajanādvā pramucyate ||
04728  saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
04728  yājanādhyāpanādyaunāttaiścānyoapi samācaran ||E

(īnvestigation throughinterrogationandthroughtorture)
04801| muṣitasaṃnidhau bāhyānāmabhyantarāṇāṃ ca sākṣiṇāmabhiśastasya deśajātigotranāmakarmasārasahāyanivāsānanuyuñjīta ||
04802| tāṃścāpadeśaiḥ pratisamānayet ||
04803| tataḥ pūrvasyāhnaḥ pracāraṃ rātrau nivāsaṃ ca<cā> grahaṇādityanuyuñjīta ||
04804| tasyāpasārapratisaṃdhāne śuddhaḥ syāt, anyathā karmaprāptaḥ ||
04805| trirātrādūrdhvamagrāhyaḥ śaṅkitakaḥ pṛcchābhāvādanyatropakaraṇadarśanāt ||
04806| acoraṃ cora ityabhivyāharataścorasamo daṇḍaḥ, coraṃ pracchādayataśca ||
04807| coreṇābhiśasto vairadveṣābhyāmapadiṣṭakaḥ śuddhaḥ syāt ||
04808| śuddhaṃ parivāsayataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
04809| śaṅkāniṣpannamupakaraṇamantrisahāyarūpavaiyāvṛtyakarānniṣpādayet ||
04810| karmaṇaśca pradeśadravyādānāṃśavibhāgaiḥ pratisamānayet ||
04811| eteṣāṃ kāraṇānāmanabhisaṃdhāne vipralapantamacoraṃ vidyāt ||
04812| dṛśyate hyacoroapi coramārge yadṛcchayā saṃnipāte coraveṣaśastrabhāṇḍasāmānyena gṛhyamāṇaścorabhāṇḍasyopavāsena vā, yathāṇimāṇḍavyaḥ karmakleśabhayādacoraḥ "coroasmi" iti bruvāṇaḥ ||
04813| tasmātsamāptakaraṇaṃ niyamayet ||
04814| mandāparādhaṃ bālaṃ vṛddhaṃ vyādhitaṃ mattamunmattaṃ kṣutpipāsādhvaklāntamatyāśitamāmakāśitaṃ durbalaṃ vā na karma kārayet ||
04815| tulya śīlapuṃścalīprāpāvikakathāvakāśabhojanadātṛbhirapasarpayet ||
04816| evamatisaṃdadhyāt, yathā vā nikṣepāpahāre vyākhyātam ||
04817| āptadoṣaṃ karma kārayet, na tveva striyaṃ garbhiṇīṃ sūtikāṃ vā māsāvaraprajātām ||
04818| striyāstvardhakarma, vākyānuyogo vā ||
04819| brāhmaṇasya sattriparigrahaḥ śrutavatastapasvinaśca ||
04820| tasyātikrama uttamo daṇḍaḥ kartuḥ kārayituśca, karmaṇā vyāpādanena ca ||
04821| vyāvahārikaṃ karmacatuṣkaṃ - ṣaḍdaṇḍāḥ, sapta kaśāḥ, dvāvuparinibandhau, udakanālikā ca ||
04822| paraṃ pāpakarmaṇāṃ nava vetralatāḥ, dvādaśa kaśāḥ, dvāvūruveṣṭau, viṃśatirnaktamālalatāḥ, dvātriṃśattalāḥ, dvau vṛścikabandhau, ullaṃbane ca dve, sūcī hastasya, yavāgūpītasya ekaparvadahanamaṅgulyāḥ, snehapītasya pratāpanamekamahaḥ, śiśirarātrau balbajāgraśayyā ca ||
04823| ityaṣṭādaśakaṃ karma ||
04824| tasyopakaraṇaṃ pramāṇaṃ praharaṇaṃ pradharaṇamavadhāraṇaṃ ca kharapaṭṭādāgamayet ||
04825| divasāntaramekaikaṃ ca karma kārayet ||
04826| pūrvakṛtāpadānaṃ pratijñāyāpaharantamekadeśadṛṣṭadravyaṃ karmaṇā rūpeṇa vā gṛhītaṃ rājakośamavastṛṇantaṃ karmavadhyaṃ vā rājavacanātsamastaṃ vyastamabhyastaṃ vā karma kārayet ||
04827| sarvāparādheṣvapīḍanīyo brāhmaṇaḥ ||
04828| tasyābhiśastāṅko lalāṭe syādvyavahārapatanāya, steyo śvā, manuṣyavadhe kabandhaḥ, gurutalpe bhagam, surāpāne madyadhvajaḥ ||
04829  brāhmaṇaṃ pāpakarmāṇamudghuṣyāṅkakṛtavraṇam |
