(Keepinga watchoverofficersof alldepartments)
04901| samāhartṛpradeṣṭāraḥ pūrvamadhyakṣāṇāmadhyakṣapuruṣāṇāṃ ca niyamanaṃ kuryuḥ ||
04902| khanisārakarmāntebhyaḥ sāraṃ ratnaṃ vāpaharataḥ śuddhavadhaḥ ||
04903| phalgudravyakarmāntebhyaḥ phalgu dravyamupaskaraṃ vā pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
04904| paṇyabhūmibhyo vā rājapaṇyaṃ māṣamūlyādūrdhvamāpādamūlyādityapaharato dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, ādvipādamūlyāditi caturviṃśatipaṇaḥ, ātripādamūlyāditi ṣaṭtriṃśatpaṇaḥ, āpaṇamūlyādityaṣṭacatvāriṃśatpaṇaḥ, ādvipaṇamūlyāditi pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, ācatuṣpaṇamūlyāditi madhyamaḥ, āṣṭapaṇamūlyādityuttamaḥ, ādaśapaṇamūlyād
iti vadhaḥ ||
04905| koṣṭhapaṇyakupyāyudhāgārebhyaḥ kupyabhāṇḍopaskarāpahāreṣvardhamūlyeṣu eta eva daṇḍāḥ ||
04906| kośabhāṇḍāgārākṣaśālābhyaścaturbhāgamūlyeṣu eta eva dviguṇā daṇḍāḥ ||
04907| corāṇāmabhipradharṣaṇe citro ghātaḥ ||
04908| iti rājaparigraheṣu vyākhyātam ||
04909| bāhyeṣu tu - pracchannamahani kṣetrakhalaveśmāpaṇebhyaḥ kupyabhāṇḍamupaskaraṃ vā māṣamūlyādūrdhvamāpādamūlyādityapaharatastripaṇo daṇḍaḥ, gomayapradehena vā pralipyāvaghoṣaṇamādvipādamūlyāditi ṣaṭpaṇaḥ, gomayabhasmanā vā pralipyāvaghoṣaṇam, ātripādamūlyāditi navapaṇaḥ, gomayabhasmanā vā pralipyāvaghoṣaṇam,
śarāvamekhalayā vā āpaṇamūlyāditi dvādaśapaṇaḥ, muṇḍanaṃ pravrājanaṃ vā ādvipaṇamūlyāditi caturviṃśatipaṇaḥ, muṇḍasyeṣṭakāśakalena pravrājanaṃ vā ācatuṣpaṇamūlyāditi ṣaṭtriṃśatpaṇahāpañcapaṇamūlyād
ityaṣṭacatvāriṃśatpaṇaḥ, ādaśapaṇamūlyāditi pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍahāviṃśatipaṇamūlyāditdviśatahātriṃśatpaṇamūlyāditi pañcaśatahācatvāriṃśatpaṇamūlyāditi sāhasrahāpañcāśatpaṇamūlyāditi vadhaḥ ||
04910| prasahya divā rātrau vāntaryāmikamapaharatoardhamūlyeṣu eta eva daṇḍāḥ ||
04911| prasahya divā rātrau vā sa-śastrasyāpaharataścaturbhāgamūlyeṣu eta eva dviguṇā daṇḍāḥ ||
04912| kuṭuṃbikādhyakṣamukhyasvāmināṃ kūṭaśāsanamudrākarmasu pūrvamadhyottamavadhā daṇḍāḥ, yathāparādhaṃ vā ||
04913| dharmasthaścedvivadamānaṃ puruṣaṃ tarjayati bhartsayatyapasārayatyabhigrasate vā pūrvamasmai sāhasadaṇḍaṃ kuryāt, vākpāruṣye dviguṇam ||
04914| pṛcchyaṃ na pṛcchati, apṛcchyaṃ pṛcchati, pṛṣṭvā vā visṛjati, śikṣayati, smārayati, pūrvaṃ dadāti vā, iti madhyamamasmai sāhasadaṇḍaṃ kuryāt ||
04915| deyaṃ deśaṃ na pṛcchati, adeyaṃ deśaṃ pṛcchati, kāryamadeśenātivāhayati, chalenātiharati, kālaharaṇena śrāntamapavāhayati, mārgāpannaṃ vākyamutkramayati, matisāhāyyaṃ sākṣibhyo dadāti, tāritānuśiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ punarapi gṛhṇāti, uttamamasmai sāhasadaṇḍaṃ kuryāt ||
04916| punaraparādhe dviguṇaṃ sthānādvyavaropaṇaṃ ca ||
04917| lekhakaśceduktaṃ na likhati, anuktaṃ likhati, duruktamupalikhati, sūktamullikhati, arthotpattiṃ vā vikalpayati, iti pūrvamasmai sāhasadaṇḍaṃ kuryād, yathāparādhaṃ vā ||
04918| dharmasthaḥ pradeṣṭā vā hairaṇyadaṇḍamadaṇḍye kṣipati kṣepadviguṇamasmai daṇḍaṃ kuryāt, hīnātiriktāṣṭaguṇaṃ vā ||
04919| śarīradaṇḍaṃ kṣipati śārīrameva daṇḍaṃ bhajeta, niṣkrayadviguṇaṃ vā ||
04920| yaṃ vā bhūtamarthaṃ nāśayati abhūtamarthaṃ karoti tadaṣṭaguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyāt ||
04921| dharmasthīye cārake bandhanāgāre vā śayyāsanabhojanoccārasaṃcārarodhabandhaneṣu tripaṇottarā daṇḍāḥ kartuḥ kārayituśca ||
04922| cārakādabhiyuktaṃ muñcato niṣpātayato vā madhyamaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, abhiyogadānaṃ ca, bandhanāgārātsarvasvaṃ vadhaśca ||
04923| bandhanāgārādhyakṣasya saṃruddhakamanākhyāya cārayataścaturviṃśatipaṇo daṇḍaḥ, karma kārayato dviguṇaḥ, sthānānyatvaṃ gamayatoannapānaṃ vā rundhataḥ ṣaṇṇavatirdaṇḍaḥ, parikleśayata utkoṭayato vā madhyamaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, ghnataḥ sāhasraḥ ||
04924| parigṛhītāṃ dāsīmāhitikāṃ vā saṃruddhikāmadhicarataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ, coraḍāmarikabhāryāṃ madhyamaḥ, saṃruddhikāmāryāmuttamaḥ ||
04925| saṃruddhasya vā tatraiva ghātaḥ ||
04926| tadevākṣaṇagṛhītāyāmāryāyāṃ vidyāt, dāsyāṃ pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
04927| cārakamabhittvā niṣpātayato madhyamaḥ, bhittvā vadhaḥ, bandhanāgārātsarvasvaṃ vadhaśca ||
04928  evamarthacarān pūrvaṃ rājā daṇḍena śodhayet |
04928  śodhayeyuśca śuddhāste paurajānapadān damaiḥ ||E

(ṛedemption froṃ the cuttingof individuallimbs)
041001| tīrthaghātagranthibhedordhvakarāṇāṃ prathameparādhe saṃdeśacchedanaṃ catuṣpañcāśatpaṇo vā daṇḍaḥ, dvitīye chedanaṃ paṇasya śatyo vā daṇḍaḥ, tṛtīye dakṣiṇahastavadhaścatuḥśato vā daṇḍaḥ, caturthe yathākāmī vadhaḥ ||
041002| pañcaviṃśatipaṇāvareṣu kukkuṭanakulamārjāraśvasūkarasteyeṣu hiṃsāyāṃ vā catuṣpañcāśatpaṇo daṇḍaḥ, nāsāgracchedanaṃ vā caṇḍālāraṇyacarāṇāmardhadaṇḍāḥ ||
041003| pāśajālakūṭāvapāteṣu baddhānāṃ mṛgapaśupakṣivyālamatsyānāmādāne tacca tāvacca daṇḍaḥ ||
041004| mṛgadravyavanānmṛgadravyāpahāre śātyo daṇḍaḥ ||
041005| biṃbavihāramṛgapakṣisteye hiṃsāyāṃ vā dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ ||
041006| kāruśilpikuśīlavatapasvināṃ kṣudrakadravyāpahāre śatyo daṇḍaḥ, sthūlakadravyāpahāre dviśataḥ, kṛṣidravyāpahāre ca ||
041007| durgamakṛtapraveśasya praviśataḥ prākāracchidrādvā nikṣepaṃ gṛhītvāpasarataḥ kāṇḍarāvadho, dviśāto vā daṇḍaḥ ||
041008| cakrayuktaṃ nāvaṃ kṣudrapaśuṃ vāpaharata ekapādavadhaḥ, triśato vā daṇḍaḥ ||
041009| kūṭakākaṇyakṣārālāśalākāhastaviṣamakāriṇa ekahastavadhaḥ, catuḥśato vā daṇḍaḥ ||
041010| stenapāradārikayoḥ sācivyakarmaṇi striyāḥ saṃgṛhītāyāśca karṇanāsācchedanam, pañcaśato vā daṇḍaḥ, puṃṣo dviguṇaḥ ||
041011| mahāpaśumekaṃ dāsaṃ dāsīṃ vāpaharataḥ pretabhāṇḍaṃ vā vikrīṇānasya dvipādavadhaḥ, ṣaṭchato vā daṇḍaḥ ||
041012| varṇottamānāṃ gurūṇāṃ ca hastapādalaṅghane rājayānavāhanādyārohaṇe caikahastapādavadhaḥ, saptaśato vā daṇḍaḥ ||
041013| śūdrasya brāhmaṇavādino devadravyamavastṛṇato rājadviṣṭamādiśato dvinetrabhedinaśca yogāñjanenāndhatvam, aṣṭaśato vā daṇḍaḥ ||
041014| coraṃ pāradārikaṃ vā mokṣayato rājaśāsanamūnamatiriktaṃ vā likhataḥ kanyāṃ dāsīṃ vā sa-hiraṇyamaparahataḥ kūṭavyavahāriṇo vimāṃsavikrayiṇaśca vāmahastadvipādavadho, navaśato vā daṇḍaḥ ||
041015| mānuṣamāṃsavikraye vadhaḥ ||

041017  puruṣaṃ cāparādhaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ gurulāghavam |

041018  uttamāvaramadhyatvaṃ pradeṣṭā daṇḍakarmaṇi |

(īnflictionof secretpunishment)
05101| durgarāṣṭrayoḥ kaṇṭakaśodhanamuktam ||
05102| rājarājyayorvakṣyāmaḥ ||
05103| rājānamavagṛhyopajīvinaḥ śatrusādhāraṇā vā ye mukhyāsteṣu gūḍhapuruṣapraṇidhiḥ kṛtyapakṣopagraho vā siddhiḥ yathoktaṃ purastād, upajāpoapasarpo vā yathā pāragrāmike vakṣyāmaḥ ||
05104| rājyopaghātinastu vallabhāḥ saṃhatā vā ye mukhyāḥ prakāśamaśakyāḥ pratiṣeddhuṃ dūṣyāḥ teṣu dharmarucirupāṃśudaṇḍaṃ prayuñjīta ||
05105| dūṣyamahāmātrabhrātaramasatkṛtaṃ sattrī protsāhya rājānaṃ darśayet ||
05106| taṃ rājā dūṣyadravyopabhogātisargeṇa dūṣye vikramayet ||
05107| śastreṇa rasena vā vikrāntaṃ tatraiva ghātayed"bhrātṛghātakoayam" iti ||
05108| tena pāraśavaḥ paricārikāputraśca vyākhyātau ||
05109| dūṣyaṃmahāmātraṃ vā sattriprotsāhito bhrātā dāyaṃ yāceta ||
05110| taṃ dūṣyagṛhapratidvāri rātrāvupaśayānamanyatra vā vasantaṃ tīkṣṇo hantā brūyād"hatoayaṃ dāyakāmukaḥ" iti ||
05111| tato hatapakṣamupagṛhyetaraṃ nigṛhṇīyāt ||
05112| dūṣyasamīpasthā vā sattriṇo bhrātaraṃ dāyaṃ yācamānaṃ ghātena paribhartsayeyuḥ ||
05113| taṃ rātrau iti samānam ||
05114| dūṣyamahāmātrayorvā yaḥ putraḥ pituḥ pitā vā putrasya dārānadhicarati, bhrātā vā bhrātuḥ, tayoḥ kāpaṭikamukhaḥ kalahaḥ pūrveṇa vyākhyātaḥ ||
05115| dūṣyamahāmātraputramātmasaṃbhāvitaṃ vā sattrī "rājaputrastvam, śatrubhayādiha nyastoasi" ityupajapet ||
05116| pratipannaṃ rājā rahasi pūjayet"prāptayauvarājyakālaṃ tvāṃ mahāmātrabhayānnābhiṣiñcāmi" iti ||
05117| taṃ sattrī mahāmātravadhe yojayet ||
05118| vikrāntaṃ tatraiva ghātayet"pitṛghātakoayam" iti ||
05119| bhikṣukī vā dūṣyabhāryāṃ sāṃvadanikībhirauṣadhībhiḥ saṃvāsya rasenātisaṃdadhyāt ||
05120| ityāpyaprayogaḥ ||
05121| dūṣyamahāmātramaṭavīṃ paragrāmaṃ vā hantuṃ kāntāravyavahite vā deśe rāṣṭrapālamantapālaṃ vā sthāpayituṃ nāgarasthānaṃ vā kupitamavagrāhituṃ sārthātivāhyaṃ pratyante vā sa-pratyādeyamādātuṃ phalgubalaṃ tīkṣṇayuktaṃ preṣayet ||
05122| rātrau divā vā yuddhe pravṛtte tīkṣṇāḥ pratirodhakavyañjanā vā hanyuḥ "abhiyoge hataḥ" iti ||
05123| yātrāvihāragato vā dūṣyamahāmātrān darśanāyāhvayet ||
05124| te gūḍhaśastraistīkṣṇaiḥ saha praviṣṭā madhyamakakṣyāyāmātmavicayamantaḥpraveśanārthaṃ dadyuḥ ||
05125| tato dauvārikābhigṛhītāstīkṣṇāḥ "dūṣyaprayuktāḥ sma" iti brūyuḥ ||
05126| te tadabhivikhyāpya dūṣyān hanyuḥ ||
05127| tīkṣṇasthāne cānye vadhyāḥ ||
05128| bahirvihāragato vā dūṣyānāsannāvāsān pūjayet ||
05129| teṣāṃ devīvyañjanā vā duḥstrī rātrāvāvāseṣu gṛhyeteti samānaṃ pūrveṇa ||
05130| dūṣyamahāmātraṃ vā "sūdo bhakṣakāro vā te śobhanaḥ" iti stavena bhakṣyabhojyaṃ yāceta, bahirvā kvacidadhvagataḥ pānīyam ||
05131| tadubhayaṃ rasena yojayitvā pratisvādane tāvevopayojayet ||
05132| tadabhivikhyāpya "rasadau" iti ghātayet ||
05133| abhicāraśīlaṃ vā siddhavyañjano "godhākūrmakarkaṭakakūṭānāṃ lakṣaṇyānāmanyatamaprāśanena manorathānavāpsyasi" iti grāhayet ||
05134| pratipannaṃ karmaṇi rasena lohamusalairvā ghātayet"karmavyāpadā hataḥ" iti ||
05135| cikitsakavyañjano vā daurātmikamasādhyaṃ vā vyādhiṃ dūṣyasya sthāpayitvā bhaiṣajyāhārayogeṣu rasenātisaṃdadhyāt ||
05136| sūdārālikavyañjanā vā praṇihitā dūṣyaṃ rasenātisaṃdadhyuḥ ||
05137| ityupaniṣatpratiṣedhaḥ ||
05138| ubhayadūṣyapratiṣedhastu ||
05139| yatra dūṣyaḥ pratiṣeddhavyastatra dūṣyameva phalgubalatīkṣṇayuktaṃ preṣayet, gaccha, amuṣmin durge rāṣṭre vā sainyamutthāpaya hiraṇyaṃ vā, vallabhādvā hiraṇyamāhāraya, vallabhakanyāṃ vā prasahyānaya, durgasetuvaṇikpathaśūnyaniveśakhanidravyahastivanakarmaṇāmanyatamadvā kāraya rāṣṭrapālyamantapālyaṃ vā yaśca tvā pratiṣedhayen
na vā te sāhāyyaṃ dadyātsa bandhavyaḥ syāt" iti ||
05140| tathaivetareṣāṃ preṣayed"amuṣyāvinayaḥ pratiṣeddhavyaḥ" iti ||
05141| tameteṣu kalahasthāneṣu karmapratighāteṣu vā vivadamānaṃ tīkṣṇāḥ śastraṃ pātayitvā pracchannaṃ hanyuḥ ||
05142| tena doṣeṇetare niyantavyāḥ ||
05143| purāṇāṃ grāmāṇāṃ kulānāṃ vā dūṣyāṇāṃ sīmākṣetrakhalaveśmamaryādāsu dravyopakaraṇasasyavāhanahiṃsāsu prekṣākṛtyotsaveṣu vā samutpanne kalahe tīkṣṇairutpādite vā tīkṣṇāḥ śastraṃ pātayitvā brūyuḥ "evaṃ kriyante yemunā kalahāyante: iti ||
05144| tena doṣeṇetare niyantavyāḥ ||
05145| yeṣāṃ vā dūṣyāṇāṃ jātamūlāḥ kalahāsteṣāṃ kṣetrakhalaveśmānyādīpayitvā bandhusaṃbandhiṣu vāhaneṣu vā tīkṣṇāḥ śastraṃ pātayitvā tathaiva brūyuḥ "amunā prayuktāḥ smaḥ" iti ||
04446| tena doṣeṇetare niyantavyāḥ ||
05147| durgarāṣṭradūṣyān vā sattriṇaḥ parasparasyāveśanikān kārayeyuḥ ||
05148| tatra rasadā rasaṃ dadyuḥ ||
05149| tena doṣeṇetare niyantavyāḥ ||
05150| bhikṣukī vā dūṣyarāṣṭramukhyaṃ "dūṣyarāṣṭramukhyasya bhāryā snuṣā duhitā vā kāmayate" ityupajapet ||
05151| pratipannasyābharaṇamādāya svāmine darśayet"asau te mukhyo yauvanotsikto bhāryāṃ snuṣāṃ duhitaraṃ vābhimanyate" iti ||
05152| tayoḥ kalaho rātrau iti samānam ||
05153| dūṣyadaṇḍopanateṣu tu - yuvarājaḥ senāpatirvā kiṃcidapakṛtyāpakrānto vikrameta ||
05154| tato rājā dūṣyadaṇḍopanatāneva preṣayetphalgubalatīkṣṇayuktāniti samānāḥ sarva eva yogāḥ ||
05155| teṣāṃ ca putreṣvanukṣiyatsu yo nirvikāraḥ sa pitṛdāyaṃ labheta ||
05156| evamasya putrapautrānanuvartate rājyamapāstapuruṣadoṣam ||
05157  svapakṣe parapakṣe vā tūṣṇīṃ daṇḍaṃ prayojayet |
05157  āyatyāṃ ca tadātve ca kṣamāvānaviśaṅkitaḥ ||E

(ṛeplenishmentof the treasury)
05201| kośamakośaḥ pratyutpannārthakṛcchraḥ saṃgṛhṇīyāt ||
05202| janapadaṃ mahāntamalpapramāṇaṃ vādevamātṛkaṃ prabhūtadhānyaṃ dhānyasyāṃśaṃ tṛtīyaṃ caturthaṃ vā yāceta, yathāsāraṃ madhyamavaraṃ vā ||
05203| durgasetukarmavaṇikpathaśūnyaniveśakhanidravyahastivanakarmopakāriṇaṃ pratyantamalpapramāṇaṃ vā na yāceta ||
05204| dhānyapaśuhiraṇyādi niviśamānāya dadyāt ||
05205| caturthamaṃśaṃ dhānyānāṃ bījabhaktaśuddhaṃ ca hiraṇyena krīṇīyāt ||
05206| araṇyajātaṃ śrotriyasvaṃ ca pariharet ||
05207| tadapyanugraheṇa krīṇīyāt ||
05208| tasyākaraṇe vā samāhartṛpuruṣā grīṣme karṣakāṇāmudvāpaṃ kārayeyuḥ ||
05209| pramādāvaskannasyātyayaṃ dviguṇamudāharanto bījakāle bījalekhyaṃ kuryuḥ ||
05210| niṣpanne haritapakvādānaṃ vārayeyuḥ, anyatra śākakaṭabhaṅgamuṣṭibhyāṃ devapitṛpūjādānārthaṃ gavārthaṃ vā ||
05211| bhikṣukagrāmabhṛtakārthaṃ ca rāśimūlaṃ parihareyuḥ ||
05212| svasasyāpahāriṇaḥ pratipātoaṣṭaguṇaḥ ||
05213| parasasyāpahāriṇaḥ pañcāśadguṇaḥ sītātyayaḥ, svavargasya, bāhyasya tu vadhaḥ ||
05214| caturthamaṃśaṃ dhānyānāṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vanyānāṃ tūlalākṣākṣaumavalkakārpāsaraumakauśeyakauṣadhagandhapuṣpaphalaśākapaṇyānāṃ kāṣṭhaveṇumāṃsavallūrāṇāṃ ca gṛhṇīyuḥ, dantājinasyārdham ||
05215| tadanisṛṣṭaṃ vikrīṇānasya pūrvaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ ||
05216| iti karṣakeṣu praṇayaḥ ||
05217| suvarṇarajatavajramaṇimuktāpravālāśvahastipaṇyāḥ pañcāśatkarāḥ ||
05218| sūtravastratāmravṛttakaṃsagandhabhaiṣajyaśīdhupaṇyāścatvāriṃśatkarāḥ ||
05219| dhānyarasalohapaṇyāḥ śakaṭavyavahāriṇaśca triṃśatkarāḥ ||
05220| kācavyavahāriṇo mahākāravaśca viṃśatikarāḥ ||
05221| kṣudrakāravo bandhakīpoṣakāśca daśakarāḥ ||
05222| kāṣṭhaveṇupāṣāṇamṛdbhāṇḍapakvānnaharitapaṇyāḥ pañcakarāḥ ||
05223| kuśīlavā rūpājīvāśca vetanārdhaṃ dadyuḥ ||
05224| hiraṇyakaraṃ karmaṇyānāhārayeyuḥ, na caiṣāṃ kaṃcidaparādhaṃ parihareyuḥ ||
05225| te hyaparigṛhītamabhinīya vikrīṇīran ||
05226| iti vyavahāriṣu praṇayaḥ ||05227| kukkuṭasūkaramardhaṃ dadyāt, kṣudrapaśavaḥ ṣaḍbhāgam, gomahiṣāśvatarakharoṣṭrāśca daśabhāgam ||
05228| bandhakīpoṣakā rājapreṣyābhiḥ paramarūpayauvanābhiḥ kośaṃ saṃhareyuḥ ||
05229| iti yonipoṣakeṣu praṇayaḥ ||
05230| sakṛdeva na dviḥ prayojyaḥ ||
05231| tasyākaraṇe vā samāhartā kāryamapadiśya paurajānapadān bhikṣeta ||
05232| yogapuruṣāścātra pūrvamatimātraṃ dadyuḥ ||
05233| etena pradeśena rājā paurajānapadān bhikṣeta ||
05234| kāpaṭikāścainānalpaṃ prayacchataḥ kutsayeyuḥ ||
05235| sārato vā hiraṇyamāḍhyān yāceta, yathopakāraṃ vā, svavaśā vā yadupahareyuḥ ||
05236| sthānacchatraveṣṭanavibhūṣāścaiṣāṃ hiraṇyena prayacchet ||
05237| pāṣaṇḍasaṃghadravyamaśrotriyopabhogyaṃ devadravyaṃ vā kṛtyakarāḥ pretasya dagdhagṛhasya vā haste nyastamityupahareyuḥ ||
05238| devatādhyakṣo durgarāṣṭradevatānāṃ yathāsvamekasthaṃ kośaṃ kuryāt, tathaiva copaharet ||
05239| daivatacaityaṃ siddhapuṇyasthānamaupapādikaṃ vā rātrāvutthāpya yātrāsamājābhyāmājīvet ||
05240| caityopavanavṛkṣeṇa vā devatābhigamanamanārtavapuṣpaphalayuktena khyāpayet ||
05241| manuṣyakaraṃ vā vṛkṣe rakṣobhayaṃ prarūpayitvā siddhavyañjanāḥ paurajānapadānāṃ hiraṇyena pratikuryuḥ ||
05242| suruṅgāyukte vā kūpe nāgamaniyataśiraskaṃ hiraṇyopahāreṇa darśayet ||
05243| nāgapratimāyāmantaśchannāyāṃ caityacchidre valmīkacchidre vā sarpadarśanamāhāreṇa pratibaddhasaṃjñaṃ kṛtvā śraddadhānānāṃ darśayet ||
05244| aśraddadhānānāmācamanaprokṣaṇeṣu rasamupacārya devatābhiśāpaṃ brūyāt, abhityaktaṃ vā daṃśayitvā ||
05245| yogadarśanapratīkāreṇa vā kośābhisaṃharaṇaṃ kuryāt ||
05246| vaidehakavyañjano vā prabhūtapaṇyāntevāsī vyavahareta ||
05247| sa yadā paṇyamūlye nikṣepaprayogairupacitaḥ syāttadainaṃ rātrau moṣayet ||
05248| etena rūpadarśakaḥ suvarṇakāraśca vyākhyātau ||
05249| vaidehakavyañjano vā prakhyātavyavahāraḥ prahavaṇanimittaṃ yācitakamavakrītakaṃ vā rūpyasuvarṇabhāṇḍamanekaṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||
05250| samāje vā sarvapaṇyasaṃdohena prabhūtaṃ hiraṇyasuvarṇamṛṇaṃ gṛhṇīyāt, pratibhāṇḍamūlyaṃ ca ||
05251| tadubhayaṃ rātrau moṣayet ||
05252| sādhvīvyañjanābhiḥ strībhirdūṣyānunmādayitvā tāsāmeva veśmasvabhigṛhya sarvasvānyāhareyuḥ ||
05253| dūṣyakulyānāṃ vā vivāde pratyutpanne rasadāḥ praṇihitā rasaṃ dadyuḥ ||
05254| tena doṣeṇetare paryādātavyāḥ ||
05255| dūṣyamabhityakto vā śraddheyāpadeśaṃ paṇyaṃ hiraṇyanikṣepamṛṇaprayogaṃ dāyaṃ vā yāceta ||
05256| dāsaśabdena vā dūṣyamālambeta, bhāryāmasya snuṣāṃ duhitaraṃ vā dāsīśabdena bhāryāśabdena vā ||
05257| taṃ dūṣyagṛhapratidvāri rātrāvupaśayānamanyatra vā vasantaṃ tīkṣṇo hatvā brūyāt"hatoayamarthakāmukaḥ" iti ||
05258| tena doṣeṇetare paryādātavyāḥ ||
05259| siddhavyañjano vā dūṣyaṃ jambhakavidyābhiḥ pralobhayitvā brūyāt"akṣayahiraṇyaṃ rājadvārikaṃ strīhṛdayamarivyādhikaramāyuṣyaṃ putrīyaṃ vā karma jānāmi" iti ||
05260| pratipannaṃ caityasthāne rātrau prabhūtasurāmāṃsagandhamupahāraṃ kārayet ||
05261| ekarūpaṃ cātra hiraṇyaṃ pūrvanikhātaṃ pretāṅgaṃ pretaśiśurvā yatra nihitaḥ syāt, tato hiraṇyamasya darśayed"atyalpam" iti ca brūyāt ||
05262| "prabhūtahiraṇyahetoḥ punarupahāraḥ kartavya iti svayamevaitena hiraṇyena śvobhūte prabhūtamaupahārikaṃ krīṇīhi" iti ||
05263| sa tena hiraṇyenāupahārikakraye gṛhyeta ||
05264| mātṛvyañjanayā vā "putro me tvayā hataḥ" ityavakupitā syāt ||
05265| saṃsiddhamevāsya rātriyāge vanayāge vanakrīḍāyāṃ vā pravṛttāyāṃ tīkṣṇā viśasyābhityaktamatinayeyuḥ ||
05266| dūṣyasya vā bhṛtakavyañjano vetanahiraṇye kūṭarūpaṃ prakṣipya prarūpayet ||
05267| karmakaravyañjano vā gṛhe karma kurvāṇaḥ stenakūṭarūpakārakopakaraṇamupanidadhyāt, cikitsakavyañjano vā garamagadāpadeśena ||
05268| pratyāsanno vā dūṣyasya sattrī praṇihitamabhiṣekabhāṇḍamamitraśāsanaṃ ca kāpaṭikamukhenācakṣīta, kāraṇaṃ ca brūyāt ||
05269| evaṃ dūṣyeṣvadhārmikeṣu ca varteta, netareṣu ||
05270  pakvaṃ pakvamivārāmātphalaṃ rājyādavāpnuyāt |
05270  ātmacchedabhayādāmaṃ varjayetkopakārakam ||E

(ṣalariesof state servants)
05301| durgajanapadaśaktyā bhṛtyakarma samudayapādena sthāpayet, kāryasādhanasahena vā bhṛtyalābhena ||
05302| śarīramavekṣeta, na dharmārthau pīḍayet ||
05303| ṛtvigācāryamantripurohitasenāpatiyuvarājarājamātṛrājamahiṣyoaṣṭacatvāriṃśatsāhasrāḥ ||
05304| etāvatā bharaṇenānāspadyatvamakopakaṃ caiṣāṃ bhavati ||
05305| dauvārikāntarvaṃśikapraśāstṛsamāhartṛsaṃnidhātāraścaturviṃśatisāhasrāḥ ||
05306| etāvatā karmaṇyā bhavanti ||
05307| kumārakumāramātṛnāyakapauravyāvahārikakārmāntikamantripariṣadrāṣṭrāntapālāśca dvādaśasāhasrāḥ ||
05308| svāmiparibandhabalasahāyā hyetāvatā bhavanti ||
05309| śreṇīmukhyā hastyaśvarathamukhyāḥ pradeṣṭāraścāṣṭasāhasrāḥ ||
05310| svavargānukarṣiṇo hyetāvatā bhavanti ||
05311| pattyaśvarathahastyadhyakṣā dravyahastivanapālāśca catuḥsāhasrāḥ ||
05312| rathikānīkasthacikitsakāśvadamakavardhakayo yonipoṣakāśca dvisāhasrāḥ ||
05313| kārtāntikanaimittikamauhūrtikapaurāṇikasūtamāgadhāḥ purohitapuruṣāḥ sarvādhyakṣāśca sāhasrāḥ ||
05314| śilpavantaḥ pādātāḥ saṃkhyāyakalekhakādivargaśca pañcaśatāḥ ||
05315| kuśīlavāstvardhatṛtīyaśatāḥ, dviguṇavetanāścaiṣāṃ tūryakarāḥ ||
05316| kāruśilpino viṃśatiśatikāḥ ||
05317| catuṣpadadvipadaparicārakapārikarmikāupasthāyikapālakaviṣṭibandhakāḥ ṣaṣṭivetanāḥ, āryayuktārohakamāṇavakaśailakhanakāḥ sarvopasthāyinaśca ||
05318| ācāryā vidyāvantaśca pūjāvetanāni yathārhaṃ labheran pañcaśatāvaraṃ sahasraparam ||
05319| daśapaṇiko yojane dūto madhyamaḥ, daśottare dviguṇavetana āyojanaśatāditi ||
05320| samānavidyebhyastriguṇavetano rājā rājasūyādiṣu kratuṣu ||
05321| rājñaḥ sārathiḥ sāhasraḥ ||
05322| kāpaṭikodāsthitagṛhapatikavaidehakatāpasavyañjanāḥ sāhasrāḥ ||
05323| grāmabhṛtakasattritīkṣṇarasadabhikṣukyaḥ pañcaśatāḥ ||
05324| cārasaṃcāriṇoardhatṛtīyaśatāḥ, prayāsavṛddhavetanā vā ||
05325| śatavargasahasravargāṇāmadhyakṣā bhaktavetanalābhamādeśaṃ vikṣepaṃ ca kuryuḥ ||
05326| avikṣepo rājaparigrahadurgarāṣṭrarakṣāvekṣaṇeṣu ca ||
05327| nityamukhyāḥ syuranekamukhyāśca ||
05328| karmasu mṛtānāṃ putradārā bhaktavetanaṃ labheran ||
05329| bālavṛddhavyādhitāścaiṣāmanugrāhyāḥ ||
05330| pretavyādhitasūtikākṛtyeṣu caiṣāmarthamānakarma kuryāt ||
05331| alpakośaḥ kupyapaśukṣetrāṇi dadyāt, alpaṃ ca hiraṇyam ||
05332| śūnyaṃ vā niveśayitumabhyutthito hiraṇyameva dadyāt, na grāmaṃ grāmasaṃjātavyavahārasthāpanārtham ||
05333| etena bhṛtānāmabhṛtānāṃ ca vidyākarmabhyāṃ bhaktavetanaviśeṣaṃ ca kuryāt ||
05334| ṣaṣṭivetanasyāḍhakaṃ kṛtvā hiraṇyānurūpaṃ bhaktaṃ kuryāt ||
05335| pattyaśvarathadvipāḥ sūryodaye bahiḥ saṃdhidivasavarjaṃ śilpayogyāḥ kuryuḥ ||
05336| teṣu rājā nityayuktaḥ syāt, abhīkṣṇaṃ caiṣāṃ śilpadarśanaṃ kuryāt ||
05337| kṛtanarendrāṅkaṃ śastrāvaraṇamāyudhāgāraṃ praveśayet ||
05338| aśastrāścareyuḥ, anyatra mudrānujñātāt ||
05339| naṣṭaṃvinaṣṭaṃ vā dviguṇaṃ dadyāt ||
05340| vidhvastagaṇanāṃ ca kuryāt ||
05341| sārthikānāṃ śastrāvaraṇamantapālā gṛhṇīyuḥ, samudramavacārayeyurvā ||
05342| yātrāmabhyutthito vā senāmudyojayet ||
05343| tato vaidehakavyañjanāḥ sarvapaṇyānyāyudhīyebhyo yātrākāle dviguṇapratyādeyāni dadyuḥ ||
05344| evaṃ rājapaṇyayogavikrayo vetanapratyādānaṃ ca bhavati ||
05345| evamavekṣitāyavyayaḥ kośadaṇḍavyasanaṃ nāvāpnoti ||
05346| iti bhaktavetanavikalpaḥ ||
05347  sattriṇaścāyudhīyānāṃ veśyāḥ kārukuśīlavāḥ |
05347  daṇḍavṛddhāśca jānīyuḥ śaucāśaucamatandritāḥ ||E

(ড়roperconductfora dependant)
05401| lokayātrāvidrājānamātmadravyaprakṛtisaṃpannaṃ priyahitadvāreṇāśrayeta ||
05402| yaṃ vā manyeta "yathāhamāśrayepsurevamasau vinayepsurābhigāmikaguṇayuktaḥ" iti, dravyaprakṛtihīnamapyenamāśrayeta, na tvevānātmasaṃpannam ||
05403| anātmavā hi nītiśāstradveṣādanarthyasaṃyogādvā prāpyāpi mahadaiśvaryaṃ na bhavati ||
05404| ātmavati labdhāvakāśaḥ śāstrānuyogaṃ dadyāt ||
05405| avisaṃvādāddhi sthānasthairyamavāpnoti ||
05406| matikarmasu pṛṣṭhastadātve cāyatyāṃ ca dharmārthasaṃyuktaṃ samarthaṃ pravīṇavadapariṣadbhīruḥ kathayet ||
05407| īpsitaḥ paṇeta "dharmārthānuyogamaviśiṣṭeṣu balavatsaṃyukteṣu daṇḍadhāraṇaṃ matsaṃyoge tadātve ca daṇḍadhāraṇamiti na kuryāḥ, pakṣaṃ vṛttiṃ guhyaṃ ca me nopahanyāḥ, saṃjñayā ca tvāṃ kāmakrodhadaṇḍaneṣu vārayeyam" iti ||
05408| ādiṣṭaḥ pradiṣṭāyāṃ bhūmāvanujñātaḥ praviśet, upaviśecca pārśvataḥ saṃnikṛṣṭaviprakṛṣṭaḥ parāsanam ||
05409| vigṛhya kathanamasabhyamapratyakṣamaśraddheyamanṛtaṃ ca vākyamuccairanarmaṇi hāsaṃ vātaṣṭhīvane ca śabdavatī na kuryāt ||
05410| mithaḥ kathanamanyena, janavāde dvandvakathanam, rājño veṣamuddhatakuhakānāṃ ca, ratnātiśayaprakāśābhyarthanam, ekākṣyoṣṭhanirbhogaṃ bhrukuṭīkarma vākyāvakṣepaṇaṃ ca bruvati, balavatsaṃyuktavirodham, strībhiḥ strīdarśibhiḥ sāmantadūtairdveṣyapakṣāvakṣiptānarthyaiśca pratisaṃsargamekārthacaryāṃ saṃghātaṃ ca varjayet
||
05411  ahīnakālaṃ rājārthaṃ svārthaṃ priyahitaiḥ saha |
05411  parārthaṃ deśakāle ca brūyāddharmārthasaṃhitam ||
05412  pṛṣṭaḥ priyahitaṃ brūyānna brūyādahitaṃ priyam |
05412  apriyaṃ vā hitaṃ brūyātśṛṇvatoanumato mithaḥ ||
05413  tūṣṇīṃ vā prativākye syādveṣyādīṃśca na varṇayet |
05413  apriyā api dakṣāḥ syustadbhāvādye bahiṣkṛtāḥ ||
05414  anarthyāśca priyā dṛṣṭāścittajñānānuvartinaḥ |
05414  abhihāsyeṣvabhihasedghorahāsāṃśca varjayet ||E
05415  parātsaṃkrāmayedghoraṃ na ca ghoraṃ pare vadet |
05415  titikṣetātmanaścaiva kṣamāvān pṛthivīsamaḥ ||
05416  ātmarakṣā hi satataṃ pūrvaṃ kāryā vijānatā |
05416  agnāviva hi saṃproktā vṛttī rājopajīvinām ||
05417  ekadeśaṃ dahedagniḥ śarīraṃ vā paraṃ gataḥ |
05417  sa-putradāraṃ rājā tu ghātayedardhayeta vā ||E

(ড়roperbehaviourfora courtier)
05501| niyuktaḥ karmasu vyayaviśuddhamudayaṃ darśayet ||
05502| ābhyantaraṃ bāhyaṃ guhyaṃ prakāśyamātyayikamupekṣitavyaṃ vā kāryaṃ "idamevam" iti viśeṣayecca ||
05503| mṛgayādyūtamadyastrīṣu prasaktaṃ nainamanuvarteta praśaṃsābhiḥ ||
05504| āsannaścāsya vyasanopaghāte prayateta, paropajāpātisaṃdhānopadhibhyaśca rakṣet ||
05505| iṅgitākārau cāsya lakṣayet ||
05506| kāmadveṣaharṣadainyavyavasāyabhayadvandvaviparyāsamiṅgitākārābhyāṃ hi mantrasaṃvaraṇārthamācarati prājñaḥ ||
05507| darśane prasīdati, vākyaṃ pratigṛhṇāti, āsanaṃ dadāti, vivikto darśayate, śaṅkāsthāne nātiśaṅkate, kathāyāṃ ramate, parijñāpyeṣvavekṣate, pathyamuktaṃ sahate, smayamāno niyuṅkte, hastena spṛśati, ślāghye nopahasati, parokṣaṃ guṇaṃ bravīti, bhakṣyeṣu smarati, saha vihāraṃ yāti, vyasanebhyupapadyate, tadbhaktīn pūjayati, guhyamācaṣṭe,
mānaṃ vardhayati, arthaṃ karoti, anarthaṃ pratihanti - iti tuṣṭajñānam ||
05508| etadeva viparītamatuṣṭasya, bhūyaśca vakṣyāmaḥ ||
05509| saṃdarśane kopaḥ, vākyasyāśravaṇapratiṣedhau, āsanacakṣuṣoradānam, varṇasvarabhedaḥ, ekākṣibhrukuṭyoṣṭhanirbhogaḥ, svedaśvāsasmitānāmasthānotpattiḥ, paramantraṇam, akasmādvrajanam, vardhanamanyasya, bhūmigātravilekhanam, anyasyopatodanam, vidyāvarṇadeśakutsā, samadoṣanindā, pratidoṣanindā, pratilomastavaḥ,
sukṛtānavekṣaṇam, duṣkṛtānukīrtanam, pṛṣṭhāvadhānam, atityāgaḥ, mithyābhibhāṣaṇam, rājadarśināṃ ca tadvṛttānyatvam ||
05510| vṛttivikāraṃ cāvekṣetāpyamānuṣāṇām ||
05511| "ayamuccaiḥ siñcati" iti kātyāyanaḥ pravavrāja, "krauñcoapasavyam" iti kaṇiṅko bhāradvājaḥ, "tṛṇam" iti dīrghaścārāyaṇaḥ, "śītā śāṭī" iti ghoṭamukhaḥ, "hastī pratyaukṣīt" iti kiñjalkaḥ, "rathāśvaṃ prāśaṃsīt" iti piśunaḥ, pratiravaṇe śunaḥ piśunaputraḥ ||
05512| arthamānāvakṣepe ca parityāgaḥ ||
05513| svāmiśīlamātmanaśca kilbiṣamupalabhya vā pratikurvīta ||
05514| mitramupakṛṣṭaṃ vāsya gacchet ||
05515  tatrastho doṣanirghātaṃ mitrairbhartari cācaret |
05515  tato bhartari jīve vā mṛte vā punarāvrajet ||E

(Continuance of the kingdom)
(Continuoussovereignty)
05601| rājavyasanamevamamātyaḥ pratikurvīta ||
05602| prāgeva maraṇābādhabhayādrājñaḥ priyahitopagraheṇa māsadvimāsāntaraṃ darśanaṃ sthāpayed"deśapīḍāpahamamitrāpahamāyuṣyaṃ putrīyaṃ vā karma rājā sādhayati" ityapadeśena ||
05603| rājavyañjanamarūpavelāyāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ darśayet, mitrāmitradūtānāṃ ca ||
05604| taiśca yathocitāṃ saṃbhāṣāmamātyamukho gacchet ||
05605| dauvārikāntarvaṃśikamukhaśca yathoktaṃ rājapraṇidhimanuvartayet ||
05606| apakāriṣu ca heḍaṃ prasādaṃ vā prakṛtikāntaṃ darśayet, prasādamevopakāriṣu ||
05607| āptapuruṣādhiṣṭhitau durgapratyantasthau vā kośadaṇḍāvekasthau kārayet, kulyakumāramukhyāṃścānyāpadeśena ||
05608| yaśca mukhyaḥ pakṣavān durgāṭavīstho vā vaiguṇyaṃ bhajeta tamupagrāhayet ||
05609| bahvābādhaṃ vā yātrāṃ preṣayet, mitrakulaṃ vā ||
05610| yasmācca sāmantādābādhaṃ paśyettamutsavavivāhahastibandhanāśvapaṇyabhūmipradānāpadeśenāvagrāhayet, svamitreṇa vā ||
05611| tataḥ saṃdhimadūṣyaṃ kārayet ||
05612| āṭavikāmitrairvā vairaṃ grāhayet ||
05613| tatkulīnamaparuddhaṃ vā bhūṃyekadeśenopagrāhayet ||
05614| kulyakumāramukhyopagrahaṃ kṛtvā vā kumāramabhiṣiktameva darśayet ||
05615| dāṇḍakarmikavadvā rājyakaṇṭakānuddhṛtya rājyaṃ kārayet ||
05616| yadi vā kaścinmukhyaḥ sāmantādīnāmanyatamaḥ kopaṃ bhajeta taṃ "ehi, rājānaṃ tvā kariṣyāmi" ityāvāhayitvā ghātayet ||
05617| āpatpratīkāreṇa vā sādhayet ||
05618| yuvarāje vā krameṇa rājyabhāramāropya rājavyasanaṃ khyāpayet ||
05619| parabhūmau rājavyasane mitreṇāmitravyañjanena śatroḥ saṃdhimavasthāpyāpagacchet ||
05620| sāmantādīnāmanyatamaṃ vāsya durge sthāpayitvāpagacchet ||
05621| kumāramabhiṣicya vā prativyūheta ||
05622| pareṇābhiyukto vā yathoktamāpatpratīkāraṃ kuryāt ||
05623| evamekāiśvaryamamātyaḥ kārayediti kauṭilyaḥ ||
05624| "naivam" iti bhāradvājaḥ ||
05625| "pramriyamāṇe vā rājanyamātyaḥ kulyakumāramukhyān parasparaṃ mukhyeṣu vā vikramayet ||
05626| vikrāntaṃ prakṛtikopena ghātayet ||
05627| kulyakumāramukhyānupāṃśudaṇḍena vā sādhayitvā svayaṃ rājyaṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||
05628| rājyakāraṇāddhi pitā putrān putrāśca pitaramabhidruhyanti, kimaṅga punaramātyaprakṛtirhyekapragraho rājyasya ||
05629| tatsvayamupasthitaṃ nāvamanyeta ||
05630| "svayamārūḍhā hi strī tyajyamānābhiśapati" iti lokapravādaḥ ||
05631  kālaśca sakṛdabhyeti yaṃ naraṃ kālakāṅkṣiṇam |
05631  durlabhaḥ sa punastasya kālaḥ karma cikīrṣataḥ ||
05632| prakṛtikopakamadharmiṣṭhamanaikāntikaṃ caitaditi kauṭilyaḥ ||
05633| rājaputramātmasaṃpannaṃ rājye sthāpayet ||
05634| saṃpannābhāvevyasaninaṃ kumāraṃ rājakanyāṃ garbhiṇīṃ devīṃ vā puraskṛtya mahāmātrān saṃnipātya brūyāt"ayaṃ vo nikṣepaḥ, pitaramasyāvekṣadhvaṃ sattvābhijanamātmanaśca, dhvajamātroayaṃ bhavanta eva svāminaḥ, kathaṃ vā kriyatām" iti ||
05635| tathā bruvāṇaṃ yogapuruṣā brūyuḥ "koanyo bhavatpurogādasmādrājñaścāturvarṇyamarhati pālayitum" iti ||
05636| "tathā" ityamātyaḥ kumāraṃ rājakanyāṃ garbhiṇīṃ devīṃ vādhikurvīta, bandhusaṃbandhināṃ mitrāmitradūtānāṃ ca darśayet ||
05637| bhaktavetanaviśeṣamamātyānāmāyudhīyānāṃ ca kārayet, "bhūyaścāyaṃ vṛddhaḥ kariṣyati" iti brūyāt ||
05638| evaṃ durgarāṣṭramukhyānābhāṣeta, yathārhaṃ ca mitrāmitrapakṣam ||
05639| vinayakarmaṇi ca kumārasya prayateta ||
05640| kanyāyāṃ samānajātīyādapatyamutpādya vābhiṣiñcet ||
05641| mātuścittakṣobhabhayātkulyamalpasattvaṃ chātraṃ ca lakṣaṇyamupanidadhyāt ||
05642| ṛtau caināṃ rakṣet ||
05643| na cātmārthaṃ kaṃcidutkṛṣṭamupabhogaṃ kārayet ||
05644| rājārthaṃ tu yānavāhanābharaṇavastrastrīveśmaparivāpān kārayet ||
05645  yauvanasthaṃ ca yāceta viśramaṃ cittakāraṇāt |
05645  parityajedatuṣyantaṃ tuṣyantaṃ cānupālayet ||
05646  nivedya putrarakṣārthaṃ gūḍhasāraparigrahān |
05646  araṇyaṃ dīrghasattraṃ vā sevetārucyatāṃ gataḥ ||
05647  mukhyairavagṛhītaṃ vā rājānaṃ tatpriyāśritaḥ |
05647  itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ bodhayedarthaśāstravit ||
05648  siddhavyañjanarūpo vā yogamāsthāya pārthivam |
05648  labheta labdhvā dūṣyeṣu dāṇḍakarmikamācaret ||E

((maṇḍalayoniḥ ṣaṣṭhamadhikaraṇam))
(ṣaṇṇavatitamaṃ prakaraṇaṃ - prakṛtisampadah)
06101| svāmyamātyajanapadadurgakośadaṇḍamitrāṇi prakṛtayaḥ ||
06102| tatra svāmisampat ||
06103| mahākulīno daivabuddhisattvasampanno vṛddhadarśī dhārmikaḥ satyavāgavisaṃvādakaḥ kṛtajñaḥ sthūlalakṣo mahotsāhoadīrghasūtraḥ śakyasāmanto dṛḍhabuddhirakṣudrapariṣatko vinayakāma ityābhigāmikā guṇāḥ ||
06104| śuśrūṣāśravaṇagrahaṇadhāraṇavijñānohāpohatattvābhiniveśāḥ prajñāguṇāḥ ||
06105| śauryamamarṣaḥ śīghratā dākṣyaṃ cotsāhaguṇāḥ ||
06106| vāgmī pragalbhaḥ smṛtimatibalavānudagraḥ svavagrahaḥ kṛtaśilpoavyasano daṇḍanāyyupakārāpakārayordṛṣṭapratīkārī hrīmānāpatprakṛtyorviniyoktā dīrghadūradarśī deśakālapuruṣakārakāryapradhānaḥ saṃdhivikramatyāgasamyamapaṇaparacchidravibhāgī saṃvṛtoadīnābhihāsyajihmabhrukuṭīkṣaṇaḥ
kāmakrodhalobhastambhacāpalopatāpapaiśunyahīnaḥ śaklaḥ smitodagrābhibhāṣī vṛddhopadeśācāra ityātmasampat ||
06107| amātyasampaduktā purastāt ||
06108| madhye cānte ca sthānavānātmadhāraṇaḥ paradhāraṇaścāpadi svārakṣaḥ svājīvaḥ śatrudveṣī śakyasāmantaḥ paṅkapāṣāṇoṣaraviṣamakaṇṭakaśreṇīvyālamṛgāṭavīhīnaḥ kāntaḥ sītākhanidravyahastivanavān gavyaḥ pauruṣeyo guptagocaraḥ paśumānadevamātṛko vāristhalapathābhyāmupetaḥ sāracitrabahupaṇyo daṇḍakarasahaḥ
karmaśīlakarṣakoabāliśasvāmyavaravarṇaprāyo bhaktaśucimanuṣya iti janapadasampat ||
06109| durgasampaduktā purastāt ||
06110| dharmādhigataḥ pūrvaiḥ svayaṃ vā hemarūpyaprāyaścitrasthūlaratnahiraṇyo dīrghāmapyāpadamanāyatiṃ saheteti kośasampat ||
06111| pitṛpaitāmaho nityo vaśyastuṣṭabhṛtaputradāraḥ pravāseṣvavisaṃvāditaḥ sarvatrāpratihato duḥkhasaho bahuyuddhaḥ sarvayuddhapraharaṇavidyāviśāradaḥ sahavṛddhikṣayikatvādadvaidhyaḥ kṣatraprāya iti daṇḍasampat ||
06112| pitṛpaitāmahaṃ nityaṃ vaśyamadvaidhyaṃ mahallaghusamutthamiti mitrasampat ||
06113| arājabījī lubdhaḥ kṣudrapariṣatko viraktaprakṛtiranyāyavṛttirayukto vyasanī nirutsāho daivapramāṇo yatkiṃcanakāryagatirananubandhaḥ klībo nityāpakārī cetyamitrasampat ||
06114| evaṃbhūto hi śatruḥ sukhaḥ samucchettuṃ bhavati ||
06115  arivarjāḥ prakṛtayaḥ saptaitāḥ svaguṇodayāḥ |
06115  uktāḥ pratyaṅgabhūtāstāḥ prakṛtā rājasampadaḥ ||
06116  sampādayatyasampannāḥ prakṛtīrātmavānnṛpaḥ |
06116  vivṛddhāścānuraktāśca prakṛtīrhantyanātmavān ||
06117  tataḥ sa duṣṭaprakṛtiścāturantoapyanātmavān |
06117  hanyate vā prakṛtibhiryāti vā dviṣatāṃ vaśam ||
06118  ātmavāṃstvalpadeśoapi yuktaḥ prakṛtisampadā |
06118 nayajñaḥ pṛthivīṃ kṛtsnāṃ jayatyeva na hīyate ||E

(śamavyāyāmikam)
06201| śamavyāyāmau yogakṣemayoryoniḥ ||
06202| karmārambhāṇāṃ yogārādhano vyāyāmaḥ ||
06203| karmaphalopabhogānāṃ kṣemārādhanaḥ śamaḥ ||
06204| śamavyāyāmayoryoniḥ ṣāḍguṇyam ||
06205| kṣayaḥ sthānaṃ vṛddhirityudayāstasya ||
06206| mānuṣaṃ nayāpanayau, daivamayānayau ||
06207| daivamānuṣaṃ hi karma lokaṃ yāpayati ||
06208| adṛṣṭakāritaṃ daivam ||
06209| tasminniṣṭena phalena yogoayaḥ, aniṣṭenānayaḥ ||
06210| dṛṣṭakāritaṃ mānuṣam ||
06211| tasmin yogakṣemaniṣpattirnayaḥ, vipattirapanayaḥ ||
06212| taccintyam, acintyaṃ daivam ||
06213| rājā ātmadravyaprakṛtisampanno nayasyādhiṣṭhānaṃ vijigīṣuḥ ||
06214| tasya samantato maṇḍalībhūtā bhūmyanantarā ariprakṛtiḥ ||
06215| tathaiva bhūmyekāntarā mitraprakṛtiḥ ||
06216| arisampadyuktaḥ sāmantaḥ śatruḥ, vyasanī yātavyaḥ, anapāśrayo durbalāśrayo vocchedanīyaḥ, viparyaye pīḍanīyaḥ karśanīyo vā ||
06217| ityariviśeṣāḥ ||
06218| tasmānmitramarimitraṃ mitramitramarimitramitraṃ cānantaryeṇa bhūmīnāṃ prasajyante purastāt, paścātpārṣṇigrāha ākrandaḥ pārṣṇigrāhāsāra ākrandāsāraḥ ||
06219| bhūmyanantaraḥ prakṛtimitraḥ, tulyābhijanaḥ sahajaḥ, viruddho virodhayitā vā kṛtrimaḥ ||
06220| bhūmyekāntaraṃ prakṛtimitram, mātāpitṛsambaddhaṃ sahajam, dhanajīvitahetorāśritaṃ kṛtrimam ||
06221| arivijigīṣvorbhūmyanantaraḥ saṃhatāsaṃhatayoranugrahasamartho nigrahe cāsaṃhatayormadhyamaḥ ||
06222| arivijigīṣumadhyānāṃ bahiḥ prakṛtibhyo balavattaraḥ saṃhatāsaṃhatānāmarivijigīṣumadhyamānāmanugrahasamartho nigrahe cāsaṃhatānāmudāsīnaḥ ||
06223| iti prakṛtayaḥ ||
06224| vijigīṣurmitraṃ mitramitraṃ vāsya prakṛtayastisraḥ ||
06225| tāḥ pañcabhiramātyajanapadadurgakośadaṇḍaprakṛtibhirekaikaśaḥ samyuktā maṇḍalamaṣṭādaśakaṃ bhavati ||
06226| anena maṇḍalapṛthaktvaṃ vyākhyātamarimadhyamodāsīnānām ||
06227| evaṃ caturmaṇḍalasaṃkṣepaḥ ||
06228| dvādaśa rājaprakṛtayaḥ ṣaṣṭirdravyaprakṛtayaḥ, saṃkṣepeṇa dvisaptatiḥ ||
06229| tāsāṃ yathāsvaṃ sampadaḥ ||
06230| śaktiḥ siddhiśca ||
06231| balaṃ śaktiḥ ||
06232| sukhaṃ siddhiḥ ||
06233| śaktistrividhā - jñānabalaṃ mantraśaktiḥ, kośadaṇḍabalaṃ prabhuśaktiḥ, vikramabalamutsāhaśaktiḥ ||
06234| evaṃ siddhistrividhaiva - mantraśaktisādhyā mantrasiddhiḥ, prabhuśaktisādhyā prabhusiddhiḥ, utsāhaśaktisādhyā utsāhasiddhiḥ ||
06235| tābhirabhyuccito jyāyān bhavati, apacito hīnaḥ, tulyaśaktiḥ samaḥ ||
06236| tasmātśaktiṃ siddhiṃ ca ghaṭetātmanyāveśayitum, sādhāraṇo vā dravyaprakṛtiṣvānantaryeṇa śaucavaśena vā ||
06237| dūṣyāmitrābhyāṃ vāpakraṣṭuṃ yateta ||
06238| yadi vā paśyet"amitro me śaktiyukto vāgdaṇḍapāruṣyārthadūṣaṇaiḥ prakṛtīrupahaniṣyati, siddhiyukto vā mṛgayādyūtamadyastrībhiḥ pramādaṃ gamiṣyati, sa viraktaprakṛtirupakṣīṇaḥ pramatto vā sādhyo me bhaviṣyati, vigrahābhiyukto vā sarvasaṃdohenaikasthoadurgastho vā sthāsyati, sa saṃhatasainyo mitradurgaviyuktaḥ sādhyo me bhaviṣyati,
"balavān vā rājā parataḥ śatrumucchettukāmaḥ tamucchidya māmucchindyād" iti balavatā prārthitasya me vipannakarmārambhasya vā sāhāyyaṃ dāsyati", madhyamalipsāyāṃ ca, ityevaṃādiṣu kāraṇeṣvamitrasyāpi śaktiṃ
siddhiṃ cecchet ||
06239  nemimekāntarān rājñaḥ kṛtvā cānantarānarān |
06239  nābhimātmānamāyacchennetā prakṛtimaṇḍale ||
06240  madhye hyupahitaḥ śatrurneturmitrasya cobhayoḥ |
06240  ucchedyaḥ pīḍanīyo vā balavānapi jāyate ||E

(ṣāḍguṇyasamuddeśaḥ - kṣayasthānavṛddhiniścayah)
07101| ṣāḍguṇyasya prakṛtimaṇḍalaṃ yoniḥ ||
07102| "saṃdhivigrahāsanayānasaṃśrayadvaidhībhāvāḥ ṣāḍguṇyam" ityācāryāḥ ||
07103| "dvaiguṇyam" iti vātavyādhiḥ ||
07104| "saṃdhivigrahābhyāṃ hi ṣāḍguṇyaṃ sampadyate" iti ||
07105| ṣāḍguṇyamevaitadavasthābhedāditi kauṭilyaḥ ||
07106| tatra paṇabandhaḥ saṃdhiḥ ||
07107| apakāro vigrahaḥ ||
07108| upekṣaṇamāsanam ||
07109| abhyuccayo yānam ||
07110| parārpaṇaṃ saṃśrayaḥ ||
07111| saṃdhivigrahopādānaṃ dvaidhībhāvaḥ ||
07112| iti ṣaḍguṇāḥ ||
07113| parasmāddhīyamānaḥ saṃdadhīta ||
07114| abhyuccīyamāno vigṛhṇīyāt ||
07115| "na māṃ paro nāhaṃ paramupahantuṃ śaktaḥ" ityāsīta ||
07116| guṇātiśayayukto yāyāt ||
07117| śaktihīnaḥ saṃśrayeta ||
07118| sahāyasādhye kārye dvaidhībhāvaṃ gacchet ||
07119| iti guṇāvasthāpanam ||
07120| teṣāṃ yasmin vā guṇe sthitaḥ paśyet"ihasthaḥ śakṣyāmi durgasetukarmavaṇikpathaśūnyaniveśakhanidravyahastivanakarmāṇyātmanaḥ pravartayitum, parasya caitāni karmāṇyupahantum" iti tamātiṣṭhet ||
07121| sā vṛddhiḥ ||
07122| "āśutarā me vṛddhirbhūyastarā vṛddhyudayatarā vā bhaviṣyati, viparītā parasya" iti jñātvā paravṛddhimupekṣeta ||
07123| tulyakālaphalodayāyāṃ vā vṛddhau saṃdhimupeyāt ||
07124| yasmin vā guṇe sthitaḥ svakarmaṇāmupaghātaṃ paśyennetarasya tasminna tiṣṭhet ||
07125| eṣa kṣayaḥ ||
07126| "ciratareṇālpataraṃ vṛddhyudayataraṃ vā kṣeṣye, viparītaṃ paraḥ" iti jñātvā kṣayamupekṣeta ||
07127| tulyakālaphalodaye vā kṣaye saṃdhimupeyāt ||
07128| yasmin vā guṇe sthitaḥ svakarmavṛddhiṃ kṣayaṃ vā nābhipaśyedetatsthānam ||
07129| "hrasvataraṃ vṛddhyudayataraṃ vā sthāsyāmi, viparītaṃ paraḥ" iti jñātvā sthānamupekṣeta ||
07130| "tulyakālaphalodaye vā sthāne saṃdhimupeyād" ityācāryāḥ ||
07131| naitadvibhāṣitamiti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07132  yadi vā paśyet"sandhau sthito mahāphalaiḥ svakarmabhiḥ parakarmāṇyupahaniṣyāmi, mahāphalāni vā svakarmāṇyupabhokṣye, parakarmāṇi vā, saṃdhiviśvāsena vā yogopaniṣatpraṇidhibhiḥ parakarmāṇyupahaniṣyāmi, sukhaṃ vā sānugrahaparihārasaukaryaṃ phalalābhabhūyastvena svakarmaṇāṃ parakarmayogāvahaṃ janamāsrāvayiṣyāmi - ||
07132  balinātimātreṇa vā saṃhitaḥ paraḥ svakarmopaghātaṃ prāpsyati, yena vā vigṛhīto mayāsaṃdhatte tenāsya vigrahaṃ dīrghaṃ kariṣyāmi, mayā vā saṃhitasya maddveṣiṇo janapadaṃ pīḍayiṣyati - ||
07132  paropahato vāsya janapado māmāgamiṣyati, tataḥ karmasu vṛddhiṃ prāpsyāmi, vipannakarmārambho vā viṣamasthaḥ paraḥ karmasu na me vikrameta - ||
07132  parataḥ pravṛttakarmārambho vā tābhyāṃ saṃhitaḥ karmasu vṛddhiṃ prāpsyāmi, śatrupratibaddhaṃ vā śatruṇā saṃdhiṃ kṛtvā maṇḍalaṃ bhetsyāmi - ||
07132  bhinnamavāpsyāmi, daṇḍānugraheṇa vā śatrumupagṛhya maṇḍalalipsāyāṃ vidveṣaṃ grāhayiṣyāmi, vidviṣṭaṃ tenaiva ghātayiṣyāmi" iti saṃdhinā vṛddhimātiṣṭhet ||
07133  yadi vā paśyet"āyudhīyaprāyaḥ śreṇīprāyo vā me janapadaḥ śailavananadīdurgaikadvārārakṣo vā śakṣyati parābhiyogaṃ pratihantum, viṣayānte durgamaviṣahyamapāśrito vā śakṣyāmi parakarmāṇyupahantuṃ - ||
07133  vyasanapīḍopahatotsāho vā paraḥ samprāptakarmopaghātakālaḥ, vigṛhītasyānyato vā śakṣyāmi janapadamapavāhayitum" iti vigrahe sthito vṛddhimātiṣṭhet ||
07134| yadi vā manyeta "na me śaktaḥ paraḥ karmāṇyupahantuṃ nāhaṃ tasya karmopaghātī vā, vyasanamasya, śvavarāhayoriva kalahe vā, svakarmānuṣṭhānaparo vā vardhiṣye" ityāsanena vṛddhimātiṣṭhet ||
07135| yadi vā manyeta "yānasādhyaḥ karmopaghātaḥ śatroḥ, prativihitasvakarmārakṣaścāsmi" iti yānena vṛddhimātiṣṭhet ||
07136| yadi vā manyeta "nāsmi śaktaḥ parakarmāṇyupahantum, svakarmopaghātaṃ vā trātum" iti, balavantamāśritaḥ svakarmānuṣṭhānena kṣayātsthānaṃ sthānādvṛddhiṃ cākāṅkṣeta ||
07137| yadi vā manyeta "saṃdhinaikataḥ svakarmāṇi pravartayiṣyāmi, vigraheṇaikataḥ parakarmāṇyupahaniṣyāmi" iti dvaidhībhāvena vṛddhimātiṣṭhet ||
07138  evaṃ ṣaḍbhirguṇairetaiḥ sthitaḥ prakṛtimaṇḍale |
07138  paryeṣeta kṣayātsthānaṃ sthānādvṛddhiṃ ca karmasu ||E

(saṃśrayavṛtti)
07201| saṃdhivigrahayostulyāyāṃ vṛddhau saṃdhimupeyāt ||
07202| vigrahe hi kṣayavyayapravāsapratyavāyā bhavanti ||
07203| tenāsanayānayorāsanaṃ vyākhyātam ||
07204| dvaidhībhāvasaṃśrayayordvaidhībhāvaṃ gacchet ||
07205| dvaidhībhūto hi svakarmapradhāna ātmana evopakaroti, saṃśritastu parasyopakaroti, nātmanaḥ ||
07206| yadbalaḥ sāmantastadviśiṣṭabalamāśrayet ||
07207| tadviśiṣṭabalābhāve tamevāśritaḥ kośadaṇḍabhūmīnāmanyatamenāsyopakartumadṛṣṭaḥ prayateta ||
07208| mahādoṣo hi viśiṣṭabalasamāgamo rājñām, anyatrārivigṛhītāt ||
07209| aśakye daṇḍopanatavadvarteta ||
07210| yadā cāsya prāṇaharaṃ vyādhimantaḥkopaṃ śatruvṛddhiṃ mitravyasanamupasthitaṃ vā tannimittāmātmanaśca vṛddhiṃ paśyettadā sambhāvyavyādhidharmakāryāpadeśenāpayāyāt ||
07211| svaviṣayastho vā nopagacchet ||
07212| āsanno vāsya cchidreṣu praharet ||
07213| balīyasorvā madhyagatastrāṇasamarthamāśrayeta, yasya vāntardhiḥ syāt, ubhau vā ||
07214| kapālasaṃśrayastiṣṭhet, mūlaharamitarasyetaramapadiśan ||
07215| bhedamubhayorvā parasparāpadeśaṃ prayuñjīta, bhinnayorupāṃśudaṇḍam ||
07216| pārśvastho vā balasthayorāsannabhayātpratikurvīta ||
07217| durgāpāśrayo vā dvaidhībhūtastiṣṭhet ||
07218| saṃdhivigrahakramahetubhirvā ceṣṭeta ||
07219| dūṣyāmitrāṭavikānubhayorupagṛhṇīyāt ||
07220| etayoranyataraṃ gacchaṃstairevānyatarasya vyasane praharet ||
07221| dvābhyāmupahato vā maṇḍalāpāśrayastiṣṭhet, madhyamamudāsīnaṃ vā saṃśrayeta ||
07222| tena sahaikamupagṛhyetaramucchindyād, ubhau vā ||
07223| dvābhyāmucchinno vā madhyamodāsīnayostatpakṣīyāṇāṃ vā rājñāṃ nyāyavṛttimāśrayeta ||
07224| tulyānāṃ vā yasya prakṛtayaḥ sukhyeyurenam, yatrastho vā śaknuyādātmānamuddhartum, yatra vā pūrvapuruṣocitā gatirāsannaḥ sambandho vā, mitrāṇi bhūyāṃsyatiśaktimanti vā bhaveyuḥ ||
07225  priyo yasya bhavedyo vā priyoasya katarastayoḥ |
07225  priyo yasya sa taṃ gacchedityāśrayagatiḥ parā ||E

(samahīnajyāyasāṃ guṇābhiniveśaḥ - hīnasaṃdhayah)
07301| vijigīṣuḥ śaktyapekṣaḥ ṣāḍguṇyamupayuñjīta ||
07302| samajyāyobhyāṃ saṃdhīyeta, hīnena vigṛhṇīyāt ||
07303| vigṛhīto hi jyāyasā hastinā pādayuddhamivābhyupaiti ||
07304| samena cāmaṃ pātramāmenāhatamivobhayataḥ kṣayaṃ karoti ||
07305| kumbhenevāśmā hīnenaikāntasiddhimavāpnoti ||
07306| jyāyāṃścenna saṃdhimiccheddaṇḍopanatavṛttamābalīyasaṃ vā yogamātiṣṭhet ||
07307| samaścenna saṃdhimicchedyāvanmātramapakuryāttāvanmātramasya pratyapakuryāt ||
07308| tejo hi saṃdhānakāraṇam ||
07309| nātaptaṃ lohaṃ lohena saṃdhatta iti ||
07310| hīnaścetsarvatrānupraṇatastiṣṭhetsaṃdhimupeyāt ||
07311| āraṇyoagniriva hi duḥkhāmarṣajaṃ tejo vikramayati ||
07312| maṇḍalasya cānugrāhyo bhavati ||
07313| saṃhitaścet"paraprakṛtayo lubdhakṣīṇāpacaritāḥ pratyādānabhayādvā nopagacchanti" iti paśyeddhīnoapi vigṛhṇīyāt ||
07314| vigṛhītaścet"paraprakṛtayo lubdhakṣīṇāpacaritā vigrahodvignā vā māṃ nopagacchanti" iti paśyejjyāyānapi saṃdhīyeta, vigrahodvegaṃ vā śamayet ||
07315| vyasanayaugapadyepi "guruvyasanoasmi, laghuvyasanaḥ paraḥ sukhena pratikṛtya vyasanamātmanoabhiyuñjyād" iti paśyejjyāyānapi saṃdhīyeta ||
07316| saṃdhivigrahayoścetparakarśanamātmopacayaṃ vā nābhipaśyejjyāyānapyāsīta ||
07317| paravyasanamapratikāryaṃ cetpaśyeddhīnoapyabhiyāyāt ||
07318| apratikāryāsannavyasano vā jyāyānapi saṃśrayeta ||
07319| saṃdhinaikato vigraheṇaikataścetkāryasiddhiṃ paśyejjyāyānapi dvaidhībhūtastiṣṭhet ||
07320| evaṃ samasya ṣāḍguṇyopayogaḥ ||
07321| tatra tu prativiśeṣaḥ ||
07322  pravṛttacakreṇākrānto rājñā balavatābalaḥ |
07322  saṃdhinopanamettūrṇaṃ kośadaṇḍātmabhūmibhiḥ ||
07323  svayaṃ saṃkhyātadaṇḍena daṇḍasya vibhavena vā |
07323  upasthātavyamityeṣa saṃdhirātmāmiṣo mataḥ ||
07324  senāpatikumārābhyāmupasthātavyamityayam |
07324  puruṣāntarasaṃdhiḥ syānnātmanetyātmarakṣaṇaḥ ||
07325  ekenānyatra yātavyaṃ svayaṃ daṇḍena vetyayam |
07325  adṛṣṭapuruṣaḥ saṃdhirdaṇḍamukhyātmarakṣaṇaḥ ||
07326  mukhyastrībandhanaṃ kuryātpūrvayoḥ paścime tvarim |
07326  sādhayedgūḍhamityete daṇḍopanatasaṃdhayaḥ ||
07327  kośadānena śeṣāṇāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ vimokṣaṇam |
07327  parikrayo bhavetsaṃdhiḥ sa eva ca yathāsukham ||
07328  skandhopaneyo bahudhā jñeyaḥ saṃdhirupagrahaḥ |
07328  niruddho deśakālābhyāmatyayaḥ syādupagrahaḥ ||
07329  viṣahyadānādāyatyāṃ kṣamaḥ strībandhanādapi |
07329  suvarṇasaṃdhirviśvāsādekībhāvagato bhavet ||
07330  viparītaḥ kapālaḥ syādatyādānābhibhāṣitaḥ |
07330  pūrvayoḥ praṇayetkupyaṃ hastyaśvaṃ vā garānvitam ||
07331  tṛtīye praṇayedarthaṃ kathayan karmaṇāṃ kṣayam |
07331  tiṣṭheccaturtha ityete kośopanatasaṃdhayaḥ ||
07332  bhūmyekadeśatyāgena śeṣaprakṛtirakṣaṇam |
07332  ādiṣṭasaṃdhistatreṣṭo gūḍhastenopaghātinaḥ ||
07333  bhūmīnāmāttasārāṇāṃ mūlavarjaṃ praṇāmanam |
07333  ucchinnasaṃdhistatreṣṭaḥ paravyasanakāṅkṣiṇaḥ ||
07334  phaladānena bhūmīnāṃ mokṣaṇaṃ syādavakrayaḥ |
07334  phalātimukto bhūmibhyaḥ saṃdhiḥ sa paridūṣaṇaḥ ||
07335  kuryādavekṣaṇaṃ pūrvau paścimau tvābalīyasam |
07335  ādāya phalamityete deśopanatasaṃdhayaḥ ||
07336  svakāryāṇāṃ vaśenaite deśe kāle ca bhāṣitāḥ |
07336  ābalīyasikāḥ kāryāstrividhā hīnasaṃdhayaḥ ||E

(vigṛhyāsanaṃ - saṃdhāyāsanaṃ - vigṛhyayānaṃ - saṃdhāyayānaṃ - sambhūyaprayāṇam)
07401| saṃdhivigrahayorāsanaṃ yānaṃ ca vyākhyātam ||
07402| sthānamāsanamupekṣaṇaṃ cetyāsanaparyāyāḥ ||
07403| viśeṣastu - guṇaikadeśe sthānam, svavṛddhiprāptyarthamāsanam, upāyānāmaprayoga upekṣaṇam ||
07404| atisaṃdhānakāmayorarivijigīṣvorupahantumaśaktayorvigṛhyāsanaṃ saṃdhāya vā ||
07405| yadā vā paśyet"svadaṇḍairmitrāṭavīdaṇḍairvā samaṃ jyāyāṃsaṃ vā karśayitumutsahe" iti tadā kṛtabāhyābhyantarakṛtyo vigṛhyāsīta ||
07406| yadā vā paśyet"utsāhayuktā me prakṛtayaḥ saṃhatā vivṛddhāḥ svakarmāṇyavyāhatāścariṣyanti parasya vā karmāṇyupahaniṣyanti" iti tadā vigṛhyāsīta ||
07407  yadā vā paśyet"parasyāpacaritāḥ kṣīṇā lubdhāḥ svacakrastenāṭavīvyathitā vā prakṛtayaḥ svayamupajāpena vā māmeṣyanti, sampannā me vārttā, vipannā parasya, tasya prakṛtayo durbhikṣopahatā māmeṣyanti; vipannā me vārttā, sampannā parasya, - ||
07407  taṃ me prakṛtayo na gamiṣyanti, vigṛhya cāsya dhānyapaśuhiraṇyānyāhariṣyāmi, svapaṇyopaghātīni vā parapaṇyāni nivartayiṣyāmi, - ||
07407  paravaṇikpathādvā saravanti māmeṣyanti vigṛhīte, netaram, dūṣyāmitrāṭavīnigrahaṃ vā vigṛhīto na kariṣyati, taireva vā vigrahaṃ prāpsyati, - ||
07407  mitraṃ me mitrabhāvyabhiprayāto bahvalpakālaṃ tanukṣayavyayamarthaṃ prāpsyati, guṇavatīmādeyāṃ vā bhūmim, - ||
07407  sarvasaṃdohena vā māmanādṛtya prayātukāmaḥ kathaṃ na yāyād" iti paravṛddhipratighātārthaṃ pratāpārthaṃ ca vigṛhyāsīta ||
07408| "tameva hi pratyāvṛtto grasate" ityācāryāḥ ||
07409| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07410| karśanamātramasya kuryādavyasaninaḥ, paravṛddhyā tu vṛddhaḥ samucchedanam ||
07411| evaṃ parasya yātavyoasmai sāhāyyamavinaṣṭaḥ prayacchet ||
07412| tasmātsarvasaṃdohaprakṛtaṃ vigṛhyāsīta ||
07413| vigṛhyāsanahetuprātilomye saṃdhāyāsīta ||
07414| vigṛhyāsanahetubhirabhyuccitaḥ sarvasaṃdohavarjaṃ vigṛhya yāyāt ||
07415| yadā vā paśyet"vyasanī paraḥ, prakṛtivyasanaṃ vāsya śeeṣaprakṛtibhirapratikāryam, svacakrapīḍitā viraktā vāsya prakṛtayaḥ karśitā nirutsāhāḥ parasparādvā bhinnāḥ śakyā lobhayitum, agnyudakavyādhimarakadurbhikṣanimittaṃ kṣīṇayugyapuruṣanicayarakṣāvidhānaḥ paraḥ" iti tadā vigṛhya yāyāt ||
07416| yadā vā paśyet"mitramākrandaśca me śūravṛddhānuraktaprakṛtiḥ, viparītaprakṛtiḥ paraḥ pārṣṇigrāhaścāsāraśca, śakṣyāmi mitreṇāsāramākrandena pārṣṇigrāhaṃ vā vigṛhya yātum" iti tadā vigṛhya yāyāt ||
07417| yadā vā phalamekahāryamalpakālaṃ paśyettadā pārṣṇigrāhāsārābhyāṃ vigṛhya yāyāt ||
07418| viparyaye saṃdhāya yāyāt ||
07419| yadā vā paśyet"na śakyamekena yātumavaśyaṃ ca yātavyam" iti tadā samahīnajyāyobhiḥ sāmavāyikaiḥ sambhūya yāyād, ekatra nirdiṣṭenāṃśena, anekatrānirdiṣṭenāṃśena ||
07420| teṣāmasamavāye daṇḍamanyatamasmānniviṣṭāṃśena yāceta ||
07421| sambhūyābhigamanena vā nirviśyeta, dhruve lābhe nirdiṣṭenāṃśena, adhruve lābhāṃśena ||
07422  aṃśo daṇḍasamaḥ pūrvaḥ prayāsasama uttamaḥ |
07422  vilopo vā yathālābhaṃ prakṣepasama eva vā ||E

(yātavyāmitrayorabhigrahacintāḥ - kṣayalobhavirāgahetavaḥ prakṛtīnāṃ - sāmavāyikaviparimarśah)
07501| tulyasāmantavyasane yātavyamamitraṃ vetyamitramabhiyāyāt, tatsiddhau yātavyam ||
07502| amitrasiddhau hi yātavyaḥ sāhāyyaṃ dadyānnāmitro yātavyasiddhau ||
07503| guruvyasanaṃ yātavyaṃ laghuvyasanamamitraṃ veti "guruvyasanaṃ saukaryato yāyād" ityācāryāḥ ||
07504| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07505| laghuvyasanamamitraṃ yāyāt ||
07506| laghvapi hi vyasanamabhiyuktasya kṛcchraṃ bhavati ||
07507| satyaṃ gurvapi gurutaraṃ bhavati ||
07508| anabhiyuktastu laghuvyasanaḥ sukhena vyasanaṃ pratikṛtyāmitro yātavyamabhisaret, pārṣṇiṃ vā gṛhṇīyāt ||
07509| yātavyayaugapadye guruvyasanaṃ nyāyavṛttiṃ laghuvyasanamanyāyavṛttiṃ viraktaprakṛtiṃ veti viraktaprakṛtiṃ yāyāt ||
07510| guruvyasanaṃ nyāyavṛttimabhiyuktaṃ prakṛtayoanugṛhṇanti, laghuvyasanamanyāyavṛttimupekṣante, viraktā balavantamapyucchindanti ||
07511| tasmādviraktaprakṛtimeva yāyāt ||
07512| kṣīṇalubdhaprakṛtimapacaritaprakṛtiṃ veti kṣīṇalubdhaprakṛtiṃ yāyāt, kṣīṇalubdhā hi prakṛtayaḥ sukhenopajāpaṃ pīḍāṃ vopagacchanti, nāpacaritāḥ pradhānāvagrahasādhyāḥ" ityācāryāḥ ||
07513| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07514| kṣīṇalubdhā hi prakṛtayo bhartari snigdhā bhartṛhite tiṣṭhanti, upajāpaṃ vā visaṃvādayanti, anurāge sārvaguṇyamiti ||
07515| tasmādapacaritaprakṛtimeva yāyāt ||
07516| balavantamanyāyavṛttiṃ durbalaṃ vā nyāyavṛttimiti balavantamanyāyavṛttiṃ yāyāt ||
07517| balavantamanyāyavṛttimabhiyuktaṃ prakṛtayo nānugṛhṇanti, niṣpātayanti, amitraṃ vāsya bhajante ||
07518| durbalaṃ tu nyāyavṛttimabhiyuktaṃ prakṛtayaḥ parigṛhṇanti, anuniṣpatanti vā ||
07519  avakṣepeṇa hi satāmasatāṃ pragraheṇa ca |
07519  abhūtānāṃ ca hiṃsānāmadharmyāṇāṃ pravartanaiḥ ||
07520  ucitānāṃ caritrāṇāṃ dharmiṣṭhānāṃ nivartanaiḥ |
07520  adharmasya prasaṅgena dharmasyāvagraheṇa ca ||
07521  akāryāṇāṃ ca karaṇaiḥ kāryāṇāṃ ca praṇāśanaiḥ |
07521  apradānaiśca deyānāmadeyānāṃ ca sādhanaiḥ ||
07522  adaṇḍanaiśca daṇḍyānāmadaṇḍyānāṃ ca daṇḍanaiḥ |
07522  agrāhyāṇāmupagrāhairgrāhyāṇāṃ cānabhigrahaiḥ ||
07523  anarthyānāṃ ca karaṇairarthyānāṃ ca vighātanaiḥ |
07523  arakṣaṇaiśca corebhyaḥ svayaṃ ca parimoṣaṇaiḥ ||
07524  pātaiḥ puruṣakārāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ guṇadūṣaṇaiḥ |
07524  upaghātaiḥ pradhānānāṃ mānyānāṃ cāvamānanaiḥ ||
07525  virodhanaiśca vṛddhānāṃ vaiṣamyeṇānṛtena ca |
07525  kṛtasyāpratikāreṇa sthitasyākaraṇena ca ||
07526  rājñaḥ pramādālasyābhyāṃ yogakṣemavadhena vā |
07526  prakṛtīnāṃ kṣayo lobho vairāgyaṃ copajāyate ||
07527  kṣīṇāḥ prakṛtayo lobhaṃ lubdhā yānti virāgatām |
07527  viraktā yāntyamitraṃ vā bhartāraṃ ghnanti vā svayam ||
07528| tasmātprakṛtīnāṃ kṣayalobhavirāgakāraṇāni notpādayet, utpannāni vā sadyaḥ pratikurvīta ||
07529| kṣīṇā lubdhā viraktā vā prakṛtaya iti ||
07530| kṣīṇāḥ pīḍanocchedanabhayātsadyaḥ saṃdhiṃ yuddhaṃ niṣpatanaṃ vā rocayante ||
07531| lubdhā lobhenāsaṃtuṣṭāḥ paropajāpaṃ lipsante ||
07532| viraktāḥ parābhiyogamabhyuttiṣṭhante ||
07533| tāsāṃ hiraṇyadhānyakṣayaḥ sarvopaghātī kṛcchrapratīkāraśca, yugyapuruṣakṣayo hiraṇyadhānyasādhyaḥ ||
07534| lobha aikadeśiko mukhyāyattaḥ parārtheṣu śakyaḥ pratihantumādātuṃ vā ||
07535| virāgaḥ pradhānāvagrahasādhyaḥ ||
07536| niṣpradhānā hi prakṛtayo bhogyā bhavantyanupajāpyāścānyeṣām, anāpatsahāstu ||
07537| prakṛtimukhyapragrahaistu bahudhā bhinnā guptā bhavantyāpatsahāśca ||
07538| sāmavāyikānāmapi saṃdhivigrahakāraṇānyavekṣya śaktiśaucayuktaiḥ sambhūya yāyāt ||
07539| śaktimān hi pārṣṇigrahaṇe yātrāsāhāyyadāne vā śaktaḥ, śuciḥ siddhau cāsiddhau ca yathāsthitakārīti ||
07540| teṣāṃ jyāyasaikena dvābhyāṃ samābhyāṃ vā sambhūya yātavyamiti dvābhyāṃ samābhyāṃ śreyaḥ ||
07541| jyāyasā hyavagṛhītaścarati, samābhyāmatisaṃdhānādhikye vā ||
07542| tau hi sukhau bhedayitum, duṣṭaścaiko dvābhyāṃ niyantuṃ bhedopagrahaṃ copagantumiti ||
07543| samenaikena dvābhyāṃ hīnābhyāṃ veti dvābhyāṃ hīnābhyāṃ śreyaḥ ||
07544| tau hi dvikāryasādhakau vaśyau ca bhavataḥ ||
07545  kāryasiddhau tu - kṛtārthājjyāyaso gūḍhaḥ sāpadeśamapasravet |
07545  aśuceḥ śucivṛttāttu pratīkṣetā visarjanāt ||
07546  sattrādapasaredyattaḥ kalatramapanīya vā |
07546  samādapi hi labdhārthādviśvastasya bhayaṃ bhavet ||
07547  jyāyastve cāpi labdhārthaḥ samoapi parikalpate |
07547  abhyuccitaścāviśvāsyo vṛddhiścittavikāriṇī ||
07548  viśiṣṭādalpamapyaṃśaṃ labdhvā tuṣṭamukho vrajet |
07548  anaṃśo vā tatoasyāṅke prahṛtya dviguṇaṃ haret ||
07549  kṛtārthastu svayaṃ netā visṛjetsāmavāyikān |
07549  api jīyeta na jayenmaṇḍaleṣṭastathā bhavet ||E

(saṃhitaprayāṇikaṃ - paripaṇitāparipaṇitāpasṛtāḥ saṃdhayah)
07601| vijigīṣurdvitīyāṃ prakṛtimevamatisaṃdadhyāt ||
07602| sāmantaṃ saṃhitaprayāṇe yojayet"tvamito yāhi, ahamito yāsyāmi, samāno lābhaḥ" iti ||
07603| lābhasāmye saṃdhiḥ, vaiṣamye vikramaḥ ||
07604| saṃdhiḥ paripaṇitaścāparipaṇitaśca ||
07605| "tvametaṃ deśaṃ yāhi, ahamimaṃ deśaṃ yāsyāmi" iti paripaṇitadeśaḥ ||
07606| "tvametāvantaṃ kālaṃ ceṣṭasva, ahametāvantaṃ kālaṃ ceṣṭiṣye" iti paripaṇitakālaḥ ||
07607| "tvametāvatkāryaṃ sādhaya, ahamidaṃ kāryaṃ sādhayiṣyāmi" iti paripaṇitārthaḥ ||
07608| yadi vā manyeta "śailavananadīdurgamaṭavīvyavahitaṃ chinnadhānyapuruṣavīvadhāsāramayavasendhanodakamavijñātaṃ prakṛṣṭamanyabhāvadeśīyaṃ vā sainyavyāyāmānāmalabdhabhaumaṃ vā deśaṃ paro yāsyati, viparītamaham" ityetasmin viśeṣe paripaṇitadeśaṃ saṃdhimupeyāt ||
07609| yadi vā manyeta "pravarṣoṣṇaśītamativyādhiprāyamupakṣīṇāhāropabhogaṃ sainyavyāyāmānāṃ cāuparodhikaṃ kāryasādhanānāmūnamatiriktaṃ vā kālaṃ paraśceṣṭiṣyate, viparītamaham" ityetasmin viśeṣe paripaṇitakālaṃ saṃdhimupeyāt ||
07610| yadi vā manyeta "pratyādeyaṃ prakṛtikopakaṃ dīrghakālaṃ mahākṣayavyayamalpamanarthānubandhamakalyamadharmyaṃ madhyamodāsīnaviruddhaṃ mitropaghātakaṃ vā kāryaṃ paraḥ sādhayiṣyati, viparītamaham" ityetasmin viśeṣe paripaṇitārthaṃ saṃdhimupeyāt ||
07611| evaṃ deśakālayoḥ kālakāryayordeśakāryayordeśakālakāryāṇāṃ cāvasthāpanātsaptavidhaḥ paripaṇitaḥ ||
07612| tasmin prāgevārabhya pratiṣṭhāpya ca svakarmāṇi parakarmasu vikrameta ||
07613| vyasanatvarāvamānālasyayuktamajñaṃ vā śatrumatisaṃdhātukāmo deśakālakāryāṇāmanavasthāpanāt"saṃhitau svaḥ" iti saṃdhiviśvāsena paracchidramāsādya praharedityaparipaṇitaḥ ||
07614| tatraitadbhavati ||
07615  sāmantenaiva sāmantaṃ vidvānāyojya vigrahe |
07615  tatoanyasya haredbhūmiṃ chittvā pakṣaṃ samantataḥ ||
07616| saṃdherakṛtacikīrṣā kṛtaśleṣaṇaṃ kṛtavidūṣaṇamavaśīrṇakriyā ca ||
07617| vikramasya prakāśayuddhaṃ kūṭayuddhaṃ tūṣṇīṃyuddham ||
07618| iti saṃdhivikramau ||
07619| apūrvasya saṃdheḥ sānubandhaiḥ sāmādibhiḥ paryeṣaṇaṃ samahīnajyāyasāṃ ca yathābalamavasthāpanamakṛtacikīrṣā ||
07620| kṛtasya priyahitābhyāmubhayataḥ paripālanaṃ yathāsambhāṣitasya ca nibandhanasyānuvartanaṃ rakṣaṇaṃ ca "kathaṃ parasmānna bhidyeta" iti kṛtaśleṣaṇam ||
07621| parasyāpasaṃdheyatāṃ dūṣyātisaṃdhānena sthāpayitvā vyatikramaḥ kṛtavidūṣaṇam ||
07622| bhṛtyena mitreṇa vā doṣāpasṛtena pratisaṃdhānamavaśīrṇakriyā ||
07623| tasyāṃ gatāgataścaturvidhaḥ - kāraṇādgatāgato, viparītaḥ, kāraṇādgatoakāraṇādāgato, viparītaśceti ||
07624| svāmino doṣeṇa gato guṇenāgataḥ parasya guṇena gato doṣeṇāgata iti kāraṇādgatāgataḥ saṃdheyaḥ ||
07625| svadoṣeṇa gatāgato guṇamubhayoḥ parityajya akāraṇādgatāgataḥ calabuddhirasaṃdheyaḥ ||
07626| svāmino doṣeṇa gataḥ parasmātsvadoṣeṇāgata iti kāraṇādgatoakāraṇādāgataḥ tarkayitavyaḥ "paraprayuktaḥ svena vā doṣeṇāpakartukāmaḥ, parasyocchettāramamitraṃ me jñātvā pratighātabhayādāgataḥ, paraṃ vā māmucchettukāmaṃ parityajyānṛśaṃsyādāgataḥ" iti ||
07627| jñātvā kalyāṇabuddhiṃ pūjayed, anyathābuddhimapakṛṣṭaṃ vāsayet ||
07628| svadoṣeṇa gataḥ paradoṣeṇāgata ityakāraṇādgataḥ kāraṇādāgataḥ tarkayitavyaḥ " chidraṃ me pūrayiṣyati, ucitoayamasya vāsaḥ, paratrāsya jano na ramate, mitrairme saṃhitaḥ, śatrubhirvigṛhītaḥ, lubdhakrūrādāvignaḥ śatrusaṃhitādvā parasmāt" iti ||
07629| jñātvā yathābuddhyavasthāpayitavyaḥ ||
07630| "kṛtapraṇāśaḥ śaktihānirvidyāpaṇyatvamāśānirvedo deśalaulyamaviśvāso balavadvigraho vā parityāgasthānam" ityācāryāḥ ||
07631| bhayamavṛttiramarṣa iti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07632| ihāpakārī tyājyaḥ, parāpakārī saṃdheyaḥ, ubhayāpakārī tarkayitavya iti samānam ||
07633| asaṃdheyena tvavaśyaṃ saṃdhātavye yataḥ prabhāvastataḥ pratividadhyāt ||
07634  sopakāraṃ vyavahitaṃ guptamāyuḥkṣayāditi |
07634  vāsayedaripakṣīyamavaśīrṇakriyāvidhau ||
07635  vikramayedbhartari vā siddhaṃ vā daṇḍacāriṇam ||
07635  kuryādamitrāṭavīṣu pratyante vānyataḥ kṣipet ||
07636  paṇyaṃ kuryādasiddhaṃ vā siddhaṃ vā tena saṃvṛtam ||
07636  tasyaiva doṣeṇādūṣya parasaṃdheyakāraṇāt ||
07637  atha vā śamayedenamāyatyarthamupāṃśunā ||
07637  āyatyāṃ ca vadhaprepsuṃ dṛṣṭvā hanyādgatāgatam ||
07638  aritoabhyāgato doṣaḥ śatrusaṃvāsakāritaḥ ||
07638  sarpasaṃvāsadharmitvānnityodvegena dūṣitaḥ ||
07639  jāyate plakṣabījāśātkapotādiva śālmaleḥ ||
07639  udvegajanano nityaṃ paścādapi bhayāvahaḥ ||
07640  prakāśayuddhaṃ nirdiṣṭe deśe kāle ca vikramaḥ ||
07640  vibhīṣaṇamavaskandaḥ pramādavyasanārdanam ||
07641  ekatra tyāgaghātau ca kūṭayuddhasya mātṛkā ||
07641  yogagūḍhopajāpārthaṃ tūṣṇīṃyuddhasya lakṣaṇam ||E

(dvaidhībhāvikāḥ saṃdhivikramāh)
07701| vijigīṣurdvitīyāṃ prakṛtimevamupagṛhṇīyāt ||
07702| sāmantaṃ sāmantena sambhūya yāyāt, yadi vā manyeta "pārṣṇiṃ me na grahīṣyati, pārṣṇigrāhaṃ vārayiṣyati, yātavyaṃ nābhisariṣyati, baladvaiguṇyaṃ me bhaviṣyati, vīvadhāsārau me pravartayiṣyati, parasya vārayiṣyati, bahvābādhe me pathi kaṇṭakānmardayiṣyati, durgāṭavyapasāreṣu daṇḍena cariṣyati, yātavyamaviṣahye doṣe saṃdhau vā  sthāpayiṣyati,
labdhalābhāṃśo vā śatrūnanyānme viśvāsayiṣyati" iti ||
07703| dvaidhībhūto vā kośena daṇḍaṃ daṇḍena kośaṃ sāmantānāmanyatamāllipseta ||
07704| teṣāṃ jyāyasoadhikenāṃśena samātsamena hīnāddhīneneti samasaṃdhiḥ ||
07705| viparyaye viṣamasaṃdhiḥ ||
07706| tayorviśeṣalābhādatisaṃdhiḥ ||
07707| vyasaninamapāyasthāne saktamanarthinaṃ vā jyāyāṃsaṃ hīno balasamena lābhena paṇeta ||
07708| paṇitastasyāpakārasamartho vikrameta, anyathā saṃdadhyāt ||
07709| evaṃbhūto vā hīnaśaktipratāpapūraṇārthaṃ sambhāvyārthābhisārī mūlapārṣṇitrāṇārthaṃ vā jyāyāṃsaṃ hīno balasamādviśiṣṭena lābhena paṇeta ||
07710| paṇitaḥ kalyāṇabuddhimanugṛhṇīyāt, anyathā vikrameta ||
07711| jātavyasanaprakṛtirandhramupasthitānarthaṃ vā jyāyāṃsaṃ hīno durgamitrapratiṣṭabdho vā hrasvamadhvānaṃ yātukāmaḥ śatrumayuddhamekāntasiddhiṃ vā lābhamādātukāmo balasamāddhīnena lābhena paṇeta ||
07712| paṇitastasyāpakārasamartho vikrameta, anyathā saṃdadhyāt ||
07713| arandhravyasano vā jyāyān durārabdhakarmāṇaṃ bhūyaḥ kṣayavyayābhyāṃ yoktukāmo dūṣyadaṇḍaṃ pravāsayitukāmo dūṣyadaṇḍamāvāhayitukāmo vā pīḍanīyamucchedanīyaṃ vā hīnena vyathayitukāmaḥ saṃdhipradhāno vā kalyāṇabuddhirhīnaṃ lābhaṃ pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
07714| kalyāṇabuddhinā sambhūyārthaṃ lipseta, anyathā vikrameta ||
07715| evaṃ samaḥ samamatisaṃdadhyādanugṛhṇīyādvā ||
07716| parānīkasya pratyanīkaṃ mitrāṭavīnāṃ vā, śatrorvibhūmīnāṃ deśikaṃ mūlapārṣṇitrāṇārthaṃ vā samo balasamena lābhena paṇeta ||
07717| paṇitaḥ kalyāṇabuddhimanugṛhṇīyāt, anyathā vikrameta ||
07718| jātavyasanaprakṛtirandhramanekaviruddhamanyato labhamāno vā samo balasamāddhīnena lābhena paṇeta ||
07719| paṇitastasyāpakārasamartho vikrameta, anyathā saṃdadhyāt ||
07720| evaṃbhūto vā samaḥ sāmantāyattakāryaḥ kartavyabalo vā balasamādviśiṣṭena lābhena paṇeta ||
07721| paṇitaḥ kalyāṇabuddhimanugṛhṇīyātanyathā vikrameta ||
07722| jātavyasanaprakṛtirandhramabhihantukāmaḥ svārabdhamekāntasiddhiṃ vāsya karmopahantukāmo mūle yātrāyāṃ vā prahartukāmo yātavyādbhūyo labhamāno vā jyāyāṃsaṃ hīnaṃ samaṃ vā bhūyo yāceta ||
07723| bhūyo vā yācitaḥ svabalarakṣārthaṃ durdharṣamanyadurgamāsāramaṭavīṃ vā paradaṇḍena marditukāmaḥ prakṛṣṭedhvani kāle vā paradaṇḍaṃ kṣayavyayābhyāṃ yoktukāmaḥ paradaṇḍena vā vivṛddhastamevocchettukāmaḥ paradaṇḍamādātukāmo vā bhūyo dadyāt ||
07724| jyāyān vā hīnaṃ yātavyāpadeśena haste kartukāmaḥ paramucchidya vā tamevocchettukāmaḥ, tyāgaṃ vā kṛtvā pratyādātukāmo balasamādviśiṣṭena lābhena paṇeta ||
07725| paṇitastasyāpakārasamartho vikrameta, anyathā saṃdadhyāt ||
07726| yātavyasaṃhito vā tiṣṭhet, dūṣyāmitrāṭavīdaṇḍaṃ vāsmai dadyāt ||
07727| jātavyasanaprakṛtirandhro vā jyāyān hīnaṃ balasamena lābhena paṇeta ||
07728| paṇitastasyāpakārasamartho vikrameta, anyathā saṃdadhyāt ||
07729| evaṃbhūtaṃ hīnaṃ jyāyān balasamāddhīnena lābhena paṇeta ||
07730| paṇitastasyāpakārasamartho vikrameta, anyathā saṃdadhyāt ||
07731  ādau budhyeta paṇitaḥ paṇamānaśca kāraṇam ||
07731  tato vitarkyobhayato yataḥ śreyaśtato vrajet ||E

(yātavyavṛttiḥ - anugrāhyamitraviśeṣāh)
07801| yātavyoabhiyāsyamānaḥ saṃdhikāraṇamādātukāmo vihantukāmo vā sāmavāyikānāmanyatamaṃ lābhadvaiguṇyena paṇeta ||
07802| paṇamānaḥ kṣayavyayapravāsapratyavāyaparopakāraśarīrābādhāṃścāsya varṇayet ||
07803| pratipannamarthena yojayet ||
07804| vairaṃ vā parairgrāhayitvā visaṃvādayet ||
07805| durārabdhakarmāṇaṃ bhūyaḥ kṣayavyayābhyāṃ yoktukāmaḥ svārabdhāṃ vā yātrāsiddhiṃ vighātayitukāmo mūle yātrāyāṃ vā prahartukāmo yātavyasaṃhitaḥ punaryācitukāmaḥ pratyutpannārthakṛcchrastasminnaviśvasto vā tadātve lābhamalpamicchet, āyatyāṃ prabhūtam ||
07806| mitropakāramamitropaghātamarthānubandhamavekṣamāṇaḥ pūrvopakārakaṃ kārayitukāmo bhūyastadātve mahāntaṃ lābhamutsṛjyāyatyāmalpamicchet ||
07807| dūṣyāmitrābhyāṃ mūlahareṇa vā jyāyasā vigṛhītaṃ trātukāmastathāvidhamupakāraṃ kārayitukāmaḥ sambandhāvekṣī vā tadātve cāyatyāṃ ca lābhaṃ na pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
07808| kṛtasaṃdhiratikramitukāmaḥ parasya prakṛtikarśanaṃ mitrāmitrasaṃdhiviśleṣaṇaṃ vā kartukāmaḥ parābhiyogātśaṅkamāno lābhamaprāptamadhikaṃ vā yāceta ||
07809| tamitarastadātve cāyatyāṃ ca kramamavekṣeta ||
07810| tena pūrve vyākhyātāḥ ||
07811| arivijigīṣvostu svaṃ svaṃ mitramanugṛhṇatoḥ śakyakalyabhavyārambhisthirakarmānuraktaprakṛtibhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
07812| śakyārambhī viṣahyaṃ karmārabhate, kalyārambhī nirdoṣam, bhavyārambhī kalyāṇodayam ||
07813| sthirakarmā nāsamāpya karmoparamate ||
07814| anuraktaprakṛtiḥ susahāyatvādalpenāpyanugraheṇa kāryaṃ sādhayati ||
07815| ta ete kṛtārthāḥ sukhena prabhūtaṃ copakurvanti ||
07816| ataḥ pratilomā nānugrāhyāḥ ||
07817| tayorekapuruṣānugrahe yo mitraṃ mitrataraṃ vānugṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
07818| mitrādātmavṛddhiṃ hi prāpnoti, kṣayavyayapravāsaparopakārānitaraḥ ||
07819| kṛtārthaśca śatrurvaiguṇyameti ||
07820| madhyamaṃ tvanugṛhṇatoryo madhyamaṃ mitraṃ mitrataraṃ vānugṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
07821| mitrādātmavṛddhiṃ hi prāpnoti, kṣayavyayapravāsaparopakārānitaraḥ ||
07822| madhyamaścedanugṛhīto viguṇaḥ syādamitroatisaṃdhatte ||
07823| kṛtaprayāsaṃ hi madhyamāmitramapasṛtamekārthopagataṃ prāpnoti ||
07824| tenodāsīnānugraho vyākhyātaḥ ||
07825| madhyamodāsīnayorbalāṃśadāne yaḥ śūraṃ kṛtāstraṃ duḥkhasahamanuraktaṃ vā daṇḍaṃ dadāti soatisaṃdhīyate ||
07826| viparītoatisaṃdhatte ||
07827| yatra tu daṇḍaḥ prahitastaṃ vā cārthamanyāṃśca sādhayati tatra maulabhṛtaśreṇīmitrāṭavībalānāmanyatamamupalabdhadeśakālaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyāt, amitrāṭavībalaṃ vā vyavahitadeśakālam ||
07828| yaṃ tu manyeta "kṛtārtho me daṇḍaṃ gṛhṇīyād, amitrāṭavyabhūmyanṛtuṣu vā vāsayed, aphalaṃ vā kuryād" iti, daṇḍavyāsaṅgāpadeśena nainamanugṛhṇīyāt ||
07829| evamavaśyaṃ tvanugrahītavye tatkālasahamasmai daṇḍaṃ dadyāt ||
07830| āsamāpteścainaṃ vāsayedyodhayecca balavyasanebhyaśca rakṣet ||
07831| kṛtārthācca sāpadeśamapasrāvayet ||
07832| dūṣyāmitrāṭavīdaṇḍaṃ vāsmai dadyāt ||
07833| yātavyena vā saṃdhāyainamatisaṃdadhyāt ||
07834  same hi lābhe saṃdhiḥ syādviṣame vikramo mataḥ ||
07834  samahīnaviśiṣṭānāmityuktāḥ saṃdhivikramāḥ ||E

(mitrahiraṇyabhūmikarmasaṃdhayaḥ, tatra mitrasaṃdhiḥ hiraṇyasaṃdhiśca)
07901| saṃhitaprayāṇe mitrahiraṇyabhūmilābhānāmuttarottaro lābhaḥ śreyān ||
07902| mitrahiraṇye hi bhūmilābhādbhavataḥ, mitraṃ hiraṇyalābhāt ||
07903| yo vā lābhaḥ siddhaḥ śeṣayoranyataraṃ sādhayati ||
07904| "tvaṃ cāhaṃ ca mitraṃ labhāvahe" ityevaṃādidhsamasaṃdhiḥ ||
07905| "tvaṃ mitram" ityevaṃādirviṣamasaṃdhiḥ ||
07906| tayorviśeṣalābhādatisaṃdhiḥ ||
07907| samasaṃdhau tu yaḥ sampannaṃ mitraṃ mitrakṛcchre vā mitramavāpnoti soatisaṃdhatte ||
07908| āpaddhi sauhṛdasthairyamutpādayati ||
07909| mitrakṛcchrepi nityamavaśyamanityaṃ vaśyaṃ veti "nityamavaśyaṃ śreyaḥ, taddhi anupakurvadapi nāpakaroti" ityācāryāḥ ||
07910| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07911| vaśyamanityaṃ śreyaḥ ||
07912| yāvadupakaroti tāvanmitraṃ bhavati, upakāralakṣaṇaṃ mitramiti ||
07913| vaśyayorapi mahābhogamanityamalpabhogaṃ vā nityamiti || mahābhogamanityaṃ śreyaḥ, mahābhogamanityamalpakālena mahadupakurvanmahānti vyayasthānāni pratikaroti" ityācāryāḥ ||
07914| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07915| nityamalpabhogaṃ śreyaḥ ||
07916| mahābhogamanityamupakārabhayādapakrāmati, upakṛtya vā pratyādātumīhate ||
07917| nityamalpabhogaṃ sātatyādalpamupakurvanmahatā kālena mahadupakaroti ||
07918| gurusamutthaṃ mahanmitraṃ laghusamutthamalpaṃ veti "gurusamutthaṃ mahanmitraṃ pratāpakaraṃ bhavati, yadā cottiṣṭhate tadā kāryaṃ sādhayati" ityācāryāḥ ||
07919| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07920| laghusamutthamalpaṃ śreyaḥ ||
07921| lagusamutthamalpaṃ mitraṃ kāryakālaṃ nātipātayati daurbalyācca yatheṣṭabhogyaṃ bhavati, netaratprakṛṣṭabhaumam ||
07922| vikṣiptasainyamavaśyasainyaṃ veti "vikṣiptaṃ sainyaṃ śakyaṃ pratisaṃhartuṃ vaśyatvād" ityācāryāḥ ||
07923| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07924| avaśyasainyaṃ śreyaḥ ||
07925| avaśyaṃ hi śakyaṃ sāmādibhirvaśyaṃ kartum, netaratkāryavyāsaktaṃ pratisaṃhartum ||
07926| puruṣabhogaṃ hiraṇyabhogaṃ vā mitramiti "puruṣabhogaṃ mitraṃ śreyaḥ, pruṣabhogaṃ mitraṃ pratāpakaraṃ bhavati, yadā cottiṣṭhate tadā kāryaṃ sādhayati" ityācāryāḥ ||
07927| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07928| hiraṇyabhogaṃ mitraṃ śreyaḥ ||
07929| nityo hi hiraṇyena yogaḥ kadāciddaṇḍena ||
07930| daṇḍaśca hiraṇyenānye ca kāmāḥ prāpyanta iti ||
07931| hiraṇyabhogaṃ bhūmibhogaṃ vā mitramiti "hiraṇyabhogaṃ gatimattvātsarvavyayapratīkārakaram" ityācāryāḥ ||
07932| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07933| mitrahiraṇye hi bhūmilābhādbhavata ityuktaṃ purastād ||
07934| tasmādbhūmibhogaṃ mitraṃ śreya iti ||
07935| tulye puruṣabhoge vikramaḥ kleśasahatvamanurāgaḥ sarvabalalābho vā mitrakulādviśeṣaḥ ||
07936| tulye hiraṇyabhoge prārthitārthatā prābhūtyamalpaprayasatā sātatyaṃ ca viśeṣaḥ ||
07937| tatraitadbhavati ||
07938  nityaṃ vaśyaṃ laghūtthānaṃ pitṛpaitāmahaṃ mahat |
07938  advaidhyaṃ ceti sampannaṃ mitraṃ ṣaḍguṇamucyate ||
07939  ṛte yadarthaṃ praṇayādrakṣyate yacca rakṣati |
07939  pūrvopacitasambandhaṃ tanmitraṃ nityamucyate ||
07940  sarvacitramahābhogaṃ trividhaṃ vaśyamucyate |
07940  ekatobhogyubhayataḥ sarvatobhogi cāparam ||
07941  ādātṛ vā dātrapi vā jīvatyariṣu hiṃsayā |
07941  mitraṃ nityamavaśyaṃ taddurgāṭavyapasāri ca ||
07942  anyato vigṛhītaṃ yallaghuvyasanameva vā |
07942  saṃdhatte copakārāya tanmitraṃ vaśyamadhruvam ||
07943  ekārthenātha sambaddhamupakāryavikāri ca |
07943  mitrabhāvi bhavatyetanmitramadvaidhyamāpadi ||
07944  mitrabhāvāddhruvaṃ mitraṃ śatrusādhāraṇāccalam |
07944  na kasyacidudāsīnaṃ dvayorubhayabhāvi tat ||
07945  vijigīṣoramitraṃ yanmitramantardhitāṃ gatam |
07945  upakāreniviṣṭaṃ vāśaktaṃ vānupakāri tat ||
07946  priyaṃ parasya vā rakṣyaṃ pūjyaṃ sambaddhameva vā |
07946  anugṛhṇāti yanmitraṃ śatrusādhāraṇaṃ hi tat ||
07947  prakṛṣṭabhaumaṃ saṃtuṣṭaṃ balavaccālasaṃ ca yat |
07947  udāsīnaṃ bhavatyetadvyasanādavamānitam ||
07948  arernetuśca yadvṛddhiṃ daurbalyādanuvartate |
07948  ubhayasyāpyavidviṣṭaṃ vidyādubhayabhāvi tat ||
07949  kāraṇākāraṇadhvastaṃ kāraṇākāraṇāgatam |
07949  yo mitraṃ samupekṣeta sa mṛtyumupagūhati ||
07950| kṣipramalpo lābhaścirānmahāniti vā "kṣipramalpo lābhaḥ kāryadeśakālasaṃvādakaḥ śreyān" ityācāryāḥ ||
07951| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
07952| cirādavinipātī bījasadharmā mahāmllābhaḥ śreyān, viparyaye pūrvaḥ ||
07953  evaṃ dṛṣṭvā dhruve lābhe lābhāṃśe ca guṇodayam |
07953  svārthasiddhiparo yāyātsaṃhitaḥ sāmavāyikaiḥ ||E

(mitrahiraṇyabhūmikarmasaṃdhayaḥ, tatra bhūmisaṃdhih)
071001| "tvaṃ cāhaṃ ca bhūmiṃ labhāvahe" iti bhūmisaṃdhiḥ ||
071002| tayoryaḥ pratyupasthitārthaḥ sampannāṃ bhūmimavāpnoti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071003| tulye sampannālābhe yo balavantamākramya bhūmimavāpnoti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071004| bhūmilābhaṃ śatrukarśanaṃ pratāpaṃ ca hi prāpnoti ||
071005| durbalādbhūmilābhe satyaṃ saukaryaṃ bhavati ||
071006| durbala eva ca bhūmilābhaḥ, tatsāmantaśca mitramamitrabhāvaṃ gacchati ||
071007| tulye balīyastve yaḥ sthitaśatrumutpāṭya bhūmimavāpnoti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071008| durgāvāptirhi svabhūmirakṣaṇamamitrāṭavīpratiṣedhaṃ ca karoti ||
071009| calāmitrādbhūmilābhe śakyasāmantato viśeṣaḥ ||
071010| durbalasāmantā hi kṣiprāpyāyanayogakṣemā bhavati ||
071011| viparītā balavatsāmantā kośadaṇḍāvacchedanī ca bhūmirbhavati ||
071012| sampannā nityāmitrā mandaguṇā vā bhūmiranityāmitreti "sampannā nityāmitrā śreyasī bhūmiḥ sampannā hi kośadaṇḍau sampādayati, tau cāmitrapratighātakau ityācāryāḥ ||
071013| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071014| nityāmitrālābhe bhūyān śatrulābho bhavati ||
071015| nityaśca śatrurupakṛte cāpakṛte ca śatrureva bhavati, anityastu śatrurupakārādanapakārādvā śāmyati ||
071016| yasyā hi bhūmerbahudurgāścoragaṇairmlecchāṭavībhirvā nityāvirahitāḥ pratyantāḥ sā nityāmitrā, viparyaye tvanityāmitrā ||
071017| alpā pratyāsannā mahatī vyavahitā vā bhūmiriti alpā pratyāsannā śreyasī ||
071018| sukhā hi prāptuṃ pālayitumabhisārayituṃ ca bhavati ||
071019| viparītā vyavahitā ||
071020| vyavahitayorapi daṇḍadhāraṇātmadhāraṇā vā bhūmiriti ātmadhāraṇā śreyasī ||
071021| sā hi svasamutthābhyāṃ kośadaṇḍābhyāṃ dhāryate ||
071022| viparītā daṇḍadhāraṇā daṇḍasthānam ||
071023| bāliśātprājñādvā bhūmilābha iti bāliśādbhūmilābhaḥ śreyān ||
071024| suprāpyānupālyā hi bhavati, apratyādeyā ca ||
071025| viparītā prājñādanuraktā ||
071026| pīḍanīyocchedanīyayorucchedanīyādbhūmilābhaḥ śreyān ||
071027| ucchedanīyo hyanapāśrayo durbalāpāśrayo vābhiyuktaḥ kośadaṇḍāvādāyāpasartukāmaḥ prakṛtibhistyajyate, na pīḍanīyo durgamitrapratiṣṭabdhaḥ ||
071028| durgapratiṣṭabdhayorapi sthalanadīdurgīyābhyāṃ sthaladurgīyādbhūmilābhaḥ śreyān ||
071029| sthāleyaṃ hi surodhāvamardāvaskandamanihśrāviśatru ca ||
071030| nadīdurgaṃ tu dviguṇakleśakaram, udakaṃ ca pātavyaṃ vṛttikaraṃ cāmitrasya ||
071031| nadīparvatadurgīyābhyāṃ nadīdurgīyādbbhūmilābhaḥ śreyān ||
071032| nadīdurgaṃ hi hastistambhasaṃkramasetubandhanaubhiḥ sādhyamanityagāmbhīryamavasrāvyudakaṃ ca ||
071033| pārvataṃ tu svārakṣaṃ duruparodhi kṛcchrārohaṇam, bhagne caikasminna sarvavadhaḥ, śilāvṛkṣapramokṣaśca mahāpakāriṇām ||
071034| nimnasthalayodhibhyo nimnayodhibhyo bhūmilābhaḥ śreyān ||
071035| nimnayodhino hyuparuddhadeśakālāḥ, sthalayodhinastu sarvadeśakālayodhinaḥ ||
071036| khanakākāśayodhibhyaḥ khanakebhyo bhūmilābhaḥ śreyān ||
071037| khanakā hi khātena śastreṇa cobhayathā yudhyante, śastreṇaivākāśayodhinaḥ ||
071038  evaṃvidhyebhyaḥ pṛthivīṃ labhamānoarthaśāstravit |
071038  saṃhitebhyaḥ parebhyaśca viśeṣamadhigacchati ||E

(mitrahiraṇyabhūmikarmasamdhayah- tatra anavasitasamdhih)
071101| "tvaṃ cāhaṃ ca śūnyaṃ niveśayāvahe" ityanavasitasaṃdhiḥ ||
071102| tayoryaḥ pratyupasthitārtho yathoktaguṇāṃ bhūmiṃ niveśayati soatisaṃdhatte ||
071103| tatrāpi sthalamaudakaṃ veti mahataḥ sthalādalpamaudakaṃ śreyaḥ, sātatyādavasthitatvācca phalānām ||
071104| sthalayorapi prabhūtapūrvāparasasyamalpavarṣapākamasaktārambhaṃ śreyaḥ ||
071105| audakayorapi dhānyavāpamadhānyavāpātśreyaḥ ||
071106| tayoralpabahutve dhānyakāntādalpānmahadadhānyakāntaṃ śreyaḥ ||
071107| mahatyavakāśe hi sthālyāścānūpyāścauṣadhayo bhavanti ||
071108| durgādīni ca karmāṇi prabhūtyena kriyante ||
071109| kṛtrimā hi bhūmiguṇāḥ ||
071110| khanidhānyabhogayoḥ khanibhogaḥ kośakaraḥ, dhānyabhogaḥ kośakoṣṭhāgārakaraḥ ||
071111| dhānyamūlā hi durgādīnāṃ karmaṇāmārambhāḥ ||
071112| mahāviṣayavikrayo vā khanibhogaḥ śreyān ||
071113| "dravyahastivanabhogayordravyavanabhogaḥ sarvakarmaṇāṃ yoniḥ prabhūtanidhānakṣamaśca, viparīto hastivanabhogaḥ" ityācāryāḥ ||
071114| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071115| śakyaṃ dravyavanamanekamanekasyāṃ bhūmau vāpayitum, na hastivanam ||
071116| hastipradhāno hi parānīkavadha iti ||
071117| vāristhalapathabhogayoranityo vāripathabhogaḥ, nityaḥ sthalapathabhogaḥ ||
071118| bhinnamanuṣyā śreṇīmanuṣyā vā bhūmiriti bhinnamanuṣyā śreyasī ||
071119| bhinnamanuṣyā bhogyā bhavati, anupajāpyā cānyeṣām, anāpatsahā tu ||
071120| viparītā śreṇīmanuṣyā, kope mahādoṣā ||
071121| tasyāṃ cāturvarṇyaniveśe sarvabhogasahatvādavaravarṇaprāyā śreyasī, bāhulyāddhruvatvācca kṛṣyāḥ karṣakavatī, kṛṣyāścānyeṣāṃ cārambhāṇāṃ prayojakatvātgorakṣakavatī, paṇyanicayarṇānugrahādāḍhyavaṇigvatī ||
071122| bhūmiguṇānāmapāśrayaḥ śreyān ||
071123| durgāpāśrayā puruṣāpāśrayā vā bhūmiriti puruṣāpāśrayā śreyasī ||
071124| puruṣavaddhi rājyam ||
071125| apuruṣā gaurvandhyeva kiṃ duhīta ||
071126| mahākṣayavyayaniveśāṃ tu bhūmimavāptukāmaḥ pūrvameva kretāraṃ paṇeta durbalamarājabījinaṃ nirutsāhamapakṣamanyāyavṛttiṃ vyasaninaṃ daivapramāṇaṃ yatkiṃcanakāriṇaṃ vā ||
071127| mahākṣayavyayaniveśāyāṃ hi bhūmau durbalo rājabījī niviṣṭaḥ sagandhābhiḥ prakṛtibhiḥ saha kṣayavyayenāvasīdati ||
071128| balavānarājabījī kṣayavyayabhayādasagandhābhiḥ prakṛtibhistyajyate ||
071129| nirutsāhastu daṇḍavānapi daṇḍasyāpraṇetā sadaṇḍaḥ kṣayavyayenāvabhajyate ||
071130| kośavānapyapakṣaḥ kṣayavyayānugrahahīnatvānna kutaścitprāpnoti ||
071131| anyāyavṛttirniviṣṭamapyutthāpayet ||
071132| sa kathamaniviṣṭaṃ niveśayet ||
071133| tena vyasanī vyākhyātaḥ ||
071134| daivapramāṇo mānuṣahīno nirārambho vipannakarmārambho vāvasīdati ||
071135| yatkiṃcanakārī na kiṃcidāsādayati ||
071136| sa caiṣāṃ pāpiṣṭhatamo bhavati ||
071137| "yatkiṃcidārabhamāṇo hi vijigīṣoḥ kadācicchidramāsādayed" ityācāryāḥ ||
071138| yathā chidraṃ tathā vināśamapyāsādayediti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071139| teṣāmalābhe yathā pārṣṇigrāhopagrahe vakṣyāmastathā bhūmimavasthāpayet ||
071140| ityabhihitasaṃdhiḥ ||
071141| guṇavatīmādeyāṃ vā bhūmiṃ balavatā krayeṇa yācitaḥ saṃdhimavasthāpya dadyāt ||
071142| ityanibhṛtasaṃdhiḥ ||
071143| samena vā yācitaḥ kāraṇamavekṣya dadyāt"pratyādeyā me bhūmirvaśyā vā, anayā pratibaddhaḥ paro me vaśyo bhaviṣyati bhūmivikrayādvā mitrahiraṇyalābhaḥ kāryasāmarthyakaro me bhaviṣyati" iti ||
071144| tena hīnaḥ kretā vyākhyātaḥ ||
071145  evaṃ mitraṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca sajanāmajanāṃ ca gām |
071145  labhamānoatisaṃdhatte śāstravitsāmavāyikān ||E

(mitrahiraṇyabhūmikarmasamdhayah, tatra karmasamdhih)
071201| "tvaṃ cāhaṃ ca durgaṃ kārayāvahe" iti karmasaṃdhiḥ ||
071202| tayoryo daivakṛtamaviṣahyamalpavyayārambhaṃ durgaṃ kārayati soatisaṃdhatte ||
071203| tatrāpi sthalanadīparvatadurgāṇāmuttarottaraṃ śreyaḥ ||
071204| setubandhayorapyāhāryodakātsahodakaḥ śreyān ||
071205| sahodakayorapi prabhūtavāpasthānaḥ śreyān ||
071206| dravyavanayorapi yo mahatsāravaddravyāṭavīkaṃ viṣayānte nadīmātṛkaṃ dravyavanaṃ chedayati soatisaṃdhatte ||
071207| nadīmātṛkaṃ hi svājīvamapāśrayaścāpadi bhavati ||
071208| hastivanayorapi yo bahuśūramṛgaṃ durbalaprativeśaṃnantāvakleśi viṣayānte hastivanaṃ badhnāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071209| tatrāpi "bahukuṇṭhālpaśūrayoḥ alpaśūraṃ śreyaḥ, śūreṣu hi yuddham, alpāḥ śūrā bahūnaśūrān bhañjanti, te bhagnāḥ svasainyāvaghātino bhavanti" ityācāryāḥ ||
071210| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071211| kuṇṭhā bahavaḥ śreyāṃsaḥ, skandhaviniyogādanekaṃ karma kurvāṇāḥ sveṣāmapāśrayo yuddhe, pareṣāṃ durdharṣā vibhīṣaṇāśca ||
071212| bahuṣu hi kuṇṭheṣu vinayakarmaṇā śakyaṃ śauryamādhātum, na tvevālpeṣu śūreṣu bahutvamiti ||
071213| khanyorapi yaḥ prabhūtasārāmadurgamārgāmalpavyayārambhāṃ khaniṃ khānayati, soatisaṃdhatte ||
071214| tatrāpi mahāsāramalpamalpasāraṃ vā prabhūtamiti "mahāsāramalpaṃ śreyaḥ, vajramaṇimuktāpravālahemarūpyadhāturhi prabhūtamalpasāramatyargheṇa grasate" ityācāryāḥ ||
071215| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071216| cirādalpo mahāsārasya kretā vidyate, prabhūtaḥ sātatyādalpasārasya ||
071217| etena vaṇikpatho vyākhyātaḥ ||
071218| tatrāpi "vāristhalapathayorvāripathaḥ śreyān, alpavyayavyāyāmaḥ prabhūtapaṇyodayaśca" ityācāryāḥ ||
071219| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071220| samruddhagatirasārvakālikaḥ prakṛṣṭabhayayonirniṣpratīkāraśca vāripathaḥ, viparītaḥ sthalapathaḥ ||
071221| vāripathe tu kūlasamyānapathayoḥ kūlapathaḥ paṇyapattanabāhulyātśreyān, nadīpatho vā, sātatyādviṣahyābādhatvācca ||
071222| sthalapathepi "haimavato dakṣiṇāpathātśreyān, hastyaśvagandhadantājinarūpyasuvarṇapaṇyāḥ sāravattarāḥ" ityācāryāḥ" ||
071223| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071224| kambalājināśvapaṇyavarjāḥ śaṅkhavajramaṇimuktāsuvarṇapaṇyāśca prabhūtatarā dakṣiṇāpathe ||
071225| dakṣiṇāpathepi bahukhaniḥ sārapaṇyaḥ prasiddhagatiralpavyayavyāyāmo vā vaṇikpathaḥ śreyān, prabhūtaviṣayo vā phalgupuṇyaḥ ||
071226| tena pūrvaḥ paścimaśca vaṇikpatho vyākhyātaḥ ||
071227| tatrāpi cakrapādapathayoścakrapatho vipulārambhatvātśreyān, deśakālasambhāvano vā kharoṣṭrapathaḥ ||
071228| ābhyāmaṃsapatho vyākhyātaḥ ||
071229| parakarmodayo netuḥ kṣayo vṛddhirviparyaye ||
071230| tulye karmapathe sthānaṃ jñeyaṃ svaṃ vijigīṣuṇā ||
071231| alpāgamātivyayatā kṣayo vṛddhirviparyaye ||
071232| samāyavyayatā sthānaṃ karmasu jñeyamātmanaḥ ||
071233| tasmādalpavyayārambhaṃ durgādiṣu mahodayam ||
071234| karma labdhvā viśiṣṭaḥ syādityuktāḥ karmasaṃdhayaḥ ||E

(pārṣṇigrāhcintā)
071301| saṃhatyārivijigīṣvoramitrayoḥ parābhiyoginoḥ pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇatoryaḥ śaktisampannasya pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071302| śaktisampanno hyamitramucchidya pārṣṇigrāhamucchindyāt, na hīnaśaktiralabdhalābhaḥ ||
071303| śaktisāmye yo vipulārambhasya pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071304| vipulārambho hyamitramucchidya pārṣṇigrāhamucchindyāt, nālpārambhaḥ saktacakraḥ ||
071305| ārambhasāmye yaḥ sarvasaṃdohena prayātasya pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071306| śūnyamūlo hyasya sukaro bhavati, naikadeśabalaprayātaḥ kṛtapārṣṇipratividhānaḥ ||
071307| balopādānasāmye yaścalāmitraṃ prayātasya pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071308| calāmitraṃ prayāto hi sukhenāvāptasiddhiḥ pārṣṇigrāhamucchindyāt, na sthitāmitraṃ prayātaḥ ||
071309| asau hi durgapratihataḥ pārṣṇigrāhe ca pratinivṛttaḥ sthitenāmitreṇāvagṛhyate ||
071310| tena pūrve vyākhyātāḥ ||
071311| śatrusāmye yo dhārmikābhiyoginaḥ pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071312| dhārmikābhiyogī hi sveṣāṃ pareṣāṃ ca dveṣyo bhavati, adhārmikābhiyogī sampriyaḥ ||
071313| tena mūlaharatādātvikakadaryābhiyogināṃ pārṣṇigrahaṇaṃ vyākhyātam ||
071314| mitrābhiyoginoḥ pārṣṇigrahaṇe ta eva hetavaḥ ||
071315| mitramamitraṃ cābhiyuñjānayoryo mitrābhiyoginaḥ pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071316| mitrābhiyogī hi sukhenāvāptasiddhiḥ pārṣṇigrāhamucchindyāt ||
071317| sukaro hi mitreṇa saṃdhirnāmitreṇa ||
071318| mitramamitraṃ coddharatoryoamitroddhāriṇaḥ pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇāti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071319| vṛddhamitro hyamitroddhārī pārṣṇigrāhamucchindyāt, netaraḥ svapakṣopaghātī ||
071320| tayoralabdhalābhāpagamane yasyāmitro mahato lābhādviyuktaḥ kṣayavyayādhiko vā sa pārṣṇigrāhoatisaṃdhatte ||
071321| labdhalābhāpagamane yasyāmitro lābhena śaktyā hīnaḥ sa pārṣṇigrāhoatisaṃdhatte, yasya vā yātavyaḥ śatrorvigrahāpakārasamarthaḥ syāt ||
071322| pārṣṇigrāhayorapi yaḥ śakyārambhabalopādānādhikaḥ sthitaśatruḥ pārśvasthāyī vā soatisaṃdhatte ||
071323| pārśvasthāyī hi yātavyābhisāro mūlābādhakaśca bhavati, mūlābādhaka eva paścātsthāyī ||
071324  pārṣṇigrāhāstrayo jñeyāḥ śatrośceṣṭānirodhakāḥ |
071324  sāmantaḥ pṛṣṭhato vargaḥ prativeśau ca pārśvayoḥ ||
071325  arernetuśca madhyastho durbaloantardhirucyate |
071325  pratighāto balavato durgāṭavyapasāravān ||
071326| madhyamaṃ tvarivijigīṣvorlipsamānayormadhyamasya pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇatorlabdhalābhāpagamane yo madhyamaṃ mitrādviyojayatyamitraṃ ca mitramāpnoti soatisaṃdhatte ||
071327| saṃdheyaśca śatrurupakurvāṇo, na mitraṃ mitrabhāvādutkrāntam ||
071328| tenodāsīnalipsā vyākhyātā ||
071329| "pārṣṇigrahaṇābhiyānayostu mantrayuddhādabhyuccayaḥ ||
Kaś071330| vyāyāmayuddhe hi kṣayavyayābhyāmubhayoravṛddhiḥ ||
071331| jitvāpi hi kṣiṇadaṇḍakośaḥ parājito bhavati" ityācāryāḥ ||
071332| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071333| sumahatāpi kṣayavyayena śatruvināśoabhyupagantavyaḥ ||
071334| tulye kṣayavyaye yaḥ purastāddūṣyabalaṃ ghātayitvā nihśalyaḥ paścādvaśyabalo yudhyeta soatisaṃdhatte ||
071335| dvayorapi purastāddūṣyabalaghātinoryo bahulataraṃ śaktimattaramatyantadūṣyaṃ ca ghātayetsoatisaṃdhatte ||
071336| tenāmitrāṭavībalaghāto vyākhyātaḥ ||
071337  pārṣṇigrāhoabhiyoktā vā yātavyo vā yadā bhavet |
071337  vijigīṣustadā tatra netrametatsamācaret ||
071338  pārṣṇigrāho bhavennetā śatrormitrābhiyoginaḥ |
071338  vigrāhya pūrvamākrandaṃ pārṣṇigrāhābhisāriṇā ||
071339  ākrandenābhiyuñjānaḥ pārṣṇigrāhaṃ nivārayet |
071339  tathākrandābhisāreṇa pārṣṇigrāhābhisāriṇam ||
071340  arimitreṇa mitraṃ ca purastādavaghaṭṭayet |
071340  mitramitramareścāpi mitramitreṇa vārayet ||
071341  mitreṇa grāhayetpārṣṇimabhiyuktoabhiyoginaḥ |
071341  mitramitreṇa cākrandaṃ pārṣṇigrāhānnivārayet ||
071342  evaṃ maṇḍalamātmārthaṃ vijigīṣurniveśayet |
071342  pṛṣṭhataśca purastācca mitraprakṛtisampadā ||
071343  kṛtsne ca maṇḍale nityaṃ dūtān gūḍhāṃśca vāsayet |
071343  mitrabhūtaḥ sapatnānāṃ hatvā hatvā ca saṃvṛtaḥ ||
071344  asaṃvṛtasya kāryāṇi prāptānyapi viśeṣataḥ |
071344  nihsaṃśayaṃ vipadyante bhinnaplava ivodadhau ||E

(hīnaśaktipūraṇam)
071401| sāmavāyikairevamabhiyukto vijigīṣuryasteṣāṃ pradhānastaṃ brūyāt"tvayā me saṃdhiḥ, idaṃ hiraṇyam, ahaṃ ca mitram, dviguṇā te vṛddhiḥ, nārhasyātmakṣayeṇa mitramukhānamitrān vardhayitum, ete hi vṛddhāstvāmeva paribhaviṣyanti" iti ||
071402| bhedaṃ vā brūyāt"anapakāro yathāhametaiḥ sambhūyābhiyuktastathā tvāmapyete saṃhitabalāḥ svasthā vyasane vābhiyokṣyante, balaṃ hi cittaṃ vikaroti, tadeṣāṃ vighātaya" iti ||
071403| bhinneṣu pradhānamupagṛhya hīneṣu vikramayet, hīnānanugrāhya vā pradhāne, yathā vā śreyoabhimanyeta tathā ||
071404| vairaṃ vā parairgrāhayitvā visaṃvādayet ||
071405| phalabhūyastvena vā pradhānamupajāpya saṃdhiṃ kārayet ||
071406| athobhayavetanāḥ phalabhūyastvaṃ darśayantaḥ sāmavāyikān "atisaṃhitāḥ stha" ityudddūṣayeyuḥ ||
071407| duṣṭeṣu saṃdhiṃ dūṣayet ||
071408| athobhayavetanā bhūyo bhedameṣāṃ kuryuḥ "evaṃ tadyadasmābhirdarśitam" iti ||
071409| bhinneṣvanyatamopagraheṇa ceṣṭeta ||
071410| pradhānābhāve sāmavāyikānāmutsāhayitāraṃ sthirakarmāṇamanuraktaprakṛktiṃ lobhādbhayādvā saṃghātamupāgataṃ vijigīṣorbhītaṃ rājyapratisambaddhaṃ mitraṃ calāmitraṃ vā pūrvānuttarābhāve sādhayet- utsāhayitāramātmanisargeṇa, sthirakarmāṇaṃ sāntvapraṇipātena, anuraktaprakṛtiṃ kanyādānayāpanābhyām, lubdhamaṃśadvaiguṇyena,
bhītamebhyaḥ kośadaṇḍānugraheṇa, svato bhītaṃ viśvāsya pratibhūpradānena, rājyapratisambaddhamekībhāvopagamanena, mitramubhayataḥ priyahitābhyām, upakāratyāgena vā, calāmitramavadhṛtamanapakāropakārābhyām
||
071411| yo vā yathāyogaṃ bhajeta taṃ tathā sādhayet, sāmadānabhedadaṇḍairvā yathāpatsu vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
071412| vyasanopaghātatvarito vā kośadaṇḍābhyāṃ deśe kāle kārye vāvadhṛtaṃ saṃdhimupeyāt ||
071413| kṛtasaṃdhirhīnamātmānaṃ pratikurvīta ||
071414| pakṣe hīno bandhumitrapakṣaṃ kurvīta, durgamaviṣahyaṃ vā ||
071415| durgamitrapratiṣṭabdho hi sveṣāṃ pareṣāṃ ca pūjyo bhavati ||
071416| mantraśaktihīnaḥ prājñapuruṣopacayaṃ vidyāvṛddhasamyogaṃ vā kurvīta ||
071417| tathā hi sadyaḥ śreyaḥ prāpnoti ||
