071418| prabhāvahīnaḥ prakṛtiyogakṣemasiddhau yateta ||
071419| janapadaḥ sarvakarmaṇāṃ yoniḥ, tataḥ prabhāvaḥ ||
071420| tasya sthānamātmanaścāpadi durgam ||
071421| setubandhaḥ sasyānāṃ yoniḥ ||
071422| nityānuṣakto hi varṣaguṇalābhaḥ setuvāpeṣu ||
071423| vaṇikpathaḥ parātisaṃdhānasya yoniḥ ||
071424| vaṇikpathena hi daṇḍagūḍhapuruṣātinayanaṃ śastrāvaraṇayānavāhanakrayaśca kriyate, praveśo nirṇayanaṃ ca ||
071425| khaniḥ saṃgrāmopakaraṇānāṃ yoniḥ, dravyavanaṃ durgakarmaṇāṃ yānarathayośca, hastivanaṃ hastinām, gavāśvakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ ca vrajaḥ ||
071426| teṣāmalābhe bandhumitrakulebhyaḥ samārjanam ||
071427| utsāhahīnaḥ śreṇīpravīrapuruṣāṇāṃ coragaṇāṭavikamlecchajātīnāṃ parāpakāriṇāṃ gūḍhapuruṣāṇāṃ ca yathālābbhamupacayaṃ kurvīta ||
071428| paramiśrāpratīkāramābalīyasaṃ vā pareṣu prayuñjīta ||
071429  evaṃ pakṣeṇa mantreṇa dravyeṇa ca balena ca |
071429  sampannaḥ pratinirgacchetparāvagrahamātmanaḥ ||E

(vigṛhyoparodhahetavah- daṇḍopanatavṛttam)
071501| durbalo rājā balavatābhiyuktastadviśiṣṭabalamāśrayeta yamitaro mantraśaktyā nātisaṃdadhyāt ||
071502| tulyamantraśaktīnāmāyattasampado vṛddhasamyogādvā viśeṣaḥ ||
071503| viśiṣṭabalābhāve samabalaistulyabalasaṃghairvā balavataḥ sambhūya tiṣṭhedyānna mantraprabhāvaśaktibhyāmatisaṃdadhyāt ||
071504| tulyamantraprabhāvaśaktīnāṃ vipulārambhato viśeṣaḥ ||
071505| samabalābhāve hīnabalaiḥ śucibhirutsāhibhiḥ pratyanīkabhūtairbalavataḥ sambhūya tiṣṭhedyānna mantraprabhāvotsāhaśaktibhiratisaṃdadhyāt ||
071506| tulyotsāhaśaktīnāṃ svayuddhabhūmilābhādviśeṣaḥ ||
071507| tulyabhūmīnāṃ svayuddhakālalābhādviśeṣaḥ ||
071508| tulyadeśakālānāṃ yugyaśastrāvaraṇato viśeṣaḥ ||
071509| sahāyābhāve durgamāśrayeta yatrāmitraḥ prabhūtasainyoapi bhaktayavasendhanodakoparodhaṃ na kuryātsvayaṃ ca kṣayavyayābhyāṃ yujyeta ||
071510| tulyadurgāṇāṃ nicayāpasārato viśeṣaḥ ||
071511| nicayāpasārasampannaṃ hi manuṣyadurgamicchediti kauṭilyaḥ ||071512 tadebhiḥ kārṇairāśrayeta - "pārṣṇigrāhamāsāraṃ madhyamamudāsīnaṃ vā pratipādayiṣyāmi, sāmantāṭavikatatkulīnāparuddhānāmanyatamenāsya rājyaṃ hārayiṣyāmi ghātayiṣyāmi vā - ||
071512  kṛtyapakṣopagraheṇa vāsya durge rāṣṭre skandhāvāre vā kopaṃ samutthāpayiṣyāmi, śastrāgnirasapraṇidhānairaupaniṣadikairvā yatheṣṭamāsannaṃ haniṣyāmi - ||
071512  svayaṃdhiṣṭhitena vā yogapraṇidhānena kṣayavyayamenamupaneṣyāmi, kṣayavyayapravāsopatapte vāsya mitravarge sainye vā krameṇopajāpaṃ prāpsyāmi - ||
071512  vīvadhāsāraprasāravadhena vāsya skandhāvārāvagrahaṃ kariṣyāmi, daṇḍopanayena vāsya randhramutthāpya sarvasaṃdohena prahariṣyāmi, pratihatotsāhena vā yatheṣṭaṃ saṃdhimavāpsyāmi, mayi pratibaddhasya vā sarvataḥ kopāḥ samutthāsyanti - ||
071512  nirāsāraṃ vāsya mūlaṃ mitrāṭavīdaṇḍairuddhātayiṣyāmi, mahato vā deśasya yogakṣemamihasthaḥ pālayiṣyāmi, svavikṣiptaṃ mitravikṣiptaṃ vā me sainyamihasthasyaikasthamaviṣahyaṃ bhaviṣyati, nimnakhātarātriyuddhaviśāradaṃ vā me sainyaṃ pathyābādhamuktamāsanne karma kariṣyati - ||
071512  viruddhadeśakālamihāgato vā svayameva kṣayavyayābhyāṃ na bhaviṣyati, mahākṣayavyayābhigamyoayaṃ deśo durgāṭavyapasārabāhulyāt- ||
071512  | pareṣāṃ vyādhiprāyaḥ sainyavyāyāmānāmalabdhabhaumaśca, tamāpadgataḥ pravekṣyati, praviṣṭo vā na nirgamiṣyati" iti ||
071513| "kāraṇābhāve balasamucchraye vā parasya durgamunmucyāpagacchet ||
071514| agnipataṅgavadamitre vā praviśet ||
071515| anyatarasiddhirhi tyaktātmano bhavati" ityācāryāḥ ||
071516| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071517| saṃdheyatāmātmanaḥ parasya copalabhya saṃdadhīta ||
071518| viparyaye vikrameṇa saṃdhimapasāraṃ vā lipseta ||
071519| saṃdheyasya vā dūtaṃ preṣayet ||
071520| tena vā preṣitamarthamānābhyāṃ satkṛtya brūyāt"idaṃ rājñaḥ paṇyāgāram, idaṃ devīkumārāṇām, devīkumāravacanāt, idaṃ rājyamahaṃ ca tvadarpaṇaḥ" iti ||
071521| labdhasaṃśrayaḥ samayācārikavadbhartari varteta ||
071522| durgādīni ca karmāṇi āvāhavivāhaputrābhiṣekāśvapaṇyahastigrahaṇasattrayātrāvihāragamanāni cānujñātaḥ kurvīta ||
071523| svabhūmyavasthitaprakṛtisaṃdhimupaghātamapasṛteṣu vā sarvamanujñātaḥ kurvīta ||
071524| duṣṭapaurajānapado vā nyāyavṛttiranyāṃ bhūmiṃ yāceta ||
071525| duṣyavadupāṃśudaṇḍena vā pratikurvīta ||
071526| ucitāṃ vā mitrādbhūmiṃ dīyamānāṃ na pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
071527| mantripurohitasenāpatiyuvarājānāmanyatamamadṛśyamāne bhartari paśyet, yathāśakti copakuryāt ||
071528| daivatasvastivācaneṣu tatparā āśiṣo vācayet ||
071529| sarvatrātmanisargaṃ guṇaṃ brūyāt ||
071530  samyuktabalavatsevī viruddhaḥ śaṅkitādibhiḥ |
071530  varteta daṇḍopanato bhartaryevamavasthitaḥ ||E

(daṇḍopanāyivṛttam)
071601| anujñātasaṃdhipaṇodvegakaraṃ balavān vijigīṣamāṇo yataḥ svabhūmiḥ svartuvṛttiśca svasainyānām, adurgāpasāraḥ śatrurapārṣṇiranāsāraśca, tato yāyāt ||
071602| viparyaye kṛtapratīkāro yāyāt ||
071603| sāmadānābhyāṃ durbalānupanamayet, bhedadaṇḍābhyāṃ balavataḥ ||
071604| niyogavikalpasamuccayaiścopāyānāmanantaraikāntarāḥ prakṛtīḥ sādhayet ||
071605| grāmāraṇyopajīvivrajavaṇikpathānupālanamujjhitāpasṛtāpakāriṇāṃ cārpaṇamiti sāntvamācaret ||
071606| bhūmidravyakanyādānamabhayasya ceti dānamācaret ||
071607| sāmantāṭavikatatkulīnāparuddhānāmanyatamopagraheṇa kośadaṇḍabhūmidāyayācanamiti bhedamācaret ||
071608| prakāśakūṭatūṣṇīṃyuddhadurgalambhopāyairamitrapragrahaṇamiti daṇḍamācaret ||
071609| evamutsāhavato daṇḍopakāriṇaḥ sthāpayet, svaprabhāvavataḥ kośopakāriṇaḥ, prajñāvato bhūmyupakāriṇaḥ ||
071610| teṣāṃ paṇyapattanagrāmakhanisaṃjātena ratnasāraphalgukupyena dravyahastivanavrajasamutthena yānavāhanena vā yadbahuśa upakaroti taccitrabhogam ||
071611| yaddaṇḍena kośena vā mahadupakaroti tanmahābhogam ||
071612| yaddaṇḍakośabhūmībhirupakaroti tatsarvabhogam ||
071613| yadamitramekataḥ pratikaroti tadekatobhogi ||
071614| yadamitramāsāraṃ cobhayataḥ pratikaroti tadubhayatobhogi ||
071615| yadamitrāsāraprativeśāṭavikān sarvataḥ pratikaroti tatsarvatobhogi ||
071616  pārṣṇigrāhaścāṭavikaḥ śatrumukhyaḥ śatrurvā bhūmidānasādhyaḥ kaścidāsādyeta, nirguṇayā bhūmyainamupagrāhayet, apratisambaddhayā durgastham, nirupajīvyayāṭavikaṃ - ||
071616  pratyādeyayā tatkulīnaṃ śatroḥ, apacchinnayā śatroraparuddhaṃ nityāmitrayā śreṇībalam, balavatsāmantayā saṃhatabalam, ubhābhyāṃ yuddhe pratilomam, - ||
071616  alabdhavyāyāmayotsāhinam, śūyayāripakṣīyam, karśitayāpavāhitam, mahākṣayavyayaniveśayā gatapratyāgatam, anapāśrayayā pratyapasṛtam, pareṇānadhivāsyayā svayameva bhartāramupagrāhayet ||
071617| teṣāṃ mahopakāraṃ nirvikāraṃ cānuvartayet ||
071618| pratilomamupāṃśunā sādhayet ||
071619| upakāriṇamupakāraśaktyā toṣayet ||
071620| prayāsataścārthamānau kuryād, vyasaneṣu cānugraham ||
071621| svayaṃāgatānāṃ yatheṣṭadarśanaṃ pratividhānaṃ ca kuryāt ||
071622| paribhavopaghātakutsātivādāṃścaiṣu na prayuñjīta ||
071623| dattvā cābhayaṃ pitevānugṛhṇīyāt ||
071624| yaścāsyāpakuryāttaddoṣamabhivikhyāpya prakāśamenaṃ ghātayet ||
071625| parodvegakāraṇādvā dāṇḍakarmikavacceṣṭeta ||
071626| na ca hatasya bhūmidravyaputradārānabhimanyeta ||
071627| kulyānapyasya sveṣu pātreṣu sthāpayet ||
071628| karmaṇi mṛtasya putraṃ rājye sthāpayet ||
071629| evamasya daṇḍopanatāḥ putrapautrānanuvartante ||
071630| yastūpanatān hatvā baddhvā vā bhūmidravyaputradārānabhimanyeta tasyodvignaṃ maṇḍalamabhāvāyottiṣṭhate ||
071631| ye cāsyāmātyāḥ svabhūmiṣvāyattāste cāsyodvignā maṇḍalamāśrayante ||
071632| svayaṃ vā rājyaṃ prāṇān vāsyābhimanyante ||
071633  svabhūmiṣu ca rājānastasmātsāmnānupālitāḥ |
071633  bhavantyanuguṇā rājñaḥ putrapautrānuvartinaḥ ||E

(samdhikarma -samādhimokṣah)
071701| śamaḥ saṃdhiḥ samādhirityekoarthaḥ ||
071702| rājñāṃ viśvāsopagamaḥ śamaḥ saṃdhiḥ samādhiriti ||
071703| "satyaṃ śapatho vā calaḥ saṃdhiḥ, pratibhūḥ pratigraho vā sthāvaraḥ" ityācāryāḥ ||
071704| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
071705| satyaṃ śapatho vā paratreha ca sthāvaraḥ saṃdhiḥ, ihārtha eva pratibhūḥ pratigraho vā balāpekṣaḥ ||
071706| "saṃhitāḥ smaḥ" iti satyasaṃdhāḥ pūrve rājānaḥ satyena saṃdadhire ||
071707| tasyātikrame śapathena agnyudakasītāprākāraloṣṭahastiskandhāśvapṛṣṭarathopasthaśastraratnabījagandharasasuvarṇahiraṇyānyālebhire "hanyuretāni tyajeyuścainaṃ yaḥ śapathamatikrāmet" iti ||
071708| śapathātikrame mahatāṃ tapasvināṃ mukhyānāṃ vā prātibhāvyabandhaḥ pratibhūḥ ||
071709| tasmin yaḥ parāvagrahasamarthān pratibhuvo gṛhṇāti, soatisaṃdhatte ||
071710| viparītoatisaṃdhīyate ||
071711| bandhumukhyapragrahaḥ pratigrahaḥ ||
071712| tasmin yo dūṣyāmātyaṃ dūṣyāpatyaṃ vā dadāti, soatisaṃdhatte ||
071713| viparītoatisaṃdhīyate
071714| pratigrahagrahaṇaviśvastasya hi paraśchidreṣu nirapekṣaḥ praharati ||
071715| apatyasamādhau tu kanyāputradāne dadattu kanyāmatisaṃdhatte ||
071716| kanyā hyadāyādā pareṣāmevārthāyākleśyā(?) ca ||
071717| viparītaḥ putraḥ ||
071718| putrayorapi yo jātyaṃ prājñaṃ śūraṃ kṛtāstramekaputraṃ vā dadāti soatisaṃdhīyate ||
071719| viparītoatisaṃdhatte ||
071720| jātyādajātyo hi luptadāyādasaṃtānatvādādhātuṃ śreyān, prājñādaprājño mantraśaktilopāt, śūrādaśūra utsāhaśaktilopāt, kṛtāstrādakṛtāstraḥ prahartavyasampallopāt, ekaputrādanekaputro nirapekṣatvāt ||
071721| jātyaprājñayorjātyamaprājñamaiśvaryaprakṛtiranuvartate, prājñamajātyaṃ mantrādhikāraḥ ||
071722| mantrādhikārepi vṛddhasamyogājjātyaḥ prājñamatisaṃdhatte ||
071723| prājñaśūrayoḥ prājñamaśūraṃ matikarmaṇāṃ yogoanuvartate, śūramaprājñaṃ vikramādhikāraḥ ||
071724| vikramādhikārepi hastinamiva lubdhakaḥ prājñaḥ śūramatisaṃdhatteś ||
071725| śūrakṛtāstrayoḥ śūramakṛtāstraṃ vikramavyavasāyoanuvartate, kṛtāstramaśūraṃ lakṣyalambhādhikāraḥ ||
071726| lakṣyalambhādhikārepi sthairyapratipattyasammoṣaiḥ śūraḥ kṛtāstramatisaṃdhatte ||
071727| bahvekaputrayorbahuputra ekaṃ dattvā śeṣapratiṣṭabdhaḥ saṃdhimatikrāmati, netaraḥ ||
071728| putrasarvasvadāne saṃdhiścetputraphalato viśeṣaḥ ||
071729| samaphalayoḥ śaktaprajananato viśeṣaḥ ||
071730| śaktaprajananayorapyupasthitaprajananato viśeṣaḥ ||
071731| śaktimatyekaputre tu luptaputrotpattirātmānamādadhyāt, na caikaputramiti ||
071732| abhyuccīyamānaḥ samādhimokṣaṃ kārayet ||
071733| kumārāsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ kāruśilpivyañjanāḥ karmāṇi kurvāṇāḥ suruṅgayā rātrāvupakhānayitvā kumāramapahareyuḥ ||
071734| naṭanartakagāyanavādakavāgjīvanakuśīlavaplavakasaubhikā vā pūrvapraṇihitāḥ paramupatiṣṭheran ||
071735| te kumāraṃ paraṃparayopatiṣṭheran ||
071736| teṣāmaniyatakālapraveśasthānanirgamanāni sthāpayet ||
071737| tatastadvyañjano vā rātrau pratiṣṭheta ||
071738| tena rūpājīvā bhāryāvyañjanāśca vyākhyātāḥ ||
071739| teṣāṃ vā tūryabhāṇḍaphelāṃ gṛhītvā nirgacchet ||
071740| sūdārālikasnāpakasaṃvāhakāstarakakalpakaprasādhakodakaparicārakairvā dravyavastrabhāṇḍaphelāśayanāsanasambhogairnirhriyeta ||
071741| paricārakacchadmanā vā kiṃcidarūpavelāyāmādāya nirgacchet, suruṅgāmukhena vā niśopahāreṇa ||
071742| toyāśaye vā vāruṇaṃ yogamātiṣṭhet ||
071743| vaidehakavyañjanā vā pakvānnaphalavyavahāreṇārakṣiṣu rasamupacārayeyuḥ ||
071744| daivatopahāraśrāddhaprahavaṇanimittamārakṣiṣu madanayogayuktamannapānaṃ rasaṃ vā prayujyāpagacchet, ārakṣakaprotsāhanena vā ||
071745| nāgarakakuśīlavacikitsakāpūpikavyañjanā vā rātrau samṛddhagṛhāṇyādīpayeyuḥ ārakṣiṇāṃ vā ||
071746| vaidehakavyañjanā vā paṇyasaṃsthāmādīpayeyuḥ ||
071747| anyadvā śarīraṃ nikṣipya svagṛhamādīpayedanupātabhayāt ||
071748| tataḥ saṃdhicchedakhātasuruṅgābhirapagacchet ||
071749| kācakumbhabhāṇḍabhāravyañjano vā rātrau pratiṣṭheta ||
071750| muṇḍajaṭilānāṃ pravāsanānyanupraviṣṭo vā rātrau tadvyañjanaḥ pratiṣṭheta, virūpavyādhikaraṇāraṇyacaracchadmanāmanyatamena vā ||071751| pretavyañjano vā gūḍhairnirhriyeta ||
071752| pretaṃ vā strīveṣeṇānugacchet ||
071753| vanacaravyañjanāścainamanyato yāntamanyatoapadiśeyuḥ ||
071754| tatoanyato gacchet ||
071755| cakracarāṇāṃ vā śakaṭavāṭairapagacchet ||
071756| āsanne cānupāte sattraṃ vā gṛhṇīyāt ||
071757| sattrābhāve hiraṇyaṃ rasaviddhaṃ vā bhakṣyajātamubhayataḥpanthānamutsṛjet ||
071758| tatoanyatoapagacchet ||
071759| gṛhīto vā sāmādibhiranupātamatisaṃdadhyāt, rasaviddhena vā pathyadanena ||
071760| vāruṇayogāgnidāheṣu vā śarīramanyadādhāya śatrumabhiyuñjīta "putro me tvayā hataḥ" iti ||
071761  upāttacchannaśastro vā rātrau vikramya rakṣiṣu ||
071761  śīghrapātairapasaredgūḍhapraṇihitaiḥ saha ||E

(madhyamacaritam - udāsīnacaritam - maṇḍalacaritam)
071801| madhyamasyātmā tṛtīyā pañcamī ca prakṛtī prakṛtayaḥ ||
071802| dvitīyā caturthī ṣaṣṭhī ca vikṛtayaḥ ||
071803| taccedubhayaṃ madhyamoanugṛhṇīyāt, vijigīṣurmadhyamānulomaḥ syāt ||
071804| na cedanugṛhṇīyāt, prakṛtyanulomaḥ syāt ||
071805| madyamaścedvijigīṣormitraṃ mitrabhāvi lipseta, mitrasyātmanaśca mitrāṇyutthāpya madhyamācca mitrāṇi bhedayitvā mitraṃ trāyeta ||
071806| maṇḍalaṃ vā protsāhayet"atipravṛddhoayaṃ madhyamaḥ sarveṣāṃ no vināśāyābhyutthitaḥ, sambhūyāsya yātrāṃ vihanāma" iti ||
071807| taccenmaṇḍalamanugṛhṇīyāt, madhyamāvagraheṇātmānamupabṛṃhayet ||
071808| na cedanugṛhṇīyāt, kośadaṇḍābhyāṃ mitramanugṛhya ye madhyamadveṣiṇo rājānaḥ parasparānugṛhītā vā bahavastiṣṭheyuḥ, ekasiddhau vā bahavaḥ sidhyeyuḥ, parasparādvā śaṅkitā nottiṣṭheran, teṣāṃ pradhānamekamāsannaṃ vā sāmadānābhyāṃ labheta ||
071809| dviguṇo dvitīyaṃ trigunastṛtīyam ||
071810| evamabhyuccito madhyamamavagṛhṇīyāt ||
071811| deśakālātipattau vā saṃdhāya madhyamena mitrasya sācivyaṃ kuryāt, dūṣyeṣu vā karmasaṃdhim ||
071812| karśanīyaṃ vāsya mitraṃ madhyamo lipseta, pratistambhayedenaṃ "ahaṃ tvā trāyeya" iti ā karśanāt ||
071813| karśitamenaṃ trāyeta ||
071814| ucchedanīyaṃ vāsya mitraṃ madhyamo lipseta, karśitamenaṃ trāyeta madhyamavṛddhibhayāt ||
071815| ucchinnaṃ vā bhūmyanugraheṇa haste kuryādanyatrāpasārabhayāt ||
071816| karśanīyocchedanīyayoścenmitrāṇi madhyamasya sācivyakarāṇi syuḥ, puruṣāntareṇa saṃdhīyeta ||
071817| vijigīṣorvā tayormitrāṇyavagrahasamarthāni syuḥ, saṃdhimupeyāt ||
071818| amitraṃ vāsya madhyamo lipseta, saṃdhimupeyāt ||
071819| evaṃ svārthaśca kṛto bhavati madhyamasya priyaṃ ca ||
071820| madhyamaścetsvamitraṃ mitrabhāvi lipseta, puruṣāntareṇa saṃdadhyāt ||
071821| sāpekṣaṃ vā "nārhasi mitramucchettum" iti vārayet ||
071822| upekṣeta vā "maṇḍalamasya kupyatu svapakṣavadhāt" iti ||
071823| amitramātmano vā madhyamo lipseta, kośadaṇḍābhyāmenamadṛśyamānoanugṛhṇīyāt ||
071824| udāsīnaṃ vā madhyamo lipseta, asmai sāhāyyaṃ dadyād"udāsīnādbhidyatām" iti ||
071825| madhyamodāsīnayoryo maṇḍalasyābhipretastamāśrayeta ||
071826| madhyamacaritenodāsīnacaritaṃ vyākhyātam ||
071827| udāsīnaścenmadhyamaṃ lipseta, yataḥ śatrumatisaṃdadhyānmitrasyopakāraṃ kuryādudāsīnaṃ vā daṇḍopakāriṇaṃ labheta tataḥ pariṇameta ||
071828| evamupabṛhyātmānamariprakṛtiṃ karśayenmitraprakṛtiṃ copagṛhṇīyāt ||
071829  satyapyamitrabhāve tasyānātmavānnityāpakārī śatruḥ śatrusaṃhitaḥ pārṣṇigrāho vā vyasanī yātavyo vyasane vā neturabhiyoktā ityaribhāvinaḥ, ekārthābhiprayātaḥ pṛthagarthābhiprayātaḥ sambhūyayātrikaḥ saṃhitaprayāṇikaḥ svārthābhiprayātaḥ sāmutthāyikaḥ kośadaṇḍayoranyatarasya kretā vikretā vā dvaidhībhāvika iti mitrabhāvinaḥ,
- ||
071829  sāmanto balavataḥ pratighātoantardhiḥ prativeśo vā balavataḥ pārṣṇigrāho vā svayamupanataḥ pratāpopanato vā daṇḍopanata iti bhṛtyabhāvinaḥ sāmantāḥ ||
071830| tairbhūmyekāntarā vyākhyātāḥ ||
071831  teṣāṃ śatruvirodhe yanmitramekārthatāṃ vrajet |
071831  śaktyā tadanugṛhṇīyādviṣaheta yayā param ||
071832  prasādhya śatruṃ yanmitraṃ vṛddhaṃ gacchedavaśyatām |
071832  sāmantaikāntarābhyāṃ tatprakṛtibhyāṃ virodhayet ||
071833  tatkulīnāparuddhābhyāṃ bhūmiṃ vā tasya hārayet |
071833  yathā vānugrahāpekṣaṃ vaśyaṃ tiṣṭhettathā caret ||
071834  nopakuryādamitraṃ vā gacchedyadatikarśitam |
071834  tadahīnamavṛddhaṃ ca sthāpayenmitramarthavit ||
071835  arthayuktyā calaṃ mitraṃ saṃdhiṃ yadupagacchati |
071835  tasyāpagamane hetuṃ vihanyānna caledyathā ||
071836  arisādhāraṇaṃ yadvā tiṣṭhettadaritaḥ śaṭham |
071836  bhedayedbhinnamucchindyāttataḥ śatrumanantaram ||
071837  udāsīnaṃ ca yattiṣṭhetsāmantaistadvirodhayet |
071837  tato vigrahasaṃtaptamupakāre niveśayet ||
071838  amitraṃ vijigīṣuṃ ca yatsaṃcarati durbalam |
071838  tadbalenānugṛhṇīyādyathā syānna parānmukham ||
071839  apanīya tatoanyasyāṃ bhūmau vā sanniveśayet |
071839  niveśya pūrvaṃ tatrānyaddaṇḍānugrahahetunā ||
071840  apakuryātsamarthaṃ vā nopakuryādyadāpadi |
071840  ucchindyādeva tanmitraṃ viśvasyāṅkamupasthitam ||
071841  mitravyasanato vāriruttiṣṭhedyoanavagrahaḥ |
071841  mitreṇaiva bhavetsādhyaśchāditavyasanena saḥ ||
071841  amitravyasanānmitramutthitaṃ yadvirajyati |
071841  arivyasanasiddhyā tatśatruṇaiva prasidhyati ||
071842  vṛddhiṃ kṣayaṃ ca sthānaṃ ca karśanocchedanaṃ tathā ||
071842  sarvopāyān samādadhyādetān yaścārthaśāstravit |
071843  evamanyonyasaṃcāraṃ ṣāḍguṇyaṃ yoanupaśyati ||
071843  sa buddhinigalairbaddhairiṣṭaṃ krīḍati pārthivaiḥ ||E

(prakṛtivyasanavargah)
08101| vyasanayaugapadye saukaryato yātavyaṃ rakṣitavyaṃ veti vyasanacintā ||
08102| daivaṃ mānuṣaṃ vā prakṛtivyasanamanayāpanayābhyāṃ sambhavati ||
08103| guṇaprātilomyamabhāvaḥ pradoṣaḥ prasaṅgaḥ pīḍā vā vyasanam ||
08104| vyasyatyenaṃ śreyasa iti vyasanam ||
08105| "svāmyamātyajanapadadurgakośadaṇḍamitravyasanānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ garīyaḥ" ityācāryāḥ ||
08106| neti bharadvājaḥ ||
08107| "svāmyamātyavyasanayoramātyavyasanaṃ garīyaḥ ||
08108| mantro mantraphalāvāptiḥ karmānuṣṭhānamāyavyayakarma daṇḍapraṇayanamamitrāṭavīpratiṣedho rājyarakṣaṇaṃ vyasanapratīkāraḥ kumārarakṣaṇamabhiṣekaśca kumārāṇāmāyattamamātyeṣu ||
08109| teṣāmabhāve tadabhāvaḥ, chinnapakṣasyeva rājñaśceṣṭānāśaśca ||
08110| vyasaneṣu cāsannaḥ paropajāpaḥ ||
08111| vaiguṇye ca prāṇābādhaḥ prāṇāntikacaratvādrājñaḥ" iti ||
08112| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08113| mantripurohitādibhṛtyavargamadhyakṣapracāraṃ puruṣadravyaprakṛtivyasanapratīkāramedhanaṃ ca rājaiva karoti ||
08114| vyasaniṣu vāmātyeṣvanyānavyasaninaḥ karoti ||
08115| pūjyapūjane dūṣyāvagrahe ca nityayuktastiṣṭhati ||
08116| svāmī ca sampannaḥ svasampadbhiḥ prakṛtīḥ sampādayati ||
08117| sa yatśīlastatśīlāḥ prakṛtayo bhavanti, utthāne pramāde ca tadāyattatvāt ||
08118| tatkūṭasthānīyo hi svāmīti ||
08119| "amātyajanapadavyasanayorjanapadavyasanaṃ garīyaḥ" iti viśālākṣaḥ ||
08120| "kośo daṇḍaḥ kupyaṃ viṣṭirvāhanaṃ nicayāśca janapadāduttiṣṭhante ||
08121| teṣāmabhāvo janapadābhāve, svāmyamātyayoścānantaraḥ" iti ||
08122| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08123| amātyamūlāḥ sarvārambhāḥ - janapadasya karmasiddhayaḥ svataḥ parataśca yogakṣemasādhanaṃ vyasanapratīkāraḥ śūnyaniveśopacayau daṇḍakarānugrahaśceti ||
08124| "janapadadurgavyasanayordurgavyasanam" iti pārāśarāḥ ||
08125| "durge hi kośadaṇḍotpattirāpadi sthānaṃ ca janapadasya ||
08126| śaktimattarāśca paurā jānapadebhyo nityāścāpadi sahāyā rājñaḥ ||
08127| jānapadāstvamitrasādhāraṇāḥ" iti ||
08128| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08129| janapadamūlā durgakośadaṇḍasetuvārttārambhāḥ ||
08130| śauryaṃ sthairyaṃ dākṣyaṃ bāhulyaṃ ca jānapadeṣu ||
08131| parvatāntardvīpāśca durgā nādhyuṣyante janapadābhāvāt ||
08132| karṣakaprāye tu durgavyasanam, āyudhīyaprāye tu janapade janapadavyasanamiti ||
08133| "durgakośavyasanayoḥ kośavyasanam" iti piśunaḥ ||
08134| "kośamūlo hi durgasaṃskāro durgarakṣaṇaṃ janapadamitrāmitranigraho deśāntaritānāmutsāhanaṃ daṇḍabalavyavahāraśca ||
08135| durgaḥ kośādupajāpyaḥ pareṣām ||
08136| kośamādāya ca vyasane śakyamapayātum, na durgam" iti ||
08137| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08138| durgārpaṇaḥ kośo daṇḍastūṣṇīṃyuddhaṃ svapakṣanigraho daṇḍabalavyavahāra āsārapratigrahaḥ paracakrāṭavīpratiṣedhaśca ||
08139| durgābhāve ca kośaḥ pareṣām ||
08140| dṛśyate hi durgavatāmanucchittiriti ||
08141| "kośadaṇḍavyasanayordaṇḍavyasanam" iti kauṇapadantaḥ ||
08142| "daṇḍamūlo hi mitrāmitranigrahaḥ paradaṇḍotsāhanaṃ svadaṇḍapratigrahaśca ||
08143| daṇḍābhāve ca dhruvaḥ kośavināśaḥ ||
08144| kośābhāve ca śakyaḥ kupyena bhūmyā parabhūmisvayaṃgrāheṇa vā daṇḍaḥ piṇḍayitum, daṇḍavatā ca kośaḥ ||
08145| svāminaścāsannavṛttitvādamātyasadharmā daṇḍaḥ" iti ||
08146| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08147| kośamūlo hi daṇḍaḥ ||
08148| kośābhāve daṇḍaḥ paraṃ gacchati, svāminaṃ vā hanti ||
08149| sarvābhiyogakaraśca kośo dharmakāmahetuḥ ||
08150| deśakālakāryavaśena tu kośadaṇḍayoranyataraḥ pramāṇībhavati ||
08151| lambhapālano hi daṇḍaḥ kośasya, kośaḥ kośasya daṇḍasya ca bhavati ||
08152| sarvadravyaprayojakatvātkośavyasanaṃ garīya iti ||
08153| "daṇḍamitravyasanayormitravyasanam" iti vātavyādhiḥ ||
08154| "mitramabhṛtaṃ vyavahitaṃ ca karma karoti, pārṣṇigrāhamāsāramamitramāṭavikaṃ ca pratikaroti, kośadaṇḍabhūmibhiścopakaroti vyasanāvasthāyogam" iti ||
08155| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08156| daṇḍavato mitraṃ mitrabhāve tiṣṭhati, amitro vā mitrabhāve ||
08157| daṇḍamitrayostu sādhāraṇe kārye sārataḥ svayuddhadeśakālalābhādviśeṣaḥ ||
08158| śīghrābhiyāne tvamitrāṭavikānabhyantarakope ca na mitraṃ vidyate ||
08159| vyasanayaugapadye paravṛddhau ca mitramarthayuktau tiṣṭhati ||
08160| iti prakṛtivyasanasampradhāraṇamuktam ||
08161  prakṛtyavayavānāṃ tu vyasanasya viśeṣataḥ |
08161  bahubhāvoanurāgo vā sāro vā kāryasādhakaḥ ||
08162  dvayostu vyasane tulye viśeṣo guṇataḥ kṣayāt |
08162  śeṣaprakṛtisādguṇyaṃ yadi syānnāvidheyakam ||
08163  śeṣaprakṛtināśastu yatraikavyasanādbhavet |
08163  vyasanaṃ tadgarīyaḥ syātpradhānasyetarasya vā ||E

(rājarājyayorvyasanacintā)
08201| rājā rājyamiti prakṛtisaṃkṣepaḥ ||
08202| rājñoabhyantaro bāhyo vā kopa iti ||
08203| ahibhayādabhyantaraḥ kopo bāhyakopātpāpīyān, antaramātyakopaścāntaḥkopāt ||
08204| tasmātkośadaṇḍaśaktimātmasaṃsthāṃ kurvīta ||
08205| "dvairājyavairājyayordvairājyamanyonyapakṣadveṣānurāgābhyāṃ parasparasaṃgharṣeṇa vā vinaśyati, vairājyaṃ tu prakṛticittagrahaṇāpekṣi yathāsthitamanyairbhujyate" ityācāryāḥ ||
08206| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08207| pitāputrayorbhrātrorvā dvairājyaṃ tulyayogakṣemamamātyāvagrahaṃ vartayati ||
08208| vairājyaṃ tu jīvataḥ parasyācchidya "naitanmama" iti manyamānaḥ karśayati, apavāhayati, paṇyaṃ vā karoti, viraktaṃ vā parityajyāpagacchatīti ||
08209| andhaścalitaśāstro vā rājeti "aśāstracakṣurandho yatkiṃcanakārī dṛḍhābhiniveśī parapraṇeyo vā rājyamanyāyenopahanti, calitaśāstrastu yatra śāstrāccalitamatirbhavati śakyānunayo bhavati" ityācāryāḥ ||
08210| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08211| andho rājā śakyate sahāyasampadā yatra tatra vā paryavasthāpayitum ||
08212| calitaśāstrastu śāstrādanyathābhiniviṣṭabuddhiranyāyena rājyamātmānaṃ copahantīti ||
08213| vyādhito navo vā rājeti "vyādhito rājā rājyopaghātamamātyamūlaṃ prāṇābādhaṃ vā rājyamūlamavāpnoti, navastu rājā svadharmānugrahaparihāradānamānakarmabhiḥ prakṛtirañjanopakāraiścarati" ityācāryāḥ ||
08214| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08215| vyādhito rājā yathāpravṛttaṃ rājapraṇidhimanuvartayati ||
08216| navastu rājā balāvarjitaṃ "mamedaṃ rājyam" iti yatheṣṭamanavagrahaścarati ||
08217| sāmutthāyikairavagṛhīto vā rājyopaghātaṃ marṣayati ||
08218| prakṛtiṣvarūḍhaḥ sukhamucchettuṃ bhavatīti ||
08219| vyādhite viśeṣaḥ pāparogyapāparogī ca ||
08220| navepyabhijātoanabhijāta iti ||
08221| durbaloabhijāto balavānanabhijāto rājeti "durbalasyābhijātasyopajāpaṃ daurbalyāpekṣāḥ prakṛtayaḥ kṛcchreṇopagacchanti, balavataścānabhijātasya balāpekṣāḥ sukhena" ityācāryāḥ ||
08222| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08223| durbalamabhijātaṃ prakṛtayaḥ svayamupanamanti, jātyamaiśvaryaprakṛtiranuvartata iti ||
08224| balavataścānabhijātasyopajāpaṃ visaṃvādayanti, anurāge sārvaguṇyamiti ||
08225| prayāsavadhātsasyavadho muṣṭivadhātpāpīyān, nirājīvatvādavṛṣṭirativṛṣṭitaḥ ||
08226  dvayordvayorvyasanayoḥ prakṛtīnāṃ balābalam |
08226  pāramparyakrameṇoktaṃ yāne sthāne ca kāraṇam ||E

(puruṣavyasanavargah)
08301| avidyāvinayaḥ puruṣavyasanahetuḥ ||
08302| avinīto hi vyasanadoṣānna paśyati ||
08303| tānupadekṣyāmaḥ ||
08304| kopajastrivargaḥ, kāmajaścaturvargaḥ ||
08305| tayoḥ kopo garīyān ||
08306| sarvatra hi kopaścarati ||
08307| prāyaśaśca kopavaśā rājānaḥ prakṛtikopairhatāḥ śrūyante, kāmavaśāḥ kṣayanimittamarivyādhibhiriti ||
08308| neti bhāradvājaḥ ||
08309| "satpuruṣācāraḥ kopo vairayātanamavajñāvadho bhītamanuṣyatā ca ||
08310| nityaśca kopena sambandhaḥ pāpapratiṣedhārthaḥ ||
08311| kāmaḥ siddhilābhaḥ sāntvaṃ tyāgaśīlatā sampriyabhāvaśca ||
08312| nityaśca kāmena sambandhaḥ kṛtakarmaṇaḥ phalopabhogārthaḥ" iti ||
08313| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08314| dveṣyatā śatruvedanaṃ duḥkhāsaṅgaśca kopaḥ ||
08315| paribhavo dravyanāśaḥ pāṭaccaradyūtakāralubdhakagāyanavādakaiścānarthyaiḥ samyogaḥ kāmaḥ ||
08316| tayoḥ paribhavāddveṣyatā garīyasī ||
08317| paribhūtaḥ svaiḥ paraiścāvagṛhyate, dveṣyaḥ samucchidyata iti ||08318| dravyanāśātśatruvedanaṃ garīyaḥ ||
08319| dravyanāśaḥ kośābādhakaḥ, śatruvedanaṃ prāṇābādhakamiti ||
08320| anarthyasamyogādduḥkhasamyogo garīyān ||
08321| anarthyasamyogo muhūrtapratīkāro, dīrghakleśakaro duḥkhānāmāsaṅga iti ||
08322| tasmātkopo garīyān ||
08323| vākpāruṣyamarthadūṣaṇaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyamiti ||
08324| "vākpāruṣyārthadūṣaṇayorvākpāruṣyaṃ garīyaḥ" iti viśālākṣaḥ ||
08325| "paruṣamukto hi tejasvī tejasā pratyārohati ||
08326| duruktaśalyaṃ hṛdi nikhātaṃ tejaḥsaṃdīpanamindriyopatāpi ca" iti ||
08327| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08328| arthapūjā vākśalyamapahanti, vṛttivilopastvarthadūṣaṇam ||
08329| adānamādānaṃ vināśaḥ parityāgo vārthasyetyarthadūṣaṇam ||
08330| "arthadūṣaṇadaṇḍapāruṣyayorarthadūṣaṇaṃ garīyaḥ" iti pārāśarāḥ ||
08331| "arthamūlau dharmakāmau ||
08332| arthapratibaddhaśca loko vartate ||
08333| tasyopaghāto garīyān" iti ||
08334| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08335| sumahatāpyarthena na kaścana śarīravināśamicchet ||
08336| daṇḍapāruṣyācca tameva doṣamanyebhyaḥ prāpnoti ||
08337| iti kopajastrivargaḥ ||
08338| kāmajastu mṛgayā dyūtaṃ striyaḥ pānamiti caturvargaḥ ||
08339| tasya "mṛgayādyūtayormṛgayā garīyasī" iti piśunaḥ ||
08340| "stenāmitravyāladāvapraskhalanabhayadinmohāḥ kṣutpipāse ca prāṇābādhastasyām ||
08341| dyūte tu jitamevākṣaviduṣā yathā jayatsenaduryodhanābhyām" iti ||
08342| netykauṭilyaḥ ||
08343| tayorapyanyataraparājayoastīti nalayudhiṣṭhirābhyāṃ vyākhyātam ||
08344| tadeva vijitadravyamāmiṣaṃ vairānubandhaśca ||
08345| satoarthasya vipratipattirasataścārjanamapratibhuktanāśo mūtrapurīṣadhāraṇabubhukṣādibhiśca vyādhilābha iti dyūtadoṣāḥ ||
08346| mṛgayāyāṃ tu vyāyāmaḥ śleṣmapittamedaḥsvedanāśaścale sthite ca kāye lakṣaparicayaḥ kopabhayasthāneṣu ca mṛgāṇāṃ cittajñānamanityayānaṃ ceti ||
08347| "dyūtastrīvyasanayoḥ kaitavavyasanam" iti kauṇapadantaḥ ||
08348| "sātatyena hi niśi pradīpe mātari ca mṛtāyāṃ dīvyatyeva kitavaḥ ||
08349| kṛcchre ca pratipṛṣṭaḥ kupyati ||
08350| strīvyasane tu snānapratikarmabhojanabhūmiṣu bhavatyeva dharmārthaparipraśnaḥ ||
08351| śakyā ca strī rājahiteniyoktum, upāṃśudaṇḍena vyādhinā vā vyāvartayitumavasrāvayituṃ vā" iti ||
08352| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08353| sapratyādeyaṃ dyūtaṃ niṣpratyādeyaṃ strīvyasanam ||
08354| adarśanaṃ kāryanirvedaḥ kālātipātanādanartho dharmalopaśca tantradaurbalyaṃ pānānubandhaśceti ||
08355| "strīpānavyasanayoḥ strīvyasanam" iti vātavyādhiḥ ||
08356| "strīṣu hi bāliśyamanekavidhaṃ niśāntapraṇidhau vyākhyātam ||
08357| pāne tu śabdādīnāmindriyārthānāmupabhogaḥ prītidānaṃ parijanapūjanaṃ karmaśramavadhaśca" iti ||
08358| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08359| strīvyasane bhavatyapatyotpattirātmarakṣaṇaṃ cāntardāreṣu, viparyayo vā bāhyeṣu, agamyeṣu sarvocchittiḥ ||
08360| tadubhayaṃ pānavyasane ||
08361| pānasampat- saṃjñānāśoanunmattasyonmattatvamapretasya pretatvaṃ kaupīnadarśanaṃ śrutaprajñāprāṇavittamitrahāniḥ sadbhirviyogoanarthyasamyogastantrīgītanaipuṇyeṣu cārthaghneṣu prasaṅga iti ||
08362| dyūtamadyayordyūtam ||
08363| ekeṣāṃ paṇanimitto jayaḥ parājayo vā prāṇiṣu niścetaneṣu vā pakṣadvaidhena prakṛtikopaṃ karoti ||
08364| viśeṣataśca saṃghānāṃ saṃghadharmiṇāṃ ca rājakulānāṃ dyūtanimitto bhedastannimitto vināśa ityasatpragrahaḥ pāpiṣṭhatamo vyasanānāṃ tantradaurbalyāditi ||
08365  asatāṃ pragrahaḥ kāmaḥ kopaścāvagrahaḥ satām |
08365  vyasanaṃ doṣabāhulyādatyantamubhayaṃ matam ||
08366  tasmātkopaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca vyasanārambhamātmavān |
08366  parityajenmūlaharaṃ vṛddhasevī jitendriyaḥ ||E

(pīḍanavargah- stambhavargah- kośasangavargah)
08401| daivapīḍanaṃ - agnirudakaṃ vyādhirdurbhikṣaṃ maraka iti ||
08402| "agnyudakayoragnipīḍanamapratikāryaṃ sarvadāhi ca, śakyāpagamanaṃ tāryābādhamudakapīḍanam" ityācāryāḥ ||
08403| netkauṭilyaḥ ||
08404| agnirgrāmamardhagrāmaṃ vā dahati, udakavegastu grāmaśatapravāhīti ||
08405| "vyādhidurbhikṣayorvyādhiḥ pretavyādhitopasṛṣṭaparicārakavyāyāmoparodhena karmāṇyupahanti, durbhikṣaṃ punarakarmopaghāti hiraṇyapaśukaradāyi ca" ityācāryāḥ ||
08406| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08407| ekadeśapīḍano vyādhiḥ śakyapratīkāraśca, sarvadeśapīḍanaṃ durbhikṣaṃ prāṇināmajīvanāyeti ||
08408| tena marako vyākhyātaḥ ||
08409| "kṣudrakamukhyakṣayayoḥ kṣudrakakṣayaḥ karmaṇāmayogakṣemaṃ karoti, mukhyakṣayaḥ karmānuṣṭhānoparodhadharmā" ityācāryāḥ ||
08410| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08411| śakyaḥ kṣudrakakṣayaḥ pratisaṃdhātuṃ bāhulyātkṣudrakāṇām, na mukhyakṣayaḥ ||
08412| sahasreṣu hi mukhyo bhavatyeko na vā sattvaprajñādhikyāttadāśrayatvātkṣudrakāṇāmiti ||
08413| "svacakraparacakrayoḥ svacakramatimātrābhyāṃ daṇḍakarābhyāṃ pīḍayatyaśakyaṃ ca vārayitum, paracakraṃ tu śakyaṃ praiyoddhumupasāreṇa saṃdhinā vā mokṣayitum" ityācāryāḥ ||
08414| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08415| svacakrapīḍanaṃ prakṛtipuruṣamukhyopagrahavighātābhyāṃ śakyate vārayitumekadeśaṃ vā pīḍayati, sarvadeśapīḍanaṃ tu paracakraṃ vilopaghātadāhavidhvaṃsanāpavāhanaiḥ pīḍayatīti ||
08416| "prakṛtirājavivādayoḥ prakṛitivivādaḥ prakṛtīnāṃ bhedakaḥ parābhiyogānāvahati, rājavivādastu prakṛtīnāṃ dviguṇabhaktavetanaparihārakaro bhavati" ityācāryāḥ ||
08417| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08418| śakyaḥ prakṛtivivādaḥ prakṛtimukhyopagraheṇa kalahasthānāpanayanena vā vārayitum ||
08419| vivadamānāstu prakṛtayaḥ parasparasaṃgharṣeṇopakurvanti ||
08420| rājavivādastu pīḍanocchedanāya prakṛtīnāṃ dviguṇavyāyāmasādhya iti ||
08421| "deśarājavihārayordeśavihārastraikālyena karmaphalopaghātaṃ karoti, rājavihārastu kāruśilpikuśīlavavāgjīvanarūpājīvāvaidehakopakāraṃ karoti" ityācāryāḥ ||
08422| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08423| deśavihāraḥ karmaśramamavadhārthamalpaṃ bhakṣayati bhakṣayitvā ca bhūyaḥ karmasu yogaṃ gacchati, rājavihārastu svayaṃ vallabhaiśca svayaṃgrāhapraṇayapaṇyāgārakāryopagrahaiḥ pīḍayatīti ||
08424| "subhagākumārayoḥ kumāraḥ svayaṃ vallabhaiśca svayaṃgrāhapraṇayapaṇyāgārakāryopagrahaiḥ pīḍayati, subhagā vilāsopabhogena" ityācāryāḥ ||
08425| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08426| śakyaḥ kumāro mantripurohitābhyāṃ vārayitum, na subhagā bāliśyādanarthyajanasamyogācceti ||
08427| "śreṇīmukhyayoḥ śreṇī bāhulyādanavagrahā steyasāhasābhyāṃ pīḍayati, mukhyaḥ kāryānugrahavighātābhyām" ityācāryāḥ ||
08428| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08429| suvyāvartyā śreṇī samānaśīlavyasanatvāt, śreṇīmukhyaikadeśopagraheṇa vā ||
08430| stambhayukto mukhyaḥ paraprāṇadravyopaghātābhyāṃ pīḍayatīti ||
08431| "sannidhātṛsamāhartroḥ sannidhātā kṛtavidūṣaṇātyayābhyāṃ pīḍayati, samāhartā karaṇādhiṣṭhitaḥ pradiṣṭaphalopabhogī bhavati" ityācāryāḥ ||
08432| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08433| sannidhātā kṛtāvasthamanyaiḥ kośapraveśyaṃ pratigṛhṇāti, samāhartā tu pūrvamarthamātmanaḥ kṛtvā paścādrājārthaṃ karoti praṇāśayati vā, parasvādāne ca svapratyayaścaratīti ||
08434| "antapālavaidehakayorantapālaścoraprasargadeyātyādānābhyāṃ vaṇikpathaṃ pīḍayati, vaidehakāstu paṇyapratipaṇyānugrahaiḥ prasādhayanti" ityācāryāḥ ||
08435| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08436| antapālaḥ paṇyasampātānugraheṇa vartayati, vaidehakāstu sambhūya paṇyānāmutkarṣāpakarṣaṃ kurvāṇāḥ paṇe paṇaśataṃ kumbhe kumbhaśatamityājīvanti ||
08437| abhijātoparuddhā bhūmiḥ paśuvrajoparuddhā veti "abhijātoparuddhā bhūmiḥ mahāphalāpyāyudhīyopakāriṇī na kṣamā mokṣayituṃ vyasanābādhabhayāt, paśuvrajoparuddhā tu kṛṣiyogyā kṣamā mokṣayitum ||
08438| vivītaṃ hi kṣetreṇa bādhyate" ityācāryāḥ ||
08439| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08440| abhijātoparuddhā bhūmiratyantamahopakārāpi kṣamā mokṣayituṃ vyasanābādhabhayāt, paśuvrajoparuddhā tu kośavāhanopakāriṇī na kṣamā mokṣayitum, anyatra sasyavāpoparodhāditi ||
08441| "pratirodhakāṭavikayoḥ pratirodhakā rātrisattracarāḥ śarīrākramiṇo nityāḥ śatasahasrāhapāriṇaḥ pradhānakopakāśca vyavahitāḥ pratyantarāraṇyacarāścāṭavikāḥ prakāśā dṛsyāścaranti, ekadeśaghātakāśca" ityācāryāḥ ||
08442| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
08443| pratirodhakāḥ pramattasyāparahanti, alpāḥ kuṇṭhāḥ sukhā jñātuṃ grahītuṃ ca, svadeśasthāḥ prabhūtā vikrāntāścāṭavikāḥ prakāśayodinoapahartāro hantāraśca deśānāṃ rājasadharmāṇa iti ||
08444| mṛgahastivanayoḥ mṛgāḥ prabhūtāḥ prabhūtamāṃsacarmopakāriṇo mandagrāsāvakleśinaḥ suniyamyāśca ||
08445| viparītā hastino gṛhyamāṇā duṣṭāśca deśavināśāyeti ||
08446| svaparasthānīyopakārayoḥ svasthānīyopakāro dhānyapaśuhiraṇyakupyopakāro jānapadānāmāpadyātmadhāraṇaḥ ||
08447| viparītaḥ parasthānīyopakāraḥ ||
08448| iti pīḍanāni - ābhyantaro mukhyastambho bāhyoamitrāṭavīstambhaḥ || [iti stambhavargah]
08449| tābhyāṃ pīḍanairyathoktaiśca pīḍitaḥ, sakto mukhyeṣu, parihāropahataḥ, prakīrṇo, mithyāsaṃhṛtaḥ, sāmantāṭavīhṛta iti kośasaṅgavargaḥ ||
08450  pīḍanānāmanutpattāvutpannānāṃ ca vāraṇe |
08450  yateta deśavṛddhyarthaṃ nāśe ca stambhasaṅgayoḥ ||E

(balavyasanavargah- mitravyasanavargah)
08501| balavyasanāni - amānitam, vimānitam, abhṛtam, vyādhitam, navāgatam, dūrāyātam, pariśrāntam, parikṣīṇam, pratihatam, hatāgravegam, anṛtuprāptam, abhūmiprāptam, āśānirvedi, parisṛptam, kalatragarbhi, antaḥśalyam, kupitamūlam, bhinnagarbham, apasṛtam, atikṣiptam, upaniviṣṭam, samāptam, uparuddham, parikṣiptam, chinnadhānyapuruṣavīvadham,
svavikṣiptam, mitravikṣiptam, dūṣyayuktam, duṣṭapārṣṇigrāham, śūnyamūlam, asvāmisaṃhatam, bhinnakūṭam, andhamiti ||
08502| teṣāmamānitavimānitāniyatayoramānitaṃ kṛtārthamānaṃ yudhyeta, na vimānitamantaḥkopam ||
08503| abhṛtavyādhitayorabhṛtaṃ tadātvakṛtavetanaṃ yudhyeta, na vyādhitamakarmaṇyam ||
08504| navāgatadūrāyātayornavāgatamanyata upalabdhadeśamanavamiśraṃ yudhyeta, na dūrāyatamāyatagataparikleśam ||
08505| pariśrāntaparikṣīṇayoḥ pariśrāntaṃ snānabhojanasvapnalabdhaviśrāmaṃ yudhyeta, na parikṣīṇamanyatrāhave kṣīṇayugyapuruṣam ||
08506| pratihatahatāgravegayoḥ pratihatamagrapātabhagnaṃ pravīrapuruṣasaṃhataṃ yudhyeta, na hatāgravegamagrapātahatavīram ||
08507| anṛtvabhūmiprāptayoranṛtuprāptaṃ yathartuyugyaśastrāvaraṇaṃ yudhyeta, nābhūmiprāptamavaruddhaprasāravyāyāmam ||
08508| āśānirvediparisṛptayorāśānirvedi labdhābhiprāyaṃ yudhyeta, na parisṛptamapasṛtamukhyam ||
08509| kalatragarbhyantaḥśalyayoḥ kalatragarbhi unmucya kalatraṃ yudhyeta, nāntaḥśalyamantaramitram ||
08510| kupitamūlabhinnagarbhayoḥ kupitamūlaṃ praśamitakopaṃ sāmādibhiryudhyeta, na bhinnagarbhamanyonyasmādbhinnam ||
08511| apasṛtātikṣiptayorapasṛtamekarājyātikrāntaṃ mantravyāyāmābhyāṃ sattramitrāpāśrayaṃ yudhyeta, nātikṣiptamanekarājyātikrāntaṃ bahvābādhatvāt ||
08512| upaniviṣṭasamāptayorupaniviṣṭaṃ pṛthagyānasthānamatisaṃdhāyāriṃ yudhyeta, na samāptamariṇaikasthānayānam ||
08513| uparuddhaparikṣiptayoruparuddhamanyato niṣkramyoparoddhāraṃ pratiyudhyeta, na parikṣiptaṃ sarvataḥ pratiruddham ||
08514| chinnadhānyapuruṣavīvadhayoḥ chinnadhānyamanyato dhānyamānīya jaṅgamasthāvarāhāraṃ vā yudhyeta, na chinnapuruṣavīvadhamanabhisāram ||
08515| svavikṣiptamitravikṣiptayoḥ svavikṣiptaṃ svabhūmau vikṣiptaṃ sainyamāpadi śakyamāvāhayitum, na mitravikṣiptaṃ viprakṛṣṭadeśakālatvāt ||
08516| dūṣyayuktaduṣṭapārṣṇigrāhayordūṣyayuktamāptapuruṣādhiṣṭhitamasaṃhataṃ yudhyeta, na duṣṭapārṣṇigrāhaṃ pṛṣṭhābhighātatrastam ||
08517| śūnyamūlāsvāmisaṃhatayoḥ śūnyamūlaṃ kṛtapaurajānapadārakṣaṃ sarvasaṃdohena yudhyeta, nāsvāmisaṃhataṃ rājasenāpatihīnam ||
08518| bhinnakūṭāndhayorbhinnakūṭamanyādhiṣṭhitaṃ yudhyeta, nāndhamadeśikaṃ - iti ||
08519  doṣaśuddhirbalāvāpaḥ sattrasthānātisaṃhitam |
08519  saṃdhiścottarapakṣasya balavyasanasādhanam ||
08520  rakṣetsvadaṇḍaṃ vyasane śatrubhyo nityamutthitaḥ |
08520  prahareddaṇḍarandhreṣu śatrūṇāṃ nityamutthitaḥ ||
08521  yato nimittaṃ vyasanaṃ prakṛtīnāmavāpnuyāt |
08521  prāgeva pratikurvīta tannimittamatandritaḥ |
08522  abhiyātaṃ svayaṃ mitraṃ sambhūyānyavaśena vā ||
08522  parityaktamaśaktyā vā lobhena praṇayena vā |
08523  vikrītamabhiyuñjāne saṃgrāme vāpavartinā ||
08523  dvaidhībhāvena vāmitraṃ yāsyatā vānyamanyataḥ |
08524  pṛthagvā sahayāne vā viśvāsenātisaṃhitam ||
08524  bhayāvamānālasyairvā vyasanānna pramokṣitam |
08525  avaruddhaṃ svabhūmibhyaḥ samīpādvā bhayādgatam ||
08525  ācchedanādadānādvā dattvā vāpyavamānitam |
08526  atyāhāritamarthaṃ vā svayaṃ paramukhena vā ||
08526  atibhāre niyuktaṃ vā bhaṅktvā paramupasthitam |
08527  upekṣitamaśaktyā vā prārthayitvā virodhitam ||
08527  kṛcchreṇa sādhyate mitraṃ siddhaṃ cāśu virajyati |
08528  kṛtaprayāsaṃ mānyaṃ vā mohānmitramamānitam |
08528  mānitaṃ vā na sadṛśaṃ śaktito vā nivāritam ||
08529  mitropaghātatrastaṃ vā śaṅkitaṃ vārisaṃhitāt |
08529  dūṣyairvā bhedituṃ mitraṃ sādhyaṃ siddhaṃ ca tiṣṭhati ||
08530  tasmānnotpādayedenān doṣānmitropaghātakān |
08530  utpannān vā praśamayedguṇairdoṣopaghātibhiḥ ||E

(śaktideśakālabalābalajnānam - yātrākālāh)
09101| vijigīṣurātmanaḥ parasya ca balābalaṃ śaktideśakālayātrākālabalasamuddānakālapaścātkopakṣayavyayalābhāpadāṃ jñātvā viśiṣṭabalo yāyāt, anyathāsīta ||
09102| "utsāhaprabhāvayorutsāhaḥ śreyān ||
09103| svayaṃ hi rājā śūro balavānarogaḥ kṛtāstro daṇḍadvitīyoapi śaktaḥ prabhāvavantaṃ rājānaṃ jetum ||
09104| alpoapi cāsya daṇḍastejasā kṛtyakaro bhavati ||
09105| nirutsāhastu prabhāvavān rājā vikramābhipanno naśyati" ityācāryāḥ ||
09106| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
09107| prabhāvavānutsāhavantaṃ rājānaṃ prabhāvenātisaṃdhatte tadviśiṣṭamanyaṃ rājānamāvāhya bhṛtvā krītvā pravīrapuruṣān ||
09108| prabhūtaprabhāvahayahastirathopakaraṇasampannaścāsya daṇḍaḥ sarvatrāpratihataścarati ||
09109| utsāhavataśca prabhāvavanto jitvā krītvā ca striyo bālāḥ paṅgavoandhāśca pṛthivīṃ jigyuriti ||
09110| "prabhāvamantrayoḥ prabhāvaḥ śreyān ||
09111| mantraśaktisampanno hi vandhyabuddhiraprabhāvo bhavati ||
09112| mantrakarma cāsya niścitamaprabhāvo garbhadhānyamavṛṣṭirivopahanti" ityācāryāḥ ||
09113| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
09114| mantraśaktiḥ śreyasī ||
09115| prajñāśāstracakṣurhi rājālpenāpi prayatnena mantramādhātuṃ śaktaḥ parānutsāhaprabhāvavataśca sāmādibhiryogopaniṣadbhyāṃ cātisaṃdhātum ||
09116| evamutsāhaprabhāvamantraśaktīnāmuttarottarādhikoatisaṃdhatte ||
09117| deśaḥ pṛthivī ||
09118| tasyāṃ himavatsamudrāntaramudīcīnaṃ yojanasahasraparimāṇaṃ tiryakcakravartikṣetram ||
09119| tatrāraṇyo grāmyaḥ parvata audako bhaumaḥ samo viṣama iti viśeṣāḥ ||
09120| teṣu yathāsvabalavṛddhikaraṃ karma prayuñjīta ||
09121| yatrātmanaḥ sainyavyāyāmānāṃ bhūmiḥ, abhūmiḥ parasya, sa uttamo deśaḥ, viparītoadhamaḥ, sādhāraṇo madhyamaḥ ||
09122| kālaḥ śītoṣṇavarṣātmā ||
09123| tasya rātrirahaḥ pakṣo māsa ṛturayanaṃ saṃvatsaro yugamiti viśeṣāḥ ||
09124| teṣu yathāsvabalavṛddhikaraṃ karmaprayuñjīta ||
09125| yatrātmanaḥ sainyavyāyāmānāmṛtuḥ anṛtuḥ parasya, sa uttamaḥ kālaḥ, viparītoadhamaḥ, sādhāraṇo madhyamaḥ ||
09126| "śaktideśakālānāṃ tu śaktiḥ śreyasī" ityācāryāḥ ||
09127| śaktimān hi nimnasthalavato deśasya śītoṣṇavarṣavataśca kālasya śaktaḥ pratīkāre bhavati ||
09128| "deśaḥ śreyān" ityeke ||
09129| "sthalagato hi śvā nakraṃ vikarṣati, nimnagato nakraḥ śvānam" iti ||
09130| "kālaḥ śreyān" ityeke ||
09131| "divā kākaḥ kauśikaṃ hanti, rātrau kauśikaḥ kākam" iti ||
09132| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
09133| parasparasādhakā hi śaktideśakālāḥ ||
09134| tairabhyuccitastṛtīyaṃ caturthaṃ vā daṇḍasyāṃśaṃ mūle pārṣṇyāṃ pratyantāṭavīṣu ca rakṣā vidhāya kāryasādhanasahaṃ kośadaṇḍaṃ cādāya kṣīṇapurāṇabhaktamagṛhītanavabhaktamasaṃskṛtadurgamamitraṃ vārṣikaṃ cāsya sasyaṃ haimanaṃ ca muṣṭimupahantuṃ mārgaśīrṣīṃ yātrāṃ yāyāt ||
09135| haimānaṃ cāsya sasyaṃ vāsantikaṃ ca muṣṭimupahantuṃ caitrīṃ yātrāṃ yāyāt ||
09136| kṣīṇakṛṇakāṣṭhodakamasaṃskṛtadurgamamitraṃ vāsantikaṃ cāsya sasyaṃ vārṣikīṃ ca muṣṭimupahantuṃ jyeṣṭhāmūlīyāṃ yātrāṃ yāyāt ||
09137| atyuṣṇamalpayavasendhanodakaṃ vā deśaṃ hemante yāyāt ||
09138| tuṣāradurdinamagādhanimnaprāyaṃ gahanatṛṇavṛkṣaṃ vā deśaṃ grīṣme yāyāt ||
09139| svasainyavyāyāmayogyaṃ parasyāyogyaṃ varṣati yāyāt ||
09140| mārgaśīrṣīṃ taiṣīṃ cāntareṇa dīrghakālāṃ yātrāṃ yāyāt, caitrīṃ vaiśākhīṃ cāntareṇa madhyamakālām, jyeṣṭhāmūlīyāmāṣāḍhīṃ cāntareṇa hrasvakālām, upoṣiṣyan vyasane caturthīm ||
09141| vyasanābhiyānaṃ vigṛhyayāne vyākhyātam ||
09142| prāyaśaścācāryāḥ "paravyasane yātavyam" ityupadiśanti ||
09143| śaktyudaye yātavyamanaikānntikatvādvyasanānāmiti kauṭilyaḥ ||
09144| yadā vā prayātaḥ karśayitumucchetuṃ vā śaknuyādamitraṃ tadā yāyāt ||
09145| atyuṣṇopakṣīṇe kāle hastibalaprāyo yāyāt ||
09146| hastino hyantaḥsvedāḥ kuṣṭhino bhavanti ||
09147| anavagāhamānāstoyamapibantaścāntaravakṣārāccāndhībhavanti ||
09148| tasmātprabhūtodake deśe varṣati ca hastibalaprāyo yāyāt ||
09149| viparyaye kharoṣṭrāśvabalaprāyo deśamalpavarṣapaṅkam ||
09150| varṣati maruprāyaṃ caturaṅgabalo yāyāt ||
09151| samaviṣamanimnasthalahrasvadīrghavaśena vādhvano yātrāṃ vibhajet ||
09152  sarvā vā hrasvakālāḥ syuryātavyāḥ kāryalāghavāt |
09152  dīrghāḥ kāryagurutvādvā varṣāvāsaḥ paratra ca ||E

(balopādānakālāh-sannāhaguṇāh-pratibalakarma)
09201| maulabhṛtakaśreṇīmitrāmitrāṭavībalānāṃ samuddānakālāḥ ||
09202| mūlarakṣaṇādatiriktaṃ maulabalam, atyāvāpayuktā vā maulā mūle vikurvīran, bahulānuraktamaulabalaḥ sārabalo vā pratiyoddhā, vyāyāmena yoddhavyam, prakṛṣṭedhvani kāle vā kṣayavyayasahatvānmaulānām, bahulānuraktasampāte ca yātavyasyopajāpabhayādanyasainyānāṃ bhṛtādīnāmaviśvāse, balakṣaye vā sarvasainyānāṃ - iti maulabalakālaḥ
||
09203| "prabhūtaṃ me bhṛtabalamalpaṃ ca maulabalamṇ" "parasyālpaṃ viraktaṃ vā maulabalam, phalguprāyamasāraṃ vā bhṛtasainyamṇ" "mantreṇa yoddhavyamalpavyāyāmenaṇ" "hrasvo deśaḥ kālo vā tanukṣayavyayahṇ" "alpāvāpaṃ śāntopajāpaṃ viśvastaṃ vā me sainyamṇ" "parasyālpaḥ prasāro hantavyahṇ" - iti bhṛtabalakālaḥ ||
09204| "prabhūtaṃ me śreṇībalam, śakyaṃ mūle yātrāyāṃ cādhātumṇ" hrasvaḥ pravāsaḥ, śreṇībalaprāyaḥ pratiyoddhā mantravyāyāmābhyāṃ pratiyoddhukāmaḥ, daṇḍabalavyavahāraḥ - iti śreṇībalakālaḥ ||
09205| "prabhūtaṃ me mitrabalaṃ śakyaṃ mūle yātrāyāṃ cādhātumṇ" "alpaḥ pravāso mantrayuddhācca bhūyo vyāyāmayuddhamṇ" "mitrabalena vā pūrvamaṭavīṃ nagarasthānamāsāraṃ vā yodhayitvā paścātsvabalena yoddhayiṣyāmiṇ" "mitrasādhāraṇaṃ vā me kāryamṇ" "mitrāyattā vā me kāryasiddhihṇ" "āsannamanugrāhyaṃ vā me mitramṇ" "atyāvāpaṃ vāsya sādayiṣyāmi"
- iti mitrabalakālaḥ ||
09206| "prabhūtaṃ me śatrubalam, śatrubalena yodhayiṣyāmi nagarasthānamaṭavīṃ vā, tatra me śvavarāhayoḥ kalahe caṇḍālasyevānyatarasiddhirbhaviṣyatiṇ" "āsārāṇāmaṭavīnāṃ vā kaṇṭakamardanametatkariṣyāmiṇ" - atyupacitaṃ vā kopabhayānnityamāsannamaribalaṃ vāsayed, anyatrābhyantarakopaśaṅkāyāḥ - śatruyuddhāvarayuddhakālaśca
- ityamitrabalakālaḥ ||
09207| tenāṭavībalakālo vyākhyātaḥ ||
09208| mārgādeśikam, parabhūmiyogyam, ariyuddhapratilomam, aṭavībalaprāyaḥ śatrurvā, "bilvaṃ bilvena hanyatāmṇ" alpaḥ prasāro hantavyaḥ - ityaṭavībalakālaḥ ||
09209| sainyamanekamanekasthamuktamanuktaṃ vā vilopārthaṃ yaduttiṣṭhati tadautsāhikaṃ - abhaktavetanaṃ vilopaviṣṭipratāpakaraṃ bhedyaṃ pareṣām, abhedyaṃ tulyadeśajātiśilpaprāyaṃ saṃhataṃ mahat || [iti balopādānakālāh]
09210| teṣāṃ kupyabhṛtamamitrāṭavībalaṃ vilopabhṛtaṃ vā kuryāt ||
09211| amitrasya vā balakāle pratyutpanne śatrubalamavagṛhṇīyāt, anyatra vā preṣayet, aphalaṃ vā kuryāt, vikṣiptaṃ vā vāsayet, kāle vātikrānte visṛjet ||
09212| parasya caitadbalasamuddānaṃ vighātayet, ātmanaḥ sampādayet ||
09213| pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ caiṣāṃ śreyaḥ sannāhayitum ||
09214| tadbhāvabhāvitvānnityasatkārānugamācca maulabalaṃ bhṛtabalātśreyaḥ ||
09215| nityānantaraṃ kṣiprotthāyi vaśyaṃ va bhṛtabalaṃ śreṇībalātśreyaḥ ||
09216| jānapadamekārthopagataṃ tulyasaṃgharṣāmarṣasiddhilābhaṃ ca śreṇībalaṃ mitrabalātśreyaḥ ||
09217| aparimitadeśakālamekārthopagamācca mitrabalamamitrabalātśreyaḥ ||
09218| āryādhiṣṭhitamamitrabalamaṭavībalātśreyaḥ ||
09219| tadubhayaṃ vilopārtham ||
09220| avilope vyasane ca tābhyāmahibhayaṃ syāt ||
09221| "brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrasainyānāṃ tejaḥprādhānyātpūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ śreyaḥ sannāhayitum" ityācāryāḥ ||
09222| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
09223| praṇipātena brāhmaṇabalaṃ paroabhihārayet ||
09224| praharaṇavidyāvinītaṃ tu kṣatriyabalaṃ śreyaḥ, bahulasāraṃ vā vaiśyaśūdrabalamiti ||
09225| tasmādevaṃbalaḥ paraḥ, tasyaitatpratibalamiti balasamuddānaṃ kuryāt ||
09226| hastiyantraśakaṭagarbhakuntaprāsahāṭakaveṇuśalyavadhastibalasya pratibalam ||
09227| tadeva pāṣāṇalaguḍāvaraṇāṅkuśakacagrahaṇīprāyaṃ rathabalasya pratibalam ||
09228| tadevāśvānāṃ pratibalam, varmiṇo vā hastinoaśvā vā varmiṇaḥ ||
09229| kavacino rathā āvaraṇinaḥ pattayaśca caturaṅgabalasya pratibalam ||
09230  evaṃ balasamuddānaṃ parasainyanivāraṇam |
09230  vibhavena svasainyānāṃ kuryādaṅgavikalpaśaḥ ||E

(paścātkopacintā - bāhyābhyantaraprakṛtikopapratīkārah)
09301| alpaḥ paścātkopo mahān purastāllābha iti alpaḥ paścātkopo garīyān ||
09302| alpaṃ paścātkopaṃ prayātassya dūṣyāmitrāṭavikā hi sarvataḥ samedhayanti, prakṛtikopo vā ||
09303| labdhamapi ca mahāntaṃ purastāllāhamevaṃbhūte bhṛtyamitrakṣayavyayā grasante ||
09304| tasmātsahasraikīyaḥ purastāllābhasyāyogaḥ śataikīyo vā paścātkopa iti na yāyāt ||
09305| sūcīmukhā hyanarthā iti lokapravādaḥ ||
09306| paścātkope sāmadānabhedadaṇḍān prayuñjīta ||
09307| purastāllābhe senāpatiṃ kumāraṃ vā daṇḍacāriṇaṃ kurvīta ||
09308| balavān vā rājā paścātkopāvagrahasamarthaḥ purastāllābhamādātuṃ yāyāt ||
09309| abhyantarakopaśaṅkāyāṃ śaṅkitānādāya yāyāt, bāhyakopaśaṅkāyāṃ vā putradārameṣām ||
09310| abhyantarāvagrahaṃ kṛtvā śūnyapālamanekabalavargamanekamukhyaṃ ca sthāpayitvā yāyāt, na vā yāyāt ||
09311| abhyantarakopo bāhyakopātpāpīyānityuktaṃ purastāt ||
09312| mantrapurohitasenāpatiyuvarājānāmanyatamakopoabhyantarakopaḥ ||
09313| tamātmadoṣatyāgena paraśaktyaparādhavaśena vā sādhayet ||
09314| mahāparādhepi purohite samrodhanamavasrāvaṇaṃ vā siddhiḥ, yuvarāje samrodhanaṃ nigraho vā guṇavatyanyasmin sati putre ||
09315| putraṃ bhrātaramanyaṃ vā kulyaṃ rājagrāhiṇamutsāhena sādhayet, utsāhābbhāve gṛhītānuvartanasaṃdhikarmabhyāmarisaṃdhānabhayāt ||
09316| anyebhyastadvidhebhyo vā bhūmidānairviśvāsayedenam ||
09317| tadviśiṣṭaṃ svayaṃgrāhaṃ daṇḍaṃ vā preṣayet, sāmantāṭavikān vā, tairvigṛhītamatisaṃdadhyāt ||
09318| aparuddhādānaṃ pāragrāmikaṃ vā yogamātiṣṭhet ||
09319| etena mantrasenāpatī vyākhyātau ||
09320| mantryādivarjānāmantaramātyānāmanyatamakopoantaramātyakopaḥ ||
09321| tatrāpi yathārhamupāyān prayuñjīta ||
09322| rāṣṭramukhyāntapālāṭavikadaṇḍopanatānāmanyatamakopo bāhyakopaḥ ||
09323| tamanyonyenāvagrāhayet ||
09324| atidurgapratiṣṭabdhaṃ vā sāmantāṭavikatatkulīnāparuddhānāmanyatamenāvagrāhayet ||
09325| mitreṇopagrāhayedvā yathā nāmitraṃ gacchet ||
09326| amitrādvā sattrī bhedayedenaṃ - "ayaṃ tvā yogapuruṣaṃ manyamāno bhartaryeva vikramayiṣyati, avāptārtho daṇḍacāriṇamamitrāṭavikeṣu kṛcchre vā prayāse yokṣyati, viputradāramante vā vāsayiṣyati ||
09327| pratihatavikramaṃ tvāṃ bhartarypaṇyaṃ kariṣyati, tvayā vā saṃdhiṃ kṛtvā bhartārameva prasādayiṣyati ||
09328| mitramupakṛṣṭaṃ vāsya gaccha" iti ||
09329| pratipannamiṣṭābhiprāyaiḥ pūjayet ||
09330| apratipannasya saṃśrayaṃ bhedayed"asau te yogapuruṣaḥ praṇihitaḥ" iti ||
09331| sattrī cainamabhityaktaśāsanairghātayet, gūḍhapuruṣairvā ||
09332| sahaprasthāyino vāsya pravīrapuruṣān yathābhiprāyakaraṇenāvāhayet ||
09333| tena praṇihitān sattrī brūyāt ||
09334| iti siddhiḥ ||
09335| parasya cainān kopānutthāpayet, ātmanaśca śamayet ||
09336| yaḥ kopaṃ kartuṃ śamayituṃ vā śaktastatropajāpaḥ kāryaḥ ||
09337| yaḥ satyasaṃdhaḥ śaktaḥ karmaṇi phalāvāptau cānugrahītuṃ vinipāte ca trātuṃ tatra pratijāpaḥ kāryaḥ, tarkayitavyaśca kalyāṇabuddhirutāho śaṭha iti ||
09338| śaṭho hi bāhyoabhyantaramevamupajapati - "bhartāraṃ ceddhatvā māṃ pratipādayiṣyati śatruvadho bhūmilābhaśca me dvividho lābho bhaviṣyati, atha vā śatrurenamāhaniṣyatīti hatabandhupakṣastulyadoṣadaṇḍenodvignaśca me bhūyānakṛtyapakṣo bhaviṣyati, tadvidhe vānyasminnapi śaṅkito bhaviṣyati, anyamanyaṃ cāsya mukhyam
abhityaktaśāsanena ghātayiṣyāmi" iti ||
09339| abhyantaro vā śaṭho bāhyamevamupajapati - "kośamasya hariṣyāmi, daṇḍaṃ vāsya haniṣyāmi, duṣṭaṃ vā bhartāramanena ghātayiṣyāmi, pratipannaṃ bāhyamamitrāṭavikeṣu vikramayiṣyāmi "cakramasya sajyatām, vairamasya prasajyatām, tataḥ svādhīno me bhaviṣyati, tato bhartārameva prasādayiṣyāmi, svayaṃ vā rājyaṃ grahīṣyāmiṇ" baddhvā vā bāhyabhūmiṃ
bhartṛbhūmiṃ cobhayamavāpsyāmi, viruddhaṃ vāvāhayitvā bāhyaṃ viśvastaṃ ghātayiṣyāmi, śūnyaṃ vāsya mūlaṃ hariṣyāmi" iti ||
09340| kalyāṇabuddhistu sahajīvyarthamupajapati ||
09341| kalyāṇabuddhinā saṃdadhīta, śaṭhaṃ "tathā" iti pratigṛhyātisaṃdadhyāt- iti ||
09342  evamupalabhya - pare parebhyaḥ sve svebhyaḥ sve parebhyaḥ svataḥ pare |
09342  rakṣyāḥ svebhyaḥ parebhyaśca nityamātmā vipaścitā ||E

(kṣayavyayalābhaviparimarśah)
09401| yugyapuruṣāpacayaḥ kṣayaḥ ||
09402| hiraṇyadhānyāpacayo vyayaḥ ||
09403| tābhyāṃ bahuguṇaviśiṣṭe lābhe yāyāt ||
09404| ādeyaḥ pratyādeyaḥ prasādakaḥ prakopako hrasvakālastanukṣayoalpavyayo mahān vṛddhyudayaḥ kalyo dharmyaḥ purogaśceti lābhasampat ||
09405| suprāpyānupālyaḥ pareṣāmapratyādeya ityādeyaḥ ||
09406| viparyaye pratyādeyaḥ ||
09407| tamādadānastatrastho vā vināśaṃ prāpnoti ||
09408| yadi vā paśyet"pratyādeyamādāya kośadaṇḍanicayarakṣāvidhānānyavasrāvayiṣyāmi, khanidravyahastivanasetubandhavaṇikpathānuddhṛtasārān kariṣyāmi, prakṛtīrasya karśayiṣyāmi, apavāhayiṣyāmi, āyogenārādhayiṣyāmi vā, tāḥ paraṃ pratiyogena kopayiṣyati, pratipakṣe vāsya paṇyamenaṃ kariṣyāmi, mitramaparuddhaṃ vāsya pratipādayiṣyāmi,
mitrasya svasya vā deśasya pīḍāmatrasthastaskarebhyaḥ parebhyaśca pratikariṣyāmi, mitramāśrayaṃ vāsya vaiguṇyaṃ grāhayiṣyāmi, tadamitraviraktaṃ tatkulīnaṃ pratipatsyate, satkṛtya vāsmai bhūmiṃ dāsyāmi iti
saṃhitasamutthitaṃ mitraṃ me cirāya bhaviṣyati" iti pratyādeyamapi lābhamādadīta ||
09409| ityādeyapratyādeyau vyākhyātau ||
09410| adhārmikāddhārmikasya lābho labhyamānaḥ sveṣāṃ pareṣāṃ ca prasādako bhavati ||
09411| viparītaḥ prakopaka iti ||
09412| mantriṇāmupadeśāllābhoalabhyamānaḥ kopako bhavati "ayamasmābhiḥ kṣayavyayau grāhitaḥ" iti ||
09413| dūṣyamantriṇāmanādarāllābho labhyamānaḥ kopako bhavati "siddhārthoayamasmān vināśayiṣyati" iti ||
09414| viparītaḥ prasādakaḥ ||
09415| iti prasādakakopakau vyākhyātau
09416| gamanamātrasādhyatvādhrasvakālah
09417| mantrasādhyatvāttanukṣayaḥ ||
09418| bhaktamātravyayatvādalpavyayah
09419| tadātvavaipulyānmahān ||
09420| arthānubandhakatvādvṛddhyudayaḥ ||
09421| nirābādhakatvātkalyaḥ ||
09422| praśastopādānāddharmyaḥ ||
09423| sāmavāyikānāmanirbandhagāmitvātpurogaḥ - iti ||
09424| tulye lābhe deśakālau śaktyupāyau priyāpriyau javājavau sāmīpyaviprakarṣau tadātvānubandhau sāratvasātatye bāhulyabāhuguṇye ca vimṛśya bahuguṇayuktaṃ lābhamādadīta ||
09425| lābhavighnāḥ - kāmaḥ kopaḥ sādhvasaṃ kāruṇyaṃ hrīranāryabhāvo mānaḥ sānukrośatā paralokāpekṣā dhārmikatvamatyāgitvaṃ dainyamasūyā hastagatāvamāno daurātmyamaviśvāso bhayamapratīkāraḥ śītoṣṇavarṣāṇāmākṣamyaṃ maṅgalatithinakṣatreṣṭitvamiti ||
09426  nakṣatramati pṛcchantaṃ bālamarthoativartate |
09426  artho hyarthasya nakṣatraṃ kiṃ kariṣyanti tārakāḥ ||
09427  nādhanāḥ prāpnuvantyarthānnarā yatnaśatairapi |
09427  arthairarthā prabadhyante gajāḥ prajigajairiva ||E

(bāhyābhyantarāścāpadah)
09501| saṃdhyādīnāmayathoddeśāvasthāpanamapanayaḥ ||
09502| tasmādāpadaḥ sambhavanti ||
09503| bāhyotpattirabhyantarapratijāpā, abhyantarotpattirbāhyapratijāpā, bāhyotpattirbāhyapratijāpā, abhyantarotpattirabhyantarapratijāpā - ityāpadaḥ ||
09504| yatra bāhyā abhyantarānupajapanti, abhyantarā vā bāhyān, tatrobhayayoge pratijapataḥ siddhirviśeṣavatī ||
09505| suvyājā hi pratijapitāro bhavanti, nopajapitāraḥ ||
09506| teṣu praśānteṣu nānyānśaknuyurupajapitumupajapitāraḥ ||
09507| kṛcchropajāpā hi bāhyānāmabhyantarāsteṣāmitare vā ||
09508| mahataśca prayatnasya vadhaḥ pareṣām, arthānubandhaścātmana iti ||
09509| abhyantareṣu pratijapatsu sāmadāne prayuñjīta ||
09510| sthānamānakarma sāntvam ||
09511| anugrahaparihārau karmasvāyogo vā dānam ||
09512| bāhyeṣu pratijapatsu bhedadaṇḍau prayuñjīta ||
09513| sattriṇo mitravyañjanā vā bāhyānāṃ cārameṣāṃ brūyuḥ "ayaṃ vo rājā dūṣyavyañjanairatisaṃdhātukāmaḥ, budhyadhvam" iti ||
09514| dūṣyeṣu vā dūṣyavyañjanāḥ praṇihitā dūṣyān bāhyairbhedayeyuḥ, bāhyān vā dūṣyaiḥ ||
09515| dūṣyānanupraviṣṭā vā tīkṣṇāḥ śastrarasābhyāṃ hanyuḥ ||
09516| āhūya vā bāhyān ghātayeyuḥ ||
09517| yatra bāhyā bāhyānupajapanti, abhyantarānabhyantarā vā, tatraikāntayoga upajapituḥ siddhirviśeṣavatī ||
09518| doṣaśuddhau hi dūṣyā na vidyante ||
09519| dūṣyaśuddhau hi doṣaḥ punaranyān dūṣayati ||
09520| tasmādbāhyeṣūpajapatsu bhedadaṇḍau prayuñjīta ||
09521| sattriṇo mitravyañjanā vā brūyuḥ "ayaṃ vo rājā svayamādātukāmaḥ, vigṛhītāḥ sthānena rājñā, budhyadhvam" iti ||
09522| pratijapiturvā dūtadaṇḍānanupraviṣṭāstīkṣṇāḥ śastrarasādibhireṣāṃ chidreṣu prahareyuḥ ||
09523| tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pratijapitāramabhiśaṃseyuḥ ||
09524| abhyantarānabhyantareṣūpajapatsu yathārhamupāyaṃ prayuñjīta ||
09525| tuṣṭaliṅgamatuṣṭaṃ viparītaṃ vā sāma prayuñjīta ||
09526| śaucasāmarthyāpadeśena vyasanābhyudayāvekṣaṇena vā pratipūjanamiti dānam ||
09527| mitravyañjano vā brūyādetān "cittajñānārthamupadhāsyati vo rājā, tadasyākhyātavyamiti ||
09528| parasparādvā bhedayedenān "asau cāsau ca vo rājanyevamupajapati" - iti bhedaḥ ||
09529| dāṇḍakarmikavacca daṇḍaḥ ||
09530| etāsāṃ catasṛṇāmāpadāmabhyantarāmeva pūrvaṃ sādhayet ||
09531| ahibhayādabhyantarakopo bāhyakopātpāpīyānityuktaṃ purastād ||
09532  pūrvāṃ pūrvāṃ vijānīyāllaghvīmāpadamāpadām |
09532  utthitāṃ balavadbhyo vā gurvīṃ laghvīṃ viparyaye ||E

(dūṣyaśatrusamyuktāh<āpadah>)
09601| dūṣyebhyaḥ śatrubhyaśca dvividhā śuddhā ||
09602| dūṣyaśuddhāyāṃ paureṣu jānapadeṣu vā daṇḍavarjānupāyān prayuñjīta ||
09603| daṇḍo hi mahājane kṣeptumaśakyaḥ ||
09604| kṣipto vā taṃ cārthaṃ na kuryāt, anyaṃ cānarthamutpādayet ||
09605| mukhyeṣu tveṣāṃ dāṇḍakarmikavacceṣṭeta ||
09606| śatruśuddhāyāṃ yataḥ śatruḥ pradhānaḥ kāryo vā tataḥ sāmādibhiḥ siddhiṃ lipseta ||
09607| svāminyāyattā pradhānasiddhiḥ, mantriṣvāyattāyattasiddhiḥ, ubhayāyattā pradhānāyattasiddhiḥ ||
09608| dūṣyādūṣyāṇāmāmiśritatvādāmiśrā ||
09609| āmiśrāyāmadūṣyataḥ siddhiḥ ||
09610| ālambanābhāve hyālambitā na vidyante ||
09611| mitrāmitrāṇāmekībhāvātparamiśrā ||
09612| paramiśrāyāṃ mitrataḥ siddhiḥ ||
09613| sukaro hi mitreṇa saṃdhiḥ, nāmitreṇeti ||
09614| mitraṃ cenna saṃdhimicchedabhīkṣṇamupajapet ||
09615| tataḥ sattribhiramitrādbhedayitvā mitraṃ labheta ||
09616| mitrasaṃghasya vā yoantasthāyī taṃ labheta ||
09617| antasthāyini labdhe madhyasthāyino bhidyante ||
09618| madhyasthāyinaṃ vā labheta ||
09619| madhyasthāyini labdhe nāntasthāyinaḥ saṃhanyante ||
09620| yathā caiṣāmāśrayabhedastānupāyān prayuñjīta ||
09621| dhārmikaṃ jātikulaśrutavṛttastavena sambandhena pūrveṣāṃ traikālyopakārānapakārābhyāṃ vā sāntvayet ||
09622| nivṛttotsāhaṃ vigrahaśrāntaṃ pratihatopāyaṃ kṣayavyayābhyāṃ pravāsena copataptaṃ śaucenānyaṃ lipsamānamanyasmādvā śaṅkamānaṃ maitrīpradhānaṃ vā kalyāṇabuddhiṃ sāmnā sādhayet ||
09623| lubdhaṃ kṣīṇaṃ vā tapasvimukhyāvasthāpanāpūrvaṃ dānena sādhayet ||
09624| tatpañcavidhaṃ - deyavisargo gṛhītānuvartanamāttapratidānaṃ svadravyadānamapūrvaṃ parasveṣu svayaṃgrāhadānaṃ ca ||
09625| iti dānakarma ||
09626| parasparadveṣavairabhūmiharaṇaśaṅkitamatoanyatamena bhedayet ||
09627| bhīruṃ vā pratighātena "kṛtasaṃdhireṣa tvayi karmakariṣyati, mitramasya nisṛṣṭam, saṃdhau vā nābhyantaraḥ" iti ||
09628| yasya vā svadeśādanyadeśādvā paṇyāni paṇyāgāratayāgaccheyuḥ tāni asya "yātavyāllabdhāni" iti sattriṇaścārayeyuḥ ||
09629| bahulībhūte śāsanamabhityaktena preṣayet"etatte paṇyaṃ paṇyāgāraṃ vā mayā te preṣitam, sāmavāyikeṣu vikramasva, apagaccha vā, tataḥ paṇaśeṣamavāpsyasi" iti ||
09630| tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pareṣu grāhayeyuḥ "etadaripradattam" iti ||
09631| śatruprakhyātaṃ vā paṇyamavijñātaṃ vijigīṣuṃ gacchet ||
09632| tadasya vaidehakavyañjanāḥ śatrumukhyeṣu vikrīṇīran ||
09633| tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pareṣu grāhayeyuḥ "etatpaṇyamaripradattam" iti ||
09634| mahāparādhānarthamānābhyāmupagṛhya vā śastrarasāgnibhiramitre praṇidadhyāt ||
09635| athaikamamātyaṃ niṣpātayet ||
09636| tasya putradāramupagṛhya rātrau hatamiti khyāpayet ||
09637| athāmātyaḥ śatrostānekaikaśaḥ prarūpayet ||
09638| te cedyathoktaṃ kuryurna cainān grāhayet ||
09639| aśaktimato vā grāhayet ||
09640| āptabhāvopagato mukhyādasyātmānaṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ kathayet ||
09641| athāmitraśāsanaṃ mukhyopaghātāya preṣitamubhayavetano grāhayet ||
09642| utsāhaśaktimato vā preṣayet"amuṣya rājyaṃ gṛhāṇa, yathāsthito naḥ saṃdhiḥ" iti ||
09643| tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pareṣu grāhayeyuḥ ||
09644| ekasya skandhāvāraṃ vīvadhamāsāraṃ vā ghātayeyuḥ ||
09645| itareṣu maitrīṃ bruvāṇāḥ "tvameteṣāṃ ghātayitavyaḥ" ityupajapeyuḥ ||
09646| yasya vā pravīrapuruṣo hastī hayo vā mriyeta gūḍhapuruṣairhanyeta hriyeta vā sattriṇaḥ parasparopahataṃ brūyuḥ ||
09647| tataḥ śāsanamabhiśastasya preṣayet"bhūyaḥ kuru tataḥ paṇaśeeṣamavāpsyasi" iti ||
09648| tadubhayavetanā grāhayeyuḥ ||
09649| bhinneṣvanyatamaṃ labheta ||
09650| tena senāpatikumāradaṇḍacāriṇo vyākhyātāḥ ||
09651| sāṃdhikaṃ ca bhedaṃ prayuñjīta ||
09652| iti bhedakarma ||
09653| tīkṣṇamutsāhinaṃ vyasaninaṃ sthitaśatruṃ vā gūḍhapuruṣāḥ śastrāgnirasādibhiḥ sādhayeyuḥ, saukaryato vā teṣāmanyatamaḥ ||
09654| tīkṣṇo hyekaḥ śastrarasāgnibhiḥ sādhayet ||
09655| ayaṃ sarvasaṃdohakarma viśiṣṭaṃ vā karoti ||
09656| ityupāyacaturvargaḥ ||
09657| pūrvaḥ pūrvaścāsya laghiṣṭhaḥ ||
09658| sāntvamekaguṇam ||
09659| dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ sāntvapūrvam ||
09660| bhedastriguṇaḥ sāntvadānapūrvaḥ ||
09661| daṇḍaścaturguṇaḥ sāntvadānabhedapūrvaḥ ||
09662| ityabhiyuñjāneṣūktam ||
09663| svabhūmiṣṭheṣu tu ta evopāyāḥ ||
09664| viśeṣastu ||
09665| svabhūmiṣṭhānāmanyatamasya paṇyāgārairabhijñātān dūtamukhyānabhīkṣṇaṃ preṣayet ||
09666| ta enaṃ saṃdhau parahiṃsāyāṃ vā yojayeyuḥ ||
09667| apratipadyamānaṃ "kṛto naḥ saṃdhiḥ" ityāvedayeyuḥ ||
09668| tamitareṣāmubhayavetanāḥ saṃkrāmayeyuḥ "ayaṃ vo rājā duṣṭaḥ" iti ||
09669| yasya vā yasmādbhayaṃ vairaṃ dveṣo vā taṃ tasmādbhedayeyuḥ "ayaṃ te śatruṇā saṃdhatte, purā tvāmatisaṃdhatte, kṣiprataraṃ saṃdhīyasva, nigrahe cāsya prayatasva" iti ||
09670| āvāhavivāhābhyāṃ vā kṛtvā samyogamasamyuktān bhedayet ||
09671| sāmantāṭavikatatkulīnāparuddhaiścaiṣāṃ rājyāni ghātayet, sārthavrajāṭavīrvā, daṇḍaṃ vābhisṛtam ||
09672| parasparāpāśrayāścaiṣāṃ jātisaṃghāśchidreṣu prahareyuḥ, gūḍhāścāgnirasaśastreṇa ||
09673  vītaṃsagilavaccārīn yogairācaritaiḥ śaṭhaḥ |
09673  ghātayetparamiśrāyāṃ viśvāsenāmiṣeṇa ca ||E

(arthānarthasamśayayuktāh<āpadah> - tāsāmupāyavikalpajāhsiddhayah)
09701| kāmādirutsekaḥ svāḥ prakṛtīḥ kopayati, apanayo bāhyāḥ ||
09702| tadubhayamāsurī vṛttiḥ ||
09703| svajanavikāraḥ kopaḥ ||
09704| paravṛddhihetuṣu āpadarthoanarthaḥ saṃśaya iti ||
09705| yoarthaḥ śatruvṛddhimaprāptaḥ karoti, prāptaḥ pratyādeyaḥ pareṣāṃ bhavati, prāpyamāṇo vā kṣayavyayodayo bhavati, sa bhavatyāpadarthaḥ ||
09706| yathā sāmantānāmāmiṣabhūtaḥ sāmantavyasanajo lābhaḥ, śatruprārthito vā svabhāvādhigamyo lābhaḥ, paścātkopena pārṣṇigrāheṇa vā vigṛhītaḥ purastāllābhaḥ, mitrocchedena saṃdhivyatikrameṇa vā maṇḍalaviruddho lābhaḥ ityāpadarthaḥ ||
09707| svataḥ parato vā bhayotpattirityanarthaḥ ||
09708| tayoḥ artho na veti, anartho na veti, arthoanartha iti, anarthoartha iti saṃśayaḥ ||
09709| śatrumitramutsāhayitumartho na veti saṃśayaḥ ||
09710| śatrubalamarthamānābhyāmāvāhayitumanartho na veti saṃśayaḥ ||
09711| balavatsāmantāṃ bhūmimādātumarthoanartha iti saṃśayaḥ ||
09712| jāyasā sambhūyayānamanarthoartha iti saṃśayaḥ ||
09713| teṣāmarthasaṃśayamupagacchet ||
09714| arthoarthānubandhaḥ, artho niranubandhaḥ, arthoanarthānubandhaḥ, anarthoarthānubandhaḥ, anartho niranubandhaḥ, anarthoanarthānubandhaḥ ityanubandhaṣaḍvargaḥ ||
09715| śatrumutpāṭya pārṣṇigrāhādānamarthoanarthānubandhaḥ ||
09716| udāsīnasya daṇḍānugrahaḥ phalena artho niranubandhaḥ ||
09717| parasyāntarucchedanamarthoanarthānubandhaḥ ||
09718| śatruprativeśasyānugrahaḥ kośadaṇḍābhyāmanarthoanarthānubandhaḥ ||
09719| hīnaśaktimutsāhya nivṛttiranartho niranubandhaḥ ||
09720| jyāyāṃsamutthāpya nivṛttiranarthoanarthānubandhaḥ ||
09721| teṣāṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyānupasamprāptum ||
09722| iti kāryāvasthāpanam ||
09723| samantato yugapadarthotpattiḥ samantatoarthāpadbhavati ||
09724| saiva pārṣṇigrāhavigṛhītā samantatoarthasaṃśayāpadbhavati ||
09725| tayormitrākrandopagrahātsiddhiḥ ||
09726| samantataḥ śatrubhyo bhayotpattiḥ samanttoanarthāpadbhavati ||
09727| saiva mitravigṛhītā samantatoanarthasaṃśayāpadbhavati ||
09728| tayoścalāmitrākrandopagrahātsiddhiḥ, paramiśrāpratīkāro vā ||
09729| ito lābha itarato lābha ityubhayatoarthāpadbhavati ||
09730| tasyāṃ samantatoarthāyāṃ ca lābhaguṇayuktamarthamādātuṃ yāyāt ||
09731| tulye lābhaguṇe pradhānamāsannamanatipātinamūno vā yena bhavettamādātuṃ yāyāt ||
09732| itoanartha itaratoanartha ityubhayatoanarthāpat ||
09733| tasyāṃ samantatoanarthāyāṃ ca mitrebhyaḥ siddhiṃ lipseta ||
09734| mitrābhāve prakṛtīnāṃ laghīyasyaikatoanarthāṃ sādhayet, ubhayatoanarthāṃ jyāyasyā, samantatoanarthāṃ mūlena pratikuryāt ||
09735| aśakye sarvamutsṛjyāpagacchet ||
09736| dṛṣṭā hi jīvataḥ punarāvṛttiryathā suyātrodayanābhyām ||
09737| ito lābha itarato rājyābhimarśa ityubhayatoarthānarthāpadbhavati ||
09738| tasyāmanarthasādhako yoarthastamādātuṃ yāyāt ||
09739| anyathā hi rājyābhimarśaṃ vārayet ||
09740| etayā samantatoarthānarthāpadvyākhyātā ||
09741| itoanartha itaratoarthasaṃśaya ityubhayatoanarthārthasaṃśayā ||
09742| tasyāṃ pūrvamanarthaṃ sādhayet, tatsiddhāvarthasaṃśayam ||
09743| etayā samantatoanarthārthasaṃśayā vyākhyātā ||
09744| itoartha itaratoanarthasaṃśaya ityubhayatoarthānarthasaṃśayāpad ||
09745| etayā samantatoarthānarthasaṃśayā vyākhyātā ||
09746| tasyāṃ pūrvāṃ pūrvāṃ prakṛtīnāmanarthasaṃśayānmokṣayituṃ yateta ||
09747| śreyo hi mitramanarthasaṃśaye tiṣṭhanna daṇḍaḥ, daṇḍo vā na kośa iti ||
09748| samagramokṣaṇābhāve prakṛtīnāmavayavānmokṣayituṃ yateta ||
09749| tatra puruṣaprakṛtīnāṃ bahulamanuraktaṃ vā tīkṣṇalubdhavarjam, dravyaprakṛtīnāṃ sāraṃ mahopakāraṃ vā ||
09750| saṃdhināsanena dvaidhībhāvena vā laghūni, viparyayairgurūṇi ||
09751| kṣayasthānavṛddhīnāṃ cottarottaraṃ lipseta ||
09752| prātilomyena vā kṣayādīnāmāyatyāṃ viśeṣaṃ paśyet ||
09753| iti deśāvasthāpanam ||
09754| etena yātrādimadhyānteṣvarthānarthasaṃśayānāmupasamprāptirvyākhyātā ||
09755| nirantarayogitvāccārthānarthasaṃśayānāṃ yātrādāvarthaḥ śreyānupasamprāptuṃ pārṣṇigrāhāsārapratighāte kṣayavyayapravāsapratyādeye mūlarakṣaṇeṣu ca bhavati ||
09756| tathānarthaḥ saṃśayo vā svabhūmiṣṭhasya viṣahyo bhavati ||
09757| etena yātrāmadhyerthānarthasaṃśayānāmupasamprāptirvyākhyātā ||
09758| yātrānte tu karśanīyamucchedanīyaṃ vā karśayitvocchidya vārthaḥ śreyānupasamprāptuṃ nānarthaḥ saṃśayo vā parābādhabhayāt ||
09759| sāmavāyikānāmapurogasya tu yātrāmadhyāntagoanarthaḥ saṃśayo vā śreyānupasamprāptumanirbandhagāmitvāt ||
09760| artho dharmaḥ kāma ityarthatrivargaḥ ||
09761| tasya pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyānupasamprāptum ||
09762| anarthoadharmaḥ śoka ityanarthatrivargaḥ ||
09763| tasya pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān pratikartum ||
09764| arthoanartha iti, dharmoadharma iti, kāmaḥ śoka iti saṃśayatrivargaḥ ||
09765| tasyottarapakṣasiddhau pūrvapakṣaḥ śreyānupasamprāptum ||
09766| iti kālāvasthāpanam ||
09767| ityāpadaḥ - tāsāṃ siddhiḥ ||
09768| putrabhrātṛbandhuṣu sāmadānābhyāṃ siddhiranurūpā, paurajānapadadaṇḍamukhyeṣu dānabhedābhyām, sāmantāṭavikeṣu bhedadaṇḍābhyām ||
09769| eṣānulomā, viparyaye pratilomā ||
09770| mitrāmitreṣu vyāmiśrā siddhiḥ ||
09771| parasparasādhakā hyupāyāḥ ||
09772| śatroḥ śaṅkitāmātyeṣu sāntvaṃ prayuktaṃ śeṣaprayogaṃ nivartayati, dūṣyāmātyeṣu dānam, saṃghāteṣu bhedaḥ, śaktimatsu daṇḍa iti ||
09773| gurulāghavayogāccāpadāṃ niyogavikalpasamuccayā bhavanti ||
09774| "anenaivopāyena nānyena" iti niyogaḥ ||
09775| "anena vānyena vā" iti vikalpaḥ ||
09776| "anenānyena ca" iti samuccayaḥ ||
09777| teṣāmekayogāścatvārastriyogāśca, dviyogāḥ ṣaṭ, ekaścaturyogaḥ ||
09778| iti pañcadaśopāyāḥ ||
09779| tāvantaḥ pratilomāḥ ||
09780| teṣāmekenopāyena siddhirekasiddhiḥ, dvābhyāṃ dvisiddhiḥ, tribhistrisiddhiḥ, caturbhiścatuḥsiddhiriti ||
09781| dharmamūlatvātkāmaphalatvāccārthasya dharmārthakāmānubandhā yārthasya siddhiḥ sā sarvārthasiddhiḥ || <iti siddhayah>
09782| daivādagnirudakaṃ vyādhiḥ pramāro vidravo durbhikṣamāsurī sṛṣṭirityāpadaḥ ||
09783| tāsāṃ daivatabrāhmaṇarpaṇipātataḥ siddhiḥ ||
09784  ativṛṣṭiravṛṣṭirvā sṛṣṭirvā yāsurī bhavet |
09784  tasyāmātharvaṇaṃ karma siddhārambhāśca siddhayaḥ ||E

(skandhāvāraniveśah)
10101| vāstukapraśaste vāstuni nāyakavardhaki mauhūrtikāḥ skandhāvāram, vṛttaṃ dīrghaṃ caturaśraṃ vā bhūmivaśena vā, caturdvāraṃ ṣaṭpathaṃ navasaṃsthānaṃ māpayeyuḥ khātavaprasāladvārāṭṭālakasampannaṃ bhaye sthāne ca ||
10102| madhyamasyottare navabhāge rājavāstukaṃ dhanuḥśatāyāmamardhavistāram, paścimārdhe tasyāntaḥpuram ||
10103| antarvaṃśikasainyaṃ cānte niviśeta ||
10104| purastādupasthānam, dakṣiṇataḥ kośaśāsanakāryakaraṇāni, vāmato rājāupavāhyānāṃ hastyaśvarathānāṃ sthānam ||
10105| ato dhanuḥśatāntarāścatvāraḥ śakaṭamethīpratatistambhasālaparikṣepāḥ ||
10106| prathame purastānmantripurohitau, daṇṣiṇataḥ koṣṭhāgāraṃ mahānasaṃ ca, vāmataḥ kupyāyudhāgāram ||
10107| dvitīye maulabhṛtānāṃ sthānamaśvarathānāṃ senāpateśca ||
10108| tṛtīye hastinaḥ śreṇyaḥ praśāstā ca ||
10109| caturthe viṣṭirnāyako mitrāmitrāṭavībalaṃ svapuruṣādhiṣṭhitam ||
10110| vaṇijo rūpājīvāścānumahāpatham ||
10111| bāhyato lubdhakaśvagaṇinaḥ satūryāgnayaḥ, gūḍhāścārakṣāḥ ||
10112| śatrūṇāmāpāte kūpakūṭāvapātakaṇṭakinīśca sthāpayet ||
10113| aṣṭādaśavargāṇāmārakṣaviparyāsaṃ kārayet ||
10114| divāyāmaṃ ca kārayedapasarpajñānārtham ||
10115| vivādasaurikasamājadyūtavāraṇaṃ ca kārayet, mudrārakṣaṇaṃ ca ||
10116| senānivṛttamāyudhīyamaśāsanaṃ śūnyapālo badhnīyāt ||
10117  purastādadhvanaḥ samyakpraśāstā rakṣaṇāni ca |
10117  yāyādvardhakiviṣṭibhyāmudakāni ca kārayet ||E

(skandhāvāraprayāṇam - balavyasanāvaskandakālarakṣaṇam)
10201| grāmāraṇyānāmadhvani niveśān yavasendhanodakavaśena parisaṃkhyāya sthānāsanagamanakālaṃ ca yātrāṃ yāyāt ||
10202| tatpratīkāradviguṇaṃ bhaktopakaraṇaṃ vāhayet ||
10203| aśakto vā sainyeṣvāyojayet, antareṣu vā nicinuyāt ||
10204| purastānnāyakaḥ, madhye kalatraṃ svāmī ca, pārśvayoraśvā bāhūtsāraḥ, cakrānteṣu hastinaḥ prasāravṛddhirvā, paścātsenāpatiryāyātniviśeta ||
10205| sarvato vanājīvaḥ prasāraḥ ||
10206| svadeśādanvāyatirvīvadhaḥ ||
10207| mitrabalamāsāraḥ ||
10208| kalatrasthānamapasāraḥ ||
10209| purastādadhyāghāte makareṇa yāyāt, paścātśakaṭena, pārśvayorvajreṇa, samantataḥ sarvatobhadreṇa, ekāyane sūcyā ||
10210| pathidvaidhībhāve svabhūmito yāyāt ||
10211| abhūmiṣṭhānāṃ hi svabhūmiṣṭhā yuddhe pratilomā bhavanti ||
10212| yojanamadhamā, adhyardhaṃ madhyamā, dviyojanamuttamā, sambhāvyā vā gatiḥ ||
10213| āśrayakārī sampannaghātī pārṣṇirāsāro madhyama udāsīno vā pratikartavyaḥ, saṃkaṭo mārgaḥ śodhayitavyaḥ, kośo daṇḍo mitrāmitrāṭavībalaṃ viṣṭyṛturvā pratīkṣyāḥ, kṛtadurgakarmanicayarakṣākṣayaḥ krītabalanirvedo mitrabalanirvedaścāgamiṣyati, upajapitāro vā nātitvarayanti, śatrurabhiprāyaṃ vā pūrayiṣyati, iti śanairyāyāt,
viparyaye śīghram ||
10214| hastistambhasaṃkramasetubandhanaukāṣṭhaveṇusaṃghātairalābucarmakaraṇḍadṛtiplavagaṇḍikāveṇikābhiścodakāni tārayet ||
10215| tīrthābhigrahe hastyaśvairanyato rātrāvuttārya sattraṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||
10216| anudake cakricatuṣpadaṃ cādhvapramāṇena śaktyodakaṃ vāhayet ||
10217| dīrghakāntāramanudakaṃ yavasendhanodakahīnaṃ vā kṛcchrādhvānamabhiyogapraskannaṃ kṣutpipāsādhvaklāntaṃ paṅkatoyagambhīrāṇāṃ vā nadīdarīśailānāmudyānāpayāne vyāsaktamekāyanamārge śailaviṣame saṃkaṭe vā bahulībhūtaṃ niveśe prasthite visannāhaṃ bhojanavyāsaktamāyatagatapariśrāntamavasuptaṃ vyādhimarakadurbhikṣapīḍitaṃ
vyādhitapattyaśvadvipamabhūmiṣṭhaṃ vā balavyasaneṣu vā svasainyaṃ rakṣet, parasainyaṃ cābhihanyāt ||
10218| ekāyanamārgaprayātasya senāniścāragrāsāhāraśayyāprastārāgninidhānadhvajāyudhasaṃkhyānena parabalajñānam ||
10219| tadātmāno gūhayet ||
10220  pārvataṃ vanadurgaṃ vā sāpasārapratigraham |
10220  svabhumau pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā yudhyeta niviśeta ca ||E

(kūṭayuddhavikalpāh- svasainyotsāhanam - svabalānyabalavyāyogah)
10301| balaviśiṣṭaḥ kṛtopajāpaḥ prativihitartuḥ svabhūmyāṃ prakāśayuddhamupeyāt ||
10302| viparyaye kūṭayuddham ||
10303| balavyasanāvaskandakāleṣu paramabhihanyāt, abhūmiṣṭhaṃ vā svabhūmiṣṭhaḥ, prakṛtipragraho vā svabhūmiṣṭham ||
10304| dūṣyāmitrāṭavībalairvā bhaṅgaṃ dattvā vibhūmiprāptaṃ hanyāt ||
10305| saṃhatānīkaṃ hastibhirbhedayet ||
10306| pūrvaṃ bhaṅgapradānenānupralīnaṃ bhinnamabhinnaḥ pratinivṛtya hanyāt ||
10307| purastādabhihatya pracalaṃ vimukhaṃ vā pṛṣṭhato hastyaśvenābhihanyāt ||
10308| pṛṣṭhatoabhihatyā pracalaṃ vimukhaṃ vā purastātsārabalenābhihanyāt ||
10309| tābhyāṃ pārśvābhigātau vyākhyātau ||
10310| yato vā dūṣyaphalgubalaṃ tatoabhihanyāt ||
10311| purastādviṣamāyāṃ pṛṣṭhatoabhihanyāt ||
10312| pṛṣṭhato viṣamāyāṃ purastādabhihanyāt ||
10313| pārśvato viṣamāyāmitaratoabhihanyāt ||
10314| dūṣyāmitrāṭavībalairvā pūrvaṃ yodhayitvā śrāntamaśrāntaḥ paramabhihanyāt ||
10315| dūṣyabalena vā svayaṃ bhaṅgaṃ dattvā "jitam" iti viśvastamaviśvastaḥ sattrāpāśrayoabhihanyāt ||
10316| sārthavrajaskandhāvārasaṃvāhavilopapramattamapramattoabhihanyāt ||
10317| phalgubalāvacchannasārabalo vā paravīrānanupraviśya hanyāt ||
10318| gograhaṇena śvāpadavadhena vā paravīrānākṛṣya sattracchannoabhihanyāt ||
10319| rātrāvavaskandena jāgarayitvā nidrāklāntānavasuptān vā divā hanyāt ||
10320| sapādacarmakośairvā hastibhiḥ sauptikaṃ dadyāt ||
10321| ahaḥsannāhapariśrāntānaparāhnebhihanyāt ||
10322| śuṣkacarmavṛttaśarkarākośakairgomahiṣoṣṭrayūthairvā trasnubhirakṛtahastyaśvaṃ bhinnamabhinnaḥ pratinivṛttaṃ hanyāt ||
10323| pratisūryavātaṃ vā sarvamabhihanyāt ||
10324| dhānvanavanasaṃkaṭapaṅkaśailanimnaviṣamanāvo gāvaḥ śakaṭavyūho nīhāro rātririti sattrāṇi ||
10325| pūrve ca praharaṇakālāḥ kūṭayuddhahetavaḥ ||
10326| saṃgrāmastu nirdiṣṭadeśakālo dharmiṣṭhaḥ ||
10327| saṃhatya daṇḍaṃ brūyāt"tulyavetanoasmi, bhavadbhiḥ saha bhogyamidaṃ rājyam, mayābhihitaiḥ paroabhihantavyaḥ" iti ||
10328| vedeṣvapyanuśrūyate samāptadakṣiṇānāṃ yajñānāmavabhṛtheṣu "sā te gatiryā śūrāṇām" iti ||
10329| apīha ślokau bhavataḥ ||
10330  "yān yajñasaṃghaistapasā ca viprāḥ svargaiṣiṇaḥ pātracayaiśca yānti |
10330  kṣaṇena tānapyatiyānti śūrāḥ prāṇān suyuddheṣu parityajantaḥ ||
10331  "navaṃ śarāvaṃ salilasya pūrṇaṃ susaṃskṛtaṃ darbhakṛtottarīyam |
10331  tattasya mā bhūnnarakaṃ ca gacchedyo bhartṛpiṇḍasya kṛte na yudhyet- iti ||
10332| mantripurohitābhyāmutsāhayedyodhān vyūhasampadā ||
10333| kārtāntikādiścāsya vargaḥ sarvajñadaivatasamyogakhyāpanābhyāṃ svapakṣamuddharṣayet, parapakṣaṃ codvejayet ||
10334| "śvo yuddham" iti kṛtopavāsaḥ śastravāhanaṃ cānuśayīta ||
10335| atharvabhiśca juhuyāt ||
10336| vijayayuktāḥ svargīyāścāśiṣo vācayet ||
10337| brāhmaṇebhyaścātmānamatisṛjet ||
10338| śauryaśilpābhijanānurāgayuktamarthamānābhyāmavisaṃvāditamanīkagarbhaṃ kurvīta ||
10339| pitṛputrabhrātṛkāṇāmāyudhīyānāmadhvajaṃ muṇḍānīkaṃ rājasthānam ||
10340| hastī ratho vā rājavāhanamaśvānubandhaḥ ||
10341| yatprāyasainyo yatra vā vinītaḥ syātt(ad) adhirohayet ||
10342| rājavyañjano vyūhādhiṣṭhānamāyojyaḥ ||
10343| sūtamāgadhāḥ śūrāṇāṃ svargamasvargaṃ bhīrūṇāṃ jātisaṃghakulakarmavṛttastavaṃ ca yodhānāṃ varṇayeyuḥ ||
10344| purohitapuruṣāḥ kṛtyābhicāraṃ brūyuḥ, yantrikavardhakimauhūrtikāḥ svakarmasiddhimasiddhiṃ pareṣām ||
10345| senāpatirarthamānābhyāmabhisaṃskṛtamanīkamābhāṣeta - "śatasāhasro rājavadhaḥ, pañcāśatsāhasraḥ senāpatikumāravadhaḥ, daśasāhasraḥ pravīramukhyavadhaḥ, pañcasāhasro hastirathavadhaḥ, sāhasroaśvavadhaḥ, śatyaḥ pattimukhyavadhaḥ, śiro viṃśatikaṃ bhogadvaiguṇyaṃ svayaṃgrāhaśca" iti ||
10346| tadeṣāṃ daśavargādhipatayo vidyuḥ ||
10347| cikitsakāḥ śastrayantrāgadasnehavastrahastāḥ striyaścānnapānarakṣiṇyaḥ puruṣāṇāmuddharṣaṇīyāḥ pṛṣṭhatastiṣṭheyuḥ ||
10348| adakṣiṇāmukhaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥsūryamanulomavātamanīkaṃ svabhūmau vyūheta ||
10349| parabhūmivyūhe cāśvāṃścārayeyuḥ ||
10350| yatra sthānaṃ prajavaścābhūmirvyūhasya tatra sthitaḥ prajavitaścobhayathā jīyeta || viparyaye jayati, ubhayathā sthāne prajave ca ||
10352| samā viṣamā vyāmiśrā vā bhūmiriti purastātpārśvābhyāṃ paścācca jñeyā ||
10353| samāyāṃ daṇḍamaṇḍalavyūhāḥ, viṣamāyāṃ bhogāsaṃhatavyūhāḥ, vyāmiśrāyāṃ viṣamavyūhāḥ ||
10354| viśiṣṭabalaṃ bhaṅktvā saṃdhiṃ yāceta ||
10355| samabalena yācitaḥ saṃdadhīta ||
10356| hīnamanuhanyāt, na tveva svabhūmiprāptaṃ tyaktātmānaṃ vā ||
10357  punarāvartamānasya nirāśasya ca jīvite |
10357  adhāryo jāyate vegastasmādbhagnaṃ na pīḍayet ||E

(yuddhabhūmayah- pattyaśvarathahastikarmāṇi)
10401| svabhūmiḥ pattyaśvarathadvipānāmiṣṭā yuddhe niveśe ca ||
10402| dhānvanavananimnasthalayodhināṃ khanakākāśadivārātriyodhināṃ ca puruṣāṇāṃ nādeyapārvatānūpasārasānāṃ ca hastināmaśvānāṃ ca yathāsvamiṣṭā yuddhabhūmayaḥ kālāśca ||
10403| samā sthirābhikāśā nirutkhātinyacakrakhurānakṣagrāhiṇyavṛkṣagulmavratatīstambhakedāraśvabhravalmīkasikatāpaṅkabhaṅgurā daraṇahīnā ca rathabhūmiḥ, hastyaśvayormanuṣyāṇāṃ ca same viṣame hitā yuddhe niveśe ca ||
10404| aṇvaśmavṛkṣā hrasvalaṅghanīyaśvabhrā mandadaraṇadoṣā cāśvabhūmiḥ ||
10405| sthūlasthāṇvaśmavṛkṣavratatīvalmīkagulmā padātibhūmiḥ ||
10406| gamyaśailanimnaviṣamā mardanīyavṛkṣā chedanīyavratatī paṅkabhaṅgurā daraṇahīnā ca hastibhūmiḥ ||
10407| akaṇṭakinyabahuviṣamā pratyāsāravatīti padātīnāmatiśayaḥ ||
10408| dviguṇapratyāsārā kardamodakakhañjanahīnā nihśarkareti vājināmatiśayaḥ ||
10409| pāṃsukardamodakanalaśarādhānavatī śvadaṇṣṭrahīnā mahāvṛkṣaśākhāghātaviyukteti hastināmatiśayaḥ ||
10410| toyāśayāpāśrayavatī nirutkhātinī kedārahīnā vyāvartanasamartheti rathānāmatiśayaḥ ||
10411| uktā sarveṣāṃ bhūmiḥ ||
10412| etayā sarvabalaniveśā yuddhāni ca vyākhyātāni bhavanti ||
10413| bhūmivāsavanavicayoaviṣamatoyatīrthavātaraśmigrahaṇaṃ vīvadhāsārayorghāto rakṣā vā viśuddhiḥ sthāpanā ca balasya prasāravṛddhirbāhūtsāraḥ pūrvaprahāro vyāveśanaṃ vyāvedhanamāśvāso grahaṇaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ mārgānusāravinimayaḥ kośakumārābhiharaṇaṃ jaghanakoṭyabhighāto hīnānusāraṇamanuyānaṃ samājakarmetyaśvakarmāṇi ||
10414| puroyānamakṛtamārgavāsatīrthakarma bāhūtsārastoyataraṇāvataraṇe sthānagamanāvataraṇaṃ viṣamasambādhapraveśoagnidānaśamanamekāṅgavijayo bhinnasaṃdhānamabhinnabhedanaṃ vyasane trāṇamabhighāto vibhīṣikā trāsanaṃaudāryaṃ grahaṇaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ sāladvārāṭṭālakabhañjanaṃ kośavāhanāpavāhanamiti hastikarmāṇi ||
10415| svabalarakṣā caturaṅgabalapratiṣedhaḥ saṃgrāme grahaṇaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ bhinnasaṃdhānamabhinnabhedanaṃ trāsanamaudāryaṃ bhīmaghoṣaśceti rathakarmāṇi ||
10416| sarvadeśakālaśastravahanaṃ vyāyāmaśceti padātikarmāṇi ||
10417| śibiramārgasetukūpatīrthaśodhanakarma yantrāyudhāvaraṇopakaraṇagrāsavahanamāyodhanācca praharaṇāvaraṇapratividdhāpanayanamiti viṣṭikarmāṇi ||
10418  kuryādgavāśvavyāyogaṃ ratheṣvalpahayo nṛpaḥ |
10418  kharoṣṭraśakaṭānāṃ vā garbhamalpagajastathā ||E

(pakṣakakṣorasyānāṃ balāgrato vyūhavibhāgah- sāraphalgubalavibhāgah- pattyaśvarathahastiyuddhāni)
10501| pañcadhanuḥśatāpakṛṣṭaṃ durgamavasthāpya yuddhamupeyāt, bhūmivaśena vā ||
10502| vibhaktamukhyāmacakṣurviṣaye mokṣayitvā senāṃ senāpatināyakau vyūheyātām ||
10503| śamāntaraṃ pattiṃ sthāpayet, triśamāntaramaśvam, pañcaśamāntaraṃ rathaṃ hastinaṃ vā ||
10504| dviguṇāntaraṃ triguṇāntaraṃ vā vyūheta ||
10505| evaṃ yathāsukhamasambādhaṃ yudhyeta ||
10506| pañcāratni dhanuḥ ||
10507| tasmin dhanvinaṃ sthāpayet, tridhanuṣyaśvam, pañcadhanuṣi rathaṃ hastinaṃ vā ||
10508| pañcadhanuranīkasaṃdhiḥ pakṣakakṣorasyānām ||
10509| aśvasya trayaḥ puruṣāḥ pratiyoddhāraḥ ||
10510| pañcadaśa rathasya hastino vā, pañca cāśvāḥ ||
10511| tāvantaḥ pādagopā vājirathadvipānāṃ vidheyāḥ ||
10512| trīṇi trikāṇyanīkaṃ rathānāmurasyaṃ sthāpayet, tāvatkakṣaṃ pakṣaṃ cobhayataḥ ||
10513| pañcacatvāriṃśadevaṃ rathā rathavyūhe bhavanti, dve śate pañcaviṃśatiścāśvāḥ, ṣaṭśatāni pañcasaptatiśca puruṣāḥ pratiyodhāraḥ, tāvantaḥ pādagopāḥ ||
10514| eṣa samavyūhaḥ ||
10515| tasya dvirathottarā vṛddhiraikaviṃśatirathāditi ||
10516| evamojā daśa samavyūhaprakṛtayo bhavanti ||
10517| pakṣakakṣorasyānāṃ mitho viṣamasaṃkhyāne viṣamavyūhaḥ ||
10518| tasyāpi dvirathottarā vṛddhiraikaviṃśatirathāditi ||
10519| evamojā daśa viṣamavyūhaprakṛtayo bhavanti ||
10520| ataḥ sainyānāṃ vyūhaśeṣamāvāpaḥ kāryaḥ ||
10521| rathānāṃ dvau tribhāgāvaṅgeṣvāvāpayet, śeṣamurasyaṃ sthāpayet ||
10522| evaṃ tribhāgono rathānāmāvāpaḥ kāryaḥ ||
10523| tena hastināmaśvānāmāvāpo vyākhyātaḥ ||
10524| yāvadaśvarathadvipānāṃ yuddhasambādhanṃ na kuryāttāvadāvāpaḥ kāryaḥ ||
10525| daṇḍabāhulyamāvāpaḥ ||
10526| pattibāhulyaṃ pratyāpāvaḥ ||
10527| ekāṅgabāhulyamanvāvāpaḥ ||
10528| dūṣyabāhulyamatyāvāpaḥ ||
10529| parāvāpātpratyāvāpācca caturguṇādāṣṭaguṇāditi vā vibhavataḥ sainyānāmāvāpaḥ ||
10530| rathavyūhena hastivyūho vyākhyātaḥ ||
10531| vyāmiśro vā hastirathāśvānāṃ - cakrānteṣu hastinaḥ pārśvayoraśvā rathā urasye ||
10532| hastināmurasyaṃ rathānāṃ kakṣāvaśvānāṃ pakṣāviti madhyabhedī ||
10533| viparītoantabhedī ||
10534| hastināmeva tu śuddhaḥ - sāmnāhyānāmurasyamaupavāhyānāṃ jaghanaṃ vyālānāṃ koṭyāviti ||
10535| aśvavyūho - varmiṇāmurasyaṃ śuddhānāṃ kakṣapakṣāviti ||
10536| pattivyūhaḥ - purastādāvaraṇinaḥ pṛṣṭhato dhanvinaḥ ||
10537| iti śuddhāḥ ||
10538| pattayaḥ pakṣayoraśvāḥ pārśvayoḥ hastinaḥ pṛṣṭhato rathāḥ purastāt, paravyūhavaśena vā viparyāsaḥ ||
10539| iti dvyaṅgabalavibhāgaḥ ||
10540| tena traṅgabalavibhāgo vyākhyātaḥ ||
10541| daṇḍasampatsārabalaṃ puṃsāṃ
10542| hastyaśvayorviśeṣaḥ kulaṃ jātiḥ sattvaṃ vayaḥsthatā prāṇo varṣma javastejaḥ śilpaṃ stairyamudagratā vidheyatvaṃ suvyañjanācārateti ||
10543| pattyaśvarathadvipānāṃ sāratribhāgamurasyaṃ sthāpayet, dvau tribhāgau kakṣaṃ pakṣaṃ cobhayataḥ, anulomamanusāram, pratilomaṃ tṛtīyasāram, phalgu pratilomam ||
10544| evaṃ sarvamupayogaṃ gamayet ||
10545| phalgubalamanteṣvavadhāya vegābhihūliko bhavati ||
10546| sārabalamagrataḥ kṛtvā koṭīṣvanusāraṃ kuryāt, jaghane tṛtiyiyasāram, madhye phalgubalam ||
10547| evametatsahiṣṇu bhavati ||
10548| vyūhaṃ tu sthāpayitvā pakṣakakṣorasyānāmekena dvābhyāṃ vā praharet, śeṣaiḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
10549| yatparasya durbalaṃ vītahastyaśvaṃ dūṣyāmātyaṃ kṛtopajāpaṃ vā tatprabhūtasāreṇābhihanyāt ||
10550| yadvā parasya sāriṣṭhaṃ taddviguṇasāreṇābhihanyāt ||
10551| yadaṅgamalpasāramātmanastadbahunopacinuyāt ||
10552| yataḥ parasyāpacayastatoabhyāśe vyūheta, yatotvā bhayaṃ syāt ||
10553| abhisṛtaṃ parisṛtamatisṛtamapasṛtamunmathyāvadhānaṃ valayo gomūtrikā maṇḍalaṃ prakīrṇikā vyāvṛttapṛṣṭhamanuvaṃśamagrataḥ pārśvābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhato bhagnarakṣā bhagnānupāta ityaśvayuddhāni ||
10554| prakīrṇikāvarjānyetānyeva caturṇāmaṅgānāṃ vyastasamastānāṃ vā ghātaḥ, pakṣakakṣorasyānāṃ ca prabhañjanamavaskandaḥ sauptikaṃ ceti hasityuddhāni ||
10555| unmathyāvadhānavarjānyetānyeva svabhūmāvabhiyānāpayānasthitayuddhānīti rathayuddhāni ||
10556| sarvadeśakālapraharaṇamupāṃśudaṇḍaśceti pattiyuddhāni ||
10557  etena vidhinā vyūhānojān yugmāṃśca kārayet |
10557  vibhavo yāvadaṅgānāṃ caturṇāṃ sadṛśo bhavet ||
10558  dve śate dhanuṣāṃ gatvā rājā tiṣṭhetpratigrahe |
10558  bhinnasaṃghātanaṃ tasmānna yudhyetāpratigrahaḥ ||E

(daṇḍabhogamaṇḍalāsamhatavyūhavyūhanam - tasya prativyūhasthānam)
10601| pakṣāvurasyaṃ pratigraha ityauśanaso vyūhavibhāgaḥ ||
10602| pakṣau kakṣāvurasyṃ pratigraha iti bārhasptyaḥ ||
10603| prapakṣakakṣorasyā ubhayoḥ daṇḍabhogamaṇḍalāsaṃhatāḥ prakṛtivyūhāḥ ||
10604| tatra tiryagvṛttirdaṇḍaḥ ||
10605| samastānāmanvāvṛttirbhogaḥ ||
10606| saratāṃ sarvatovṛttirmaṇḍalaḥ ||
10607| sthitānāṃ pṛthaganīkavṛttirasaṃhataḥ ||
10608| pakṣakakṣorasyaiḥ samaṃ vartamāno daṇḍaḥ ||
10609| sa kakṣātikrāntaḥ pradaraḥ ||
10610| sa eva pakṣakakṣābhyāṃ pratikrānto dṛḍhakaḥ ||
10611| sa evātikrāntaḥ pakṣābhyāmasahyaḥ ||
10612| pakṣāvavasthāpyorasyātikrāntaḥ śyenaḥ ||
10613| viparyaye cāpaṃ cāpakukuṣiḥ pratiṣṭhaḥ supratiṣṭhaśca ||
10614| cāpapakṣaḥ saṃjayaḥ ||
10615| sa evorasyātikrānto vijayaḥ ||
10616| sthūlakarṇapakṣaḥ sthūṇākarṇaḥ ||
10617| dviguṇapakṣasthūṇo viśālavijayaḥ ||
10618| tryabhikrāntapakṣaścamūmukhaḥ ||
10619| viparyaye jhaṣāsyaḥ ||
10620| ūrdhvarājirdaṇḍaḥ sūcī ||
10621| dvau daṇḍau valayaḥ ||
10622| catvāro durjayaḥ ||
10623| iti daṇḍavyūhāḥ ||
10624| pakṣakakṣorasyairviṣamaṃ vartamāno bhogaḥ ||
10625| sa sarpasārī gomūtrikā vā ||
10626| sa yugmorasyo daṇḍapakṣaḥ śakaṭaḥ ||
10627| viparyaye makaraḥ ||
10628| hastyaśvarathairvyatikīrṇaḥ śakaṭaḥ pāripatantakaḥ ||
10629| iti bhogavyūhāḥ ||
10630| pakṣakakṣorasyānāmekībhāve maṇḍalaḥ ||
10631| sa sarvatomukhaḥ sarvatobhadraḥ ||
10632| aṣṭānīko durjayaḥ ||
10633| iti maṇḍalavyūhāḥ ||
10634| pakṣakakṣorasyānāmasaṃhatādasaṃhataḥ ||
10635| sa pañcānīkānāmākṛtisthāpanādvajro godhā vā ||
10636| caturṇāmuddhānakaḥ kākapadī vā ||
10637| trayāṇāmardhacandrakaḥ karkaṭakaśṛṅgī vā ||
10638| ityasaṃhatavyūhāḥ ||
10639| rathorasyo hastikakṣoaśvapṛṣṭhoariṣṭaḥ ||
10640| pattayoaśvā rathā hastinaścānupṛṣṭhamacalaḥ ||
10641| hastinoaśvā rathāḥ pattayaścānupṛṣṭhamapratihataḥ ||
10642| teṣāṃ pradaraṃ dṛḍhakena ghātayet, dṛḍhakamasahyena, śyenaṃ cāpena, pratiṣṭhaṃ supratiṣṭhena, saṃjayaṃ vijayena, sthūṇākarṇaṃ viśālavijayena, pāripatantakaṃ sarvatobhadreṇa ||
10643| durjayena sarvān prativyūheta ||
