10644| pattyaśvarathadvipānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvamuttareṇa ghātayet, hīnāṅgamadhikāṅgena ceti ||
10645| aṅgadaśakasyaikaḥ patiḥ patikaḥ, patikadaśakasyaikaḥ senāpatiḥ, taddaśakasyaiko nāyaka iti ||
10646| sa tūryaghoṣadhvajapatākābhirvyūhāṅgānāṃ saṃjñāḥ sthāpayedaṅgavibhāge saṃghāte sthāne gamane vyāvartane praharaṇe ca ||
10647| same vyūhe deśakālasārayogātsiddhiḥ ||
10648  yantrairupaniṣadyogaistīkṣṇairvyāsaktaghātibhiḥ |
10648  māyābhirdevasamyogaiḥ śakaṭairhastibhīṣaṇaiḥ ||
10649  dūṣyaprakopairgoyūthaiḥ skandhāvārapradīpanaiḥ |
10649  koṭījaghanaghātairvā dūtavyañjanabhedanaiḥ ||
10650  "durgaṃ dagdhaṃ hṛtaṃ vā te kopaḥ kulyaḥ samutthitaḥ |
10650  śatrurāṭaviko vā" iti parasyodvegamācaret ||
10651  ekaṃ hanyānna vā hanyādiṣuḥ kṣipto dhanuṣmatā |
10651  prajñānena tu matiḥ kṣiptā hanyādgarbhagatānapi ||E

(bhedopādānāni - upāmśudaṇḍāh)
11101| saṃghalābho daṇḍamitralābhānāmuttamaḥ ||
11102| saṃghā hi saṃhatatvādadhṛṣyāḥ pareṣām ||
11103| tānanuguṇān bhuñjīta sāmadānābhyām, viguṇān bhedadaṇḍābhyām ||
11104| kāmbojasurāṣṭrakṣatriyaśreṇyādayo vārttaśastropajīvinaḥ ||
11105| licchivikavṛjikamallakamadrakakukurakurupāñcālādayo rājaśabdopajīvinaḥ ||
11106| sarveṣāmāsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ saṃghānāṃ parasparanyaṅgadveṣavairakalahasthānānyupalabhya kramābhinītaṃ bhedamupacārayeyuḥ "asau tvā vijalpati" iti ||
11107| evamubhayatobaddharoṣāṇāṃ vidyāśilpadyūtavaihārikeṣvācāryavyañjanā bālakalahānutpādayeyuḥ ||
11108| veśaśauṇḍikeṣu vā pratilomapraśaṃsābhiḥ saṃghamukhyamanuṣyāṇāṃ tīkṣṇāḥ kalahānutpādayeyuḥ, kṛtyapakṣopagraheṇa vā ||
11109| kumārakān viśiṣṭacchindikayā hīnacchindikānutsāhayeyuḥ ||
11110| viśiṣṭānāṃ caikapātraṃ vivāhaṃ vā hīnebhyo vārayeyuḥ ||
11111| hīnān vā viśiṣṭairekapātre vivāhe vā yojayeyuḥ ||
11112| avahīnān vā tulyabhāvopagamane kulataḥ pauruṣataḥ sthānaviparyāsato vā ||
11113| vyavahāramavasthitaṃ vā pratilomasthāpanena niśāmayeyuḥ ||
11114| vivādapadeṣu vā dravyapaśumanuṣyābhighātena rātrau tīkṣṇāḥ kalahānutpādayeyuḥ ||
11115| sarveṣu ca kalahasthāneṣu hīnapakṣaṃ rājā kośadaṇḍābhyāmupagṛhya pratipakṣavadhe yojayet ||
11116| bhinnānapavāhayedvā ||
11117| bhūmau caiṣāṃ pañcakulīṃ daśakulīṃ vā kṛṣyāyāṃ niveśayet ||
11118| ekasthā hi śastragrahaṇasamarthāḥ syuḥ ||
11119| samavāye caiṣāmatyayaṃ sthāpayet ||
11120| rājaśabdibhiravaruddhamavakṣiptaṃ vā kulyamabhijātaṃ rājaputratve sthāpayet ||
11121| kārtāntikādiścāsya vargo rājalakṣaṇyatāṃ saṃgheṣu prakāśayet ||
11122| saṃghamukhyāṃśca dharmiṣṭhānupajapet"svadharmamamuṣya rājñaḥ putre bhrātari vā pratipadyadhvam" iti ||
11123| pratipanneṣu kṛtyapakṣopagrahārthamarthaṃ daṇḍaṃ ca preṣayet ||
11124| vikramakāle śauṇḍikavyañjanāḥ putradārapretāpadeśena "naiṣecanikam" iti madanarasayuktānmadyakumbhānśataśaḥ prayaccheyuḥ ||
11125| caityadaivatadvārarakṣāsthāneṣu ca sattriṇaḥ samayakarmanikṣepaṃ sahiraṇyābhijñānamudrāṇi hiraṇyabhājanāni ca prarūpayeyuḥ ||
11126| dṛśyamāneṣu ca saṃgheṣu "rājakīyāḥ" ityāvedayeyuḥ ||
11127| athāvaskandaṃ dadyāt ||
11128| saṃghānāṃ vā vāhanahiraṇye kālike gṛhītvā saṃghamukhyāya prakhyātaṃ dravyaṃ prayacchet ||
11129| tadeṣāṃ yācite "dattamamuṣmai mukhyāya" iti brūyāt ||
11130| etena skandhāvārāṭavībhedo vyākhyātaḥ ||
11131| saṃghamukhyaputramātmasambhāvitaṃ vā sattrī grāhayet"amuṣya rājñaḥ putrastvam, śatrubhayādiha nyastoasi" iti ||
11132| pratipannaṃ rājā kośadaṇḍābhyāmupagṛhya saṃgheṣu vikramayet ||
11133| avāptārthastamapi pravāsayet ||
11134| bandhakīpoṣakāḥ plavakanaṭanartakasaubhikā vā praṇihitāḥ strībhiḥ paramarūpayauvanābhiḥ saṃghamukhyānunmādayeyuḥ ||
11135| jātakāmānāmanyatamasya pratyayaṃ kṛtvānyatra gamanena prasabhaharaṇena vā kalahānutpādayeyuḥ ||
11136| kalahe tīkṣṇāḥ karma kuryuḥ "hatoayamitthaṃ kāmukaḥ" iti ||
11137| visaṃvāditaṃ vā marṣayamāṇamabhisṛtya strī brūyāt"asau māṃ mukhyastvayi jātakāmāṃ bādhate, tasminjīvati neha sthāsyāmi" iti ghātamasya prayojayet ||
11138| prasahyāpahṛtā vā vanānte kriḍāgṛhe vāpahartāraṃ rātrau tīkṣṇena ghātayet, svayaṃ vā rasena ||
11139| tataḥ prakāśayet"amunā me priyo hataḥ" iti ||
11140| jātakāmaṃ vā siddhavyañjanaḥ sāṃvadanikībhirauṣadhībhiḥ saṃvāsya rasenātisaṃdhāyāpagacchet ||
11141| tasminnapakrānte sattriṇaḥ paraprayogamabhiśaṃseyuḥ ||
11142| āḍhyavidhavā gūḍhājīvā yogastriyo vā dāyanikṣepārthaṃ vivadamānāḥ saṃghamukhyānunmādayeyuḥ, aditikauśikastriyo nartakīgāyanā vā ||
11143| pratipannān gūḍhaveśmasu rātrisamāgamapraviṣṭāṃstīkṣṇā hanyurbaddhvā hareyurvā ||
11144| sattrī vā strīlolupaṃ saṃghamukhyaṃ prarūpayet"amuṣmin grāme daridrakullamapasṛtam, tasya strī rājārhā, gṛhāṇainām" iti ||
11145| gṛhītāyāmardhamāsānantaraṃ siddhavyañjano dūṣyasaṃghamukhyamadhye prakrośet"asau me mukhyo bhāryāṃ snuṣāṃ bhaginīṃ duhitaraṃ vādhicarati" iti ||
11146| taṃ cetsaṃgho nigṛhṇīyāt, rājainamupagṛhya viguṇeṣu vikramayet ||
11147| anigṛhīte siddhavyañjanaṃ rātrau tīkṣṇāḥ pravāsayeyuḥ ||
11148| tatastadvyañjanāḥ prakrośeyuḥ "asau brahmahā brāhmaṇījāraśca" iti ||
11149| kārtāntikavyañjano vā kanyāmanyena vṛtāmanyasya prarūpayet"amuṣya kanyā rājapatnī rājaprasavinī ca bhaviṣyati, sarvasvena prasahya vaināṃ labhasva" iti ||
11150| alabhyamānāyāṃ parapakṣamuddharṣayet ||
11151| labdhāyāṃ siddhaḥ kalahaḥ ||
11152| bhikṣukī vā priyabhāryaṃ mukhyaṃ brūyāt"asau te mukhyo yauvanotsikto bhāryāyāṃ māṃ prāhiṇot, tasyāhaṃ bhayāllekhyamābharaṇaṃ gṛhītvāgatāsmi, nirdoṣā te bhāryā, gūḍhamasmin pratikartavyam, ahamapi tāvatpratipatsyāmi" iti ||
11153| evaṃādiṣu kalahasthāneṣu svayamutpanne vā kalahe tīkṣṇairutpādite vā hīnapakṣaṃ rājā kośadaṇḍābhyāmupagṛhya viguṇeṣu vikramayedapavāhayedvā ||
11154| saṃgheṣvevamekarājo varteta ||
11155| saṃghāścāpyevamekarājādetebhyoatisaṃghānebhyo rakṣayeyuḥ ||
11156  saṃghamukhyaśca saṃgheṣu nyāyavṛttirhitaḥ priyaḥ |
11156  dānto yuktajanastiṣṭhetsarvacittānuvartakaḥ ||E

(dūtakarma)
12101| "balīyasābhiyukto durbalaḥ sarvatrānupraṇato vetasadharmā tiṣṭhet ||
12102| indrasya hi sa praṇamati yo balīyaso namati" iti bhāradvājaḥ ||
12103| "sarvasaṃdohena balānāṃ yudhyeta ||
12104| parākramo hi vyasanamapahanti ||
12105| svadharmaścaiṣa kṣatriyasya, yuddhe jayaḥ parājayo vā" iti viśālākṣaḥ ||
12106| neti kauṭilyaḥ ||
12107| sarvatrānupraṇataḥ kulaiḍaka iva nirāśo jīvite vasati ||
12108| yudhyamānaścālpasainyaḥ samudramivāplavoavagāhamānaḥ sīdati ||
12109| tadviśiṣṭaṃ tu rājānamāśrito durgamaviṣahyaṃ vā ceṣṭeta ||
12110| trayoabhiyoktāro dharmalobhāsuravijayina iti ||
12111| teṣāmabhyavapattyā dharmavijayī tuṣyati ||
12112| tamabhyavapadyeta, pareṣāmapi bhayāt ||
12113| bhūmidravyaharaṇena lobhavijayī tuṣyati ||
12114| tamarthenābhyavapadyeta ||
12115| bhūmidravyaputradāraprāṇaharaṇenāsuravijayī ||
12116| taṃ bhūmidravyābhyāmupagṛhyāgrāhyaḥ pratikurvīta ||
12117| teṣāmanyatamamuttiṣṭhamānaṃ saṃdhinā mantrayuddhena kūṭayuddhena vā prativyūheta ||
12118| śatrupakṣamasya sāmadānābhyām, svapakṣaṃ bhedadaṇḍābhyām ||
12119| durgaṃ rāṣṭraṃ skandhāvāraṃ vāsya gūḍhāḥ śastrarasāgnibhiḥ sādhayeyuḥ ||
12120| sarvataḥ pārṣṇimasya grāhayet ||
12121| aṭavībhirvā rājyaṃ ghātayet, tatkulīnāparuddhābhyāṃ vā hārayet ||
12122| apakārānteṣu cāsya dūṭaṃ preṣayet ||
12123| anapakṛtya vā saṃdhānam ||
12124| tathāpyabhiprayāntaṃ kośadaṇḍayoḥ pādottaramahorātrottaraṃ vā saṃdhiṃ yāceta ||
12125| sa ceddaṇḍasaṃdhiṃ yāceta, kuṇṭhamasmai hastyaśvaṃ dadyād, utsāhitaṃ vā garayuktam ||
12126| puruṣasaṃdhiṃ yāceta, dūṣyāmitrāṭavībalamasmai dadyādyogapuruṣādhiṣṭhitam ||
12127| tathā kuryādyathobhayavināśaḥ syāt ||
12128| tīkṣṇabalaṃ vāsmai dadyādyadavamānitaṃ vikurvīta, maulamanuraktaṃ vā yadasya vyasanepakuryāt ||
12129| kośasaṃdhiṃ yāceta, sāramasmai dadyādyasya kretāraṃ nādhigacchet, kupyamayuddhayogyaṃ vā ||
12130| bhūmisaṃdhiṃ yāceta, pratyādeyāṃ nityāmitrāmanapāśrayāṃ mahākṣayavyayaniveśāṃ vāsmai bhūmiṃ dadyāt ||
12131| sarvasvena vā rājadhānīvarjena saṃdhiṃ yāceta balīyasaḥ ||
12132  yatprasahya haredanyastatprayaccedupāyataḥ |
12132  rakṣetsvadehaṃ na dhanaṃ kā hyanitye dhane dayā ||E

(mantrayuddha)
12201| sa cetsaṃdhau nāvatiṣṭheta, brūyādenaṃ - "ime śatruṣaḍvargavaśagā rājāno vinaṣṭāḥ, teṣāmanātmavatāṃ nārhasi mārgamanugantum ||
12202| dharmamarthaṃ cāvekṣasva ||
12203| mitramukhā hyamitrāste ye tvā sāhasamadharmamarthātikramaṃ ca grāhayanti ||
12204| śūraistyaktātmabhiḥ saha yoddhuṃ sāhasam, janakṣayamubhayataḥ kartumadharmaḥ, dṛṣṭamarthaṃ mitramaduṣṭaṃ ca tyaktumarthātikramaḥ ||
12205| mitravāṃśca sa rājā, bhūyaścaitenārthena mitrāṇyudyojayiṣyati yāni tvā sarvatoabhiyāsyanti ||
12206| na ca madhyamodāsīnayormaṇḍalasya vā parityaktaḥ, bhavāṃstu parityaktaḥ yattvā samudyuktamupaprekṣante "bhūyaḥ kṣayavyayābhyāṃ yujyatām, mitrācca bhidyatām, athainaṃ parityaktamūlaṃ sukhenocchetsyāmaḥ" iti ||
12207| sa bhavānnārhati mitramukhānāmamitrāṇāṃ śrotum, mitrāṇyudvejayitumamitrāṃśca śreyasā yoktum, prāṇasaṃśayamanarthaṃ copagantum" iti yacchet ||
12208| tathāpi pratiṣṭhamānasya prakṛtikopamasya kārayedyathā saṃghavṛtte vyākhyātaṃ yogavāmane ca ||
12209| tīkṣṇarasadaprayogaṃ ca ||
12210| yaduktamātmarakṣitake rakṣyaṃ tatra tīkṣṇān rasadāṃśca prayuñjīta ||
12211| bandhakīpoṣakāḥ paramarūpayauvanābhiḥ strībhiḥ senāmukhyānunmādayeyuḥ ||
12212| bahūnāmekasyāṃ dvayorvā mukhyayoḥ kāme jāte tīkṣṇāḥ kalahānutpādayeyuḥ ||
12213| kalahe parājitapakṣaṃ paratrāpagamane yātrāsāhāyyadāne vā bharturyojayeyuḥ ||
12214| kāmavaśān vā siddhavyañjanāḥ sāṃvadanikībhiroṣadhībhiratisaṃdhānāya mukhyeṣu rasaṃ dāpayeyuḥ ||
12215| vaidehakavyañjane vā rājamahiṣyāḥ subhagāyāḥ preṣyāmāsannāṃ kāmanimittamarthenābhivṛṣya parityajet ||
12216| tasyaiva paricārakavyañjanopadiṣṭaḥ siddhavyañjanaḥ sāṃvadanikīmoṣadhīṃ dadyāt"vaidehakaśarīrevaghātavyā" iti ||
12217| siddhe subhagāyā apyenaṃ yogamupadiśet"rājaśarīrevadhātavyā" iti ||
12218| tato rasenātisaṃdadhyāt ||
12219| kārtāntikavyañjano vā mahāmātraṃ "rājalakṣaṇasampannam" kramābhinītaṃ brūyāt ||
12220| bhāryāmasya bhikṣukī "rājapatnī rājaprasavinī vā bhaviṣyasi" iti ||
12221| bhāryāvyañjanā vā mahāmātraṃ brūyāt"rājā kila māmavarodhayiṣyati, tavāntikāya pattralekhyamābharaṇaṃ cedaṃ parivrājikayāhṛtam" iti ||
12222| sūdārālikavyañjano vā rasaprayogārthaṃ rājavacanamarthaṃ cāsya lobhanīyamabhinayet ||
12223| tadasya vaidehakavyañjanaḥ pratisaṃdadhyāt, kāryasiddhiṃ ca brūyāt ||
12224| evamekena dvābhyāṃ tribhirityupāyairekaikamasya mahāmātraṃ vikramāyāpagamanāya vā yojayet- iti ||
12225| durgeṣu cāsya śūnyapālāsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ paurajānapadeṣu maitrīnimittamāvedayeyuḥ - "śūnyapālenoktā yodhāścādhikaraṇasthāśca "kṛcchragato rājā jīvannāgamiṣyati, na vā, prasahya vittamārjayadhvam, amitrāṃśca hata" iti ||
12226| bahulībhūte tīkṣṇāḥ paurānniśāsvāhārayeyuḥ, mukhyāṃścābhihanyuḥ "evaṃ kriyante ye śūnyapālasya na śuśrūṣante" iti ||
12227| śūnyapālasthāneṣu ca saśoṇitāni śastravittabandhanānyutsṛjeyuḥ ||
12228| tataḥ sattriṇaḥ "śūnyapālo ghātayati vilopayati ca" ityāvedayeyuḥ ||
12229| evaṃ jānapadān samāharturbhedayeyuḥ ||
12230| samāhartṛpuruṣāṃstu grāmamadhyeṣu rātrau tīkṣṇā hatvā brūyuḥ "evaṃ kriyante ye janapadamadharmeṇa bādhante" iti ||
12231| samutpanne doṣe śūnyapālaṃ samāhartāraṃ vā prakṛtikopena ghātayeyuḥ ||
12232| tatkulīnamaparuddhaṃ vā pratipādayeyuḥ ||
12233  antaḥpurapuradvāraṃ dravyadhānyaparigrahān |
12233  daheyustāṃśca hanyurvā brūyurasyārtavādinaḥ ||E

(senāmukhyavadhah<dvitīyo bhāgah> - maṇḍalaprotsāhanam)
12301| rājño rājavallabhānāṃ cāsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ pattyaśvarathadvipamukhyānāṃ "rājā kruddhaḥ" iti suhṛdviśvāsena mitrasthānīyeṣu kathayeyuḥ ||
12302| bahulībhūte tīkṣṇāḥ kṛtarātricārapratīkārā gṛheṣu "svāmivacanenāgamyatām" iti brūyuḥ ||
12303| tānnirgacchata evābhihanyuḥ, "svāmisaṃdeśaḥ" iti cāsannān brūyuḥ ||
12304| ye cāpravāsitāstān sattriṇo brūyuḥ "etattadyadasmābhiḥ kathitam, jīvitukāmenāpakrāntavyam" iti ||
12305| yebhyaśca rājā yācito na dadāti tān sattriṇo brūyuḥ - "uktaḥ śūnyapālo rājñā "ayācyamarthamasau cāsau ca mā yācate, mayā pratyākhyātāḥ śatrusaṃhitāḥ, teṣāmuddharaṇe prayatasva" iti ||
12306| tataḥ pūrvavadācaret ||
12307| yebhyaśca rājā yācito dadāti tān sattriṇo brūyuḥ - "uktaḥ śūnyapālo rājñā "ayācyamarthamasau cāsau ca mā yācate, tebhyo mayā soartho viśvāsārthaṃ dattaḥ, śatrusaṃhitāḥ, teṣāmuddharaṇe prayatasva" iti ||
12308| tataḥ pūrvavadācaret ||
12309| ye cainaṃ yācyamarthaṃ na yācante tān sattriṇo brūyuḥ - "uktaḥ śūnyapālo rājñā "yācyamarthamasau cāsau ca mā na yācate, kimanyatsvadoṣaśaṅkitatvāt, teṣāmuddharaṇe prayatasva" iti ||
12310| tataḥ pūrvavadācaret ||
12311| etena sarvaḥ kṛtyapakṣo vyākhyātaḥ ||
12312| pratyāsanno vā rājānaṃ sattrī grāhayet"asau cāsau ca te mahāmātraḥ śatrupuruṣaiḥ sambhāṣate" iti ||
12313| pratipanne dūṣyānasya śāsanaharān darśayet"etattat" iti ||
12314| senāmukhyaprakṛtipuruṣān vā bhūmyā hiraṇyena vā lobhayitvā sveṣu vikramayedapavāhayedvā ||
12315| yoasya putraḥ samīpe durge vā prativasati taṃ sattriṇopajāpayet"ātmasampannatarastvaṃ putraḥ, tathāpyantarhitaḥ, tatkimupekṣase vikramya gṛhāṇa, purā tvā yuvarājo vināśayati" iti ||
12316| tatkulīnamaparuddhaṃ vā hiraṇyena pratilobhya brūyāt"antarbalaṃ pratyantaskandhamantaṃ vāsya pramṛdnīhi" iti ||
12317| āṭavikānarthamānābhyāmupagṛhya rājyamasya ghātayet ||
12318| pārṣṇigrāhaṃ vāsya brūyāt"eṣa khalu rājā māmucchidya tvāmucchetsyati, pārṣṇimasya gṛhāṇa, tvayi nivṛttasyāhaṃ pārṣṇiṃ grahīṣyāmi" iti ||
12319| mitrāṇi vāsya brūyāt"ahaṃ vaḥ setuḥ, mayi vibhinne sarvāneṣa vo rājā plāvayiṣyati, sambhūya vāsya yātrāṃ vihanāma" iti ||
12320| tatsaṃhatānāmasaṃhatānāṃ ca preṣayet"eṣa khalu rājā māmutpāṭya bhavatsu karma kariṣyati, budhyadhvam, ahaṃ vaḥ śreyānabhyupapattum" iti ||
12321  madhyamasya prahiṇuyādudāsīnasya vā punaḥ |
12321  yathāsannasya mokṣārthaṃ sarvasvena tadarpaṇam ||E

(śastrāgnirasapraṇidhayah- vīvadhāsāraprrasāravadhah)
12401| ye cāsya durgeṣu vaidehakavyañjanāḥ, grāmesu gṛhapatikavyañjanāḥ, janapadasaṃdhiṣu gorakṣakatāpasavyañjanāḥ, te sāmantāṭavikatatkulīnāparuddhānāṃ paṇyāgārapūrvaṃ preṣayeyuḥ "ayaṃ deśo hāryaḥ" iti ||
12402| āgatāṃścaiṣāṃ durge gūḍhapuruṣānarthamānābhyāmabhisatkṛtya prakṛticchidrāṇi pradarśayeyuḥ ||
12403| teṣu taiḥ saha prahareyuḥ ||
12404| skandhāvāre vāsya śauṇḍikavyañjanaḥ putramabhityaktaṃ sthāpayitvāvaskandakāle rasena pravāsayitvā "naiṣecanikam" iti madanarasayuktānmadyakumbhānśataśaḥ prayacchet ||
12405| śuddhaṃ vā madyaṃ pādyaṃ vā madyaṃ dadyādekamahaḥ, uttaraṃ rassiddhaṃ prayacchet ||
12406| śuddhaṃ vā madyaṃ daṇḍamukhyebhyaḥ pradāya madakāle rasasiddhaṃ prayacchet ||
12407| daṇḍamukhyavyañjano vā putramabhityaktamiti samānam ||
12408| pākvamāṃsikāudanikāuṇḍikāpūpikavyañjanā vā paṇyaviśeṣamavaghoṣayitvā parasparasaṃgharṣeṇa kālikaṃ samarghataramiti vā parānāhūya rasena svapaṇyānyapacārayeyuḥ ||
12409| surākṣīradadhisarpistailāni vā tadvyavahartṛhasteṣu gṛhītā striyo bālāśca rasayukteṣu svabhājaneṣu parikireyuḥ ||
12410| "anenārgheṇa, viśiṣṭaṃ vā bhūyo dīyatām" iti tatraivāvākireyuḥ ||
12411| etānyeva vaidehakavyañjanāḥ, paṇyavireyeṇāhartāro vā ||
12412| hastyaśvānāṃ vidhāyavaseṣu rasamāsannā dadyuḥ ||
12413| karmakaravyañjanā vā rasāktaṃ yavasamudakaṃ vā vikrīṇīran ||
12414| cirasaṃsṛṣṭā vā govāṇijakā gavāmajāvīnāṃ vā yūthānyavaskandakāleṣu pareṣāṃ mohasthāneṣu pramuñceyuḥ, aśvakharoṣṭramahiṣādīnāṃ duṣṭāṃśca ||
12415| tadvyañjanā vā cucchundarīśoṇitāktākṣān ||
12416| lubdhakavyañjanā vā vyālamṛgān pañjarebhyaḥ pramuñceyuḥ, sarpagrāhā vā sarpānugraviṣān, hastijīvino vā hastinaḥ ||
12417| agnijīvino vāgnimavasṛjeyuḥ ||
12418| gūḍhapuruṣā vā vimukhān pattyaśvarathadvipamukhyānabhihanyuḥ, ādīpayeyurvā mukhyāvāsān ||
12419| dūṣyāmitrāṭavikavyañjanāḥ praṇihitāḥ pṛṣṭhābhighātamavaskandapratigrahaṃ vā kuryuḥ ||
12420| vanagūḍhā vā pratyantaskandhamupaniṣkṛṣyābhihanyuḥ, ekāyane vīvadhāsāraprasārān vā ||
12421| sasaṃketaṃ vā rātriyuddhe bhūritūryamāhatya brūyuḥ "anupraviṣṭāḥ smo, labdhaṃ rājyam" iti ||
12422| rājāvāsamanupraviṣṭā vā saṃkuleṣu rājānaṃ hanyuḥ ||
12423| sarvato vā prayātamena(?eva?) mlecchāṭavikadaṇṭacāriṇaḥ sattrāpāśrayāḥ stambhavāṭāpāśrayā vā hanyuḥ ||
12424| lubdhakavyañjanā vāvaskandasaṃkuleṣu gūḍhayuddhahetubhirabhihanyuḥ ||
12425| ekāyane vā śailastambhavāṭakhañjanāntarudake vā svabhūmibalenābhihanyuḥ ||
12426| nadīsarastaṭākasetubandhabhedavegena vā plāvayeyuḥ ||
12427| dhānvanavanadurganimnadurgasthaṃ vā yogāgnidhūmābhyāṃ nāśayeyuḥ ||
12428| saṃkaṭagatamagninā, dhānvanagataṃ dhūmena, nidhānagataṃ rasena, toyāvagāḍhaṃ duṣṭagrāhairudakacaraṇairvā tīkṣṇāḥ sādhayeyuḥ, ādīptāvāsānniṣpatantaṃ vā ||
12429  yogavāmanayogābhyāṃ yogenānyatamena vā |
12429  amitramatisaṃdadhyātsaktamuktāsu bhūmiṣu ||E

(yogātisamdhānam - daṇḍātisamdhānam - ekavijayah)
12501| daivatejyāyām(devatejyāyām?) yātrāyāmamitrasya bahūni pūjāgamasthānāni bhaktitaḥ ||
12502| tatrāsya yogamubjayet ||
12503| devatāgṛhapraviṣṭasyopari yantramokṣaṇena gūḍhabhittiṃ śilāṃ vā pātayet ||
12504| śilāśastravarṣamuttamāgārāt, kapāṭamavapātitaṃ vā, bhittipraṇihitamekadeśabaddhaṃ vā parighaṃ mokṣayet ||
12505| devatādehadhvajapraharaṇāni vāsyopariṣṭātpātayet ||
12506| sthānāsanagamanabhūmiṣu vāsya gomayapradehena gandhodakaprasekena vā rasamaticārayet, puṣpacūrṇopahāreṇa vā ||
12507| gandhapraticchannaṃ vāsya tīkṣṇaṃ dhūmamatinayet ||
12508| śūlakūpamavapātanaṃ vā śayanāsanasyādhastādyantrabaddhatalamenaṃ kīlamokṣaṇena praveśayet ||
12509| pratyāsanne vāmitre janapadājjanamavarodhakṣamamatinayet ||
12510| durgāccānavarodhakṣamamapanayet, pratyādeyamariviṣayaṃ vā preṣayet ||
12511| janapadaṃ caikasthaṃ śailavananadīdurgeṣvaṭavīvyavahiteṣu vā putrabhrātṛparigṛhītaṃ sthāpayet ||
12512| uparodhahetavo daṇḍopanatavṛtte vyākhyātāḥ ||
12513| tṛṇakāṣṭhamāyojanāddāhayet ||
12514| udakāni ca dūṣayet, avasrāvayecca ||
12515| kūpakūṭāvapātakaṇṭakinīśca bahirubjayet ||
12516| suruṅgāmamitrasthāne bahumukhīṃ kṛtvā nicayamukhyānabhihārayet, amitraṃ vā ||
12517| paraprayuktāyāṃ vā suruṅgāyāṃ parikhāmudakāntikīṃ khānayet, kūpaśālāmanusālaṃ vā ||
12518| toyakumbhān kāṃsyabhāṇḍāni vā śaṅkāsthāneṣu sthāpayetkhātābhijñānārtham ||
12519| jñāte suruṅgāpathe pratisuruṅgāṃ kārayet ||
12520| madhye bhittvā dhūmamudakaṃ vā prayacchet ||
12521| prativihitadurgo vā mūle dāyād<am> kṛtvā pratilomāmasya diśaṃ gacchet, yato vā mitrairbandhubhirāṭavikairvā saṃsṛjyeta parasyāmitrairdūṣyairvā mahadbhiḥ, yato vā gatoasya mitrairviyogaṃ kuryātpārṣṇiṃ vā gṛhṇīyātrājyaṃ vāsya hārayetvīvadhāsāraprasārān vā vārayet, yato vā śaknuyādākṣikavadapakṣepeṇāsya prahartum, yato vā
svaṃ rājyaṃ trāyeta mūlasyopacayaṃ vā kuryāt ||
12522| yataḥ saṃdhimabhipretaṃ labheta tato vā gacchet ||
12523| sahaprasthāyino vāsya preṣayeyuḥ "ayaṃ te śatrurasmākaṃ hastagataḥ, paṇyaṃ viprakāraṃ vāpadiśya hiraṇyamantaḥsārabalaṃ ca preṣaya yasyainamarpayema baddhaṃ pravāsitaṃ vā" iti ||
12524| pratipanne hiraṇyaṃ sārabalaṃ cādadīta ||
12525| antapālo vā durgasampradāne balaikadeśamatinīya viśvastaṃ ghātayet ||
12526| janapadamekasthaṃ vā ghātayitumamitrānīkamāvāhayet ||
12527| tadavaruddhadeśamatinīya viśvastaṃ ghātayet ||
12528| mitravyañjano vā bāhyasya preṣayet"kṣīṇamasmin durge dhānyaṃ snehāḥ kṣāro lavaṇaṃ vā, tadamuṣmin deśe kāle ca pravekṣyati, tadupagṛhāṇa" iti ||
12529| tato rasaviddhaṃ dhānyaṃ snehaṃ kṣāraṃ lavaṇaṃ vā dūṣyāmitrāṭavikāḥ praveśayeyuḥ, anye vābhityaktāḥ ||
12530| tena sarvabhāṇḍavīvadhagrahaṇaṃ vyākhyātam ||
12531| saṃdhiṃ vā kṛtvā hiraṇyaikadeśamasmai dadyāt, vilambamānaḥ śeṣam ||
12532| tato rakṣāvidhānānyavasrāvayet ||
12533| agnirasaśastrairvā praharet ||
12534| hiraṇyapratigrāhiṇo vāsya vallabhānanugṛhṇīyāt ||
12535| parikṣīṇo vāsmai durgaṃ dattvā nirgacchet ||
12536| suruṅgayā kukṣipradareṇa vā prākārabhedena nirgacchet ||
12537| rātrāvavaskandaṃ dattvā siddhastiṣṭhet, asiddhaḥ pārśvenāpagacchet ||
12538| pāṣaṇḍacchadmanā mandaparivāro nirgacchet ||
12539| pretavyañjano vā gūḍhairnihriyeta ||
12540| strīveṣadhārī vā pretamanugacchet ||
12541| daivatopahāraśrāddhaprahavaṇeṣu vā rasaviddhamannapānamavasṛjya ||
12542| kṛtopajāpo dūṣyavyañjanairniṣpatya gūḍhasainyoabhihanyāt ||
12543| evaṃ gṛhītadurgo vā prāśyaprāśaṃ caityamupasthāpya daivatapratimācchidraṃ praviśyāsīta, gūḍhabhittiṃ vā, daivatapratimāyuktaṃ vā bhūmigṛham ||
12544| vismṛte suruṅgayā rātrau rājāvāsamanupraviśya suptamamitraṃ hanyāt ||
12545| yantraviśleṣaṇaṃ vā viśleṣyādhastādavapātayet ||
12546| rasāgniyogenāvaliptaṃ gṛhaṃ jatugṛhaṃ vādhiśayānamamitramādīpayet ||
12547| pramadavanavihārāṇāmanyatame vā vihārasthāne pramattaṃ bhūmigṛhasuruṅgāgūḍhabhittipraviṣṭāstīkṣṇā hanyuḥ, gūḍhapraṇihitā vā rasena ||
12548| svapato vā niruddhe deśe gūḍhāḥ striyaḥ sarparasāgnidhūmānupari muñceyuḥ ||
12549| pratyutpanne vā kāraṇe yadyadupapadyeta tattadamitrentaḥpuragate gūḍhasaṃcāraḥ prayuñjīta ||
12550| tato gūḍhamevāpagacchet, svajanasaṃjñāṃ ca prarūpayet ||
12451  dvāhsthān varṣadharāṃścānyānnigūḍhopahitān pare |
12451 tūryasaṃjñābhirāhūya dviṣatśeṣāṇi ghātayet ||E

(upajāpah)
13101| vijigīṣuḥ paragrāmamavāptukāmaḥ sarvajñadaivatasamyogakhyāpanābhyāṃ svapakṣamuddharṣayet, parapakṣaṃ codvejayet ||
13102| sarvajñakhyāpanaṃ tu - gṛhaguhyapravṛttijñānena pratyādeśo mukhyānām, kaṇṭakaśodhanāpasarpāvagamena prakāśanaṃ rājadviṣṭakāriṇām, vijñāpyopāyanakhyāpanamadṛṣṭasaṃsargavidyāsaṃjñādibhiḥ, videśapravṛttijñānaṃ tadahareva gṛhakapotena mudrāsamyuktena ||
13103| daivatasamyogakhyāpanaṃ tu - suruṅgāmukhenāgnicaityadaivatapratimācchidrānanupraviṣṭairagnicaityadaivatavyañjanaiḥ sambhāṣaṇaṃ pūjanaṃ ca, udakādutthitairvā nāgavaruṇavyañjanaiḥ sambhāṣaṇaṃ pūjanaṃ ca, rātrāvantarudake samudravālukākośaṃ praṇidhāyāgnimālādarśanam, śilāśikyāvagṛhīte plavake sthānam, udakabastinā
jarāyuṇā vā śiroavagūḍhanāsaḥ pṛṣatāntrakulīranakraśiṃśumārodravasābhirvā śatapākyaṃ tailaṃ nastaḥ prayogaḥ ||
13104| tena rātrigaṇaścarati ||
13105| ityudakacaraṇāni ||
13106| tairvaruṇanāgakanyāvākyakriyā sambhāṣaṇaṃ ca, kopasthāneṣu mukhādagnidhūmotsargaḥ ||
13107| tadasya svaviṣaye kārtāntikanaimittikamauhūrtikapaurāṇikekṣaṇikagūḍhapuruṣāḥ sācivyakarāstaddarśinaśca prakāśayeyuḥ ||
13108| parasya viṣaye daivatadarśanaṃ divyakośadaṇḍotpattiṃ cāsya brūyuḥ ||
13109| daivatapraśnanimittavāyasāṅgavidyāsvapnamṛgapakṣivyāhāreṣu cāsya vijayaṃ brūyuḥ, viparītamamitrasya ||
13110| sadundubhimulkāṃ ca parasya nakṣatre darśayeyuḥ ||
13111| parasya mukhyānmitratvenopadiśanto dūtavyañjanāḥ svāmisatkāraṃ brūyuḥ, svapakṣabalādhānaṃ parapakṣapratighātaṃ ca ||
13112| tulyayogakṣemamamātyānāmāyudhīyānāṃ ca kathayeyuḥ ||
13113| teṣu vyasanābhyudayāvekṣaṇamapatyapūjanaṃ ca prayuñjīta ||
13114| tena parapakṣamutsāhayedyathoktaṃ purastāt ||
13115| bhūyaśca vakṣyāmaḥ ||
13116| sādhāraṇagardabhena dakṣān, lakuṭaśākhāhananābhyāṃ daṇḍacāriṇaḥ, kulaiḍakena codvignān, aśanivarṣeṇa vimānitān, vidulenāvakeśinā vāyasapiṇḍena kaitavajamegheneti vihatāśān durbhagālaṃkāreṇa dveṣiṇeti pūjāphalān, vyāghracarmaṇā mṛtyukūṭena copahitān, pīluvikhādanena karakayoṣṭrayā gardabhīkṣīrābhimanthaneneti
dhruvopakāriṇa iti ||
13117| pratipannānarthamānābhyāṃ yojayet
13118| dravyabhaktacchidreṣu cainān dravyabhaktadānairanugṛhṇīyāt ||
13119| apratigṛhṇatāṃ strīkumārālaṃkārānabhihareyuḥ ||
13120| durbhikṣastenāṭavyupaghāteṣu ca paurajānapadānutsāhayantaḥ sattriṇo brūyuḥ "rājānamanugrahaṃ yācāmahe niranugrahāḥ paratra gacchāmaḥ" iti ||
13121  tatheti pratipanneṣu dravyadhānyānyaparigrahaiḥ |
13121  sācivyaṃ kāryamityetadupajāpādbhūtaṃ mahat ||E

(yogavāmanam)
13201| muṇḍo jaṭilo vā parvataguhāvāsī caturvarṣaśatāyurbruvāṇaḥ prabhūtajaṭilāntevāsī nagarābhyāśe tiṣṭhet ||
13202| śiṣyāścāsya mūlaphalopagamanairamātyān rājānaṃ ca bhagavaddarśanāya yojayeyuḥ ||
13203| samāgatāśca rājñā pūrvarājadeśābhijñānāni kathayet, "śate śate ca varṣāṇāṃ pūrṇehamagniṃ praviśya punarbālo bhavāmi, tadiha bhavatsamīpe caturthamagniṃ pravekṣyāmi, avaśyaṃ me bhavānmānayitavyaḥ, trīn varān vṛṇīṣṇa(vṛṣīṣva)" iti ||
13204| pratipannaṃ brūyāt"saptarātramiha saputradāreṇa prekṣāprahavaṇapūrvaṃ vastavyam" iti ||
13205| vasantamavaskandeta ||
13206| muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vā sthānikavyañjanaḥ prabhūtajaṭilāntevāsī vastaśoṇitadigdhāṃ veṇuśalākāṃ suvarṇacūrṇenāvalipya valmīke nidadhyādupajihvikānusaraṇārtham, svarṇanālikāṃ vā ||
13207| tataḥ sattrī rājñaḥ kathayet"asau siddhaḥ puṣpitaṃ nidhiṃ jānāti" iti ||
13208| sa rājñā pṛṣṭhaḥ "tathā" iti brūyāt, taccābhijñānaṃ darśayet, bhūyo vā hiraṇyamantarādhāya ||
13209| brūyāccainaṃ "nāgarakṣitoayaṃ nidhiḥ praṇipātasādhyaḥ" iti ||
13210| pratipannaṃ brūyāt"saptarātram" iti samānam ||
13211| sthānikavyañjanaṃ vā rātrau tejanāgniyuktamekānte tiṣṭhantaṃ sattriṇaḥ kramābhīnītaṃ rājñaḥ kathayeyuḥ "asau siddhaḥ sāmedhikaḥ" iti ||
13212| taṃ rājā yamarthaṃ yāceta tamasya kariṣyamāṇaḥ "saptarātram" iti samānam ||
13213| siddhavyañjano vā rājānaṃ jambhakavidyābhiḥ pralobhayet ||
13214| taṃ rājeti samānam ||
13215| siddhavyañjano vā deśadevatāmabhyarhitāmāśritya prahavaṇairabhīkṣṇaṃ prakṛtimukhyānabhisaṃvāsya krameṇa rājānamatisaṃdadhyāt ||
13216| jaṭilavyañjanamantarudakavāsinaṃ vā sarvaśvetaṃ taṭasuruṅgābhūmigṛhāpasaraṇaṃ varuṇaṃ nāgarājaṃ vā sattriṇaḥ kramābhinītaṃ rājñaḥ kathayeyuḥ ||
13217| taṃ rājeti samānam ||
13218| janapadāntevāsī siddhavyañjano vā rājānaṃ śatrudarśanāya yojayet ||
13219| pratipannaṃ bimbaṃ kṛtvā śatrumāvāhayitvā niruddhe deśe ghātayet ||
13220| aśvapaṇyopayātā vaidehakavyañjanāḥ paṇyopāyananimittamāhūya rājānaṃ paṇyaparīkṣāyāmāsaktamaśvavyatikīrṇaṃ vā hanyuḥ, aśvaiśca prahareyuḥ ||
13221| nagarābhyāśe vā caityamāruhya rātrau tīkṣṇāḥ kumbheṣu nālīn vā vidulāni dhamantaḥ "svāmino mukhyānāṃ vā māṃsāni bhakṣayiṣyāmaḥ, pūjā no vartatām" ityavyaktaṃ brūyuḥ ||
13222| tadeṣāṃ naimittikamauhūrtikavyañjanāḥ khyāpayeyuḥ ||
13223| maṅgalye vā hrade taṭākamadhye vā rātrau tejanatailābhyaktā nāgarūpiṇaḥ śaktimusalānyayomayāni niṣpeṣayantastathaiva brūyuḥ ||
13224| ṛkṣacarmakañcukino vāgnidhūmotsargayuktā rakṣorūpaṃ vahantastrirapasavyaṃ nagaraṃ kurvāṇāḥ śvasṛgālavāśitāntareṣu tathaiva brūyuḥ ||
13225| caityadaivatapratimāṃ vā tejanatailenābhrapaṭalacchannenāgninā vā rātrau prajvālya tathaiva brūyuḥ ||
13226| tadanye khyāpayeyuḥ ||
13227| daivatapratimānāmabhyarhitānāṃ vā śoṇitena prasrāvamatimātraṃ kuryuḥ ||
13228| tadanye devarudhirasaṃsrāve saṃgrāme parājayaṃ brūyuḥ ||
13229| saṃdhirātriṣu śmaśānapramukhe vā caityamūrdhvabhakṣitairmanuṣyaiḥ prarūpayeyuḥ ||
13230| tato rakṣorūpī manuṣyakaṃ yāceta ||
13231| yaścātra śūravādikoanyatamo vā draṣṭumāgacchettamanye lohamusalairhanyuḥ, yathā rakṣobhirhata iti jñāyeta ||
13232| tadadbhutaṃ rājñastaddarśinaḥ sattriṇaśca kathayeyuḥ ||
13233| tato naimititkamauhūrtikavyañjanāḥ śāntiṃ prāyaścittaṃ brūyuḥ "anyathā mahadakuśalaṃ rājño deśasya ca" iti ||
13234| pratipannaṃ "eteṣu saptarātramekaikamantrabalihomaṃ svayaṃ rājñā kartavyam" iti brūyuḥ ||
13235| tataḥ samānam ||
13236| etān vā yogānātmani darśayitvā pratikurvīta pareṣāmupadeśārtham ||
13237| tataḥ prayojayedyogān ||
13238| yogadarśanapratīkāreṇa vā kośābhisaṃharaṇaṃ kuryāt ||
13239| hastikāmaṃ vā nāgavanapālā hastinā lakṣaṇyena pralobhayeyuḥ ||
13240| pratipannaṃ gahanamekāyanaṃ vātinīya ghātayeyuḥ, baddhvā vāpahareyuḥ ||
13241| tena mṛgayākāmo vyākhyātaḥ ||
13242| dravyastrīlolupamāḍhyavidhavābhirvā paramarūpayauvanābhiḥ strībhirdāyanikṣepārthamupanītābhiḥ sattriṇaḥ pralobhayeyuḥ ||
13243| pratipannaṃ rātrau sattracchannāḥ samāgame śastrarasābhyāṃ ghātayeyuḥ ||
13244| siddhapravrajitacaityastūpadaivatapratimānāmabhīkṣṇābhigamaneṣu vā bhūmigṛhasuruṅgārūḍhabhittipraviṣṭāstīkṣṇāḥ paramabhihanyuḥ ||
13245  yeṣu deśeṣu yāḥ prekṣāḥ prekṣate pārthivaḥ svayam |
KāK13245  yātrāvihāre ramate yatra krīḍati vāmbhasi ||
13246  dhiguktyādiṣu sarveṣu yajñaprahavaṇeṣu vā |
13246  sūtikāpretarogeṣu prītiśokabhayeṣu vā |
13247  pramādaṃ yāti yasmin vā viśvāsātsvajanotsave ||
13247  yatrāsyārakṣisaṃcāro durdine saṃkuleṣu vā |
13248  viprasthāne pradīpte vā praviṣṭe nirjanepi vā |
13248  vastrābharaṇamālyānāṃ phelābhiḥ śayanāsanaiḥ ||
13249  madyabhojanaphelābhistūryairvābhigatāḥ saha |
13249  prahareyurariṃ tīkṣṇāḥ pūrvapraṇihitaiḥ saha ||E
13250  yathaiva praviśeyuśca dviṣataḥ sattrahetubhiḥ |
13250  tathaiva cāpagaccheyurityuktaṃ yogavāmanam ||

(apasarpapraṇidhih)
13301| śreṇīmukhyamāptaṃ niṣpātayet ||
13302| sa paramāśrtya pakṣāpadeśena svaviṣayātsācivyakarasahāyopādānaṃ kurvīta ||
13303| kṛtāpasarpopacayo vā paramanumānya svāmino dūṣyagrāmaṃ vītahastyaśvaṃ dūṣyāmātyaṃ daṇḍamākrandaṃ vā hatvā parasya preṣayet ||
13304| janapadaikadeśaṃ śreṇīmaṭavīṃ vā sahāyopādānārthaṃ saṃśrayeta ||
13305| viśvāsamupagataḥ svāminaḥ preṣayet ||
13306| tataḥ svāmī hastibandhanamaṭavīghātaṃ vāpadiśya gūḍhameva praharet ||
13307| etenāmātyāṭavikā vyākhyātāḥ ||
13308| śatruṇā maitrīṃ kṛtvāmātyānavakṣipet ||
13309| te tatśatroḥ preṣayeyuḥ "bhartāraṃ naḥ prasādaya" iti ||
13310| sa yaṃ dūtaṃ preṣayet, tamupālabheta "bhartā te māmamātyairbhedayati, na ca punarihāgantavyam" iti ||
13311| athaikamamātyaṃ niṣpātayet ||
13312| sa paramāśritya yogāpasarpāparaktadūṣyānaśaktimataḥ stenāṭavikānubhayopaghātakān vā parasyopaharet ||
13313| āptabhāvopagataḥ pravīrapuruṣopaghātamasyopaharedantapālamāṭavikaṃ daṇḍacāriṇaṃ vā "dṛḍhamasau cāsau ca te śatruṇā saṃdhatte" iti ||
13314| atha paścādabhityaktaśāsanairenān ghātayet ||
13315|  daṇḍabalavyavahāreṇa vā śatrumudyojya ghātayet ||
13316| kṛtyapakṣopagraheṇa vā parasyāmitraṃ rājānamātmanyapakārayitvābhiyuñjīta ||
13317| tataḥ parasya preṣayet"asau te vairī mamāpakaroti, tamehi sambhūya haniṣyāvaḥ, bhūmau hiraṇye vā te parigrahaḥ" iti ||
13318| pratipannamabhisatkṛtyāgatamavaskandena prakāśayuddhena vā śatruṇā ghātayet ||
13319| abhiviśvāsanārthaṃ bhūmidānaputrābhiṣekarakṣāpadeśena vā grāhayet ||
13320| aviṣahyamupāṃśudaṇḍena vā ghātayet ||
13321| sa ceddaṇḍaṃ dadyānna svayamāgacchettamasya vairiṇā ghātayet ||
13322| daṇḍena vā prayātumicchenna vijigīṣuṇā tathāpyenamubhayataḥsampīḍanena ghātayet ||
13323| aviśvasto vā pratyekaśo yātumicchedrājyaikadeśaṃ vā yātavyasyādātukāmaḥ, tathāpyenaṃ vairiṇā sarvasaṃdohena vā ghātayet ||
13324| vairiṇā vā saktasya daṇḍopanayena mūlamanyato hārayet ||
13325| śatrubhūmyā vā mitraṃ paṇeta, mitrabhūmyā vā śatrum ||
13326| tataḥ śatrubhūmilipsāyāṃ mitreṇātmanyapakārayitvābhiyuñjīta - iti samānāḥ pūrveṇa sarva eva yogāḥ ||
13327| śatruṃ vā mitrabhūmilipsāyāṃ pratipannaṃ daṇḍenānugṛhṇīyāt ||
13328| tato mitragatamatisaṃdadhyāt ||
13329| kṛtapratividhāno vā vyasanamātmano darśayitvā mitreṇāmitramutsāhayitvātmānamabhiyojayet ||
13330| tataḥ sampīḍanena ghātayet, jīvagrāheṇa vā rājyavinimayaṃ kārayet ||
13331| mitreṇāśritaścetśatruragrāhye sthātumicchetsāmantādibhirmūlamasya hārayet ||
13332| daṇḍena vā trātumichettamasya ghātayet ||
13333| tau cenna bhidyeyātāṃ prakāśamevānyonyabhūmyā paṇeta ||
13334| tataḥ parasparaṃ mitravyañjanā vā ubhayavetanā vā dūtān preṣayeyuḥ "ayaṃ te rājā bhūmiṃ lipsate śatrusaṃhitaḥ" iti ||
13335| tayoranyataro jātāśaṅkāroṣaḥ, pūrvavacceṣteta ||
13336| durgarāṣṭradaṇḍamukhyān vā kṛtyapakṣahetubhirabhivikhyāpya pravrājayet ||
13337| te yuddhāvaskandāvarodhavyasaneṣu śatrumatisaṃdadhyuḥ ||
13338| bhedaṃ vāsya svavargebhyaḥ kuryuḥ ||
13339| abhityaktaśāsanaiḥ pratisamānayeyuḥ ||
13340| lubdhakavyañjanā vā māṃsavikrayeṇa dvāhsthā dauvārikāpāśrayāścorābhyāgamaṃ parasya dvistririti nivedya labdhapratyayā bharturanīkaṃ dvidhā niveśya grāmavadhevaskande ca dviṣato brūyuḥ "āsannaścoragaṇaḥ, mahāṃścākrandaḥ, prabhūtaṃ sainyamāgacchatu" iti ||
13341| tadarpayitvā grāmaghātadaṇḍasya sainyamitaradādāya rātrau durgadvāreṣu brūyuḥ "hataścoragaṇaḥ, siddhayātramidaṃ sainyamāgatam, dvāramapāvriyatām" iti ||
13342| pūrvapraṇihitā vā dvārāṇi dadyuḥ ||
13343| taiḥ saha prahareyuḥ ||
13344| kāruśilpipāṣaṇḍakuśīlavavaidehakavyañjanānāyudhīyān vvā paradurge praṇidadhyāt ||
13345| teṣāṃ gṛhapatikavyañjanāḥ kāṣṭhatṛṇadhānyapaṇyaśakaṭaiḥ praharaṇāvaraṇānyabhihareyuḥ, devadhvajapratimābhirvā ||
13346| tatastadvyañjanāḥ pramattavadhamavaskandapratigrahamabhipraharaṇaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ śaṅkhadundubhiśabdena vā "praviṣṭam" ityāvedayeyuḥ ||
13347| prākāradvārāṭṭālakadānamanīkabhedaṃ ghātaṃ vā kuryuḥ ||
13348| sārthagaṇavāsibhirātivāhikaiḥ kanyāvāhikairaśvapaṇyavyavahāribhirupakaraṇahārakairdhānyakretṛvikretṛbhirvā pravrajitaliṅgibhirdūtaiśca daṇaḍatinayanam, saṃdhikarmaviśvāsanārtham ||
13349| iti rājāpasarpāḥ ||
13350| eta evāṭavīnāmapasarpāḥ kaṇṭakaśodhanoktāśca  ||
13351| vrajamaṭavyāsannamapasarpāḥ sārthaṃ vā corairghātayeyuḥ ||
13352| kṛtasaṃketamannapānaṃ cātra madanarasaviddhaṃ vā kṛtvāpagaccheyuḥ ||
13353| gopālakavaidehakāśca tataścorān gṛhītaloptrabhārānmadanarasavikārakālevaskandayeyuḥ ||
13354| saṃkarṣaṇadaivatīyo vā muṇḍajaṭilavyañjanaḥ prahavaṇakarmaṇā madanarasayogenātisaṃdadhyāt ||
13355| athāvaskandaṃ dadyāt ||
13356| śauṇḍikavyañjano vā daivatapretakāryotsavasamājeṣvāṭavikān surāvikrayopāyananimittaṃ madanarasayogenātisaṃdadhyāt ||
13357| athāvaskandaṃ dadyāt ||
13358  grāmaghātapraviṣṭāṃ vā vikṣipya bahudhāṭavīm |
13358  ghātayediti corāṇāmapasarpāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||E

(paryupāsanakarma - avamardah)
13401| karśanapūrvaṃ paryupāsanakarma ||
13402| janapadaṃ yathāniviṣṭamabhaye sthāpayet ||
13403| utthitamanugrahaparihārābhyāṃ niveṣayet, anyatrāpasarataḥ ||
13404| saṃgrāmādanyasyāṃ bhūmau niveśayet, ekasyāṃ vā vāsayet ||
13405| na hyajano janapado rājyamajanapadaṃ vā bhavatīti kauṭilyaḥ ||
13406| viṣamasthasya muṣṭiṃ sasyaṃ vā hanyād, vīvadhaprasārau ca ||
13407  prasāravīvadhacchedānmuṣṭisasyavadhādapi |
13407  vamanādgūḍhaghātācca jāyate prakṛtikṣayaḥ ||
13408| "prabhūtaguṇabaddha(vaddha)anyakupyayantraśastrāvaraṇaviṣṭiraśmisamagraṃ me sainyam, ṛtuśca purastāt, apartuḥ parasya, vyādhidurbhikṣanicayarakṣākṣayaḥ krītabalanirvedo mitrabalanirvedaśca" iti paryupāsīta ||
13409| kṛtvā skandhāvārasya rakṣāṃ vīvadhāsārayoḥ pathaśca, parikṣipya durgaṃ khātasālābhyām, dūṣayitvodakam, avasrāvya parikhāḥ sampūrayitvā vā, suruṅgābalakuṭikābhyāṃ vapraprākārau hārayet, dāraṃ ca guḍena ||
13410| nimnaṃ vā pāṃsumālayācchādayet ||
13411| bahulārakṣaṃ yantrairghātayet ||
13412| niṣkirādupaniṣkṛṣyāśvaiśca prahareyuḥ ||
13413| vikramāntareṣu ca niyogavikalpasamuccayaiścopāyānāṃ siddhiṃ lipseta ||
13414| durgavāsinaḥ śyenakākanaptṛbhāsaśukasārikolūkakapotān grāhayitvā puccheṣvagniyogayuktān paradurge visṛjet ||
13415| apakṛṣṭaskandhāvārāducchritadhvajadhanvārakṣo vā mānuṣeṇāgninā paradurgamādīpayet ||
13416| gūḍhapurṣāścāntardurgapālakā nakulavānarabiḍālaśunāṃ puccheṣvagniyogamādhāya kāṇḍanicayarakṣāvidhānaveśmasu visṛjeyuḥ ||
13417| śuṣkamatsyānāmudareṣvagnimādhāya vallūre vā vāyasopahāreṇa vayobhirhārayeyuḥ ||
13418| saraladevadārupūtitṛṇagugguluśrīveṣṭakasarjarasalākṣāgulikāḥ kharoṣṭrājāvīnāṃ leṇḍaṃ cāgnidhāraṇam ||
13419| priyālacūrṇamavalgujamaṣīmadhūcchiṣṭamaśvakharoṣṭragoleṇḍamityeṣa kṣepyoagniyogaḥ ||
13420| sarvalohacūrṇamagnivarṇaṃ vā kumbhīsīsatrapucūrṇaṃ vā pāribhadrakapalāśapuṣpakeśamaṣītailamadhūcchiṣṭakaśrīveṣṭakayuktoagniyogo viśvāsaghātī vā ||
13421| tenāvaliptaḥ śaṇatrapusavalkaveṣṭito bāṇa ityagniyogaḥ ||
13422| na tveva vidyamāne parākramegnimavasṛjet ||
13423| aviśvāsyo hyagnirdaivapīḍanaṃ ca, apratisaṃkhyātaprāṇidhānyapaśuhiraṇyakupyadravyakṣayakaraḥ ||
13424| kṣīṇanicayaṃ cāvāptamapi rājyaṃ kṣayāyaiva bhavati || (iti paryupāsanakarma)
13425| "sarvārambhopakaraṇaviṣṭisampannoasmi, vyādhitaḥ para upadhāviruddhaprakṛtirakṛtadurgakarmanicayo vā, nirāsāraḥ sāsāro vā purā mitraiḥ saṃdhatte" ityavamardakālaḥ ||
13426|  svayamagnau jāte samutthāpite vā prahavaṇe prekṣānīkadarśanasaṅgasaurikakalaheṣu nityayuddhaśrāntabale bahulayuddhapratividdhapretapuruṣe jāgaraṇaklāntasuptajane durdine nadīvege vā nīhārasamplave vāvamṛdnīyāt ||
13427| skandhāvāramutsṛjya vā vanagūḍhaḥ śatruṃ niṣkrāntaṃ ghātayet ||
13428| mitrāsāramukhyavyañjano vā samruddhena maitrīṃ kṛtvā dūtamabhityaktaṃ preṣayet- "idaṃ te chidram, ime dūṣyāḥ" "samroddhurvā chidram, ayaṃ te kṛtyapakṣaḥ" iti ||
13429| taṃ pratidūtamādāya nirgacchantaṃ vijigīṣurgṛhītvā doṣamabhivikhyāpya pravāsya apagacchet ||
13430| tato mitrāsāravyañjano vā samruddhaṃ brūyāt"māṃ trātumupanirgaccha, mayā vā saha samroddhāraṃ jahi" iti ||
13431| pratipannamubhayataḥsampīḍanena ghātayet, jīvagrāheṇa vā rājyavinimayaṃ kārayet ||
13432| nagaraṃ vāsya pramṛdnīyāt ||
13433| sārabalaṃ vāsya vamayitvābhihanyāt ||
13434| tena daṇḍopanatāṭavikā vyākhyātāḥ ||
13435| daṇḍopanatāṭavikayoranyataro vā samruddhasya preṣayet- "ayaṃ samroddhā vyādhitaḥ, pārṣṇigrāheṇābhiyuktaḥ, chidramanyadutthitam, anyasyāṃ bhūmāvapayātukāmaḥ" iti ||
13436| pratipanne samroddhā skandhāvāramādīpyāpayāyāt ||
13437| tataḥ pūrvavadācaret ||
13438| paṇyasampātaṃ vā kṛtvā paṇyenainaṃ rasaviddhenātisaṃdadhyāt ||
13439| āsāravyañjano vā samruddhasya dūtaṃ preṣayet- "mayā bāhyamabhihatamupanirgacchābhihantum" iti ||
13440| pratipannaṃ pūrvavadācaret ||
13441| mitraṃ bandhuṃ vāpadiśya yogapuruṣāḥ śāsanamudrāhastāḥ praviśya durgaṃ grāhayeyuḥ ||
13442| āsāravyañjñano vā samruddhasya preṣayet- "amuṣmin deśe kāle ca skandhāvāramabhihaniṣyāmi, yuṣmābhirapi yoddhavyam" iti ||
13443| pratipannaṃ yathoktamabhyāghātasaṃkulaṃ darśayitvā rātrau durgānniṣkrāntaṃ ghātayet ||
13444| yadvā mitramāvāhayedāṭavvikaṃ vā, tamutsāhayet"vikramya samruddhe bhūmimasya pratipadyasva" iti ||
13445| vikrāntaṃ prakṛtibhirdūṣyamukhyopagraheṇa vā ghātayet, svayaṃ vā rasena "mitraghātakoayam" ityavāptārthaḥ ||
13446| vikramitukāmaṃ vā mitravyañjanaḥ parasyābhiśaṃset ||
13447| āptabhāvopagataḥ pravīrapuruṣānasyopaghātayet ||
13448| saṃdhiṃ vā kṛtvā janapadamenaṃ niveśayet ||
13449| niviṣṭamasya janapadamavijñāto hanyāt ||
13450| apakārayitvā dūṣyāṭavikeṣu vā balaikadeśamatinīya durgamavaskandena hārayet ||
13451| dūṣyāmitrāṭavikadveṣyapratyapasṛtāśca kṛtārthamānasaṃjñācihnāḥ paradurgamavaskandeyuḥ ||
13452| paradurgamavaskandya skandhāvāraṃ vā patitaparānmukhābhipannamuktakeśaśastrabhayavirūpebhyaścābhayamayudhyamānebhyaśca dadyuḥ ||
13453| paradurgamavāpya viśuddhaśatrupakṣaṃ kṛtopāṃśudaṇḍapratīkāramantarbahiśca praviśet ||
13454| evaṃ vijigīṣuramitrabhūmiṃ labdhvā madhyamaṃ lipseta, tatsiddhāvudāsīnam ||
13455| eṣa prathamo mārgaḥ pṛthivīṃ jetum ||
13456| madhyamodāsīnayorabhāve guṇātiśayenāriprakṛtīḥ sādhayet, tata uttarāḥ prakṛtīḥ ||
13457| eṣa dvitīyo mārgaḥ ||
13458| maṇḍalasyābhāve śatruṇā mitraṃ mitreṇa vā śatrumubhayataḥsampīḍanena sādhayet ||
13459| eṣatṛtīyo mārgaḥ ||
13460| śakyamekaṃ vā sāmantaṃ sādhayet, tena dviguṇo dvitīyam, triguṇastṛtīyam ||
13461| eṣa caturtho mārgaḥ pṛthivīṃ jetum ||
13462| jitvā ca pṛthivīṃ vibhaktavarṇāśramāṃ svadharmeṇa bhuñjīta ||
13463  upajāpoapasarpaśca vāmanaṃ paryupāsanam |
13463  avamardaśca pañcaite durgalambhasya hetavaḥ ||E

(labdhapraśamanam)
13501| dvividhaṃ vijigīṣoḥ samutthānaṃ - aṭavyādikamekagrāmādikaṃ ca ||
13502| trividhaścāsya lambhaḥ - navo, bhūtapūrvaḥ, pitrya iti ||
13503| navamavāpya lābhaṃ paradoṣān svaguṇaiśchādayet, guṇān guṇadvaiguṇyena ||
13504| svadharmakarmānugrahaparihāradānamānakarmabhiśca prakṛtipriyahitānyanuvarteta ||
13505| yathāsambhāṣitaṃ ca kṛtyapakṣamupagrāhayet, bhūyaśca kṛtaprayāsam ||
13506| aviśvāso hi visaṃvādakaḥ sveṣāṃ pareṣāṃ ca bhavati, prakṛtiviruddhācāraśca ||
13507| tasmātsamānaśīlaveṣabhāṣācāratāmupagachet ||
13508| deśadaivatasmājotsavavihāreṣu ca bhaktimanuvarteta ||
13509| deśagrāmajātisaṃghamukhyeṣu cābhīkṣṇaṃ sattriṇaḥ parasyāpacāraṃ darśayeyuḥ, māhābhāgyaṃ bhaktiṃ ca teṣu svāminaḥ, svāmisatkāraṃ ca vidyamānam ||
13510| ucitaiścainān bhogaparihārarakṣāvekṣaṇairbhuñjīta ||
13511| sarvadevatāśramapūjanaṃ ca vidyāvākyadharmaśūrapuruṣāṇāṃ ca bhūmidravyadānaparihārān kārayet, sarvabandhanamokṣaṇamanugrahaṃ dīnānāthavyādhitānāṃ ca ||
13512| cāturmāsyeṣvardhamāsikamaghātam, paurṇamāsīṣu ca cātūrātrikaṃ rājadeśanakṣatreṣvaikarātrikam ||
13513| yonibālavadhaṃ puṃstvopaghātaṃ ca pratiṣedhayet ||
13514| yacca kośadaṇḍopaghātakamadharmiṣṭhaṃ vā caritraṃ manyeta tadapanīya dharmyavyavahāraṃ sthāpayet ||
13515| coraprakṛtīnāṃ mlecchajātīnāṃ ca sthānaviparyāsamanekasthaṃ kārayet, durgarāṣṭradaṇḍamukhyānāṃ ca ||
13516| paropagṛhītānāṃ ca mantripurohitānāṃ parasya pratyanteṣvanekasthaṃ vāsaṃ kārayet ||
13517| apakārasamarthānanukṣiyato vā bhartṛvināśamupāṃśudaṇḍena praśamayet ||
13518| svadeśīyān vā pareṇa vāparuddhānapavāhitasthāneṣu sthāpayet ||
13519| yaśca tatkulīnaḥ pratyādeyamādātuṃ śaktaḥ, pratyantāṭavīstho vā prabādhitumabhijātaḥ, tasmai viguṇāṃ bhūmiṃ prayacchet, guṇavatyāścaturbhāgaṃ vā kośadaṇḍadānamavasthāpya, yadupakurvāṇaḥ paurajānapadān kopayet ||
13520| kupitaistairenaṃ ghātayet ||
13521| prakṛtibhirupakruṣṭamapanayet, aupaghātike vā deśe niveśayet- iti ||
13522| bhūtapūrve yena doṣeṇāpavṛttastaṃ prakṛtidoṣaṃ chādayet, yena ca guṇenopāvṛttastaṃ tīvrīkuryāt- iti ||
13523| pitrye piturdoṣāṃśchādayet, guṇāṃśca prakāśayet- iti ||
13524  caritramakṛtaṃ dharmyaṃ kṛtaṃ cānyaiḥ pravartayet |
13524  pravartayenna cādharmyaṃ kṛtaṃ cānyairnivartayet ||E

parabalaghātaprayogah)
14101| cāturvarṇyarakṣārthamaupaniṣadikamadharmiṣṭheṣu prayuñjīta ||
14102| kālakūṭādirviṣavargaḥ śraddheyadeśaveṣaśilpabhāṣābhijanāpadeśaiḥ kubjavāmanakirātamūkabadhirajaḍāndhacchadmabhirmlecchajātīyairabhipretaiḥ strībhiḥ pumbhiśca paraśarīropabhogeṣvavadhātavyaḥ ||
14103| rājakrīḍābhāṇḍanidhānadravyopabbhogeṣu gūḍhāḥ śastranidhānaṃ kuryuḥ, sattrājīvinaśca rātricāriṇoagnijīvinaścāgninidhānam ||
14104| citrabhekakauṇḍinyakakṛkaṇapañcakuṣṭhaśatapadīcūrṇamuccidiṇgakambalīśatakanda(kardama?)idhmakṛkalāsacūrṇaṃ gṛhagolikāndhāhikakrakaṇṭakapūtikīṭagomārikācūrṇaṃ bhallātakāvalgujarasamyuktaṃ sadyaḥprāṇaharam, eteṣāṃ vā dhūmaḥ ||
14105  kīṭo vānyatamastaptaḥ kṛṣṇasarpapriyaṅgubhiḥ |
14105  śoṣayedeṣa samyogaḥ sadyaḥprāṇaharo mataḥ ||
14106| dhāmārgavayātudhānamūlaṃ bhallātakapuṣpacūrṇayuktamārdhamāsikaḥ ||
14107| vyāghātakamūlaṃ bhallātakapuṣpacūrṇayuktaṃ kīṭayogo māsikaḥ ||
14108| kalāmātraṃ puruṣāṇām, dviguṇaṃ kharāśvānām, caturguṇaṃ hastyuṣṭrāṇām ||
14109| śatakardamoccidiṅgakaravīrakaṭutumbīmatsyadhūmo madanakodravapalālena hastikarṇapalāśapalālena vā pravātānuvāte praṇīto yāvaccarati tāvanmārayati ||
14110| pūkikīṭamastyakaṭutumbīśatakardamedhmendragopacūrṇaṃ pūtikīṭakṣudrārālāhemavidārīcūrṇaṃ vā bastaśṛṅgakhuracūrṇayuktamandhīkaro dhūmaḥ ||
14111| pūtikarañjapattraharitālamanaḥśilāguñjāraktakārpāsapalālānyāsphoṭakācagośakṛdrasapiṣṭamandhīkaro dhūmaḥ ||
14112| sarpanirmokaṃ goaśvapurīṣamandhāhikaśiraścāndhīkaro dhūmaḥ ||
14113| pārāvataplavakakravyādānāṃ hastinaravarāhāṇāṃ ca mūtrapurīṣaṃ kāsīsahiṅguyavatuṣakaṇataṇḍulāḥ kārpāsakuṭajakośātakīnāṃ ca bījāni gomūtrikābhāṇḍīmūlaṃ nimbaśigruphaṇirjakākṣīvapīlukabhaṅgaḥ sarpaśapharīcarma hastinakhaśṛṅgacūrṇamityeṣa dhūmo madanakodravapalālena hastikarṇapalāśapalālena vā praṇītaḥ pratyekaśo
yāvaccarati tāvanmārayati ||
14114| kālīkuṣṭhanaḍaśatāvalīmūlaṃ sarpapracalākakṛkaṇapañcakuṣṭhacūrṇaṃ vā dhūmaḥ pūrvakalpenārdraśuṣkapalālena vā praṇītaḥ saṃgrāmāvataraṇāvaskandanasaṃkuleṣu kṛtanejanodakākṣipratīkāraiḥ praṇītaḥ sarvaprāṇināṃ netraghnaḥ ||
14115| śārikākapotabakabalākāleṇḍamarkākṣipīlukasnuhikṣīrapiṣṭamandhīkaraṇamañjanamudakadūṣaṇaṃ ca  ||
14116| yavakaśālimūlamadanaphalajātīpattranaramūtrayogaḥ plakṣavidārīmūlayukto mūkodumbaramadanakodravakvāthayukto hastikarṇapalāśakvāthayukto vā madanayogaḥ ||
14117| śṛṅgigautamavṛkakaṇṭakāramayūrapadīyogo guñjālāṅgalīviṣamūlikeṅgudīyogaḥ karavīrākṣipīlukārkamṛgamāraṇīyogo madnakodravakvāthayukto hastikarṇapalāśakvāthayukto vā madanayogaḥ ||
14118| samastā vā yavasendhanodakadūṣaṇāḥ ||
14119| kṛtakaṇḍalakṛkalāsagṛhagolikāndhāhikadhūmo netravadhamunmādaṃ ca karoti ||
14120| kṛkalāsagṛhagolikāyogaḥ kuṣṭhakaraḥ ||
14121| sa eva citramekāntramadhuyuktaḥ pramehamāpādayati, manuṣyalohitayuktaḥ śoṣam ||
14122| dūṣīviṣaṃ madanakodravacūrṇamapajihvikāyogaḥ ||
14123| mātṛvāhakāñjalikārapracalākabhekākṣipīlukayogo viṣūcikākaraḥ ||
14124| pañcakuṣṭhakakauṇḍinyakarājavṛkṣapuṣpamadhuyogo jvarakaraḥ ||
14125| bhāsanakulajihvāgranthikāyogaḥ kharīkṣīrapiṣṭo mūkabadhirakaro māsārdhamāsikaḥ ||
14126| kalāmātraṃ puruṣāṇāmitsamānaṃ pūrveṇa ||
14127| bhaṅgakvāthopanayanamauṣadhānām, cūrṇaṃ prāṇabhṛtām, sarveṣāṃ vā kvāthopanayanam, evaṃ vīryavattaraṃ bhavti ||
14128| iti yogasampat ||
14129| śālmalī vidārīdhānyasiddho mūlavatsanābhasamyuktaścucchundarīśoṇitapralepena digdho bāṇo yaṃ vidhyati sa viddhoanyān daśapuruṣān daśati, te daṣṭā daśānyān daśanti puruṣān ||
14130| ballātakayātudhānāvānudhāmārgavabāṇānāṃ puṣpairelakākṣigugguluhālāhalānāṃ ca kaṣāyaṃ bastanaraśoṇitayuktaṃ daṃśayogaḥ ||
14131| tatoardhadharaṇiko yogaḥ saktupiṇyākābhyāmudake praṇīto dhanuḥśatāyāmamudakāśayaṃ dūṣayati ||
14132| matsyaparamparā hyetena daṣṭābhimṛṣṭā vā viṣībhavati, yaścaitadudakaṃ pibati spṛśati vā ||
14133| raktaśvetasarṣapairgodhā tripakṣamuṣṭrikāyāṃ bhūmau nikhātāyāṃ nihitā vadhyenoddhṛtā yāvatpaśyati tāvanmārayati, kṛṣṇasarpo vā ||
14134| vidyutpradagdhoaṅgāro jvālo vā vidyutpradagdhaiḥ kāṣṭhairgṛhītaścānuvāsitaḥ kṛttikāsu bharaṇīṣu vā raudreṇa karmaṇābhihutoagniḥ praṇītaśca nispratīkāro dahati ||
14135  karmārādagnimāhṛtya kṣaudreṇa juhuyātpṛthak |
14135  surayā śauṇḍikādagniṃ mārgatoagniṃ ghṛtena ca ||
14136  mālyena caikapatnyagniṃ puṃścalyagniṃ ca sarṣapaiḥ |
14136  dadhnā ca sūtikāsvagnimāhitāgniṃ ca taṇḍulaiḥ ||
14137  caṇḍālāgniṃ ca māṃsena citāgniṃ mānuṣeṇa ca ||
14137  samastān bastavasayā mānuṣeṇa dhruveṇa ca ||
14138  juhuyādagnimantreṇa rājavṛkṣasya dārubhiḥ |
14138  eṣa niṣpratikāroagnirdviṣatāṃ netramohanaḥ ||
14139| adite namaste, anumate namaste, sarasvati namaste, deva savitarnamāste ||
14140| agnaye svāhā, somāya svāhā, bhūḥ svāhā bhuvaḥ svāhā ||E

(pralambhanam, tatra adbhutotpādanam)
14201| śirīṣodumbaraśamīcūrṇaṃ sarpiṣā saṃhṛtyārdhamāsikaḥ kṣudyogaḥ ||
14202| kaśerukotpalakandekṣumūlabisadūrvākṣīraghṛtamaṇḍasiddho māsikaḥ ||
14203| māṣayavakulatthadarbhamūlacūrṇaṃ vā kṣīraghṛtābhyām, vallīkṣīraghṛtaṃ vā samasiddham, sālapṛśniparṇīmūlakalkaṃ payasā pītvā, payo vā tatsiddhaṃ madhughṛtābhyāmaśitvā māsamupavasati ||
14204| śvetabastamūtre saptarātroṣitaiḥ siddhārthakaiḥ siddhaṃ tailaṃ kaṭukālābau māsārdhamāsasthitaṃ catuṣpadadvipadānāṃ virūpakaraṇam ||
14205| takrayavabhakṣasya saptarātrādūrdhvaṃ śvetagardabhasya leṇḍayavaiḥ siddhaṃ gaurasarṣapatailaṃ virūpakaraṇam ||
14206| etayoranyatarasya mūtraleṇdarasasiddhaṃ siddhārthakatailamarkatūlapataṅgacūrṇapratīvāpaṃ śvetīkaraṇam ||
14207| śvetakukkuṭājagaraleṇḍayogaḥ śvetīkaraṇam ||
14208| śvetabastamūtre śvetasarṣapāḥ saptarātroṣitāstakra(?)markakṣīralavaṇaṃ dhānyaṃ ca pakṣasthito yogaḥ śvetīkaraṇam ||
14209| kaṭukālābau valīgate gataṃrdhamāsasthitaṃ gaurasarṣapapiṣṭaṃ romṇāṃ śvetīkaraṇam ||
14210  alojuneti yaḥ kīṭaḥ śvetā ca gṛhagolikā |
14210  etena piṣtenābhyaktāḥ keśāḥ syuḥ śaṅkhapāṇḍarāḥ ||
14211| gomayena tindukāriṣṭakalkena vā marditāṅgasya bhallātakarasānuliptasya māsikaḥ kuṣṭhayogaḥ ||
14212| kṛṣṇasarpamukhe gṛhagolikāmukhe vā saptarātroṣitā gujjāḥ kuṣṭhayogaḥ ||
14213| śukapittāṇḍarasābhyaṅgaḥ kuṣṭhayogaḥ ||
14214| kuṣṭhasyapriyālakalkakaṣāyaḥ pratīkāraḥ ||
14215| kukkuṭakośātakī(?)śatāvarīmūlayuktamāhārayamāṇo māsena gauro bhavati ||
14216| vaṭakaṣāyasnātaḥ sahacarakalkadigdhaḥ kṛṣṇo bhavati ||
14217| śakunakaṇgutailayuktā haritālamanaḥśilāḥ śyāmīkaraṇam ||
14218| khadyotacūrṇaṃ sarṣapatailayuktaṃ rātrau jvalati ||
14219| khadyotagaṇḍūpadacūrṇaṃ samudrajantūnāṃ bhṛṅgakapālānāṃ khadirakarṇikārāṇāṃ puṣpacūrṇaṃ vā śakunakaṅgutailayuktaṃ tejanacūrṇam ||
14220| pāribhadrakatvanmaṣī maṇḍūkavasayā yuktā gātraprajvālanamagninā ||
14221| paribhadrakatvaktilakalkapradigdhaṃ śarīramagninā jvalati ||
14222| pīlutvanmaṣīmayaḥ piṇḍo haste jvalati ||
14223| maṇḍūkavasādigdhoagninā jvalati ||
14224| tena pradigdhamaṅgaṃ kuśāmraphalatailasiktaṃ samudramaṇḍūkīphenakasarjarasacūrṇayuktaṃ vā jvalati ||
14225| maṇḍūkakulīrādīnāṃ vasayā samabhāgaṃ tailaṃ siddhamabhyaṅgaṃ gātrāṇāmagniprajvālanam ||
14226| veṇumūlaśaivalaliptamaṅgaṃ maṇḍūkavasādigdhamagninā jvalati ||
14227| pāribhadrakappatibalāvañjulavajrakadalīmūlakalkena maṇḍūkavasāsiddhena tailenābhyaktapādoaṅgāreṣu gacchati ||
14228  upodakā pratibalā vañjulaḥ pāribhadrakaḥ |
14228  eteṣāṃ mūlakalkena maṇḍūkavasayā saha ||
14229  sādhayettailametena pādāvabhyajya nirmalau |
14229  aṅgārarāśau vicaredyathā kusumasaṃcaye ||
14230| haṃsakrauñcamayūrāṇāmanyeṣāṃ vā mahāśakunīnāmudakaplavānāṃ puccheṣu baddhā naladīpikā rātrāvulkādarśanam ||
14231| vaidyutaṃ bhasmāṅgiśamanam ||
14232| strīpuṣpapāyitā māṣā vrajakulīmūlaṃ maṇḍūkavasāmiśraṃ culluyāṃ dīptāyāmapācanam ||
14233| cullīśodhanaṃ pratīkāraḥ ||
14234| pīlumayo maṇiragnigarbhaḥ suvarcalāmūlagranthiḥ sūtragranthirvā picupariveṣṭito mukhyādagnidhūmotsargaḥ ||E
14235| kuśāmraphalatailasiktoagnirvarṣapravāteṣu jvalati ||
14236| samudraphenakastailayuktoambhasi plavamāno jvalati ||
14237| plavamānānāmasthiṣu kalmāṣaveṇunā nirmathitoagnirnodakena śāmyati, udakena jvalati ||
14238| śastrahatasya śūlaprotasya vā puruṣasya vāmapārśvaparśukāsthiṣu kalmāṣaveṇunā nirmathitoagniḥ striyāḥ puruṣasya vāsthiṣu manuṣyaparśukayā nirmathitoagniryatra trirapasavyaṃ gacchati na cātrānyoagnirjvalati ||
14239  cuccundarī khañjarīṭaḥ khārakīṭaśca piṣyate |
14239  aśvamūtreṇa saṃsṛṣṭā nigalānāṃ tu bhañjanam ||
14240| ayaskānto vā pāṣāṇaḥ kulīradardurakhārakīṭavasāpradehena dviguṇaḥ ||
14241| nārakagarbhaḥ kaṅkabhāsapārśvotpalodakapiṣṭaścatuṣpadadvipadānāṃ pādalepaḥ ||
14242| ulūkagṛdhravasābhyāmuṣṭracarmopānahāvabhyajya vaṭapattraiḥ praticchādya pañcāśadyojanānyaśrānto gacchati ||
14243| śyenakaṅkakākagṛdhrahaṃsakrauñcavīcīrallānāṃ majjāno retāṃsi vā yojanaśatāya, siṃhavyāghradvīpakākolūkānāṃ majjāno retāṃsi vā ||
14244| sārvavarṇikāni garbhapatanānyuṣṭrikāyāmabhiṣūya śmaśāne pretaśiśūn vā tatsamutthitaṃ medo yojanaśatāya ||
14245  aniṣṭairadbhutotpātaiḥ parasyodvegamācaret |
14245  ārājyāyeti nirvādaḥ samānaḥ kopa ucyate ||E

(pralambhanam, tatra bhaiṣajyamantrayogah)
14301| mārjāroṣṭravṛkavarāhaśvāvidvāgulīnaptṛkākolūkānāmanyeṣāṃ vā niśācarāṇāṃ sattvānāmekasya dvayorbahūnāṃ vā dakṣiṇāni vāmāni cākṣīṇi gṛhītvā dvidhā cūrṇaṃ kārayet ||
14302| tato dakṣiṇaṃ vāmena vāmaṃ dakṣiṇena samabhyajya rātrau tamasi ca paśyati ||
14303  ekāmlakaṃ varāhākṣi khadyotaḥ kālaśārivā |
14303  etenābhyaktanayano rātrau rūpāṇi paśyati ||
14304|trirātropoṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa śastrahatasya śūlaprotasya vā puṃsaḥ śiraḥkapāle mṛttikāyāṃ yavānāvāsyāvikṣīreṇa secayet ||
14305| tato yavavirūḍhamālāmābadhya naṣṭacchāyārūpaścarati ||
14306| triratropoṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa śvamārjārolūkavāgulīnāṃ dakṣiṇāni vāmāni cākṣīṇi dvidhā cūrṇaṃ kārayet ||
14307| tato yathāsvamabhyaktākṣo naṣṭacchāyārūpaścarati ||
14308| trirātropoṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa puruṣaghātinaḥ kāṇḍakasya śalākāmañjanīṃ ca kārayet ||
14309| tato anyatamenākṣicūrṇenābhyaktākṣo naṣṭacchāyārūpaścarati ||
14310| trirātropoṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa kālāyasīmañjanīṃ śalākāṃ ca kārayet ||
14311| tato niśācarāṇāṃ sattvānāmanyatamasya śiraḥkapālamañjanena pūrayitvā mṛtāyāḥ striyā yonau praveśya dāhayet ||
14312| tadañjanaṃ puṣyeṇoddhṛtya tasyāmañjanyāṃ nidadhyāt ||
14313| tenābhyaktākṣo naṣṭachāyārūpaścarati ||
14314| yatra brāhmaṇamāhitāgniṃ dagdhaṃ dahyamānaṃ vā paśyettatra trirātropoṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa svayaṃmṛtasya vāsasā prasevaṃ kṛtvā citābhasmanā pūrayitvā tamābadhya naṣṭacchāyārūpaścarati ||
14315| brāhmaṇasya pretakārye yo gaurmāryate tasyāsthimajjacūrṇapūrṇāhibhastrā paśūnāmantardhānam ||
14316| sarpadaṣṭasya bhasmanā pūrṇā pracalākabhastrā mṛgāṇāmantardhānam ||
14317| ulūkavāgulīpucchapurīṣajānvasthicūrṇapūrṇāhibhastrā pakṣiṇāmantardhānam ||
14318| ityaṣṭāvantardhānayogaḥ ||
14319  "baliṃ vairocanaṃ vande śatamāyaṃ ca śambaram |
14319  bhaṇḍīrapākaṃ narakaṃ nikumbhaṃ kumbhameva ca ||
14320  devalaṃ nāradaṃ vande vande sāvarṇigālavam |
14320  eteṣāmanuyogena kṛtaṃ te svāpanaṃ mahat ||
14321  yathā svapantyajagarāḥ svapantyapi camūkhalāḥ |
14321  tathā svapantu puruṣā ye ca grāme kutūhalāḥ ||
14322  bhaṇḍakānāṃ sahasreṇa rathanemiśatena ca |
14322  imaṃ gṛhaṃ pravekṣyāmi tūṣṇīmāsantu bhāṇḍakāḥ ||
14323  namaskṛtvā ca manave baddhvā śunakaphelakāḥ |
14323  ye devā devalokeṣu mānuṣeṣu ca brāhmaṇāḥ ||
14324  adhyayanapāragāḥ siddhā ye ca kaulāsa tāpasāḥ |
14324  etebhyaḥ sarvasiddhebhyaḥ kṛtaṃ te svāpanaṃ mahat ||
14325| atigacchanti ca mayyapagacchantu saṃhatāḥ ||
14326| alite, valite, manave svāhā ||
14327| etasya prayogaḥ ||
14328| trirātropoṣitaḥ kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ puṣyayoginyāṃ śvapākīhastādvilakhāvalekhanaṃ krīṇīyāt ||
14329| tanmāṣaiḥ saha kaṇḍolikāyāṃ kṛtvāsaṃkīrṇa ādahane nikhānayet ||
14330| dvitīyasyāṃ caturdaśyāmuddhṛtya kumāryā peṣayitvā gulikāḥ kārayet ||
14331| tata ekāṃ gulikāmabhimantrayitvā yatraitana mantreṇa kṣipati tatsarvaṃ prasvāpayati ||
14332| etenaiva kalpena śvāvidhaḥ śalyakaṃ trikālaṃ triśvetamasaṃkīrṇa ādahane nikhānayet ||
14333| dvitīyasyāṃ caturdaśyāmuddhṛtyādahanabhasmanā saha yatraitena mantreṇa kṣipati tatsarvaṃ prasvāpayati ||
14334  "suvarṇapuṣpīṃ brahmāṇīṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca kuśadhvajam |
14334  sarvāśca devatā vande vande sarvāṃśca tāpasān ||
14335  vaśaṃ me brāhmaṇā yāntu bhūmipālāśca kṣatriyāḥ |
14335  vaśaṃ vaiśyāśca śūdrāśca vaśatāṃ yāntu me sadā ||
14336| svāhā - amile kimile vayucāre prayoge phakke vayuhve vihāle dantakaṭake svāhā ||
14337  sukhaṃ svapantu śunakā ye ca grāme kutūhalāḥ |
14337  śvāvidhaḥ śalyakaṃ caitattriśvetaṃ brahmanirmitam ||
14338  prasuptāḥ sarvasiddhā hi etatte svāpanaṃ kṛtam |
14338  yāvadgrāmasya sīmāntaḥ sūryasyodgamanāditi ||
14339| svāhā" ||
14340| etasya prayogaḥ ||
14341| śvāvidhaḥ śalyakāni triśvetāni, saptarātropoṣitaḥ kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ khādirābhiḥ samidhāmir(?) agnimetena mantreṇāṣṭaśatasampātaṃ kṛtvā madhughṛtābhyāmabhijuhuyāt ||
14342| tata ekametena mantreṇa grāmadvāri gṛhadvāri vā yatra nikhanyate tatsarvaṃ prasvāpayati ||
14343  "baliṃ vairocanaṃ vande śatamāyaṃ ca śambaram |
14343  nikumbhaṃ narakaṃ kumbhaṃ tantukacchaṃ mahāsuram ||
14344  armālavaṃ pramīlaṃ ca maṇḍolūkaṃ ghaṭobalam |
14344  kṛṣṇakaṃsopacāraṃ ca paulomīṃ ca yaśasvinīm ||
14345  abhimantrayitvā gṛhṇāmi siddhyarthaṃ śavaśārikām |
14345  jayatu jayati ca namaḥ śalakabhūtebhyaḥ svāhā ||
14346  sukhaṃ svapantu śunakā ye ca grāme kutūhalāḥ |
14346  sukhaṃ svapantu siddhārthā yamarthaṃ mārgayāmahe |
14346   yāvadastamayādudayo yāvadarthaṃ phalaṃ mama ||
14347| iti svāhā ||
14348| etasya prayogaḥ ||
14349| caturbhaktopavāsī kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāmasaṃkīrṇa ādahane baliṃ kṛtvaitena mantreṇa śavaśārikāṃ gṛhītvā pautrīpoṭṭalikaṃ badhnīyāt ||
14350| tanmadhye śvāvidhaḥ śalyakena viddhvā yatraitena mantreṇa nikhanyate tatsarvaṃ prasvāpayati ||
14351  "upaimi śaraṇaṃ cāgniṃ daivatāni diśo daśa |
14351  apayāntu ca sarvāṇi vaśatāṃ yāntu me sadā ||
14352| svāhā" ||
14353| etasya prayogaḥ ||
14354| trirātroposṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa śarkarā ekaviṃśatisampātaṃ kṛtvā madhughṛtābhyāmabhijuhuyāt ||
14355| tato gandhamālyena pūjayitvā nikhānayet ||
14356| dvitīyena puṣyeṇoddhṛtyaikāṃ śarkarāmabhimantrayitvā kapāṭamāhanyāt ||
14357| abhyantarṃ catasṛṇāṃ śarkarāṇāṃ dvāramapāvriyate ||
14358| caturbhaktopavāsī kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ bhagnasya puruṣasyāsthnā ṛṣabhaṃ kārayet, abhimantrayeccaitena ||
14359| dvigoyuktaṃ goyānamāhṛtaṃ bhavati ||
14360| tataḥ paramākāśe virāmati ||
14361| ravisagandhaḥ parighamati sarvaṃ pṛṇāti ||
14362| "caṇḍālīkumbhītumbakaṭukasāraughaḥ sanārībhagoasi - svāhā ||
14363| tālodghāṭanaṃ prasvāpanaṃ ca ||
14364| trirātropoṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa śastrahatasya śūlaprotasya vā puṃsaḥ śiraḥkapāle mṛttikāyāṃ tuvarīrāvāsyodakena secayet ||(?)
14365| jātānāṃ puṣyeṇaiva gṛhītvā rajjukāṃ vartayet ||
14366| tataḥ sajyānāṃ dhanuṣāṃ yantrāṇāṃ ca purastācchedanaṃ jyācchedanaṃ karoti ||
14367| udakāhibhastrāmucchvāsamṛttikayā striyāḥ puruṣasya vā pūrayet, nāsikābandhanaṃ mukhagrahaśca ||
14368| varāhabhastrāmucchvāsamṛttikayā pūrayitvā markaṭasnāyunāvabadhnīyāt, ānāhakāraṇam ||
14369| kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ śastrahatāyā goḥ kapilāyāḥ pittena rājavṛkṣamayīmamitrapratimāmañjyāt, andhīkaraṇam ||
14370| caturbhaktopavāsī kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ baliṃ kṛtvā śūlaprotasya puruṣasyāsthnā kīlakān kārayet ||
14371| eteṣāmekaḥ purīṣe mūtre vā nikhāta ānāhaṃ karoti, padesyāsane vā nikhātaḥ śoṣeṇa mārayati, āpaṇe kṣetre gṛhe vā vṛtticchedaṃ karoti ||
14372| etenaiva kalpena vidyuddagdhasya vṛkṣasya kīlakā vyākhyātāḥ ||
14373  punarnavamavācīnaṃ nimbaḥ kāmamadhuśca yaḥ |
14373  kapiroma manuṣyāsthi baddhvā mṛtakavāsasā ||
14374  nikhanyate gṛhe yasya dṛṣṭvā vā yatpadaṃ nayet |
14374  saputradāraḥ sadhanastrīn pakṣānnātivartate ||
14375  punarnavamavācīnaṃ nimbaḥ kāmamadhuśca yaḥ |
14375  svayaṃguptā manuṣyāsthi pade yasya nikhanyate ||
14376  dvāre gṛhasya senāyā grāmasya nagarasya vā |
14376 saputradāraḥ sadhanastrīn pakṣānnātivartate ||
14377  ajamarkaṭaromāṇi mārjāranakulasya ca |
14377  brāhmaṇānāṃ śvapākānāṃ kākolūkasya cāharet |
14377  etena viṣṭhāvakṣuṇṇā sadya utsādakārikā ||
14378  pretanirmālikā kiṇvaṃ romāṇi nakulasya ca |
14378  vṛścikāly(?)ahikṛttiśca pade yasya nikhanyate |
14378   bhavatyapuruṣaḥ sadyo yāvattannāpanīyate ||
14379| trirātropoṣitaḥ puṣyeṇa śastrahatasya śūlaprotasya vā puṃsaḥ śiraḥkapāle mṛttikāyāṃ guñjā āvāsyodakena secayet ||
14380| jātānāmamāvāsyāyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vā puṣyayoginyāṃ guñjavallīrgrāhayitvā maṇḍalikāni kārayet ||
14381| teṣvannapānabhājanāni nyastāni na kṣīyante ||
14382| rātriprekṣāyāṃ pravṛttāyāṃ pradīpāgniṣu mṛtadhenoḥ stanānutkṛtya dāhayet ||
14383| dagdhān vṛṣamūtreṇa peṣayitvā navakumbhamantarlepayet ||
14384| taṃ grāmamapasavyaṃ pariṇīya yattatra nyastaṃ navanītameṣāṃ tatsarvamāgacchati ||
14385| kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ puṣyayoginyāṃ śuno lagnakasya yonau kālāyasīṃ mudrikāṃ preṣayet ||
14385| tāṃ svayaṃ patitāṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||
14387| tayā vṛkṣaphalānyākāritānyāgacchanti ||
14388  mantrabhaiṣajyasamyuktā yogā māyākṛtāśca ye |
14388  upahanyādamitrāṃstaiḥ svajanaṃ cābhipālayet ||E

(svabalopaghātapratīkārah)
14401| svapakṣe paraprayuktānāṃ dūṣīviṣagarāṇāṃ pratīkāraḥ ||
14402| śleṣmātakakapitthadantidantaśaṭhagojiśirīṣapāṭalībalāsyonāgapunarnavāśvetavāraṇakvāthayuktam(?) candanasālāvṛkīlohitayuktaṃ nejanodakaṃ rājopabhogyānāṃ guhyaprakṣālanaṃ strīṇām, senāyāśca viṣapratīkāraḥ ||
14403| pṛṣatanakulanīlakaṇṭhagodhāpittayuktaṃ mahīrājīcūrṇaṃ sinduvāritavaraṇavāruṇītaṇḍulīyakaśataparvāgrapiṇḍītakayogo madanadoṣaharaḥ ||
14404| sṛgālavinnāmadanasinduvāritavaraṇavāraṇavalīmūlakaṣāyāṇāmanyatamasya samastānāṃ vā kṣīrayuktaṃ pānaṃ madanadoṣaharam ||
14405| kaiḍaryapūtitilatailamunmādaharaṃ nastaḥkarma ||
14406| priyaṅgunaktamālayogaḥ kuṣṭhaharaḥ ||
14407| kuṣṭhalodhrayogaḥ pākaśoṣaghnaḥ ||
14408| kaṭaphaladravantīvilaṅgacūrṇaṃ nastaḥkarma śirorogaharam ||
14409| priyaṅgumañjiṣṭhātagaralākṣārasamadhukaharidrākṣaudrayogo rajjūdakaviṣaprahārapatananihsaṃjñānāṃ punaḥpratyānayanāya ||
14410| manuṣyāṇāmakṣamātram, gavāśvānāṃ dviguṇam, caturguṇaṃ hastyuṣṭrāṇām ||
14411| rukmagarbhaścaiṣāṃ maṇiḥ sarvaviṣaharaḥ ||
14412| jīvantīśvetāmuṣkakapuṣpavandākānāmakṣīve jātasyāśvatthasya maṇiḥ sarvaviṣaharaḥ ||
14413  tūryāṇāṃ taiḥ praliptānāṃ śabdo viṣavināśanaḥ |
14413  liptadhvajaṃ patākāṃ vā dṛṣṭvā bhavati nirviṣaḥ ||
14414  etaiḥ kṛtvā pratīkāraṃ svasainyānāmathātmanaḥ |
14414  amitreṣu prayuñjīta viṣadhūmāmbudūṣaṇān ||E

(tantrayuktayah)
15101| manuṣyāṇāṃ vṛttirarthaḥ, manuṣyavatī bhūmirityarthaḥ ||
15102| tasyāḥ pṛthivyā lābhapālanopāyaḥ śāstramarthaśāstramiti ||
15103| taddvātriṃśadyuktiyuktaṃ - adhikaraṇam, vidhānam, yogaḥ, padārthaḥ, hetvarthaḥ, uddeśaḥ, nirdeśaḥ, upadeśaḥ, apadeśaḥ, atideśaḥ, pradeśaḥ, upamānam, arthāpattiḥ, saṃśayaḥ, prasaṅgaḥ, viparyayaḥ, vākyaśeṣaḥ, anumatam, vyākhyānam, nirvacanam, nidarśanam, apavargaḥ, svasaṃjñā, pūrvapakṣaḥ, uttarapakṣaḥ, ekāntaḥ, anāgatāvekṣaṇam,
atikrāntāvekṣaṇam, niyogaḥ, vikalpaḥ, samuccayaḥ ūhyamiti ||
15104| yamarthamadhikṛtyocyate tadadhikaraṇam ||
15105| "pṛthivyā lābhe pālane ca yāvantyarthaśāstrāṇi pūrvācāryaiḥ prasthāpitāni prāyaśastāni saṃhṛtyaikamidamarthaśāstraṃ kṛtam" iti ||
15106| śāstrasya prakaraṇānupūrvī vidhānam ||
15107| "vidyāsamuddeśaḥ, vṛddhasamyogaḥ, indriyajayaḥ, amātyotpattiḥ" ityevaṃādikamiti ||
15108| vākyayojanā yogaḥ ||
15109| "caturvarṇāśramo lokaḥ" iti ||
15110| padāvadhikaḥ padārthaḥ ||
15111| mūlahara iti padam ||
15112| "yaḥ pitṛpaitāmahamarthamanyāyena bhakṣayati sa mūlaharaḥ" ityarthaḥ ||
15113| heturarthasādhako hetvarthaḥ ||
15114| "arthamūlau hi dharmakāmau" iti ||
15115| samāsavākyamuddeśaḥ ||
15116| "vidyāvinayaheturindriyajayaḥ" iti ||
15117| vyāsavākyaṃ nirdeśaḥ ||
15118| "karṇatvagakṣijihvāghrāṇendriyāṇāṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandheṣvavipratipattirindriyajayaḥ"iti ||
15119| evaṃ vartitavyamityupadeśaḥ ||
15120| "dharmārthavirodhena kāmaṃ seveta, na nihsukhaḥ syāt" iti ||
15121| evamasāvāhetyapadeśaḥ ||
15122| "mantripariṣadaṃ dvādaśāmātyān kurvīteti mānavāḥ - ṣoḍaśeti bārhaspatyāḥ - viṃśatimityauśanasāḥ - yathāsāmarthyamiti kauṭilyaḥ" iti ||
15123| uktena sādhanamatideśaḥ ||
15124| "dattasyāpradānamṛṇādānena vyākhyātam" iti ||
15125| vaktavyena sādhanaṃ pradeśaḥ ||
15126| "sāmadānabhedadaṇḍairvā, yathāpatsu vyākhyāsyāmaḥ" iti ||
15127| dṛṣṭenādṛṣṭasya sādhanamupamānam ||
15128| "nivṛttaparihārān pitevānugṛhṇīyāt" iti ||
15129| yadanuktamarthādāpadyate sārthāpattiḥ ||
15130| "lokayātrāvidrājānamātmadravyaprakṛtisampannaṃ priyahitadvāreṇāśrayeta"
15131| "nāpriyahitadvāreṇāśrayeta" ityarthādāpannaṃ bhavatīti ||
15132| ubhayatohetumānarthaḥ saṃśayaḥ ||
15133| "kṣīṇalubdhaprakṛtimapacaritaprakṛtiṃ vā" iti ||
15134| prakaraṇāntareṇa samānoarthaḥ prasaṅgaḥ ||
15135| "kṛṣikarmapradiṣṭāyāṃ bhūmau - iti samānaṃ pūrveṇa" iti ||
15136| pratilomena sādhanaṃ viparyayaḥ ||
15137| "viparītamatuṣṭasya" iti ||
15138| yena vākyaṃ samāpyate sa vākyaśeṣaḥ ||
15139| "chinnapakṣasyeva rājñaśceṣṭānāśaśca" iti ||
15140| tatra "śakuneḥ" iti vākyaśeṣaḥ ||
15141| paravākyamapratiṣiddhamanumatam ||
15142| "pakṣāvurasyaṃ pratigraha ityauśanaso vyūhavibhāgaḥ" iti ||
15143| atiśayavarṇanā vyākhyānam ||
15144| "viśeṣataśca saṃghānāṃ saṃghadharmiṇāṃ ca rājakulānāṃ dyūtanimitto bhedastannimitto vināśa ityasatpragrahaḥ pāpiṣṭhatamo vyasanānāṃ tantradaurbalyāt" iti ||
15145| guṇataḥ śabdaniṣpattirnirvacanam ||
15146| "vyasyatyenaṃ śreyasa iti vyasanam" iti ||
15147| dṛṣṭānto dṛṣṭāntayukto nidarśanam ||
15148| "vigṛhīto hi jyāyasā hastinā pādayuddhamivābhyupaiti" iti ||
15149| abhiplutavyapakarṣaṇamapavargaḥ ||
15150| "nityamāsannamaribalaṃ vāsayedanyatrābhyantarakopaśaṅkāyāḥ" iti ||
15151| parairasamitaḥ śabdaḥ svasaṃjñā ||
15152| "prathamā prakṛtiḥ, tasya bhūmyanantarā dvitīyā, bhūmyekāntarā tṛtīyā" iti ||
15153| pratiṣeddhavyaṃ vākyaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ ||
15154| "svāmyamātyavyasanayoramātyavyasanaṃ garīyaḥ" iti ||
15155| tasya nirṇayanavākyamuttarapakṣaḥ ||
15156| "tadāyattatvāt, tatkūṭasthānīyo hi svāmī" iti ||
15157| sarvatrāyattamekāntaḥ ||
15158| "tasmādutthānamātmanaḥ kurvīta" iti ||
15159| paścādevaṃ vihitamityanāgatāvekṣaṇam ||
15160| "tulāpratimānaṃ pautavādhyakṣe vakṣyāmaḥ" iti ||
15161| purastādevaṃ vihitamityatikrāntāveṣkaṇam ||
15162| "amātyasampaduktā purastāt" iti ||
15163| evaṃ nānyatheti niyogaḥ ||
15164| "tasmāddharmyamarthyaṃ cāsyopadiśet, nādharmyamanarthayṃ ca" iti ||
15165| anena vānena veti vikalpaḥ ||
15166| "duhitaro vā dharmiṣṭheṣu vivāheṣu jātāḥ" iti ||
15167| anena cānena ceti samuccayaḥ ||
15168| "svayaṃjātaḥ piturbandhūnāṃ ca dāyādaḥ" iti ||
15169| anuktakaraṇamūhyam ||
15170| "yathā ca dātā pratigrahītā ca nopahatau syātāṃ tathānuśayaṃ kuśalāḥ kalpayeyuḥ" iti
15171  evaṃ śāstramidaṃ yuktametābhistantrayuktibhiḥ |
15171  avāptau pālane coktaṃ lokasyāsya parasya ca ||
15172  dharmamarthaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca pravartayati pāti ca |
15172  adharmānarthavidveṣānidaṃ śāstraṃ nihanti ca ||
15173  yena śāstraṃ ca śastraṃ ca nandarājagatā ca bhūḥ |
15173  amarṣeṇoddhṛtānyāśu tena śāstramidaṃ kṛtam ||
